*Yuexuan Wu,Yang Xiang,Hamid Laga,Dip Das,Anuj Srivastava,Zhengwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证器引导的增强框架，利用全局和模式局部PCA生成低成本候选样本，通过基于对应关系的骨骼比例和身体部位几何进行筛选，并在接受的候选样本上重新训练扩散模型。弹性配准提供分布式PCA所用的模式结构以及用于可扩展筛选的密集解剖对应关系，无需对每个候选样本进行人体模型拟合。盲测人类研究支持验证器作为保守把关者，偏好验证器接受而非拒绝的输出。在4,498个注册的DFAUST表面数据上，分布式PCA增强实现了86.32%的接受率，最高的CP-AUC（0.871）和最高的EAUC（0.752），相比仅真实数据和自增强扩散模型，EAUC提升32%。结果表明，模式局部、验证器引导的提议在保持与校准身体测量高度一致的同时，扩大了扩散生成的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D人体生成模型受限于训练数据多样性，导致模型过于保守或生成违反基本人体比例的结果。需要一种方法在不牺牲人体合理性前提下扩展生成多样性。

Method: 采用全局与模式局部主成分分析（PCA）生成候选样本；通过弹性配准获取解剖对应关系与模式结构；利用基于对应关系的骨骼比例和身体部件几何进行筛选；对通过验证的样本重新训练扩散模型。

Result: 在4,498个注册的DFAUST表面数据上，分布式PCA增强实现86.32%接受率，最高CP-AUC（0.871）和最高EAUC（0.752），相比仅真实数据和自增强扩散模型，EAUC提升32%。验证器有效控制生成质量并提升多样性。

Conclusion: 模式局部、验证器引导的生成策略能够在保持与真实人体测量高度一致的前提下，显著拓展扩散模型的生成能力，为高质量3D人体生成提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Limited training data diversity constrains generative modeling of 3D human bodies: conservative models remain close to observed examples, whereas exploratory models often violate basic body proportions. We introduce a verifier-guided augmentation framework that uses global and mode-local PCA to generate inexpensive candidates, screens them using correspondence-derived skeletal proportions and body-part geometry, and retrains a diffusion model on accepted candidates. Elastic registration provides both the modal structure used by distributed PCA and the dense anatomical correspondence needed for scalable screening without per-candidate body-model fitting. A blinded human study supports the verifier as a conservative gatekeeper, favoring verifier-accepted over rejected outputs. We evaluate full-pool verifier acceptance separately from the coverage and departure of accepted samples and combine them through EAUC. On 4,498 registered DFAUST surfaces, distributed-PCA augmentation achieves 86.32% acceptance, the highest CP-AUC (0.871), and the highest EAUC (0.752), improving EAUC by 32% over real-only and self-augmented diffusion. These results show that mode-local, verifier-guided proposals broaden diffusion generation while maintaining high agreement with calibrated body measurements.

</details>


### [16] [VolS-GS: Relightable Gaussian Splatting with Volumetric Subsurface Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04007)
*Junyeong Ahn,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VolS-GS 是一种基于单光位捕获的可重光照高斯点云框架，通过引入可微分的有限体积传输求解器，实现光线在物体内部的传播，有效建模次表面散射等非局部效应。该方法利用小网络预测每个高斯体的散射与吸收系数，并通过求解器重新分布入射光，从而生成更真实的光照效果。为避免阴影和镜面反射项主导，设计了基于可见性与透射率的阴影预测机制及正则化策略抑制未照亮区域的高光。在多个单光位基准测试中，该方法在新光照与视角下的重光照质量显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可重光照高斯点云方法通常在每个基本体上独立建模外观，难以有效表示非局部光照效应（如次表面散射），尤其当光线进入物体某处后从另一位置出射时。传统方法依赖神经网络或局部核函数处理此类现象，但无法捕捉光在物体内部的连续传播路径。因此需要一种能结合几何结构与物理传播机制的新方法来提升重光照真实感。

Method: 提出 VolS-GS 框架，将高斯场景的空间支持作为可微分有限体积传输求解器的域，使光线可在物体内部传播；使用小型神经网络预测每个高斯体的散射与吸收系数；通过求解器根据这些系数重新分布入射光，形成运输形状的外观聚合路径；设计基于可见性、透射率和散射结果的阴影项，并引入正则化抑制未照亮区域的镜面高光。

Result: 在三个单光位捕获（OLAT）基准数据集上的实验表明，VolS-GS 在未见光照和视角下均显著优于现有方法，提升了重光照的真实感与视觉质量，特别是在处理次表面散射等复杂光学现象时表现突出。

Conclusion: VolS-GS 通过将高斯点云与可微分物理传输模型相结合，成功解决了传统方法在建模非局部光照效应方面的局限，实现了高质量、可推广的重光照渲染，为动态光照下的三维重建与可视化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present VolS-GS, a relightable Gaussian splatting framework that reconstructs objects from one-light-at-a-time (OLAT) captures and renders them under novel lighting and viewpoints. Relightable Gaussian Splatting methods typically model appearance independently at each primitive, which makes non-local effects difficult to represent. This limitation is particularly apparent for subsurface scattering, where light entering the object at one location can emerge at another. Rather than modeling this effect solely with a neural network or a local kernel at each primitive, we use the spatial support of the Gaussian scene as the domain of a differentiable finite-volume transport solver, so that light can propagate through the object's interior. A small network predicts scattering and absorption coefficients for each Gaussian, and the solve redistributes incident light through the resulting field. The coefficients are fit to images rather than measured, so the solve supplies a transport-shaped path for aggregating per-primitive appearance, not a measurement of the material. To keep the learned shadow and specular terms from taking over the other components, our shadow term is predicted from visibility together with the transmittances and the scattering the solve produces, and a regularizer suppresses specular highlights in regions the shadow term predicts to be unlit. Experiments on three OLAT benchmarks show that VolS-GS consistently improves relighting quality on held-out lights and views.

</details>


### [17] [PhysMamba: Selective State Space Models as Learned Articulated Body Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04014)
*Haochuan Zhang,Sinisa Todorovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMamba 是首个基于选择性状态空间模型（SSM）的可学习关节体模拟器，能从位置、旋转和关节动作历史预测下一帧全身状态，无需速度输入。在部分观测下，Mamba2 在从头训练协议中表现出色（s10 = 43 mm，仅2/50发散），而两阶段教师引导训练使GRU稳定但对Mamba2无效。结合CUDA图编译，Mamba2 实现每帧0.107毫秒（9,334 FPS），仅比未编译的GRU慢1.1倍，并在30 Hz HMR流水线中增加不到1%延迟，可作为可微物理模块集成用于视频驱动的网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理模拟器在关节体建模中依赖复杂动力学或需速度输入，难以高效集成到端到端视频重建流程中。本文旨在开发一个轻量、高效且可微分的关节体模拟器，以支持实时视频驱动的全身运动建模。

Method: 提出基于选择性状态空间模型（SSM）的PhysMamba，利用位置、旋转和关节动作历史进行全身体态预测；对比四种架构在部分与完整观测下的表现，评估三种训练策略，采用从头训练与两阶段教师引导训练，并结合CUDA图编译优化推理性能。

Result: Mamba2在部分观测下实现高精度（s10 = 43 mm）和良好稳定性（2/50发散），在H100 GPU上达到9,334 FPS，延迟增加不足1%，可无缝集成至30 Hz HMR系统中作为可微物理模块。

Conclusion: PhysMamba成功构建了一个高效、可微、实时的关节体模拟器，其基于SSM的架构在精度与速度之间取得良好平衡，为视频驱动的全身运动建模提供了强大工具。

Abstract: We introduce the first learned articulated body simulator based on a selective state space model (SSM), called PhysMamba. PhysMamba predicts next-frame full-body state from position, rotation, and joint-action history, without velocity inputs. We compare four architectures under partial- and full-observation inputs and three training protocols. The from-scratch rollout training protocol gives Mamba2 strong short- and mid-horizon accuracy under partial observation (s10 = 43 mm, 2/50 diverged), while the two-stage teacher-based rollout protocol stabilizes GRU but fails for Mamba2. With CUDA graph compilation, Mamba2 reaches 0.107 ms per frame (9,334 FPS) on an H100 GPU, within 1.1$\times$ of GRU's un-compiled throughput, adding under 1% latency to a 30 Hz HMR pipeline and enabling integration as a differentiable physics module for video-based mesh recovery.

</details>


### [18] [ClasSAE: Class-Aligned Sparse Autoencoders via Differentiable Feature-Class Affinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04020)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClasSAE 是一种新型稀疏自编码器方法，通过在训练过程中自动为特征分配类别并引导编码器学习可区分的类别表示，解决了现有方法依赖后验评分进行特征-概念匹配的问题。它利用可微分的 top-k 操作和可训练的特征-类别亲和矩阵，使特征选择与类别目标耦合，并实现编码器与亲和矩阵的协同优化。该方法无需后验探测即可获得可解释、类分离且带类别标注的字典，在 ImageNet 上使用 CLIP ViT-L/14 嵌入验证了其有效性，支持直接从编码器和亲和矩阵进行类别预测，并提升了目标探针扰动评估中的分离性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAEs）在主动干预任务中面临特征与目标概念难以可靠匹配的问题，主要依赖于训练后对冻结字典的后验评分，缺乏在训练阶段对类别可分性的显式建模。为此，需要一种能自动标注特征类别并引导编码器学习类可分表示的方法，以提升可解释性和干预效果。

Method: 提出 ClasSAE，引入可微分 top-k 操作与可训练的特征-类别亲和矩阵，结合每特征预算约束，将样本激活的特征与其所属类别绑定；通过梯度反向传播至特征选择过程，实现编码器与亲和矩阵的联合优化，从而在训练中自然生成类可分且带类别标签的特征字典。设计三种变体以平衡稀疏性与性能。

Result: 在 ImageNet 使用 CLIP ViT-L/14 嵌入的实验表明，所学亲和矩阵与独立估计的后验特征-类别矩阵高度一致；模型可仅凭编码器与亲和矩阵完成直接类别预测，无需额外分类器；且其更类对齐的编码器在目标探针扰动评估中表现出更强的表示分离能力。

Conclusion: ClasSAE 通过联合优化特征选择与类别亲和，实现了无需后验探测的自动类别标注与类可分表示学习，显著提升了稀疏自编码器在概念编辑、偏见缓解等主动干预任务中的可解释性与实用性。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) began as an unsupervised tool for decomposing neural representations into sparse, interpretable features, and are increasingly used not only for passive analysis but also for active interventions such as unlearning, bias mitigation, and concept editing. A central challenge for these editing and steering methods is reliably matching features to target concepts; most current approaches address this by computing post-hoc scores over an already-trained, frozen dictionary. We instead introduce ClasSAE, a novel method that both automatically assigns classes to features and guides the encoder toward class-separable representations during training. Specifically, we apply a differentiable top-$k$ operator to a trainable feature--class affinity matrix with per-feature budgets, coupling the features selected for each sample to the classes they are trained to represent. Because gradients flow through the selection of active features rather than only through their magnitudes, the encoder and the affinity matrix co-adapt rather than being fit in separate stages. The result is a dictionary that is both class-separable and class-annotated, with no need for post-hoc probing. We propose three variants for enforcing sparsity within this framework, which achieve comparable overall performance with slightly different trade-offs. Using CLIP ViT-L/14 embeddings on ImageNet, we show that the learned affinity matrix agrees closely with an independently estimated post-hoc feature-class matrix computed on held-out data. The model also supports direct class prediction from the encoder and affinity matrix alone, without a separately fitted classifier, and its more class-aligned encoder yields improved separation in Targeted Probe Perturbation evaluations. https://github.com/St0pien/ClasSAE.

</details>


### [19] [Learning Subject-Specific Anatomical Representations via Manifold Expansion: Application to Accelerated Multi-Contrast MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04028)
*Ruimin Feng,Wanyu Bian,Albert Jang,Zachary Stewart,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAX提出一种基于受试者特异性框架的多对比度MRI加速重建方法，通过从单个高分辨率参考对比度学习与对比度无关的解剖表示，利用保持解剖结构的强度增强扩展多对比度流形，并采用解耦隐式神经表示分离解剖与对比度特征。该方法在脑部和膝关节MRI中分别于8倍和6倍加速下实现了最高的平均PSNR和SSIM，显著优于现有基线，且对运动、结构异质性和噪声具有鲁棒性，适用于多种参考辅助的MRI逆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前加速MRI方法通常独立重建各对比度图像，未充分利用不同对比度间的共享解剖信息，导致重建质量受限。为提升多对比度加速MRI的重建性能，需学习与对比度无关的解剖表示以实现跨对比度信息共享。

Method: 提出MAX（MAnifold eXpansion）框架，通过单个完全采样的参考对比度图像学习解剖表示；使用解剖保持的强度增强扩展多对比度流形；构建解耦的隐式神经表示，其中空间坐标对应解剖结构，非空间坐标编码对比度外观；固定解剖表示后，适应目标欠采样数据并进行迭代优化。

Result: 在脑部MRI（R=8）和膝关节MRI（R=6）上，MAX在所有任务中均取得最高平均PSNR和SSIM，PSNR提升超过1 dB，更准确恢复细微解剖与病理结构，对运动、结构差异和噪声具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MAX提供了一种有效利用高质量参考扫描的通用策略，可推广至其他参考辅助的MRI逆问题，显著提升加速多对比度MRI的重建性能。

Abstract: Clinical MRI routinely acquires multiple contrast-weighted images of the same anatomy for complementary tissue characterization. However, current accelerated MRI methods typically reconstruct each contrast independently, without fully exploiting shared anatomical information. This work aims to learn anatomical representations invariant to contrast-dependent appearance for reconstruction of accelerated multi-contrast MRI. We propose MAX (MAnifold eXpansion), a subject-specific framework that learns anatomical representations from a single fully sampled reference contrast. To address the under-constrained separation of shared anatomy and contrast-dependent components from a single image, MAX expands the multi-contrast manifold using anatomy-preserving intensity augmentations. A disentangled implicit neural representation models augmented samples using shared spatial coordinates for anatomy and spatially invariant coordinates for contrast appearance. The learned anatomical representation is then fixed, with the contrast representation adapted to the undersampled target data, followed by unrolled refinement. Theoretical analyses further provide insight into the disentangled representation learning and explain how the learned anatomical representation improves the target contrast reconstruction. At R = 8 for brain MRI and R = 6 for knee MRI, MAX achieves the highest mean PSNR and SSIM across all tasks, improving PSNR by more than 1 dB over the strongest baseline for both brain contrasts. MAX more faithfully recovers subtle anatomical and pathological structures and remains robust to inter-contrast motion, structural heterogeneity between reference and target contrasts, and measurement noise. Therefore, MAX provides a general strategy for leveraging high-quality reference scans in accelerated MRI and has the potential to be extended to other reference-assisted MRI inverse problems.

</details>


### [20] [MAGEFormer: Learning Metric-Consistent Representations for Anisotropic CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04036)
*Jiaying Li,Paolo Remagnino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGEFormer 是一种几何校准框架，通过引入度量自适应空间嵌入（MASE）、几何约束注意力（GCA）和几何视图投票（GVV），解决视觉变换器在临床CT中因各向异性采样导致的度量不匹配和精度下降问题，在BTCV和FLARE 22数据集上均实现了领先的边界准确率和Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉变换器（ViT）在体积分割中表现优异，但其基于各向同性欧几里得网格的假设与临床CT的各向异性采集方式冲突，导致体素索引与解剖结构之间的度量不匹配以及因各向同性重采样引起的性能下降。

Method: 提出MAGEFormer框架，包含三项核心技术：1）度量自适应空间嵌入（MASE），根据体素间距校准位置频率；2）几何约束注意力（GCA），抑制物理上不合理的特征关联；3）几何视图投票（GVV），在推理阶段减少离散化偏差。

Result: 在BTCV和FLARE 22两个多器官腹部CT基准测试中，MAGEFormer在统一评估协议下优于多种强基线模型，取得10.58 mm HD95（BTCV）和3.40 mm HD95（FLARE 22）的边界精度，并在相同条件下持续提升Dice分数。

Conclusion: 几何感知的内部校准比依赖传统各向同性预处理更有效，适用于各向异性CT分割任务。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have shown strong performance in volumetric segmentation, but their effectiveness on clinical CT is limited by an isotropic Euclidean lattice assumption. This conflicts with anisotropic CT acquisition, leading to two key issues: (1) a metric mismatch between voxel indices and physical anatomy, and (2) accuracy degradation from isotropic resampling. To address this, we propose MAGEFormer, a geometry-calibrated framework that embeds physical metric constraints directly into representation learning. Our method introduces Metric-Adaptive Spatial Embedding (MASE) to calibrate positional frequencies using voxel spacing, Geometry-Constrained Attention (GCA) to suppress physically implausible feature correlations, and Geometric View Voting (GVV) to reduce discretization bias during inference. We evaluate MAGEFormer on two multi-organ abdominal CT benchmarks, BTCV and FLARE 22, under a unified protocol against strong CNN and Transformer-based baselines. MAGEFormer achieves the strongest boundary accuracy among the compared methods, with 10.58 mm HD95 on BTCV and 3.40 mm HD95 on FLARE 22, and shows consistent gains in Dice under the same protocol. These results show that geometry-aware internal calibration is more effective than relying on conventional isotropic preprocessing alone for anisotropic CT segmentation.

</details>


### [21] [Dynamic Quadtree Tokenization and Transformer for Adaptive Mesh PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04044)
*Yilin Zhuang,Noah Zambrano,Karthik Duraisamy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAMRViT 是一种基于视觉变换器（ViT）的新型模型，通过波浪函数启发的自适应网格细化（AMR）策略，以平衡四叉树形式对输入进行分块，结合3D旋转位置嵌入编码位置与细化级别，并在推理过程中于单元空间中重新网格化，实现稳定的长时程滚动预测。其多尺度变体保留每个叶节点在其原始源分辨率下，让模型学习跨分辨率层级。相比固定预算的自适应分块方法，WAMRViT 不设定预定义的令牌数量，支持在整个自回归滚动过程中动态自适应拓扑结构。该模型是首个原生处理多级自适应网格细化数据的机器学习代理模型。在均匀网格基准测试中，其细粒度区域误差显著优于传统统一分块ViT，同时使用更少令牌；多尺度版本在全场和精细区域上均取得最低初始步误差，并在长时程滚动中保持更高精度。结合并行重网格化，其端到端滚动成本介于最细粒度与近似令牌匹配的粗粒度ViT之间。在复杂燃烧问题中，由于传统基线无法表示最细特征，而WAMRViT可直接处理自适应单元，显著降低相同模型容量下的最细级别误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers (ViTs) 的二次注意力开销限制了空间分辨率与时间滚动范围之间的权衡，尤其在涉及小尺度、动态变化区域的微分方程（PDEs）模拟中，如冲击波、反应前沿和材料界面等。传统神经网络代理缺乏动态调整分辨率的能力。因此，亟需一种能够自适应地根据物理特征分布调节网格分辨率并高效建模的新型架构。

Method: 提出WAMRViT，采用波浪函数启发的细化准则将输入数据以平衡四叉树方式分块；引入3D旋转位置嵌入联合编码空间位置与细化层级信息；在推理阶段执行单元空间中的重网格化以保障稳定性；设计多尺度变体，使每个叶节点保持其原始分辨率，允许模型在不同分辨率层级间学习；整个过程不预设令牌数量，支持完全自适应的拓扑结构演化。

Result: 在均匀网格基准上，统一分块的WAMRViT在保持更少令牌的同时，显著降低了精细区域的虚拟真实均方误差（VRMSE）；多尺度版本在所有基准上均达到最低的初始步全域和精细区域RMSE；在长时程滚动中，其平均全域与精细区域精度表现优异；在并行重网格化支持下，端到端滚动计算成本位于最细粒度与近似令牌匹配的粗粒度ViT之间；对于复杂自适应网格燃烧问题，传统均匀网格基线无法捕捉最细特征，而WAMRViT能直接作用于自适应单元，大幅降低相同模型容量下的最细级别误差。

Conclusion: WAMRViT 是首个原生支持多级自适应网格细化（AMR）数据的机器学习代理模型，通过自适应分块、动态拓扑建模与重网格化机制，在保证高精度的同时有效缓解了传统ViT的空间分辨率与滚动长度之间的权衡问题。其在复杂非均匀物理系统中展现出优越性能，为下一代物理信息神经网络提供了新范式。

Abstract: The quadratic attention cost of Vision Transformers (ViTs) forces a trade-off between spatial resolution and rollout horizon, particularly for fine-scale PDEs where shocks, reaction fronts, and material interfaces occupy small, evolving regions of the domain. Conventional neural surrogates also lack mechanisms to adapt resolution dynamically. We propose WAMRViT, a ViT that tokenizes inputs as balanced quadtrees using a wavelet-inspired refinement criterion, jointly encodes position and refinement level with 3D rotary positional embeddings, and regrids in cell space during inference for stable long-horizon rollouts. A multi-scale variant retains each leaf at its native source resolution and lets the model learn across resolution levels. Unlike fixed-budget adaptive-tokenization methods, WAMRViT imposes no predetermined token count and supports fully adaptive topology throughout autoregressive rollout. To our knowledge, it is the first machine-learning surrogate to natively tokenize multi-level Adaptive Mesh Refinement (AMR) data. On uniform-grid benchmarks, uniform-patch WAMRViT improves finest-level region-of-interest VRMSE over a finest-patch uniform ViT while using substantially fewer tokens. The multi-scale variant achieves the lowest first-step full-field RMSE and VRMSE on both benchmarks and improves rollout-averaged full-field and refined-region accuracy at long horizons. With parallelized regridding, its end-to-end rollout cost lies between finest-patch and approximately token-matched coarser-patch ViTs. On a complex AMR combustion problem whose finest features cannot be represented natively by the evaluated uniform-grid baselines, WAMRViT operates directly on adaptive cells and substantially reduces finest-level error at matched transformer capacity. Code: https://github.com/tonyzyl/wamrvit

</details>


### [22] [ASD-FEAT: A Multi-Modal Infant Video-Derived Dataset for Early ASD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04051)
*Sidrah Liaqat,Halil Helvaci,Sen-Ching Cheung,Chongruo Wu,Dongjie Chen,Chen Nee Chuah,Sally Ozonoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASD-FEAT 是一个纵向覆盖婴儿期至36个月的多模态数据集，包含重复互动会话、临床验证的发展结果、专家级帧级行为标注和隐私友好的多模态特征表示。该数据集用于评估基于计算机视觉的端到端自动识别自闭症风险的机器学习方法，实现了76.2%的分类准确率和0.82的AUROC；引入‘访视内伙伴对比’信号后，性能进一步提升，超越人工标注基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期准确筛查自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）对及时干预和改善认知行为结果至关重要。现有数据集缺乏在大规模下同时具备纵向覆盖、重复会话、临床验证结果、精细标注和多模态特征的综合能力，因此需要构建更全面的数据集以支持自动化检测方法的研究。

Method: 构建 ASD-FEAT 多模态数据集，整合面部与眼动地标、面部动作单元、注视方向、头部位置、梅尔频谱音频表示及光流等特征，采用端到端机器学习框架进行自动行为分析，并引入‘访视内伙伴对比’机制增强模型对社交行为差异的捕捉能力。

Result: 自动化系统在无专家标注情况下达到76.2%准确率和0.82 AUROC；加入‘访视内伙伴对比’后，马修相关系数分别提升至0.49（Look Face + Smile）和0.45（Look Face + Vocal），超过人工标注基线的0.42。

Conclusion: ASD-FEAT 为自闭症早期筛查提供了高质量、多模态、可扩展的数据基础，所提出的自动化方法具有实用潜力，尤其在引入情境化对比信号后显著提升了性能，推动了非侵入式智能辅助诊断的发展。

Abstract: Accurate early screening for Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) is a precursor to timely intervention, which is critical for improving cognitive and behavioral outcomes. We present ASD-FEAT (ASD - Feature Extraction And Tracking), a multimodal dataset derived from video recordings of infant-adult interaction sessions. The key contribution of ASD-FEAT is the combination of longitudinal coverage from infancy through 36 months, repeated interaction sessions, clinically validated developmental outcomes, expert frame-level behavioral annotations, and privacy-conscious multimodal feature representations. To the best of our knowledge, existing ASD behavioral datasets do not jointly provide these characteristics at comparable scale. To demonstrate the utility of ASD-FEAT, we use it to evaluate a computer-vision-based end-to-end pipeline relying on machine learning techniques to automatically identify ASD risk. ASD-FEAT integrates both expert-defined and deep-learned features, including face and eye landmarks, facial action units, gaze direction, head position, mel-spectrogram audio representations, and optical flow, to identify behavioral markers of social interaction. Our automated pipeline achieves an ASD classification accuracy of 76.2% and an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) of 0.82, compared to classifiers trained on manually labeled behaviors, which yielded 81.3% accuracy and an AUROC of 0.88. We further introduce a within-visit partner contrast: a per-visit signal contrasting examiner-directed and parent-directed social behavior which, when added to the classifier, lifts the fully automated Look Face + Smile and Look Face + Vocal configurations to Matthews correlations of 0.49 and 0.45 respectively, exceeding the human-coded single-partner baseline of 0.42.

</details>


### [23] [A Theory of Shape Reconstruction from Heat Conduction and Shading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04052)
*Akihiko Oharazawa,Sriram Narayanan,Mani Ramanagopal,Srinivasa G. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新理论，通过结合阴影和热传导线索，解决单张图像下朗伯表面形状恢复中的局部形状模糊问题（如凸/凹歧义），无需依赖平滑性等先验知识。该方法在光照方向未知的情况下仍能同时满足阴影与拉普拉斯算子的数学约束，验证了其在复杂形状模拟、噪声环境及真实世界物体热视频上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状从阴影（SfS）在光照方向未知时存在锥形歧义，而热传导方法虽能估计形状拉普拉斯算子，但存在局部凸/凹模糊。现有方法依赖平滑性等先验，限制了精度与适用性。因此需要一种不依赖先验、可联合利用多源信息的新方法来消除局部歧义。

Method: 结合阴影模型与热传导方程，建立统一优化框架，通过同时满足光照反射模型与热扩散的数学约束，求解表面法向量，从而消除局部形状歧义。关键在于设计一个能兼容两种物理模型的正则化机制，实现无先验条件下的精确形状恢复。

Result: 所提方法在复杂形状仿真中成功消除了局部凸/凹歧义；在含噪声条件下保持稳定；在真实世界的单帧热视频上，对具有复杂几何结构和不同反照率的物体实现了高精度形状重建。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种新颖且鲁棒的形状恢复框架，通过融合阴影与热传导信息，在无光照先验和无平滑性假设的前提下，有效解决了传统方法中存在的局部形状模糊问题，为单图像三维重建提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Shape from shading using a single image of a Lam- bertian surface is inherently ambiguous. When the light source direction is known, the surface normal estimation has a cone- ambiguity, which worsens when the source is unknown. Recently, shape from heat conduction has emerged as an approach that leverages heat transport equations to estimate the Shape Lapla- cian operator, an intrinsic measure of shape. However, deriving surface normals from the Laplacian operator encounters a local binary convex/concave ambiguity. Our contribution introduces a novel theory to resolve these local shape ambiguities (excluding a few degeneracies) without relying on priors like smoothness, by combining the cues from shading and heat conduction. Our method ensures the mathematical constraints of both shading and the Laplacian are satisfied simultaneously, even with an unknown light source. We validate our theory through simulations of complex shapes and analyze its performance in the presence of noise, as well as on a noisy single thermal video of real-world objects with complex shapes and material properties, including varying albedo.

</details>


### [24] [Evaluating Zone-Guided Front Extraction for Glacier Calving-Front Delineation in SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04066)
*Chhaya Kulkarni,Emam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了直接预测冰川裂解前沿与基于区域引导的前沿提取方法在合成孔径雷达图像中的表现。使用U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer-B0模型，在相同的裁剪边界框设置下评估了两种策略：一种是直接预测二值裂解前沿掩码，另一种是先预测四个语义区域类别，再从预测的冰-海边界中提取前沿。结果表明，尽管基于真实区域标签提取的前沿误差最低，但由模型预测的区域标签生成的前沿仍表现不佳，即使区域分割性能中等。测试时适应性也未能稳定提升性能。因此，区域分割性能不能替代前沿级别的评估，有效利用区域标签可能需要边界感知训练、标签融合或显式前沿监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰川裂解前沿在合成孔径雷达图像中通常表现为模糊且细长的边界，难以准确识别。现有的CaFFe数据集提供了二值裂解前沿掩码和更广泛的语义区域掩码，这为探索是否可以用区域级监督支持前沿恢复提供了可能。然而，当前方法在利用区域信息提取前沿方面仍存在局限，亟需系统评估不同策略的有效性。

Method: 采用三种主流分割模型（U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer-B0），在相同裁剪的CaFFe数据集上进行对比实验。分为两个设置：1）直接前端预测，即模型输出二值裂解前沿掩码；2）区域引导的前沿提取，即模型先预测四个语义区域类别，然后从预测的冰-海洋边界中提取前沿。评估包括区域级别和前沿级别的性能，并进行真实区域边界检查，同时测试轻量级测试时适应性在传感器特异性和冰川特异性子集上的效果。

Result: 基于真实区域标签提取的前沿具有最低的平均距离误差，表明区域标签包含有用的信息。然而，由模型预测的区域标签生成的前沿性能仍然较弱，即使区域分割得分中等。测试时适应性未能一致改善区域引导的前沿恢复效果。结果说明，区域分割表现不能作为前沿水平评价的替代指标。要有效利用区域标签，可能需要边界感知训练、标签融合或显式前沿监督机制。

Conclusion: 区域标签虽蕴含前沿边界信息，但仅靠区域分割模型难以有效转化为高质量的前沿预测。未来工作应关注如何通过边界感知学习、标签融合或引入显式前沿监督来增强区域引导的前沿提取能力，避免将区域性能误认为前沿性能。

Abstract: Automatic calving-front delineation from synthetic aperture radar imagery is challenging because the front is a thin and often ambiguous boundary between glacier ice, ocean, and surrounding rock or terrain. The CAlving Fronts and where to Find thEm (CaFFe) dataset provides both binary calving-front masks and broader semantic zone masks, making it possible to study whether zone-level supervision can support front recovery. In this paper, we compare direct front prediction with zone-guided front extraction using U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer-B0 under the same bounding-box-cropped CaFFe setting. In the direct setting, models predict the binary calving-front mask. In the zone-guided setting, the model first predicts four semantic zone classes, and the front is then extracted from the predicted glacier-ocean boundary. We evaluate both zone-level and front-level performance, include a ground-truth-zone boundary check, and examine lightweight test-time adaptation on sensor-specific and glacier-specific subsets. The results show that zone labels contain useful front-boundary information: extracting the front from ground-truth zones gives the lowest mean distance error. However, fronts extracted from model-predicted zones remain weak, even when zone segmentation scores are moderate. Test-time adaptation also does not consistently improve zone-guided front recovery. These results indicate that zone segmentation performance should not be treated as a substitute for front-level evaluation and that effective use of zone labels may require boundary-aware training, label fusion, or explicit front supervision.

</details>


### [25] [Why Convolution Still Matters: Evaluating Inductive Biases in Cryospheric Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04073)
*Chhaya Kulkarni,Emam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了经典CNN（AlexNet）、现代CNN（ConvNeXt-Tiny）、纯注意力模型（Swin-Tiny）和混合卷积-注意力模型（CoAtNet-0）在格陵兰冰盖遥感图像分类中的表现。结果表明，尽管模型复杂度增加，但并非所有模型在细粒度纹理和类别不平衡的冰冻圈数据上表现更优；AlexNet在整体准确率和宏平均F1上最优，而混合模型在罕见和结构独特的类别上提升了召回率，卷积模型则在纹理主导类别上更可靠。研究强调模型架构的归纳偏置需与数据特征相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究注意力机制在冰冻圈遥感图像分类中的适用性，特别是面对细粒度纹理和类别不平衡问题时，现有深度学习模型的表现是否优于传统CNN。

Method: 在相同训练与评估协议下，对四种不同架构模型（AlexNet、ConvNeXt-Tiny、Swin-Tiny、CoAtNet-0）进行受控比较，基于格陵兰冰盖基准数据集进行实验分析。

Result: AlexNet在整体准确率和宏平均F1上表现最佳；ConvNeXt-Tiny具有最高的宏平均AUC，说明其类别可分性强但决策一致性较弱；混合模型提升稀有及结构独特类别的召回率，而卷积模型在纹理主导类别上更稳定可靠。

Conclusion: 模型复杂度的提升并不必然带来性能改善；在冰盖表面分类任务中，应根据数据特征选择合适的模型架构，强调归纳偏置与数据特性的匹配重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in attention-based deep learning have motivated their adoption for remote sensing image classification; however, their benefits for cryospheric imagery, where surface states are dominated by fine-grained textures and class imbalance, remain unclear. In this work, we revisit a benchmark Greenland Ice Sheet image dataset, previously shown to favor convolutional neural networks (CNNs), to examine whether modern attention-based and hybrid architectures improve class-wise reliability. We conduct a controlled comparison between a classical CNN (AlexNet), a modern CNN (ConvNeXt-Tiny), a pure attention-based model (Swin-Tiny), and a hybrid convolution-attention model (CoAtNet-0) under identical training and evaluation protocols. Results show that AlexNet achieves the highest accuracy and the strongest balanced performance as measured by macro-averaged F1, while ConvNeXt-Tiny exhibits the highest macro-averaged AUC, indicating strong class separability but less consistent final decision quality. Class-wise analysis reveals that hybrid architectures improve recall for rare and structurally distinct surface classes, whereas convolutional models remain more reliable for texture-dominated categories. These findings highlight the importance of aligning architectural inductive bias with cryospheric data characteristics and suggest that increased model complexity does not necessarily translate to improved reliability for ice-sheet surface classification.

</details>


### [26] [VCURF: Virtual Camera-based Uncertainty of Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04076)
*Liyan Chen,Nathaniel Burgdorfer,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VCURF方法，通过在目标视角附近采样虚拟相机并测量渲染不一致性来实现辐射场模型的像素级不确定性量化。该方法无需修改模型即可适用于NeRF和GS等不同辐射场模型，实验验证了其在多种数据集和模型上的有效性，并对现有视图选择方法提出了质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辐射场模型生成的视图虽逼真但存在误差，缺乏有效的像素级不确定性量化方法，尤其在视图选择方面存在不足。

Method: 通过在目标视角附近采样多个虚拟相机，利用辐射场模型渲染多视角图像，基于渲染结果的不一致性计算像素级不确定性，将模型视为黑箱，不依赖具体结构。

Result: VCURF在多种数据集、辐射场模型（包括NeRF和GS）及基线方法上均表现出良好的不确定性估计性能，有效识别出高误差区域。

Conclusion: VCURF为辐射场模型提供了通用且高效的像素级不确定性评估手段，同时揭示了当前视图选择策略可能存在的问题，呼吁重新审视相关研究范式。

Abstract: Radiance fields, implemented with either implicit (NeRF) or explicit (Gaussian Splatting) representations, are advancing the state of the art in novel view synthesis at a rapid pace. Even though the rendered views they generate are often compelling, they are not free of errors. In this paper, we propose a new approach for pixel-wise uncertainty quantification based on measuring the inconsistencies among renderings by the radiance field model in virtual cameras sampled near the target viewpoint. We named our approach VCURF for Virtual Camera-based Uncertainty of Radiance Fields. VCURF treats the radiance field model as a black box, only assuming that it is capable of rendering color and depth on demand. This property makes our approach applicable to both NeRF and GS models without any modification. Our experiments on a combination of datasets, radiance field models and baselines demonstrate VCURF's effectiveness in pixel-wise uncertainty estimation. We conclude the paper with findings that question the way view selection is tackled by the majority of the current literature.

</details>


### [27] [Dependable AI-Assisted Engineering: A Formal Framework for AI Participation and Assurance in Safety-Critical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04084)
*Puxue Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个形式化框架，用于在安全关键工作流中确定生成式AI的参与和保障级别。该框架针对每个工作流单元定义了参与与保障记录，涵盖工程需求、经批准的操作形式化、适用机制、备用方案、证据义务及保证，并包含部署就绪状态。框架区分了确定性验证、统计校准准入、受权人类判断支持的AI建议、受权人类对AI产出的裁决、保留确定性工具路径以及明确不参与等不同安排，每种安排提供不同类型而非同一量级的保证。框架还分离了形式化保真度与验证器完备性，提供分阶段分类与就绪程序，并在重复群体假设下推导出比较门控AI辅助单元与现有流程的条件。研究通过一个包含17个单元的机翼翼梁结构分析工作流实例化该框架，结合确定性门控的AI生成CAD、保留的确定性计算和人类判断。AI生成的CAD程序一次性通过全部23个确定性检查，被成功接纳。当应力幅值虽有利但因预声明的网格收敛证据不足而无法满足应力标准时，相关标准转由工程判断处理。案例展示了单位级别的选择性AI参与和显式证据管理，但未宣称工作流整体的可靠性、认证、结构安全或生产率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽能生成工程产物，但其是否进入安全关键工作流及其保障方式需明确规范。现有方法缺乏对AI参与程度和保障机制的细粒度建模，尤其在多类型任务混合的复杂工作流中，难以确保可追溯性、可验证性和安全性。因此亟需一种形式化框架来精确界定AI在各工作流单元中的角色与责任边界。

Method: 提出一套形式化框架，以工作流单元为基本单位，建立包含工程需求、操作形式化、适用机制、备用方案、证据义务、保证类型及部署就绪状态的参与与保障记录。引入六类参与模式：确定性验证、统计校准准入、受权人类判断支持的AI建议、受权人类对AI产出的裁决、保留确定性工具路径、明确非参与。区分形式化保真度与验证器完备性，设计分阶段分类与就绪评估流程，并基于重复群体假设推导出与传统流程的比较条件。通过实际应用验证框架有效性。

Result: 在17单元的机翼翼梁结构分析工作流中，框架成功指导了AI生成的CAD程序的准入与验证。该程序一次性通过所有23个确定性检查并被接纳。当应力条件虽有利但网格收敛证据不足时，系统自动将判定交由工程判断，避免了错误接受。结果表明框架能够实现选择性AI参与和显式证据管理，支持灵活且可追溯的决策过程。

Conclusion: 本框架为安全关键工作流中生成式AI的参与与保障提供了可操作、可验证的形式化基础。它通过单元级精细控制实现了对AI角色的清晰界定与证据管理，提升了系统的透明性与可控性。尽管未证明整个工作流的可靠性或安全性，但为未来构建可信AI辅助工程系统提供了坚实的方法论支撑。

Abstract: Generative AI can produce engineering artefacts, but generation alone does not determine whether or how those artefacts should enter safety-critical workflows. This paper develops a formal framework for assigning AI participation and assurance at the level of individual workflow units. Each unit has a participation and assurance record covering its engineering requirement, an approved operational formalization where applicable, the applicable mechanism, fallback where applicable, evidence obligations and the applicable guarantee, plus a deployment-readiness status. The framework distinguishes deterministic verification, statistically calibrated admission, authorized human judgement supported by AI advice, authorized human adjudication of AI-produced artefacts, retained deterministic tool paths and explicit non-participation; these arrangements carry different kinds of guarantee rather than levels on a common scale. The framework also separates formalization fidelity from verifier soundness, provides a staged classification and readiness procedure, and derives conditions for comparing a gated AI-assisted unit with an incumbent process under recurring-population assumptions. We instantiate and apply the framework in an executed 17-unit wing-spar structural-analysis workflow combining deterministically gated AI-generated CAD, retained deterministic computation and human judgement. The AI-generated CAD program passed all 23 deterministic checks and was admitted at the first attempt. Favourable stress magnitudes did not suffice to pass the stress criteria where the predeclared mesh-convergence evidence was insufficient; those criteria were instead referred to engineering judgement. The case demonstrates selective AI participation and explicit evidence handling at unit level; no claim is made of workflow-level dependability, certification, structural safety or productivity.

</details>


### [28] [UniBRep: Learning Unified Geometry and Topology for Image-conditioned B-Rep Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04092)
*Haiyang Ying,Allen Tu,Jiaye Wu,Tom Goldstein,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniBRep是一个以几何为核心的框架，通过预训练的图像到3D模型生成特征网格作为统一中间表示，利用几何和特征引导的构建流程，实现复杂形状的边界表示（B-rep）生成。其方法在保持拓扑有效性的同时支持可扩展的面数，显著提升重建精度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在单图像生成边界表示时难以同时保证几何精度、拓扑正确性以及对复杂形状的支持，尤其受限于预定义的面数限制。因此需要一种能够灵活适应复杂形状、兼具几何与拓扑建模能力的新框架。

Method: UniBRep采用双解码器分支分别生成几何与面分离特征；基于特征网格的几何骨架和空间对齐的语义特征，结合几何与特征引导的构建流程，使用CAD内核拟合参数曲面、恢复边界曲线与连通性，最终生成显式B-rep。该方法避免了预设面数限制，使面数可随形状复杂度动态调整。

Result: 在DeepCAD基准上，UniBRep成功生成有效B-rep的比例达到80.49%，面级Chamfer距离从0.1096降低至0.0345，优于CADDreamer；在与HoLa公开演示的对比中，在所有指标上均表现更优。此外，模型展现出对高复杂度形状的可扩展性、跨分布泛化能力及真实照片的迁移潜力。

Conclusion: UniBRep通过几何优先的统一特征网格设计，实现了高质量、可扩展的单图像到B-rep生成，突破了传统方法在拓扑与面数上的限制，为复杂三维建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generating a boundary representation (B-rep) conditioned on a single image requires faithful reconstruction of geometry, valid topology, and support for complex shapes. We present UniBRep, a geometry-first framework that adapts a pretrained image-to-3D model to generate a feature mesh as a unified intermediate representation. Its surface provides a geometric scaffold, while spatially aligned learned features encode face-separation cues for topology recovery. Dual decoder branches generate the geometry and face-separation features; a geometry- and feature-guided construction pipeline then fits parametric surfaces, recovers boundary curves and connectivity, and assembles an explicit B-rep using a CAD kernel. Recovering topology from mesh regions avoids predefined architectural face-count limits, allowing face count to scale with shape complexity. On the standard DeepCAD benchmark, UniBRep produces valid B-reps for 80.49\% of inputs and reduces face Chamfer distance from 0.1096 to 0.0345 relative to CADDreamer. In a matched comparison, UniBRep also outperforms the HoLa public demo across all reported metrics. Further evaluations demonstrate scalability to high-complexity shapes beyond the standard 30-face range, generalization to objects outside the CAD training distribution, and qualitative transfer to real photographs.

</details>


### [29] [Scaling 3D Visual Grounding in Abdominal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04095)
*Sam Church,Danyal Maqbool,Joshua D. Warner,Andrew Voter,Junjie Hu,Meghan G. Lubner,Tyler J. Bradshaw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化管道，利用临床影像系统中已有的2D标注（如测量线、箭头）生成大规模的3D视觉定位监督数据，无需额外放射科医生标注。通过元数据匹配将标注与报告中的发现关联，并使用可提示的3D分割模型将其转换为体积分割掩码，构建了包含10.5万组短语-掩码-体积三元组的数据集。基于此，提出了两个腹部CT视觉定位基准（LocusBench-Onc和LocusBench-ED），并训练出LocusCT模型，在两个基准上分别达到0.725和0.773的命中率，显著优于现有方法。结果表明，常规PACS标注是3D视觉定位任务中一种可扩展且未被充分利用的监督来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D视觉定位模型训练依赖大量人工标注的短语-区域配对数据，而此类标注成本高昂。然而，放射科医生在日常报告中常在图像上添加2D标注以辅助测量和解释，这些标注隐含了丰富的监督信息，但尚未被有效利用。因此，本文旨在挖掘这些已有临床标注作为自动化的监督信号，以降低数据标注成本。

Method: 提出一个自动化流程：首先通过元数据匹配将临床影像系统中的2D标注与报告中的描述关联；然后利用可提示的3D分割模型将2D标注投影并转化为体积分割掩码；最终生成大规模的短语-掩码-体积三元组数据集，用于训练3D视觉定位模型。

Result: 该方法从59,000例腹部CT中生成了10.5万个短语-掩码-体积三元组；构建了两个新基准（LocusBench-Onc和LocusBench-ED），涵盖240个肿瘤学和260个急诊科病例；所提出的LocusCT模型在两个基准上的命中率分别为0.725和0.773，显著优于对比模型。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，常规临床影像系统中的2D标注是一种可扩展、未被充分使用的监督资源，能够有效支持3D视觉定位模型的训练，大幅降低对人工标注的依赖，具有重要的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Visual grounding models can enhance radiology workflows by linking report findings to image regions. This is particularly valuable for 3D CT, where findings often occupy a tiny fraction of the volume. Training 3D grounding models requires large sets of paired phrases and regions, and building such datasets is expensive, requiring radiologists to annotate images by hand. We posit that this supervision is already created implicitly during routine reporting, as radiologists frequently place 2D annotations (e.g., distance measurement, arrows) on key images to make measurements and to support report interpretation. We introduce an automated pipeline that converts these routine clinical annotations into large-scale phrase-region supervision for 3D visual grounding. The pipeline links each annotation to the corresponding finding in the report through metadata matching, then uses a promptable 3D segmentation model to convert the 2D annotation into a volumetric mask. This produces phrase-mask-volume datasets without requiring additional radiologist annotation. Applied to a single institution's clinical picture archiving and communication system (PACS), our approach generated 105K phrase-mask-volume triplets from 59K abdominal CT exams. We also introduce two abdominal CT grounding benchmarks, LocusBench-Onc and LocusBench-ED, which comprise 240 oncology and 260 emergency-department radiologist-reviewed phrase-mask-volume triplets, respectively, with the latter spanning 13 distinct categories such as appendicitis, hematoma, and hernia. We further introduce LocusCT, a 3D visual grounding model trained on this dataset, which achieves hit rates of 0.725 on LocusBench-Onc and 0.773 on LocusBench-ED, substantially outperforming comparator models. These results show that routine PACS annotations are a scalable, previously unused source of supervision for 3D visual grounding.

</details>


### [30] [Where Does the Semantic Gain Come From? A Reproduction and Extension of Semantic Knowledge-driven Contrastive Learning for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04104)
*Sushrut Ghimire*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SKCL claims a 2.01-point gain over BCL on CIFAR-100-LT (beta=100), but reimplementation shows it underperforms BCL by 0.56 points. The performance gap is largely explained by the use of ConCutMix and longer training (300 epochs), not the semantic graph. The semantic graph contributes only marginally (~0.23 points), and BCL already shares significant neighbor overlap with SKCL's graph. Adding CutMix or adaptive graph variants brings small gains within noise.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To validate SKCL's claimed improvement over BCL by re-implementing it in a unified framework and testing under controlled conditions.

Method: Reimplement SKCL, BCL, and ConCutMix; compare across multiple seeds and configurations; test variations including CutMix integration and adaptive graph updates.

Result: SKCL underperforms BCL by 0.56 points in the reimplementation. The published gain is primarily due to ConCutMix and extended training, not the semantic graph. Semantic graph provides minimal benefit (~0.23 points). Combining SKCL with CutMix improves performance by 1.06 points; adaptive graph yields slight gains (~0.34/0.28) within seed variation.

Conclusion: The reported performance boost of SKCL is largely attributable to implementation details (ConCutMix and training duration), not the semantic knowledge component. The semantic graph contributes little beyond what BCL already captures through confusion patterns.

Abstract: Semantic Knowledge-driven Contrastive Learning (SKCL) uses a language model to decide which classes are related, and pulls each image towards the prototypes of its semantic neighbours. On CIFAR-100-LT (beta = 100) it reports 54.02% top-1 accuracy, 2.01 points above Balanced Contrastive Learning (BCL), the method it builds on. The code and the class descriptions are not public. I reimplement SKCL, BCL and ConCutMix in one framework, check it against the public baseline code, and run every configuration with three seeds. The two baselines reproduce within 1.5 points, but SKCL built on BCL, as the paper describes it, ends up 0.56 points below BCL. To find out why, I add SKCL to the authors' own ConCutMix code. Trained for the paper's 300 epochs, it reaches 53.69, only 0.33 below the published number. At the same budget, however, the semantic graph adds just 0.23 points over ConCutMix, while training ConCutMix for 100 more epochs adds 1.04. Together with ConCutMix's published lead over BCL (1.15), this explains the claimed gain. A BCL model that never sees the graph already shares 41.2% of the graph's top-2 neighbours with its own most-confused classes (2.0% by chance), which shows why the graph adds so little on these benchmarks. I also test several changes to SKCL. Combining it with the CutMix branch improves it by 1.06 points, and an adaptive version of the graph improves it slightly (+0.34 and +0.28 in two codebases), although these gains are within seed noise.

</details>


### [31] [From Sight to Foresight: Predictive Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04139)
*Feiran Wang,Xiaoqi Wang,Ziwei Li,Wenbin He,Yan Yan,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialMind 是一个用于空间推理和未来预测的度量尺度视觉语言模型，通过度量深度适配器和渐进状态链实现对当前及未来空间状态的预测。该模型在驾驶和日常第一人称场景中表现优异，并构建了 SpatialMind-30K 数据集与 SpatialMind-2K 基准测试，涵盖从当前状态理解到未来预测的八项任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型主要关注已观测场景的空间推理，缺乏对观测区间之外未来状态预测的研究，因此亟需一个能够进行度量尺度下空间未来预测的模型与基准。

Method: 引入度量深度适配器以锚定真实世界尺度的空间推理；采用渐进状态链建立当前状态与已观测动态作为未来预测的基础；利用可扩展的数据引擎生成基于度量几何的问答对与状态监督信号。

Result: SpatialMind 在自建基准上显著优于通用及专用空间模型，在 VSI-Bench、OSI-Bench 和 VLM4D 上也展现出具有竞争力的零样本性能。

Conclusion: SpatialMind 为未来空间推理提供了有效的解决方案，推动了视觉语言模型在动态环境中对未来状态预测的能力发展。

Abstract: Predicting future spatial states supports collision avoidance and timely decision-making in dynamic environments. However, existing vision-language models (VLMs) and benchmarks for spatial reasoning primarily focus on observed scenes, leaving predictive spatial reasoning beyond the observed interval underexplored. To this end, we introduce SpatialMind, a metric-scale VLM for spatial reasoning and future prediction. Its metric depth adapter anchors spatial reasoning to real-world scale, while its progressive state chain establishes current spatial states and observed dynamics as the foundation for future prediction. Given a video prefix, SpatialMind predicts distances, motion directions, and spatial relations in both observed and unseen future frames. For training and evaluation, we build a scalable data engine that grounds entity descriptions in metric geometry to generate question-answer pairs and state supervision. Using this engine, we construct the SpatialMind-30K dataset and the SpatialMind-2K benchmark, both covering driving and everyday egocentric scenes. The benchmark spans eight tasks across three levels: current-state understanding, observed-dynamics understanding, and future prediction. Experiments show that SpatialMind substantially outperforms both general and spatially specialized models on our benchmark while achieving competitive zero-shot performance on VSI-Bench, OSI-Bench, and VLM4D.

</details>


### [32] [Watermarks and Fingerprints as Soft Bindings for Content Provenance: An Open-Licence Benchmark for Images, Audio and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04151)
*Seyedmahdi Kazempourradi,Ramtin Mojtahedi,Behrang Mohseni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了C2PA内容溯源标准下的水印与指纹技术，涵盖25种图像、7种音频和7种视频水印配置，以及35种指纹方法。PixelSeal在图像和视频水印中表现最佳，AudioSeal在音频中表现最佳；但所有TrustMark变体的纠错检测器在未标记图像上误报率高达5.9%至15.3%，建议验证预期载荷。指纹方面，基于非商业数据训练的复制检测器在变换图像中最高可检出75.5%，配对级误匹配率低至$10^{-7}$，而DINOv2是许可最宽松的最佳方法（65.0%）。近似拷贝在产品目录中主导误匹配，几何验证未提升检测性能。在攻击样本中，水印与指纹互补：联合成功覆盖69%图像拷贝，指纹更优音频，预期密钥水印更优视频。水印嵌入使62.0%图像的ISCC码超出匹配阈值，且平台依赖的颜色转换导致x86与ARM主机间水印位变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估C2PA内容溯源标准下水印与指纹技术在真实场景中的性能，确保其在公开媒体、合法模型和受控环境下的可靠性与实用性，以支持可信数字内容管理。

Method: 在统一协议下，对公开可用模型进行审计，使用公共媒体数据集，通过保留负样本校准假阳性率，并采用源级自举区间估计性能。针对水印，测试25种图像、7种音频、7种视频配置，评估感知质量、鲁棒性、假阳性率与成本；针对指纹，评估35种方法在最多98,985张图像的注册表上的表现，涵盖部分编辑与对抗攻击。

Result: PixelSeal在图像和视频水印中表现最佳，AudioSeal在音频中表现最佳；所有TrustMark变体在未标记图像上存在5.9%–15.3%的误报率，需验证预期载荷。指纹方面，基于非商业数据训练的复制检测器可识别75.5%的变换图像，假匹配率$10^{-7}$，DINOv2为最宽松许可下的最优方法（65.0%）。近似拷贝导致主要误匹配，几何验证未提升性能。联合水印与指纹可覆盖69%图像拷贝，指纹在音频上表现更好，预期密钥水印在视频上更优。水印嵌入使62.0%图像的ISCC码超出匹配阈值，且颜色转换在不同平台间引起水印位变化。

Conclusion: 水印与指纹各有优势，在实际应用中应结合使用以提升整体溯源能力。需注意验证预期载荷、考虑平台差异，并警惕近似拷贝带来的误匹配风险。当前技术在复杂攻击和跨平台环境下仍存局限，需进一步优化。

Abstract: Content-provenance standards such as C2PA let a platform recover a stripped manifest through a soft binding: an invisible watermark read from the content, or a fingerprint looked up in a registry. We benchmarked both families under one protocol, restricted to openly available models whose licences we audited, on public media, with false-match rates calibrated on held-out negatives and source-level bootstrap intervals for performance estimates. For watermarking we evaluated 25 image, 7 audio and 7 video configurations from 12 methods on perceptual quality, robustness, false positives and cost; for fingerprinting, 35 methods on registries of up to 98,985 images, partial edits and adversarial attacks. PixelSeal gave the best balance for image and video watermarks and AudioSeal for audio, but the error-correcting detector of every TrustMark variant fired on 5.9 to 15.3% of unmarked images, so a verifier should test the expected payload. Among fingerprints, copy detectors trained on non-commercial data detected up to 75.5% of transformed images at a pair-level false-match rate of $10^{-7}$ and DINOv2, the best permissively licensed method, 65.0%; near-copies in a product catalogue dominated the false matches, and no detection improvement from geometric verification was observed under a matched calibration false-binding constraint in the evaluated image pipelines. On the same attacked copies the two families failed differently: the union of watermark and fingerprint successes covered 69% of image copies; fingerprints covered more audio queries, whereas expected-key watermark verification covered more video queries. Embedding a watermark moved the ISCC code of 62.0% of images past its match threshold, and platform-dependent colour conversion changed watermark bits between x86 and ARM hosts.

</details>


### [33] [Kepler4D: Controllable Future Video Generation via 4D Scene State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04152)
*Feiran Wang,Bin Duan,Junyi Wu,Gaowen Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kepler4D 是一种用于未来视频生成的框架，通过显式的 4D 场景状态演化实现。它基于单目视频构建共享的 3D 表示，包括背景几何、物体运动历史、粗略空间支持和语义上下文。通过 Chain-of-Motion 模块总结观测到的运动，并利用视觉-语言模型选择结构化的速度与方向决策，决定是否对物体中心高度进行下界约束。随后，通过确定性滚动将这些决策转化为未来物体轨迹，便于检查与编辑。最后，将动态代理渲染为几何控制信号，输入预训练视频生成器，从而分离粗粒度运动与外观及动作的合成。实验表明，该方法在真实世界视频上实现了可控的物体运动、合理的未来预测，并保持场景一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在生成未来视频时难以精确控制物体运动并保持场景一致性，尤其在缺乏多视角信息的情况下。因此，需要一种能够显式建模 4D 场景状态演变的机制，以实现对物体运动的可控性和生成结果的合理性。

Method: Kepler4D 首先从单目视频中提取并构建共享的 3D 场景表示，包含背景几何、物体运动历史、粗略空间支持和语义上下文；然后使用 Chain-of-Motion 模块总结观测运动，并借助视觉-语言模型生成结构化的速度与方向决策；进一步通过确定性滚动生成未来物体轨迹，供人工检查与编辑；最终将这些轨迹作为几何控制信号，驱动预训练视频生成器，分离运动与外观/动作的合成过程。

Result: 在真实世界视频数据集上的实验表明，Kepler4D 能够生成具有合理未来运动轨迹的视频，支持对物体运动的可控编辑，同时保持场景结构的一致性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Kepler4D 通过显式建模 4D 场景状态演化，实现了对物体运动的可控生成和高保真未来视频合成，在真实场景中表现出良好的一致性和可编辑性。

Abstract: Video world models aim to preserve scene structure and predict how dynamic objects evolve beyond visual observations. We present Kepler4D, a framework for future video generation through explicit 4D scene state evolution. Given a monocular video, Kepler4D constructs a shared 3D representation of background geometry, object motion histories, coarse spatial supports, and semantic context. Chain-of-Motion summarizes observed motion and uses a vision-language model to select structured speed and heading decisions and decide whether to bound object-center height from below. A deterministic rollout converts these decisions into future object trajectories for inspection and editing before synthesis. We render the evolving proxies into geometric controls for a pretrained video generator, separating coarse object motion from the synthesis of appearance and articulation. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate that Kepler4D enables controllable object motion and plausible future rollout while preserving scene consistency.

</details>


### [34] [Diagnosis-Conditioned Spatial Gating and Decoder-Level Supervised Contrastive Learning for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04159)
*Md Mustafizur Rahman,Mylene C. Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Position-Aware Gate (PAG)和Decoder-level Supervised Contrastive Loss (DSCL)，以改善放射科报告生成中的发现级准确性。PAG通过预测的发现表示对视觉补丁进行空间调制，无需区域监督；DSCL则利用共享的正向发现来结构化解码器表示。在MIMIC-CXR上，PAG+DSCL将临床有效性F1从0.484提升至0.502，优于单独使用PAG（0.491）或DSCL（0.495）。在IU X-Ray上，组合模型在无额外微调的情况下达到0.226的CE F1，优于现有方法的0.211。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射科报告生成模型虽能生成流畅文本，但存在发现级不准确的问题。诊断驱动方法虽通过预测发现进行条件化，但未直接修改视觉补丁特征，且全局门控在空间上应用相同调制，限制了性能提升。因此需要更精细的视觉-语义对齐机制和解码器表示学习策略。

Method: 引入Position-Aware Gate (PAG)，利用预测发现表示对视觉补丁进行空间感知调制，实现非监督的空间注意力；提出Decoder-level Supervised Contrastive Loss (DSCL)，通过共享正向发现构建解码器表示，增强语义一致性。二者结合实现更准确的报告生成。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，PAG+DSCL使临床有效性F1从0.484提升至0.502，优于单独使用PAG（0.491）或DSCL（0.495）。在未微调的IU X-Ray数据集上，组合模型达到0.226的CE F1，超过PromptMRG的0.211。

Conclusion: PAG与DSCL的结合有效提升了放射科报告生成中发现级别的准确性，通过空间感知调制与结构化解码器表示，显著增强了模型的临床实用性。

Abstract: Radiology report generation models can produce fluent text while still containing finding-level inaccuracies. Diagnosis-driven methods improve generation by conditioning on predicted findings, but these predictions do not directly modify the visual patch features provided to the decoder, and global gating applies the same modulation across spatial locations. We introduce a Position-Aware Gate (PAG) that uses predicted finding representations to modulate visual patches spatially without region supervision. We also propose a Decoder-level Supervised Contrastive Loss (DSCL) that structures decoder representations using shared positive findings rather than instance identity. On MIMIC-CXR, PAG+DSCL improves clinical efficacy (CE) F1 from 0.484 to 0.502 over a matched global-gate reference, while PAG and DSCL individually reach 0.491 and 0.495. Without additional fine-tuning, the combined model achieves 0.226 CE F1 on IU X-Ray, compared with 0.211 reported by PromptMRG.

</details>


### [35] [Referring Multi-Object Tracking in Moving-Camera Videos via Global Motion Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04185)
*Hsin-Chen Pai,Jyun-Kai Wang,Yi-Cheng Peng,Wei-Ta Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在驾驶员视角视频中通过估计相机运动来提取残差运动的方法，以解决因相机移动导致的误判问题。该方法将运动匹配程度与查询表达式进行对比，并通过后融合方式整合到多目标跟踪模型中，显著提升语言引导多目标跟踪（RMOT）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RMOT方法虽然关联了运动与文本描述，但未显式去除由相机移动引起的运动干扰，导致对物体真实运动状态的误判。例如，在移动相机拍摄的视频中，静止车辆可能表现为运动，而实际运动的车辆可能显示很小位移。因此需要引入相机运动补偿机制以更准确地捕捉物体的真实运动特性。

Method: 通过估计驱动视角视频中的相机运动，提取帧间残差运动；结合查询语句中描述的运动特征，计算运动匹配度；最后将匹配结果与原始跟踪预测通过后融合方式整合，增强跟踪准确性。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的运动补偿模块可作为即插即用组件，在多种不同基线的RMOT系统上均带来显著性能提升，验证了其通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了显式建模和去除相机运动干扰对于提高语言引导多目标跟踪精度的重要性，提出的残差运动分析框架为复杂动态场景下的精准跟踪提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Referring multi-object tracking (RMOT) takes a video and a language expression as input and tracks all referred objects. Many tracking requirements involve how an object moves rather than how it appears. However, in a video captured by a moving camera, a parked vehicle may appear to move, while a moving vehicle may show little displacement. Existing RMOT methods relate motion with text but do not explicitly remove camera-induced motion. In this paper, we propose extracting residual motion across frames by estimating camera motion in driver-view videos and compare motion characteristics with the query expression. We consider the motion-matching extent and integrate it with the RMOT method's prediction result through late fusion. In the evaluation, we verify the performance gain of taking the motion compensation module as a plug-in across different RMOT hosts.

</details>


### [36] [CellSplat4D: PSF-Aware 4D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse Robotic Live-Cell Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04199)
*Yingda Tao,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于4D高斯模型的细胞追踪方法，通过重建稀疏采样下的细胞动态过程，实现对活细胞的连续观测。利用3D U-Net在粗尺度上监督缺失时间步的预测，不依赖运动场，以应对细胞分裂。实验在C. elegans胚胎和CHO细胞数据集上验证，该方法在所有时间间隔内均保持最高核定位精度，间隙衰减最平缓，并能优雅地恢复未观测到的焦平面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人显微镜因光子消耗和时间成本限制，无法频繁采集图像，导致细胞追踪记录断裂；现有分析软件无法弥补硬件采样不足的问题，关键在于优化采集策略而非后期处理。

Method: 采用4D高斯模型构建连续的细胞时空表示，每个高斯斑点携带位置、形状和寿命信息；通过自适应估计点扩散函数（从数据中获取，而非依赖元数据或优化器），并使用3D U-Net直接预测中间体积，避免运动场建模，适用于细胞分裂场景。

Result: 在两个Cell Tracking Challenge数据集（C. elegans胚胎和CHO细胞）上，该方法在任意距离下均达到最高的细胞核定位精度，间隙内精度衰减最平缓，且能有效恢复未观测焦平面，表现出良好的泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将采集频率与分析频率解耦，该方法实现了在低采样率下的高质量细胞追踪，显著提升了稀疏采集条件下的时空重建能力，为资源受限的活细胞成像提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: A robotic microscope watching living cells cannot afford to look as often as it would like. Every volume it acquires costs photons the specimen does not get back, and time owed to other wells. What such a platform exists to produce is a record of individual cells through time: which cell is which from one volume to the next, and which cell divided into which two. Sampling sparsely breaks that record exactly where it matters, and the fault lies in the acquisition schedule rather than in the analysis software. We fill the gaps by reconstructing them, fitting a 4D Gaussian model to whatever volumes the hardware could afford. The model is a cloud of light-emitting blobs, each carrying a position, a shape and a lifetime. Being continuous in time, it renders any missing volume on demand, decoupling how often the robot analyses from how often it can afford to look. The microscope's point-spread function is measured from the data rather than inherited from acquisition metadata or left to the optimizer, because metadata inflates it and the optimizer cannot recover it at all: a wider blur around a smaller blob fits the images equally well. Each blob's lifetime is stored in frames rather than as a fraction of the recording, so that it denotes a fixed duration on any sequence. Unmeasured timesteps are supervised at coarse scale by a 3D U-Net that predicts the intermediate volume directly and estimates no motion field, since a dividing cell becomes two and no motion describes that. On two Cell Tracking Challenge sequences, a C. elegans embryo and a Chinese Hamster Ovarian (CHO), with fidelity scored per cell nucleus, our reconstruction holds the highest nucleus fidelity at every distance from an acquired frame, has the flattest decay across the gap, and best recovers focal planes it was never shown with graceful degradation across the gap.

</details>


### [37] [Sparse-GS2Mesh: 3D Gaussian Splatting Guided by Novel Stereo Views and 2DGS for Sparse View Surface Reconstruction}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04203)
*Younghyun Noh,Minje Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sparse-GS2Mesh提出了一种针对稀疏视图下表面重建的立体感知框架。通过利用对极深度先验初始化3DGS，缓解过拟合问题，并引入自适应基线选择、基于立体匹配网络的微调以及2D/3D协同正则化三个关键组件，显著提升稀疏视图下的几何重建质量，在低重叠场景中相比现有方法提升15%，在高重叠场景中表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图下的表面重建面临几何线索不足的问题，传统体积渲染方法易产生过度平滑表面，而3DGS因缺乏可靠的深度监督和仅依赖输入视角优化，导致几何不完整。因此需要一种更鲁棒且能有效利用有限视图信息的方法。

Method: 首先使用对极深度先验初始化3DGS以减少过拟合；随后引入自适应基线选择，自动为每个稀疏视图确定合成基线；接着通过立体匹配网络反向传播深度优化梯度，微调3DGS以增强其在立体匹配中的表现；最后采用2D与3D联合正则化策略，提升重建稳定性，尤其在近距离立体视图中改善弱几何线索问题。

Result: 在低重叠设置下，Sparse-GS2Mesh相比最先进方法提升15%；在高重叠设置下达到相当水平，验证了其在不同重叠程度下的鲁棒性与有效性。

Conclusion: Sparse-GS2Mesh成功将3DGS与立体匹配技术扩展至稀疏视图场景，通过多阶段优化与协同正则化机制，显著提升了稀疏视图下表面重建的质量与完整性，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Surface reconstruction under sparse-view settings remains challenging due to limited geometric cues. Volume rendering methods based on signed distance functions often produce over-smoothed surfaces, while 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), though time-efficient, suffers from incomplete geometry due to the lack of reliable depth supervision and the limitation of being optimized only from given input views. In this paper, we present Sparse-GS2Mesh, a stereo-aware framework for surface reconstruction from sparse views. While 3DGS and stereo matching have been leveraged for surface reconstruction under dense view settings, we extend them to operate effectively under sparse view conditions by first initializing 3DGS using epipolar depth priors to mitigate the 3DGS overfitting problem, followed by our three key components: (I) adaptive baseline selection, (II) fine-tuning with a stereo matching network, and (III) 2D/3D co-regularized fine-tuning. Given a warmed-up 3DGS initialized with epipolar depth, the adaptive baseline selection automatically determines a baseline to synthesize for each sparse view. We then fine-tune 3DGS by backpropagating depth-refining gradients from the stereo matching network, effectively specializing the 3DGS for stereo matching. The 2D/3D co-regularization further helps obtain stable reconstruction, addressing weak geometric cues in close stereo views. Sparse-GS2Mesh achieves a 15\% improvement over state-of-the-art methods in little-overlap settings and comparable results in large-overlap settings. Codes will be publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [FlashGaze: Training-Free Multi-Scale Patch Pruning For Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04225)
*Ziye Zhu,Yanghao Zhou,Lixing Tan,Jialiang Kang,Shuxuan Li,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashGaze 是一种无需训练的高效方法，通过在视觉变换器（ViT）编码前减少视频的时空冗余来提升多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）处理长高分辨率视频的效率。它利用像素级差异作为信息损失的代理，结合四叉树动态规划，在固定预算下联合优化补丁的删除、合并与保留，避免了额外的训练和推理开销。实验表明，该方法在保持高准确率的同时显著提升了速度并降低了内存占用，例如在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上实现最高 17 倍的 MLLM 预填充加速和 5.4 倍的 ViT 编码加速，同时将峰值 GPU 内存使用减少 1.8 倍，使模型可在相同硬件上处理更长、更高分辨率的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理长高分辨率视频时面临计算开销大、冗余视觉标记过多的问题。虽然部分方法在编码后或编码中进行标记剪枝，但未解决编码阶段本身的高成本；而提前剪枝的方法依赖于额外的辅助网络，带来额外的训练与推理负担。因此需要一种无需训练、能有效降低编码前冗余且不引入额外开销的方法。

Method: FlashGaze 利用像素空间差异作为信息损失的代理指标，采用四叉树动态规划算法，在固定计算预算下联合优化视觉补丁的删除、合并与保留策略，从而在编码前高效压缩视频输入，减少冗余，同时尽可能保留关键信息。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，FlashGaze 在两个 MLLM 架构上均表现出显著的效率提升：在 Qwen3-VL-8B 上，保留 98% 的基线准确率，同时实现最高 5.4 倍的 ViT 编码加速、17 倍的 MLLM 预填充加速，并将峰值 GPU 内存使用减少 1.8 倍。

Conclusion: FlashGaze 为长视频理解提供了一种高效、无训练负担的解决方案，能够在不牺牲准确性的情况下大幅降低计算成本，使高分辨率长视频处理成为可能，推动 MLLMs 在大规模视频理解任务中的应用。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in video understanding, yet efficiently processing long, high-resolution videos remains challenging. Such videos often contain substantial spatiotemporal redundancy, and processing redundant visual tokens can incur avoidable computational overhead. Many existing methods prune visual tokens during or after vision transformer (ViT) encoding, leaving much of the encoding cost unaddressed. Some approaches prune patches before encoding but rely on learned auxiliary networks for patch selection, incurring additional training and inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose FlashGaze, a training-free method that reduces spatiotemporal redundancy before ViT encoding without introducing auxiliary networks. FlashGaze uses pixel-space differences as a proxy for information loss and employs Quadtree Dynamic Programming to jointly optimize patch dropping, merging, and keeping under a fixed budget. Experiments on two MLLM backbones across multiple benchmarks demonstrate substantial efficiency gains while largely preserving accuracy. On Qwen3-VL-8B, FlashGaze retains 98% of the full-input baseline accuracy on LongVideoBench while achieving up to 5.4x and 17x speedups in ViT encoding and MLLM prefill, respectively, and reducing peak GPU memory usage by a factor of 1.8. These efficiency gains enable the model to process videos with more frames and higher resolutions on the same GPU hardware, unlocking video understanding at scales previously out of reach.

</details>


### [39] [OctMesh: A Unified Octree-Hierarchical Framework for Lossless Triangle Mesh Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04281)
*Shiyu Feng,Xihua Sheng,Lingyu Zhu,Chunyang Fu,Shiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctMesh 是一种基于共享八叉树层次结构的有损/无损三角网格压缩框架，通过将几何与拓扑信息联合编码，在保持顶点位置和连接性的同时实现高效压缩。其核心思想是利用八叉树池化后父边仅具有一或二至八个子边的特性，按端点父节点类型及是否共享父节点对子边进行分组，形成四种固定模式的预测任务。其中，由父图唯一决定的连接无需编码，其余由三个神经网络预测器估计概率，指导算术编码实际边符号。局部几何、父连接性和全局形状信息被整合为图感知特征提取器，粗层级使用专用权重，细层级共享权重以提升效率。残差边与最细层级面选择数据包完成重建。在8个MPEG V-DMC测试序列共256帧上，平均达到7.033比特/面，比V-Mesh低12.8%；同时支持九级渐进式顶点与边细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统八叉树适用于点云几何编码与渐进重构，但难以直接扩展到网格，因边的细分方式多样且连接性复杂，缺乏紧凑的预测先验。现有方法在保持拓扑完整性的前提下难以实现高效压缩，亟需一种能统一处理几何与连接性的学习型框架。

Method: 提出OctMesh框架，构建共享八叉树层次结构，通过八叉树池化分析父边的子边分布规律，依据端点父节点类型及共享性将子边划分为四类固定结构。对于可由父图确定的连接直接继承，不编码；其余三类由神经网络预测器建模概率，结合算术编码生成边符号。采用图感知父特征提取器融合局部几何、父连接与全局形状信息，粗层用独立权重，细层共享权重。最终通过残差边与最细层级面选择实现完整重建。

Result: 在256帧的MPEG V-DMC测试集上，平均压缩效率达7.033比特/面，较V-Mesh降低12.8%；支持九级渐进式顶点与边重构，具备良好的可扩展性与精度控制能力。

Conclusion: OctMesh成功实现了网格几何与连接性的联合学习编码，通过结构化预测机制与层次化特征设计，在保证无损恢复的前提下显著提升了压缩效率，并支持多级渐进重构，为三维网格压缩提供了新范式。

Abstract: Lossless triangle mesh compression must preserve both vertex positions and connectivity. Octrees support learned point cloud geometry coding and progressive refinement, but extending them to meshes requires a compatible connectivity representation. Unlike the eight occupancy decisions of a voxel, a parent edge can develop into varied child connections, making edge refinement difficult to model with a compact prediction prior. We propose OctMesh, a learned framework that codes geometry and connectivity on a shared octree hierarchy. Its key observation is that octree pooling produces parents with either one child or two to eight children. Child edges are then grouped by their endpoint parents' types and whether the endpoints share a parent. Each candidate group contains children from just one parent or two connected parents. The resulting four categories define small, fixed-shape prediction tasks: connections uniquely determined by the parent graph are inherited without bits, while three neural predictors estimate probabilities for the remaining candidates. These probabilities guide arithmetic coding of the actual edge symbols. Binarized predictions of within-parent connections provide context for predicting connections between different parents. A graph-aware parent feature extractor combines local geometry, parent connectivity and global shape. The connectivity models use dedicated weights at coarse levels and share weights at fine levels. Residual edges and a finest-level face-selection payload complete the reconstruction. On 256 frames from eight MPEG V-DMC test sequences, OctMesh losslessly recovers the finest-level vertex-coordinate, edge and unoriented face sets at an average of 7.033 bits per face, 12.8% below V-Mesh. The same hierarchical representation supports nine levels of progressive vertex-and-edge refinement.

</details>


### [40] [A Geometric-Transformation Feature-Adaptive Manifold Restoration Method for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04300)
*Jianzheng Wang,Huan Ni,Xiaonan Niu,Danfeng Hong,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二面体群D4几何变换的特征自适应流形修复方法，以提升遥感图像开放词汇语义分割（OVSS）对几何变换的稳定性。通过多尺度调和引导的D4视图选择（MH-D4VS）选取互补候选视图，利用原始视图作为锚点，结合跨视图信息选择性修复局部不可靠视觉特征（OAMR），并设计像素解码器测试时自适应（PD-TTA），仅在线微调GroupNorm层参数以增强模型对样本级分布偏移的适应能力。实验表明，该方法在八个遥感语义分割基准上平均mIoU达55.6%，且在不同SAM3基线框架下均表现稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3-based开放词汇语义分割方法在遥感图像中对不同几何变换响应不一致，而语义信息应具备对D4群几何变换的不变性，因此亟需提升模型在几何变换下的稳定性与一致性。

Method: 提出MH-D4VS用于选择互补几何变换视图；设计OAMR利用原始视图锚定，融合可靠跨视图信息修复不可靠特征；引入PD-TTA，在推理阶段仅微调GroupNorm层参数以实现在线适应。

Result: 在八个遥感语义分割基准上达到平均55.6% mIoU，显著提升SAM3基线在多种几何变换下的性能一致性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效利用了遥感图像语义对D4群变换的不变性，通过视图选择、特征修复与测试时自适应策略，显著增强了开放词汇语义分割在遥感场景中的稳定性与泛化能力。

Abstract: The semantic information of objects in remote sensing images is typically invariant to geometric transformations from the dihedral group D4. However, SAM3-based open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) methods often exhibit inconsistent responses to different geometric transformations. To exploit this property and improve the stability of OVSS for remote sensing images, we propose a feature-adaptive manifold repair method based on dihedral-group geometric transformations. First, we introduce multi-scale harmonic-guided D4 view selection (MH-D4VS) to select complementary candidate views from a set of geometrically transformed views. Next, we propose original-view-anchored adaptive manifold repair (OAMR), which uses the original view as an anchor and reliable cross-view information to selectively repair locally unreliable visual features. Finally, we develop pixel decoder test-time adaptation (PD-TTA) for SAM3, which fine-tunes only the parameters of the GroupNorm layers online during inference, thereby enhancing the model's ability to adapt to sample-level distribution shifts. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves an average mIoU of 55.6% across eight remote sensing semantic segmentation benchmarks and delivers consistent performance improvements under different SAM3-based inference frameworks.

</details>


### [41] [Rethinking Long-Video Efficiency: A Joint Allocation Perspective on Frames, Pixels, and Front-End Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04318)
*Sixun Dong,Wei Li,Andong Deng,Qi Qian,Victor Zhu,Zhengping Ji,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoHi是一种无需训练、单次遍历的框架，通过结合密集低分辨率视频流与稀疏高分辨率图像帧，提升长视频理解效率。它在保持相同令牌预算下，相比原生分辨率基线平均准确率提升10.6个百分点，优于现有最高效方法5.2个百分点，并将长达一小时视频的前端解码延迟降低至1/7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法通常依赖于固定分辨率下选择信息量丰富的帧或视觉标记，但这种方式难以平衡时间覆盖密度与分辨率。本文旨在探索如何在不增加计算负担的前提下，通过调整分辨率与时间密度之间的权衡来提升性能。

Method: 提出LoHi框架，利用视频的低分辨率流和高分辨率图像帧，分别通过I帧元数据（LoHi-Anchor）或基于查询相关性和视觉多样性（LoHi-SemDiv）选择关键帧，借助VLM的天然视频与图像路径实现融合处理。

Result: 在三个长视频基准测试中，LoHi在相同令牌预算下平均准确率提升10.6个百分点，优于最强的先前效率方法5.2个百分点；同时前端解码延迟减少高达7倍，尤其适用于小时级视频。

Conclusion: 通过将分辨率资源转化为更密集的时间采样，可显著提升长视频理解的效率与精度。LoHi无需训练、单次处理，是高效且实用的解决方案，为大规模长视频分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding with vision-language models (VLMs) is often framed as selecting informative frames or visual tokens at a fixed native resolution. We show that per-frame resolution can instead be traded for denser temporal coverage, while front-end decoding latency depends on the size of the candidate pool rather than the final token budget. An empirical study across multiple VLMs and long-video benchmarks yields three findings: dense low-resolution sampling outperforms sparse native-resolution sampling at matched token budgets; resolution-sensitive tasks benefit from selected high-resolution frames; and front-end decoding dominates wall time for hour-long videos. Motivated by these findings, we introduce LoHi, a training-free, single-pass framework that combines a dense low-resolution video stream with sparse high-resolution image frames through the VLM's native video and image pathways. LoHi-Anchor selects high-resolution frames using codec I-frame metadata, while LoHi-SemDiv uses query relevance and visual diversity over CLIP features. Across three long-video benchmarks, LoHi improves average accuracy by 10.6 percentage points over the native-resolution baseline at a matched token budget and by 5.2 percentage points over the strongest prior efficiency method. It also reduces front-end decoding latency by up to 7x on hour-long videos. Project page: https://sixundong.com/projects/lohi

</details>


### [42] [Trust the View That Sees the Target: Mining Cross-View Conflicts for Reliability-Gated Disaster Damage Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04327)
*Yifan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This paper investigates conflict cases in post-disaster building damage assessment where two views (overhead and ground-level) disagree. It shows that simply trusting the correct view outperforms all fusion methods, and proposes a visibility-conditioned reliability gate to decide which view to trust based on visibility features, calibrated confidences, and disagreement. The method significantly improves performance on wildfire data and matches top fusion methods on panoramic datasets. A field-of-view experiment reveals that focusing on the building enhances fusion benefits. Conflict density also predicts tile-level damage without labels.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing fusion methods treat overhead and ground-level views symmetrically, but this assumption fails in conflict cases where the two views disagree. Understanding when and why one view is more reliable than the other can improve damage assessment accuracy.

Method: The authors mine conflict cases from paired datasets (wildfire and hurricane data). They propose a visibility-conditioned reliability gate—a linear model using building-visibility features, per-view confidences, and disagreement—to decide which view to trust. They evaluate this approach against various fusion methods and conduct a controlled field-of-view experiment.

Result: The reliability gate significantly outperforms calibrated probability averaging and end-to-end fusion on wildfire data (+0.051 and +0.072 accuracy on conflicts, respectively), and matches top fusion methods on panoramic datasets. Conflicts' spatial density correlates with tile-level damage (Spearman r=0.615, p=0.001).

Conclusion: Fusion helps not by combining views equally, but by intelligently selecting the more reliable view based on visibility and context. Mining conflicts shifts the focus from 'does fusion help?' to 'which view should be trusted, where, and why?'

Abstract: After a disaster, building damage is assessed from overhead tiles and ground-level photographs, and most methods fuse the two views symmetrically, trusting both equally for every building. This paper focuses on the samples where that assumption fails: the conflict cases, on which two independently trained single-view models disagree. We mine such cases from three paired collections (inspection photographs from the 2025 Eaton wildfire and street-view panoramas from Hurricanes Ian and Milton, each matched to very-high-resolution overhead tiles), where they make up 10-33% of the data. On these samples an oracle that simply trusts the correct view beats every fusion method we tested by 0.37-0.41 accuracy, and the gap survives longer training, calibration, and backbone changes. We recover part of it with a visibility-conditioned reliability gate: a linear model that decides which view to trust from building-visibility features, calibrated per-view confidences, and the disagreement itself. On the wildfire data the gate is the only method that significantly beats calibrated probability averaging (+0.051 on conflicts, p=0.0001) and end-to-end fusion (+0.072, p<10^-4); on the panoramic datasets it matches them. A controlled field-of-view experiment explains why: cropping panoramas toward the building doubles the benefit of fusion, whereas random crops of the same size do not. Finally, the spatial density of conflicts predicts tile-level damage without labels (Spearman r=0.615, p=0.001). Mining conflicts turns "does fusion help?" into "which view should be trusted, where, and why?".

</details>


### [43] [Synthetic-to-Real ViT-Based Pose Estimation of a Noncooperative UAV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04335)
*Krishnanujam Srinivas,Hanish Acharla,Brij Agrawal,Leonardo Herrera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于Vision Transformer (ViT)的单目无人机姿态估计算法，采用自监督DINOv2骨干网络，仅在标注的合成图像上训练，评估时使用真实世界图像数据集。通过引入姿态模糊感知策略和推理阶段的$\alpha$-$\beta$滤波器，有效缓解了三维目标投影到二维图像平面及对称性带来的模糊问题。在包含77,077张标注无人机图像的真实数据集上，未过滤时平均角度误差（MAE）为19.18°，推理时间为13.25毫秒；经过滤波后，MAE降低至8.74°，推理时间略增至13.42毫秒，验证了模型在真实场景下的良好泛化能力与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程非合作无人机姿态估计对安全监控至关重要，但真实世界大规模带姿态标签的数据集获取成本高、难度大。虽然合成图像可提供替代方案，但存在合成到真实域间的差距，限制模型在真实场景中的泛化性能。因此，亟需探索仅在合成数据上训练却能有效迁移到真实世界的深度学习方法。

Method: 采用自监督预训练的DINOv2作为骨干网络，构建基于ViT的单目无人机姿态估计模型，仅使用合成图像进行训练。在训练与推理阶段引入姿态模糊感知策略以应对由3D投影和目标对称性引起的不确定性，并在推理阶段集成$\alpha$-$\beta$滤波器优化输出稳定性与精度。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上，未使用滤波时模型达到19.18°的平均角度误差（MAE）和13.25毫秒的推理时间；经$\alpha$-$\beta$滤波后，MAE显著降低至8.74°，推理时间略微增加至13.42毫秒，表明该方法在保持实时性的同时显著提升了姿态估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于DINOv2的ViT模型在仅依赖合成数据训练的情况下，具备较强的合成到真实域的泛化能力。结合姿态模糊感知策略与$\alpha$-$\beta$滤波器，可在真实世界中实现高精度且实时的无人机姿态估计，为非合作无人机监控提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Remote pose estimation of noncooperative Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) from imagery is critical, as they cannot be influenced or instrumented in advance. Deep-learning-based approaches offer a promising solution; however, their development is constrained by the cost and difficulty of acquiring large-scale real-world datasets with accurate pose labels. Synthetic imagery provides an alternative, but models trained on synthetic data must overcome the synthetic-to-real domain gap to generalize to real-world imagery. This work investigates the inherent synthetic-to-real generalization capability of a Vision Transformer (ViT)-based model for monocular UAV pose estimation. The proposed approach employs a self-supervised DINOv2 backbone and is trained exclusively on labeled synthetic imagery while being evaluated on labeled real-world imagery. Pose ambiguity-aware strategies are incorporated during training and inference to address ambiguities arising from the projection of a three-dimensional target onto a two-dimensional image plane and from target symmetries. An $α$-$β$ filter is further integrated during inference to improve pose estimations. To assess the model under operational requirements, it is evaluated in terms of Mean Angular Error (MAE) and inference time, both before and after filtering, using a real-world dataset containing 77,077 labeled UAV images. Before filtering, the model achieves an MAE of $19.18^{\circ}$ and an inference time of $13.25$ ms, whereas after filtering, these values are $8.74^{\circ}$ and $13.42$ ms, respectively.

</details>


### [44] [A differentiable Lagrangian-coupled 3D Gaussian Splatting-SPH model for forward simulation and inverse analysis in solid mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04336)
*Tian Xu,Soroush Atashi,Tianju Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微分的拉格朗日耦合3DGS-光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）模型，用于可变形固体的正向模拟与逆向分析。通过多视角校准视觉数据集重建物体为3DGS渲染模型，并基于包络线生成独立的SPH支持结构，避免直接使用渲染基元作为力学粒子。通过参考构型的拉格朗日映射将SPH形变传递至高斯位置与协方差，实现物理模型与图像观测模型的耦合，并保持可微计算路径。该模型支持线弹性、超弹性及凯尔文-沃伊特粘弹性响应，以及固定、自由和罗宾型边界条件。数值实验验证了其对有限元结果的准确性，对比传统以高斯中心作为表面SPH粒子的模型，展示了更高的精度与效率，并在梁、桥和肝形等实例上成功进行正向模拟。逆向分析进一步从渲染形变观测中估计材料参数与边界条件，包括含噪声情况，证明了该模型在基于图像数据的力学参数识别中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维重建技术如3DGS虽能精确捕捉物体外观与几何，但在将这些表示扩展至可解释的机械行为建模方面仍存在不足，尤其缺乏对本构关系、边界条件及逆向参数识别的支持。因此，亟需一种既能保留高质量视觉表征，又能提供可微分物理模拟能力的统一框架。

Method: 首先利用多视角校准视觉数据集构建3DGS渲染模型；随后采用包络线方法生成独立于渲染模型的SPH粒子系统；通过参考构型下的拉格朗日映射将SPH形变传递至高斯分布参数，实现物理与视觉模型的可微耦合；采用SPH框架支持多种材料本构模型与边界条件，并确保整个流程的可微性，以支持反向优化。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提模型在正向模拟中能准确再现复杂形变，且计算效率优于传统以高斯中心作为粒子的方法；逆向分析可在含噪声条件下有效估计本构参数与边界条件，验证了其在基于图像数据的力学参数识别中的实用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的可微分拉格朗日耦合3DGS-SPH模型，成功实现了高质量视觉重建与可解释物理模拟的融合，为从图像数据中进行力学参数识别提供了有效的可微分框架，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative world models have increased interest in digital models that reproduce both the appearance of real objects and their response to physical interaction. Three-dimensional reconstruction techniques, including 3D Gaussian Splatting, capture detailed surface geometry and appearance from images and videos. However, extending these representations beyond plausible animation to mechanically interpretable models for constitutive behavior, boundary conditions, and inverse parameter identification remains less explored. In this work, a differentiable Lagrangian-coupled 3DGS-smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH) model is proposed for forward simulation and inverse analysis of deformable solids. The observed object is first reconstructed from multi-view calibrated visual dataset as a 3DGS rendering model. An envelope-based procedure then generates an independent SPH support for the solid-mechanics model, avoiding the direct use of rendering primitives as mechanical particles. A reference-configuration Lagrangian transfer maps SPH deformation to Gaussian positions and covariances, thereby coupling the physical model and the image observation model while preserving a differentiable computational path. The SPH formulation supports linear elastic, hyperelastic, and Kelvin--Voigt viscoelastic responses, together with fixed, free, and Robin-type boundary conditions. Numerical studies validate the SPH response against finite-element results, assess accuracy and efficiency against a conventional model using Gaussian centers as surface SPH particles, and demonstrate forward simulations on beam, bridge, and liver-shaped examples. Inverse analyses further estimate constitutive and boundary parameters from rendered deformation observations, including noisy cases, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed model for mechanics-based parameter identification from image data.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于任意尺度超分辨率的任意尺度目标检测方法，通过连续调整目标图像尺度来提升检测性能。为避免传统超分辨率带来的大量误检，提出尺度提议图以指导各尺度超分辨率图像的使用，从而有效抑制误检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多尺度检测依赖离散的缩放策略，难以适应连续变化的目标尺度；而直接使用超分辨率会产生大量误检，因此需要一种机制来智能选择合适的超分辨率尺度。

Method: 设计尺度提议图，用于预测每个像素是否适合特定超分辨率尺度，仅在合适区域进行超分辨率处理，并将结果输入检测器，实现精确的尺度自适应检测。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个基准数据集上显著提升了小目标检测性能，同时有效减少了误检数量，优于现有主流多尺度检测方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过引入尺度提议图实现了连续尺度下的高效、准确目标检测，为解决尺度多样性问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes any-scale object detection using arbitrary-scale super-resolution for continuously rescaling object images, while general multi-scale object detection uses discretely rescaled appearance representations. However, a naive usage of super-resolution produces many false-positive detections if many super-resolution images are independently fed into an object detector. Our method suppresses these false positives by predicting scale proposal maps, each of which represents a set of pixels appropriate for each super-resolution scale.

</details>


### [46] [LoCoSplat: Real-Time Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Minimal 3D Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04351)
*Sinan Wang,Jinjin He,Yuchen Sun,Duowen Chen,Shenyifan Lu,Bo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoCoSplat提出一种轻量级的3D高斯点云渲染方法，利用局部上下文信息替代复杂的3D网络。通过在精细和粗糙网格上投影点特征，并用一个小型MLP读取局部平均特征，实现高效推理与训练。在RealEstate10K、ACID和ScanNet上均取得领先性能，速度比前序方法快4.2倍，内存占用减少6.7倍，且无需动态稀疏计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到高斯点是局部原语，一旦深度预测完成，其尺度、旋转、透明度等属性仅依赖于周围点云的局部上下文，因此无需使用复杂的3D网络，只需局部平均即可满足需求。

Method: 将点特征进行16维线性投影，分别存入精细和粗糙网格中；在每个锚点处通过一个0.14M参数的小型MLP读取这两个网格的局部平均特征，实现无学习3D网络的全局上下文建模。整个编码器以fp16 CUDA图形式运行，无需动态稀疏计算。

Result: 在RealEstate10K上，于6、12、24视图下均优于所有先前的前馈方法，在PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS指标上表现优异，尤其在24视图时比VolSplat高出3.3 PSNR；在零样本迁移至ACID和微调于ScanNet时性能持续领先；重建6视图场景仅需33毫秒，为七种前馈方法中最快，比之前状态方法快4.2倍；训练速度提升2.7倍（24视图时达5.9倍），推理内存降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: LoCoSplat通过利用局部上下文信息，实现了无需复杂3D网络的高效、快速、低内存的3D高斯点云渲染，显著优于现有方法，具备极强的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) increasingly aggregates multi-view evidence with heavy learned 3D networks. We propose LoCoSplat (Local-Context Splatting), motivated by the observation that a Gaussian is a local primitive: once depth is predicted, what the 3D stage must add (scale, rotation, opacity) depends on the point cloud around each anchor, and a fixed local average of that neighbourhood is enough to supply it, no heavy network required. LoCoSplat realises exactly this average: it splats a 16-d linear projection of the point features into a fine and a coarse grid and reads both back at each anchor with a 0.14M-parameter pointwise MLP; with no learned 3D network and no dynamic sparse computation, its whole encoder runs as one fp16 CUDA graph.
