On RealEstate10K, LoCoSplat outperforms every prior feed-forward method on PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS at 6, 12, and 24 views, with a margin that widens as views densify (+3.3 PSNR over VolSplat, the prior voxel-aligned state of the art, at 24 views) and grows further under zero-shot transfer to ACID and fine-tuning on ScanNet. It reconstructs a 6-view scene in 33 ms on one NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 GPU, the fastest of seven feed-forward methods and $4.2\times$ faster than the previous state of the art, trains $2.7\times$ faster ($5.9\times$ at 24 views), and uses $6.7\times$ less inference memory.

</details>


### [47] [SelectOccFlow: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation for 3D Occupancy and Scene Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04356)
*Yuhang Wang,Kai Luo,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SelectOccFlow框架，通过选择性时空聚合提升自动驾驶中的3D场景理解，解决相机基占用和场景流预测中因语义不兼容、时序错位和体素结构不完整导致的敏感问题。方法包括语义引导采样（SGS）、状态条件时序聚合（SCTA）和范围感知空间聚合（ESA），在OpenOcc上取得44.9的最优OccScore，较前人提升4.2%，并在nuScenes-C噪声下提升11.1%的平均表现，显示更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机基3D场景理解面临几何、语义和运动建模挑战，现有方法在空间和时间聚合中易受语义不兼容、历史观测错位和体素结构不完整的影响，导致性能下降。

Method: 设计了三个核心模块：语义引导采样（SGS）以获取语义一致的图像特征；状态条件时序聚合（SCTA）根据体素状态选择性地融合历史信息；范围感知空间聚合（ESA）利用方向性结构支持增强体素前景几何完整性。

Result: 在OpenOcc数据集上达到44.9的OccScore，优于之前最佳结果4.2个百分点；在Occ3D-nus上保持竞争力；在nuScenes-C噪声条件下平均OccScore提升11.1%，表明对视觉退化具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SelectOccFlow通过多域选择性聚合有效提升了3D场景理解的精度与鲁棒性，为自动驾驶中的复杂环境感知提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding for autonomous driving requires modeling geometry, semantics, and motion. However, camera-based occupancy and scene flow prediction are sensitive to unreliable spatial and temporal aggregation, caused by semantically incompatible image features, misaligned historical observations, and incomplete voxel structures. To address this issue, we propose SelectOccFlow, a selective spatiotemporal aggregation framework that progressively refines contextual evidence across image, temporal, and voxel domains. To obtain semantically compatible image evidence, we design Semantic-Guided Sampling (SGS) to regulate feature sampling with semantic priors. Since reliable image evidence alone cannot resolve temporal inconsistency, we then present State-Conditioned Temporal Aggregation (SCTA) to selectively retrieve historical evidence according to voxel states. To further enhance the structural completeness of voxel representations, we introduce Extent-Aware Spatial Aggregation (ESA), which exploits directional structural support to refine foreground geometry. Experiments on OpenOcc demonstrate that SelectOccFlow achieves a state-of-the-art OccScore of 44.9, improving the previous best by +4.2%. It also maintains competitive occupancy performance on Occ3D-nus and improves the mean OccScore under nuScenes-C corruptions by +11.1%, demonstrating improved robustness to visual corruptions. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/muchen1021/SelectOccFlow.

</details>


### [48] [TRIM-ReID: Duplication-Aware Token Reduction and Modality-Aligned Interaction for Multi-Modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04361)
*Wanke Xia,Ruiding Zhu,Xingguo Xu,Zhengbo Zhang,Dongxia Liu,Yuan Jin,Taojie Zhu,Yiting Zhao,Yihang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIM-ReID是一种紧凑的多模态物体重识别框架，通过密集特征提取、模态内令牌压缩和跨模态对齐交互，有效解决现有方法中细粒度身份线索丢失和令牌冗余问题。其核心包括：1）基于DINOv3的密集身份表示（DIR）以保留空间一致性与语义丰富性；2）令牌多样性挖掘（TDM）减少重复，保留互补局部证据；3）模态关系交互（MRI）促进跨模态信息交换，并通过三角对齐损失保证语义一致性。在RGBNT201、RGBNT100和MSVR310数据集上均达到当前最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖全局图像-文本对齐优化的视觉编码器，并使用学习的重要性分数选择令牌，导致细粒度身份信息丢失、局部证据不足以及令牌冗余，引发噪声和计算开销增加。

Method: 提出TRIM-ReID框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 密集身份表示（DIR），利用DINOv3提取语义丰富且空间一致的补丁特征；(2) 令牌多样性挖掘（TDM），通过抑制重复补丁并保留信息多样性的策略生成紧凑的模态特定令牌集；(3) 模态关系交互（MRI），实现跨模态信息融合，并引入三角对齐损失以规范联合关系，确保跨模态语义一致性。

Result: 在RGBNT201、RGBNT100和MSVR310三个公开数据集上，TRIM-ReID均取得当前最优性能，验证了其在多模态物体重识别任务中的有效性与先进性。

Conclusion: TRIM-ReID通过统一的密集特征提取、令牌压缩与跨模态对齐机制，显著提升了多模态重识别的精度与效率，为处理复杂场景下的目标检索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-modal object re-identification exploits complementary RGB, near-infrared (NIR), and thermal-infrared (TIR) observations to retrieve target objects. However, existing methods commonly employ visual encoders optimized for global image-text alignment and select tokens using learned importance scores. Such designs fail to preserve fine-grained identity cues or explicitly account for token redundancy, resulting in underrepresented local evidence and duplicated tokens that lead to noisy and costly cross-modal interaction. To address this gap, we propose TRIM-ReID, a compact framework that unifies dense feature extraction, intra-modal token reduction, and inter-modal aligned interaction. Specifically, semantically rich and spatially coherent patch features are extracted by Dense Identity Representation (DIR), which leverages DINOv3 to preserve fine-grained identity information. We then introduce Token Diversity Mining (TDM) to identify complementary local evidence and construct compact modality-specific token sets by suppressing repetitive patches while preserving informative diversity. Retained tokens are subsequently fused by Modal Relational Interaction (MRI) to enable effective information exchange across modalities, while a triangular alignment loss explicitly regularizes their joint relationships to maintain cross-modal semantic consistency under independent token selection. Extensive experiments on RGBNT201, RGBNT100, and MSVR310 demonstrate that TRIM-ReID achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [Beyond Plausibility: Verifiable Fine-Grained Image Editing on Structured Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04381)
*Muyao Wang,Chen Zhu,Shiqi Yang,DongHyun Hwan,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VeriEdit-Bench，一个用于精细图像编辑的基准测试，基于153个SVG图形、图表、网页界面和演示文稿的源代码，包含1740个案例，支持确定性的四轴评估（编辑保真度、保留性、定位性和幅度），可实现像素级编辑掩码和精确的编辑规范，从而提供可复现的评分，揭示模型在不同任务中的具体表现与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在真实感与可验证性之间存在关键差距：基于真实图像的基准依赖人工或视觉-语言模型判断，而确定性评估多集中于合成形状画布，应用扩展主要限于图表。这使得难以精确衡量编辑执行程度、识别意外变化位置，以及判断模型间微小差异是真实能力差异还是评价者不确定性所致。

Method: 从153个SVG图形、图表、网页界面和演示文稿的源代码中构建数据集，通过受控的源代码修改生成目标图像，确保视觉上下文一致，并提供像素级编辑掩码和明确的编辑说明，实现四轴（编辑保真度、保留性、定位性、幅度）的确定性评估。

Result: 对11个编辑器进行评估发现，即使最强模型也未达到完全准确；同一着色操作在图表与SVG图形上的排名反转；具有相似像素精度的输出在定位性和变化幅度上仍存在显著差异。该分解评估提供了分级、可验证的反馈，揭示了模型特异性能力与失效模式，而这些在整体评分或依赖评价者的判断中可能被掩盖。

Conclusion: VeriEdit-Bench通过提供可复现、多维度的评估框架，填补了精细图像编辑中真实感与可验证性之间的空白，为模型性能分析提供了更精准、透明的工具，有助于推动该领域向更可靠、可解释的方向发展。

Abstract: Fine-grained image editing requires more than producing a visually plausible result: an editor must execute the requested attribute change precisely while leaving everything else intact. However, existing benchmarks leave a critical gap between realism and verifiability: benchmarks built on realistic images typically rely on human or vision--language model judgments, while deterministic evaluation has largely focused on synthetic shape canvases, with application-oriented extensions primarily limited to charts. This makes it difficult to determine precisely how much of a requested edit was executed, where unintended changes occurred, and whether small differences between models reflect genuine editing capability or evaluator uncertainty. To bridge this gap, we present VeriEdit-Bench, a benchmark for fine-grained, instruction-faithful image editing across realistic structured assets with deterministic, four-axis evaluation. Its 1,740 cases are compiled from the source code of 153 Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) graphics, charts, web interfaces, and presentation slides. Controlled source-code edits preserve the original visual context while yielding exact target images, pixel-level edit masks, and explicit edit specifications, enabling reproducible scoring along four axes: edit fidelity, preservation, localization, and magnitude. Evaluating eleven editors, we find that even the strongest model remains far from full credit; rankings for the same recoloring operation reverse between charts and SVG graphics; and outputs with similar pixel-accuracy profiles can still differ substantially in localization and change magnitude. This decomposition yields graded, verifiable feedback and exposes model-specific capability and failure profiles that holistic scores or evaluator-dependent judgments may obscure.

</details>


### [50] [Detecting Defects that Matter: An Application-Driven Benchmark for Anomaly Detection in Manufacturing and Retail Logistics (VAND 4.0 Challenge)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04392)
*Lars Heckler-Kram,Dorian Henning,Ashwin Vaidya,Jan-Hendrik Neudeck,Ulla Scheler,Anton Milan,Samet Akcay,Paula Ramos,Sebastian Höfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAND 4.0 Challenge introduces a realistic, hidden-test benchmark in industrial manufacturing (MVTec AD 2) and retail logistics (Kaputt 2). Results show unsupervised anomaly segmentation remains challenging (~57% SegF1), zero-shot methods lag by ~15 points, and DINOv3 backbones excel. In retail, supervised models are near saturation for common defects, but performance drops sharply on rare ones (spillage: ~53 AP, missing units: ~27 AP). VLMs underperform specialized models by ~28 AP; reference images offer no benefit. A new low-prevalence dataset (Kaputt-Rare) is released. Efficiency is prioritized via a novel metric—top methods are computationally heavy, revealing neglect of efficiency. Conclusion: need for efficiency-aware design and true anomaly detection for rare/shifted conditions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing anomaly detection benchmarks are saturated and unrealistic. There is a need for more practical, application-driven evaluation with hidden tests, robustness to distribution shifts, and consideration of computational efficiency. The challenge aims to identify real-world gaps, especially in rare defect detection and model efficiency.

Method: The VAND 4.0 Challenge evaluates methods on two new benchmarks: MVTec AD 2 (industrial) and Kaputt 2 (retail). It uses hidden test sets, measures performance (SegF1, AP), and introduces a novel efficiency metric combining throughput, memory, power, and accuracy. Methods are evaluated across supervised, unsupervised, zero-shot, and VLM-based approaches.

Result: In MVTec AD 2: best unsupervised method achieves ~57% pixel-level SegF1; zero-shot lags by ~15 points; DINOv3 backbones dominate. In Kaputt 2: supervised models near saturation for common defects; VLMs trail by ~28 AP; reference images ineffective; performance collapses on rare defects (spillage ~53 AP, missing units ~27 AP). Top-performing models are inefficient. A new dataset, Kaputt-Rare, is introduced.

Conclusion: The community must develop more efficiency-aware models and advance true anomaly detection techniques capable of handling rare defects and distribution shifts. Realistic benchmarks with hidden testing and holistic evaluation (performance + efficiency) are essential for meaningful progress.

Abstract: Existing Anomaly Detection benchmarks are saturated and often unrealistic. As part of the VAND 4.0 Challenge, we introduce a hidden-test, application-driven benchmark across two deployment-critical domains: industrial manufacturing and retail logistics. In the Industrial Track (MVTec AD 2), the results reveal that unsupervised anomaly segmentation remains challenging: the best regular-setting method achieves only ~57\% pixel-level $SegF_1$, indicating substantial room for improvement. Zero-shot approaches trail by ~15 $SegF_1$ points, confirming that task-specific training on normal data remains essential for precise defect localization. Robustness to distribution shifts remains a key open challenge and DINOv3-backbones clearly dominate this track. In the Retail Track (Kaputt 2), the results reveal that (1) supervised defect detection is approaching saturation for common defect types; (2) the best off-the-shelf VLM approach trails specialized models by ~28 AP, confirming that currently VLMs cannot replace fine-tuned detectors, (3) reference images did not prove helpful for top-performing approaches. Performance collapses on rare defects (spillage ~53 AP, missing units ~27 AP), where the supervised ceiling is bounded by data availability. To drive future progress in this domain, we provide a new low-prevalence retail AD dataset (Kaputt-Rare). Across both tracks, computational efficiency is assessed as a first-class metric combining performance, throughput, memory, and power consumption. We introduce a novel metric for measuring efficiency and reveal that that top-performing methods rely on heavy architectures while efficiency is largely neglected. Overall, we conclude that the community needs (a) more efficiency-aware method development, and (b) true anomaly detection approaches for rare defects and shifting conditions. https://sites.google.com/view/vand4-cvpr2026/challenge

</details>


### [51] [AgroGround: Multi-Granularity Grounded Recognition in Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04425)
*Abdulla Alshehhi,Zongyan Han,Rao Anwer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgroGround，一个大规模的农业视觉定位数据集，用于识别植物病害和其他农业目标并定位其图像区域。通过自动化管道将八个农业VQA数据集的标签转换为病斑和整体对象的标注，生成794,850个指令样本。利用健康图像作为负样本监督，使模型在无病害时返回空预测。通过联合微调视觉-语言模型，结合仅定位与识别+定位的指令，在1,480张人工验证图像上评估，结果显示：仅定位微调使识别准确率从51.8%降至29.1%，加入识别+定位指令后提升至72.6%；联合准确率从19.2%提升至43.3%，且在健康图像上的拒绝率高达95.0%。强化学习进一步优化了病斑级定位效果。最终2B参数模型在基准测试和外部PlantSeg数据集上均超越教师模型的定位F1分数。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业视觉模型通常只评估识别或定位，而实际诊断需要同时准确识别目标并精确定位其在图像中的位置。尽管农业VQA数据集包含丰富语义标签，但缺乏与图像区域的关联，手动标注成本高。因此亟需一个大规模、自动构建的带区域标注的农业视觉问答数据集，以支持联合识别与定位任务，并实现对健康图像的正确拒绝。

Method: 设计自动化流程，将八个农业VQA数据集的标签转化为病斑和整体对象的图像区域标注；利用健康图像作为负样本进行监督训练，使模型学会在无病害情况下输出空预测；采用共享视觉-语言模型，分别进行仅定位和识别+定位的微调；引入强化学习优化病斑级定位精度；在独立于训练数据的人工验证集上评估模型性能。

Result: 仅定位微调导致识别准确率下降（51.8% → 29.1%），但加入识别+定位指令后识别准确率回升至72.6%；联合识别与定位准确率从19.2%提升至43.3%；健康图像拒绝率达95.0%；强化学习显著改善病变区域定位；最终2B模型在自身基准和外部PlantSeg测试集上均优于教师模型的定位F1分数。

Conclusion: AgroGround成功构建了一个大规模、高质量的农业视觉定位基准数据集，能够有效评估模型在识别、定位及健康图像拒绝方面的综合能力。该框架支持端到端的联合建模，显著提升了农业视觉理解系统的可靠性与实用性，为未来智能农业诊断提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Agricultural visual models are typically evaluated for either recognition or localization, but reliable diagnosis requires identifying what is present and localizing the evidence. Agricultural visual question answering (VQA) datasets carry rich semantic labels but rarely link them to image regions, and adding such annotations by hand is costly at scale. We introduce AgroGround, a large-scale dataset for grounded agricultural recognition: identifying plant diseases and other agricultural targets and localizing their image regions. An automated pipeline converts the labels of eight agricultural VQA datasets into annotations for disease lesions and whole objects, producing 794,850 instruction examples. Healthy images provide negative supervision for disease queries, teaching the model to return empty predictions. We fine-tune a shared vision-language model on known-target grounding instructions combined with instructions requiring both recognition and localization. We evaluate predicted identities, regions, joint correctness, and healthy-image abstention on 1,480 human-verified images disjoint from all training data. Grounding-only fine-tuning reduces recognition accuracy from 51.8\% to 29.1\%, while adding recognition-and-localization instructions raises it to 72.6\%. With images and annotations held fixed, combining the two formats raises joint accuracy from 19.2\% to 43.3\% at comparable grounding. Healthy negatives raise abstention on healthy images to 95.0\%, and reinforcement learning improves lesion-level grounding. The resulting 2B model exceeds its annotation teacher in grounding F1 on our benchmark and on the external PlantSeg test set. AgroGround establishes a benchmark for grounded agricultural recognition, measuring joint correctness of identity and localization along with abstention on healthy images. The code is available at https://github.com/AB-Abdulla/AgroGround.

</details>


### [52] [Video2World: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive World Modeling from Embodied Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04432)
*Jinzhou Tang,Zijun Zhang,Jing Yang,Yuchen Yan,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Ruobing Han,Jinglin Cao,Wenpeng Xu,Lukun He,Minghao Fu,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用前沿基础模型和编码代理实现从真实世界视频到模拟环境的端到端自动化重建。提出将该任务视为软件工程问题，并引入新基准Video2World，涵盖222个重建实例，评估几何、动态和功能正确性。实验表明，自Claude Opus 5起任务成功率显著提升（从不足5%升至15%以上），但与人工重建仍有较大差距；且视觉保真度高并不等于任务成功高，反映出生成模型中感知真实与事实正确之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前构建交互式模拟器仍严重依赖人工环境搭建与校准，限制了具身数据的规模化。为突破这一瓶颈，本文探索利用前沿基础模型和编码代理实现从真实视频到仿真环境的全自动重建，旨在降低人工干预、提升可扩展性。

Method: 将视频到模拟的重建过程建模为一个软件工程任务：编码代理观察具身视频，构建对应仿真环境与机器人行为，并通过执行反馈进行迭代优化。引入新基准Video2World，包含来自189段机器人与人类示范视频的222个重建实例，从几何、动态和功能三个维度评估重建质量。

Result: 在9个前沿编码代理系统上评估显示，自Claude Opus 5起任务成功率显著提升（从低于5%跃升至超过15%），但与人工辅助重建相比仍有明显差距。此外，发现视觉保真度高的环境未必任务表现更好，揭示了感知真实与实际功能性之间的不一致。

Conclusion: 尽管编码代理在视频到模拟的自动化重建中展现出显著进步，但距离完全自主化仍存在挑战。视觉保真度并非任务成功的充分条件，未来需更注重物理合理性与功能正确性，推动模型向更深层次的因果理解发展。

Abstract: Building interactive simulators from real-world observations is a promising way to scale embodied data, but current pipelines still rely heavily on manual environment construction and calibration. We study whether frontier foundation models and coding agents can automate this process end to end. We formulate \emph{autonomous video-to-simulation} as a software engineering task in which an agent observes an embodied video, constructs the corresponding simulated environment and robot behavior, and iteratively refines the result through execution feedback. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{Video2World}, a benchmark comprising 222 reconstruction instances derived from 189 robot and human demonstration videos. Video2World measures reconstructed worlds along geometric fidelity, dynamic fidelity, and functional correctness, capturing spatial perception, physical reasoning, and executable interaction. Evaluating 9 frontier coding-agent systems reveals a sharp improvement in Task success beginning with Claude Opus 5, rising from below 5\% to over 15\%, while substantial gaps to human-assisted reconstruction remain. We further find that worlds that look better could work worse: better visual fidelity does not always lead to higher task success. This echoes the broader gap between perceptual realism and factual correctness observed in generative models.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Crop Leaf Disease Recognition: A Unified Benchmark and Cross-Region Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04456)
*Rosemary Nalwanga,Sebastian Bunda,Godliver Owomugisha,Luuk Spreeuwers,Estefania Talavera Martinez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出MLD多区域作物叶片疾病数据集，整合了来自美国、亚洲和非洲的六个公开数据集，构建了一个包含18种作物、56个病害类别（每种作物一个健康类）的统一层次分类体系，共167,427张图像。研究定义了单源与多源标准化评估协议以检验跨区域泛化能力，并探索基于作物-病害依赖关系的层次化模型HiLeaD是否能提升跨区域识别性能。结果显示，仅在PlantVillage上训练的模型在域内疾病F1达到99.07%，但在PlantDoc上骤降至12.88%，暴露出严重的跨区域域偏移问题。而使用合并的MLD数据集训练后，跨区域F1提升至39.64%（提升26.76个百分点），且层次化模型带来1.71至4.88个百分点的额外增益，表明当前进展受限于数据覆盖范围与多样性，而非模型设计本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在受控实验室条件下报告近乎完美的准确率，但其在真实世界跨区域场景下的泛化能力缺乏充分评估。为更真实地衡量模型性能，亟需一个涵盖多地区、多作物、多病害的统一基准数据集及评估协议，以揭示并解决跨区域域偏移问题。

Method: 构建MLD数据集，整合六个来自不同地区的公开作物病害数据集，建立统一的层次分类结构；设计单源与多源评估协议以测试跨区域泛化能力；引入层次化疾病识别模型HiLeaD，通过将病害预测条件化于作物预测来建模作物-病害间的内在依赖关系。

Result: 在仅使用PlantVillage训练时，模型在跨区域测试中表现极差（F1=12.88%），而使用合并的MLD数据集训练后，跨区域疾病F1显著提升至39.64%（提升26.76个百分点）。层次化模型（HiLeaD）在所有设置下均带来1.71至4.88个百分点的额外性能增益，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 当前作物叶片疾病识别的瓶颈主要在于训练数据的覆盖范围与多样性不足，而非模型架构设计。通过构建更全面、跨区域的数据集并采用合理的层次化建模策略，可有效缓解跨区域域偏移问题，推动领域向更真实、可靠的实用化发展。

Abstract: Deep learning models for crop leaf disease recognition routinely report near-perfect accuracy yet are typically trained and evaluated on a single dataset collected under controlled laboratory conditions, leaving their behavior under realistic cross-region domain shift poorly understood. We introduce MLD (Multi-crop Leaf Disease) dataset, a unified multi-region benchmark that combines six public crop-disease datasets from the USA, Asia, and Africa into a shared hierarchical taxonomy spanning 18 crops, 56 crop-disease classes (including one healthy class per crop) making 167,427 images. We define standardized single-source and pooled multi-source evaluation protocols that explicitly probe cross-region generalization. We also investigate whether exploiting the inherent crop-to-disease dependency via a hierarchical formulation (HiLeaD) that conditions disease prediction on the predicted crop improves recognition under cross-region shift. Under the HiLeaD, the model trained on PlantVillage achieves 99.07% in-domain disease F1 but collapses to 12.88% when tested on PlantDoc, exposing a severe cross-region domain gap. The model trained on the pooled MLD dataset partly recovers cross-region disease F1 from 12.88% to 39.64% on PlantDoc (HiLeaD), achieving a 26.76 percentage point improvement. The hierarchical formulation provides a consistent additional gain, ranging from 1.71 to 4.88 percentage points in disease F1 over the flat baseline under the MLD dataset indicating that progress in this area is currently limited more by data coverage and diversity than by model design.

</details>


### [54] [RPFQ-ViT: Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization for Extremely Low-Bit Weights in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04457)
*Mengyuan Fan,Bokai Huang,JiaMing Pan,Xiaokun Yuan,Peizhuang Cong,Zhewen Tan,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RPFQ-ViT提出一种旋转相位帧量化方法，通过在二维相位平面中量化成对通道，更好地保持Transformer投影方向，同时用轻量缩放恢复幅度。该方法可作为nn.Linear的即插即用量化训练替换，不改变标准注意力、归一化和激活计算图。在ImageNet-1K上，W2/A4下ViT-B/16达到79.33% Top-1，Swin-T和DeiT-S在W2/A8下分别达79.30%和77.41% Top-1。模型压缩比达5.4–7.1×，端到端设备延迟降低1.4–1.6×，适用于移动端极低比特部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers虽性能优异，但高维线性投影与注意力计算带来巨大存储与推理开销。传统低比特量化因实值标量码本不匹配Transformer投影的方向几何，导致精度严重下降。需设计更适配方向特性的量化方法以实现高效低比特部署。

Method: 提出RPFQ-ViT：将成对通道在二维相位平面中进行量化，引入可学习旋转、相位锚点学习与残差相位精炼，利用轻量缩放恢复幅度，保留投影方向信息。支持即插即用的QAT替换，兼容原模型结构。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K上，RPFQ-ViT-B/16在W2/A4下达到79.33% Top-1；Swin-T在W2/A8下为79.30%；DeiT-S在W2/A8下为77.41%。模型大小压缩5.4–7.1倍，设备端延迟降低1.4–1.6倍。各项分析验证了通道配对、可学习旋转等模块的有效性。

Conclusion: RPFQ-ViT通过相位空间中的方向感知量化，在极低比特下实现了高精度、强压缩与实用移动端部署的良好平衡，是低比特Vision Transformer的重要进展。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition and mobile vision applications, but their high-dimensional linear projections and attention computations still impose substantial storage and inference costs. Extremely low-bit quantization is a promising solution, yet ViTs often suffer severe accuracy degradation because conventional real-valued scalar codebooks are poorly matched to the directional geometry of Transformer projections. We present RPFQ-ViT, a Rotated Phase-Frame Quantization method that quantizes paired channels in two-dimensional phase planes, enabling low-bit codes to better preserve projection directions while recovering magnitude with lightweight scaling. RPFQ-ViT serves as a drop-in QAT replacement for nn.Linear and does not modify the standard real-valued attention, normalization, or activation computation graph. On ImageNet-1K, RPFQ-ViT-B/16 reaches 79.33% Top-1 / 94.48% Top-5 under W2/A4, Swin-T reaches 79.30% Top-1 / 94.79% Top-5 under W2/A8, and DeiT-S reaches 77.41% Top-1 / 93.11% Top-5 under W2/A8. Ablations, phase-geometry analysis, and direction-preservation metrics show that channel pairing, learnable rotation, phase-anchor learning, and residual phase refinement each improve quantization quality. We further deploy RPFQ-ViT image-classification models on native iOS and Android runtime stacks; with 2-bit packed weights, model size shrinks by roughly $5.4$-$7.1\times$ relative to FP32 and end-to-end on-device latency drops by $1.4$-$1.6\times$. All ImageNet results trained in our codebase use a matched 300-epoch recipe and are reported as mean accuracies over three independent runs. These results show that RPFQ-ViT provides a favorable trade-off among accuracy, compression, and practical mobile deployment for extremely low-bit ViTs.

</details>


### [55] [Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04499)
*Erjian Zhang,Jiayuan Ma,Liejun Wang,Yikemaiti Sataer,Xiaoming Tao,Zhiqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HSA-HER框架，通过显式同质分布约束和分层专家路由机制，解决视觉异常线索被稀释的问题，实现更精准的放射学报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在跨模态交互和外部先验融合方面进展显著，但存在潜在表示分布偏移及异构信息刚性耦合问题，导致微弱视觉异常线索被大量文本先验和生成惯性掩盖。

Method: 引入隐空间中的同质分布约束以消除跨模态分布偏移，提取纯净视觉特征作为语义锚点；设计基于疾病语义锚点的分层专家路由机制，动态激活专家网络对多源证据进行定向挖掘与语义重建，并自适应分配融合权重。

Result: 在三个主流基准数据集上实验表明，HSA-HER达到领先性能，能准确描述复杂的影像细节和关键诊断信息。

Conclusion: 所提HSA-HER框架有效缓解了视觉异常线索稀释问题，提升了放射学报告生成的准确性与可解释性，具有良好的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) aims to convert medical images into diagnostic texts to assist in clinical decision-making and alleviate the workload of physicians. Although existing methods have made extensive progress in cross-modal interaction and the incorporation of external priors, the distribution shift of underlying representations and the undifferentiated rigid coupling of heterogeneous information cause weak visual abnormality cues to be easily diluted by massive text priors and generation inertia during decoding. To overcome this bottleneck, inspired by cognitive science, we propose a novel two-stage Homogeneous Semantic Alignment and Hierarchical Expert Routing (HSA-HER) framework. First, the model introduces an explicit homogeneous distribution constraint in the underlying latent space to effectively eliminate the cross-modal distribution shift between visual and textual features, thereby extracting purified visual features as semantic anchors that accurately align with diseases. Second, for heterogeneous clinical evidence composed of visual features, local entities, and global retrievals, we design a hierarchical expert routing mechanism guided by these disease semantic anchors. This mechanism abandons the undifferentiated rigid coupling paradigm. Specifically, it dynamically activates expert networks to perform targeted mining and semantic reconstruction on multi-source evidence, and adaptively allocates fusion weights. Extensive experiments on three mainstream benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSA-HER achieves state-of-the-art performance, accurately depicting complex imaging details and key diagnostic information.

</details>


### [56] [Localization Lens for Improving Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04502)
*Hasan Farooq,Murtaza Taj,Mehwish Nasim,Arif Mahmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种定位感知增强方法，通过数据、架构和对齐三个层面改进医学视觉-语言模型（Med-VLM），以提升其对解剖结构和空间位置的理解能力。引入专家验证的定位透镜（localization lens）提供更丰富的解剖与空间上下文；在架构中采用像素洗牌（pixel shuffle）处理复杂输入，保留解剖连续性的同时增强空间信息处理；使用解耦对比损失（DCL）加强定位表示与文本特征的对齐。实验表明，该方法在多个Med-VQA数据集上显著提升定位驱动任务性能，整体准确率最高提升6.2%，且为模型无关设计，可无缝集成至现有Med-VLM系统。代码与数据将公开于GitHub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学视觉-语言模型在理解解剖结构和空间定位方面表现不足，而这些能力对临床推理至关重要。因此需要一种有效机制来增强模型对空间与解剖信息的理解能力。

Method: 提出三层次改进：1）引入专家验证的定位透镜以丰富输入上下文；2）在模型架构中引入像素洗牌以过滤和优化表示，提升空间信息处理能力；3）结合解耦对比损失（DCL）与标准损失，实现定位表示与文本特征的有效对齐。

Result: 在多个医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）数据集上，所提方法显著提升了定位相关任务的性能，尤其在解剖与空间推理方面带来高达6.2%的整体准确率提升。该方法具有模型无关性，可广泛适配现有Med-VLM框架。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的定位感知增强框架有效提升了医学视觉-语言模型在解剖结构与空间定位理解上的能力，为临床医学智能推理提供了有力支持，且具备良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in clinical tasks. However, they often struggle to understand anatomical structures and spatial positioning, which are crucial for medical reasoning. To address this, we propose a localization-aware enhancement to the Med-VLM pipeline, introducing improvements at three levels: data,architecture, and alignment. First, we introduce localization lens, a set of expert-validated representations that provide richer anatomical and positional context. However, as these representations increase input complexity, we integrate pixel shuffle within the model architecture to filter and refine representations, enhancing spatial information processing while preserving anatomical continuity. Lastly, to effectively align the localization lens representations with textual features, we incorporate decoupled contrastive loss (DCL) alongside the standard loss function. This ensures better feature discrimination and robustness, particularly in data limited medical settings. Through extensive evaluations on medical visual question answering (Med-VQA) datasets, we show that our methodology improves localization-driven performance across different Med-VLM architectures. Our analysis of localization-based questions further reveals that improvements in anatomy and spatial reasoning directly enhance the overall accuracy of Med-VQA upto 6.2%. The proposed approach is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into existing Med-VLM pipelines. The dataset, code, and trained models will be made publicly available at https://github.com/CVLABLUMS/localizationlens.

</details>


### [57] [EgoExo-Next:Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Visual-Option Next-State and Cross-View Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04506)
*Yutong Li,Molin Wang,Xiaotong Li,Yanyan Fang,Daoguo Dong,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoExo-Next基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在动态视觉状态推理方面的能力，特别是对未来视觉状态的预测。该基准包含2,503个精心策划的问题，涵盖四种子任务，评估模型在第一人称和第三人称视角下的未来状态预测能力。实验表明，当前模型与人类表现之间存在显著差距，尤其在组合任务中表现最差，揭示了现有VLM在时空推理和跨视角推理融合方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型评估多集中于语义事件理解、时间关系或已观察视图间的对应，缺乏对模型直接推理未来视觉状态能力的考察。为填补这一空白，需要一个专门针对动态视觉状态推理的新基准。

Method: 构建EgoExo-Next基准，包含来自六个公开数据集的2,503个四选一问题，涵盖四个子任务：第一人称未来状态预测、双向第一人称-第三人称状态对应、第三人称未来状态预测及其组合任务（Ego-to-Exo Next-State）。通过广泛评测多种主流VLMs，分析其在不同任务上的表现。

Result: 最佳模型平均准确率为43.81%，而人类达到98.55%；在组合任务Ego-to-Exo Next-State中性能下降最为严重，显示出当前模型在融合时空进展与跨视角推理方面存在明显短板。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在动态视觉状态推理方面仍存在重大局限，尤其是在需要同时处理时间演进和跨视角映射的复杂任务中，亟需更先进的建模方法以提升其泛化与推理能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly evaluated for egocentric and cross-view video reasoning, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on semantic event understanding, temporal relations, or correspondence between already observed views, leaving their ability to reason directly about future visual states underexplored. We introduce EgoExo-Next, a visual-option benchmark for dynamic visual-state reasoning, where models must identify how an observed action trajectory subsequently appears rather than predict only an action label or textual description. EgoExo-Next contains 2,503 human-curated four-choice questions from six public egocentric and ego--exo video sources and comprises four interconnected subtasks that evaluate egocentric next-state prediction, bidirectional ego--exo state correspondence, exocentric next-state prediction, and their composition in Ego-to-Exo Next-State. Extensive evaluation of proprietary, open-source, and spatial reasoning VLMs reveals a substantial human--model gap, with the best model achieving 43.81\% average accuracy compared with 98.55\% for humans, and the largest degradation occurring on the composed Ego-to-Exo task. These results suggest that current VLMs remain substantially limited in dynamic visual-state reasoning, particularly when temporal progression and cross-view reasoning must be composed. The benchmark is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/yutongli2024/EgoExo-Next}.

</details>


### [58] [TAME:Topology-Aware Text-Driven Motion Editing across Heterogeneous Humanoid Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04529)
*Qichen Zheng,Siyuan Yang,Chong Wang,Jun Liu,Shijian Lu,Alex Kot,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME提出了一种拓扑感知的运动编辑方法，通过引入拓扑约束的骨骼传播（TCSP）和聚焦编辑的表示对齐（EFRA），实现对不同骨骼拓扑的真人型角色运动序列进行文本驱动的精准编辑。该方法在多拓扑数据集TopoMotionFix上表现出色，尤其在未见拓扑上的泛化能力显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动运动编辑方法通常依赖固定骨骼拓扑，难以适应动画流程中角色骨骼结构差异大的问题。为解决这一局限性，需要一种能够处理不同拓扑结构的通用运动编辑框架。

Method: TAME采用基于流匹配的Transformer架构，将运动表示为每关节每帧的令牌，并通过骨骼、时间与文本跨注意力层建模关节间、帧间及与文本指令的关系。其中，使用拓扑约束的骨骼传播（TCSP）仅允许关注骨骼邻接矩阵中的一跳邻居，以保持层级结构；同时引入自蒸馏的表示对齐策略EFRA，仅在与编辑相关的关节-时间令牌上对齐学生与教师特征，提升指令忠实度。

Result: TAME在MotionFix数据集上实现了更高的编辑对齐度和源运动保真度，在扩展的TopoMotionFix数据集上展现出对未见骨骼拓扑的强大泛化能力，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TAME成功实现了对多种骨骼拓扑的文本驱动运动编辑，具备良好的可迁移性和实用性，为复杂动画管线中的运动编辑提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-driven motion editing modifies an existing motion sequence according to a text instruction while preserving the content of the source motion. Existing methods are typically built for a single, fixed skeletal topology, which limits their use in animation pipelines where characters differ in joint count and skeletal hierarchy. We present Topology-Aware Motion Editor (TAME), a flow-matching transformer that edits motions on humanoid skeletons of varying topology. TAME represents motion as per-joint, per-frame tokens and models interactions among joints, across frames, and with the text instruction through skeletal, temporal, and text cross-attention layers. To make the skeletal attention follow each character's hierarchy, TAME replaces full joint attention with Topology-Constrained Skeletal Propagation (TCSP), which restricts attention to one-hop kinematic neighbors in the skeleton's adjacency matrix. We further introduce Edit-Focused Representation Alignment (EFRA), a self-distilled representation alignment strategy that aligns student features with cleaner EMA-teacher features exclusively on edit-relevant joint-time tokens, making edits faithful to the instruction. To make this setting trainable and comparable, we construct TopoMotionFix, a multi-topology extension of MotionFix with seen- and unseen-topology evaluation protocols. TAME outperforms previous methods in edit alignment and source preservation on MotionFix and reliably edits motions on unseen skeletons in TopoMotionFix.

</details>


### [59] [FASTER: Fast Adjoint Stochastic Transport for Endpoint Refinement in Reward-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04538)
*Yimiao Zhou,Zejia Zhong,Jingya Wang,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FASTER 是一种高效且可扩展的奖励引导图像编辑方法，通过训练小型网络在测试时实现低成本编辑，同时利用预训练生成模型和奖励模型提供反馈。该方法通过重用候选样本及其反馈，在不依赖大模型反复执行或反向传播的情况下，显著提升编辑速度，相比基线方法在 Stable Diffusion 3 上提速最高达 6.91 倍，在 Stable Diffusion 1.5 上高达 24.14 倍，同时在多个指标上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在测试时进行奖励引导图像编辑通常依赖昂贵的大模型重复运行或反向传播，导致效率低下。需要一种既能保持源内容与视觉合理性，又能高效优化的方法。

Method: 提出一个理论框架，联合建模奖励、源内容保留与预训练先验偏好；基于此框架设计 FASTER，为每个源图像和目标训练小型网络，利用预训练生成器与奖励模型对候选输出进行反馈，并通过重用反馈信息减少重复采样与监督查询。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion 3 上，FASTER 在四个目标指标和多个验证指标上均优于其他方法；相较于基于生成轨迹优化控制的基线方法，编辑速度提升最高达 6.91 倍（SD3）和 24.14 倍（SD1.5）。

Conclusion: FASTER 实现了高效、可扩展的测试时图像编辑，在保证质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，是现有方法的有效改进。

Abstract: Reward-guided image editing at test time seeks to improve a specified reward while preserving source content and visual plausibility. Many existing approaches optimize candidates through pretrained generation processes, making repeated adjustment depend on costly large-model execution and, in some cases, backbone backpropagation. We develop a theoretical framework that jointly accounts for reward, source preservation, and pretrained-prior preferences, allowing the desired output distribution to be specified separately from the dynamics used to realize it. Based on this framework, we introduce FASTER, which trains a small network for each source and objective to perform inexpensive editing, while pretrained and reward models provide feedback on candidate outputs. By reusing each candidate and its feedback across multiple small-network updates, FASTER reduces repeated sampling and supervision queries without placing the pretrained generative backbone inside the inner optimization loop. On SD3, FASTER leads all four target metrics and several validation metrics among the evaluated methods. Compared with the evaluated baseline that optimizes controls along pretrained generation trajectories, FASTER achieves editing-time speedups of up to \({6.91\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 3 and \({24.14\times}\) on Stable Diffusion 1.5.

</details>


### [60] [EagleDepth: Efficient Fine-Grained Depth Estimation via Pixel Diffusion Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04554)
*Bowen Chai,Tianbao Zhang,Shuyu Wu,Dexin Zuo,Zhaoxin Fan,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EagleDepth 是一种高效的单目深度估计框架，结合了潜在扩散模型的几何先验与细粒度像素空间生成，通过在像素空间直接生成深度图来避免传统方法中由潜在空间解码带来的细节损失和推理开销。该框架分两阶段训练：首先微调预训练的潜在扩散模型以获取深度感知的潜在表示；随后使用预训练的像素扩散解码器（PiD）在多分辨率下逐步预测深度图，从1024到4K。潜变量分支处理低分辨率图像，而像素分支在目标分辨率上生成深度图，无需依赖原始VAE解码器，从而保留几何知识并提升推理速度。在五个常用数据集及高分辨率Synth4K数据集上，EagleDepth实现了最先进的性能，同时更好地保持了精细结构和物体边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖潜在空间建模和VAE重建，在高分辨率图像中容易丢失几何细节，且从潜在编码解码带来显著推理开销。为解决这些问题，需要一种既能保留几何先验又能高效生成高分辨率深度图的方法。

Method: 提出EagleDepth框架，分两阶段训练：第一阶段微调预训练潜在扩散模型，利用RGB-深度配对监督学习深度感知的潜在表示；第二阶段将预训练的像素扩散解码器PiD适配为条件深度预测模型，从1024分辨率开始逐级扩展至4K分辨率，直接在像素空间生成深度图。潜变量分支处理低分辨率输入，像素分支在目标分辨率输出深度，跳过原VAE解码器。

Result: 在五个标准深度估计数据集和高分辨率Synth4K数据集上，EagleDepth均达到当前最优性能，不仅精度更高，而且推理速度更快，对细粒度结构和物体边界的保留效果更优。

Conclusion: EagleDepth通过融合潜在扩散的几何先验与像素空间的精细生成能力，有效解决了高分辨率深度估计中的细节损失与推理效率问题，是一种高效、精确的单目深度估计新范式。

Abstract: Recovering detailed geometry from high-resolution images is critical for precise perception of the surroundings and objects. However, existing methods which use latent-space modeling and VAE reconstruction can compromise geometric details. Furthermore, decoding from latent codes introduces substantial inference overhead. To address those issues, we present EagleDepth, an efficient framework for high-resolution monocular depth estimation that combines the geometric priors of latent diffusion with fine-grained pixel-space generation. Our key idea is to retain depth-aware latent representations as guidance while generating the final depth map directly in pixel space. We train the latent and pixel components sequentially: first, we fine-tune a pretrained latent diffusion model using paired RGB--depth supervision; then, we adapt a pretrained pixel diffusion decoder, PiD, to predict depth conditioned on the learned features. Training of the pixel component starts at 1024 resolution and continues across multiple resolutions up to 4K. The latent branch processes resized, lower-resolution RGB images, while the pixel branch generates depth at the target resolution, bypassing the original VAE decoder. This design preserves learned geometric knowledge without requiring the latent backbone to operate at the output resolution. On five commonly used depth estimation datasets and the high-resolution Synth4K dataset, our framework achieves state-of-the-art depth estimation performance, with faster inference and better preservation of fine structures and object boundaries.

</details>


### [61] [Frozen in a Frame: The Velocity Blind Spot in JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04585)
*Tinghe Zhang,Chunyu Liu,Yu Leon Liu,Zerui Zhao,Jiaheng Chen,Yucheng Xiao,Jiaxing Li,Yunlong Wang,Alex Lamb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JEPAs suffer from a structural blind spot: single-frame embeddings lack velocity information, leading to poor motion prediction. This paper introduces RateIdent, a diagnostic protocol, and TI-JEPA, a lightweight fix that splits the latent into pose and explicit motion codes. TI-JEPA significantly improves planning and rollout accuracy across multiple environments, outperforms baselines with better interpretability, and requires no privileged supervision.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Single-frame embeddings in JEPAs cannot capture velocity due to the absence of motion blur, limiting their ability to model dynamic systems effectively. This leads to poor performance in tasks requiring motion prediction.

Method: Proposes RateIdent for diagnosing velocity representation and TI-JEPA, which separates latent space into pose and finite-difference motion codes, enabling joint prediction without relying on single-frame targets.

Result: TI-JEPA achieves significant improvements in planning tasks (e.g., 55% lower final distance on Pendulum, 64% on CartPole), outperforms memory-based and recurrent baselines, maintains probeability for pose and motion, and shows robust gains across six evaluated environments.

Conclusion: Predicting single-frame embeddings is inadequate when velocity matters; splitting the latent into pose and motion components provides a simple, effective, and interpretable solution without requiring additional supervision.

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) for world modeling train an encoder so a predictor maps a current embedding and action to the next frame's embedding, always from a single rendered frame. This has a structural blind spot: a renderer without motion blur draws a scene from configuration alone, so a single-frame embedding carries no velocity information, for any encoder, including the official released LeWM weights. We confirm this on official checkpoints across four real benchmarks (PushT, Reacher, Cube, TwoRoom): every linear velocity probe sits at or below chance while position probes reach R^2 about 0.95. We introduce RateIdent, a three-stage diagnostic protocol, and TI-JEPA, a lightweight fix splitting the latent into a pose code and an explicit finite-difference motion code, predicted jointly. Across three physically grounded environments, TI-JEPA gives a significant, seed-robust gain on a stop-at-goal planning task over a matched-memory baseline, e.g. 55% lower final distance on Pendulum (p=3.2x10^-10) and 64% on CartPole (p=5.1x10^-15). We reproduce this at official ViT-Tiny plus AdaLN-transformer scale, then push the same recipe onto real dm_control Reacher photographs trained from scratch, where TI-JEPA's branch separation exceeds the memory-having baseline's by roughly 38x, the paper's largest margin. Against a same-footprint recurrent RSSM-style predictor, TI-JEPA matches or beats its rollout accuracy on two of three environments, stays separately probeable for pose and motion, and wins outright on the most coupled one. A checkable formal argument and six evaluated environments show single-frame targets are the wrong object to predict when velocity matters, and a small, interpretable structural change fixes it with no privileged supervision. Code, checkpoints, and the project page are linked below the title.

</details>


### [62] [WASP: Weakly Aligned Spatiotemporal Pairs for Fetal Brain MRI-Ultrasound Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04601)
*Francesco Correnti,Gabriele Magrini,Marco Mistretta,Niccolò Biondi,Pietro Pala,Alessandro Ramalli,Simona Fiori,Andrew D. Bagdanov,Matteo Lenge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WASP框架，利用临床元数据（如胎龄和诊断切面）将超声（US）与磁共振成像（MRI）在弱对齐条件下进行跨模态对齐，使仅用超声预训练的模型能够理解未见过的胎儿MRI，无需微调主干网络。实验表明，在无MRI预训练的情况下，该方法显著提升胎龄估计准确性和标准切面分类性能；即使在双模态预训练模型中也带来一定改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏公开的配对胎儿MRI-US数据集，传统基于配对数据训练跨模态模型的方法不可行。如何让仅使用超声数据训练的模型理解有限的胎儿MRI信息成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出弱对齐时空对（WASP），将跨模态对应关系建模为由胎龄和诊断平面驱动的熵正则最优传输问题，构建轻量级对齐模块，将MRI表示映射到超声潜在空间，且不需微调主干网络。

Result: 在无MRI预训练情况下，胎龄估计误差从21.9天降至17.4天，标准切面分类准确率从61.9%升至69.0%；在双模态预训练模型中也有小幅度提升，如BioMedParse模型胎龄误差从6.5天降至6.0天，SAM-Med2d切面准确率从83.3%升至88.1%。

Conclusion: WASP框架有效解决了胎儿医学中跨模态对齐难题，可在无配对数据条件下实现超声模型对MRI的理解，具有重要应用价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is widely regarded as the optimal sensor for fetal brain analysis due to its superior soft-tissue contrast and anatomical detail. However, its high cost and operational burden make it invasive and difficult to obtain at scale. Ultrasound (US), in contrast, is cheap, safe, and routinely acquired, and as a result it has produced substantially larger datasets and a growing ecosystem of pretrained models. This asymmetry raises a natural question: Can we teach a US-only model to understand fetal MRI from only a limited set of examples? The standard recipe, training a foundation model on subject-to-subject paired MRI-US scans, is not viable since no such paired fetal dataset is publicly available. In this paper we address this gap with Weakly Aligned Spatiotemporal Pairs (WASP), a framework that formulates cross-modal correspondence as an entropic Optimal Transport problem driven by clinical metadata, in particular Gestational Age (GA) and diagnostic planes, enabling the fitting of a lightweight alignment module that lifts MRI representations into the US latent space, without fine-tuning the backbone. Empirically, WASP yields its largest gains when MRI is unseen by the model during pretraining (on USFM, GA estimation error drops from 21.9 to 17.4 days and standard plane classification accuracy climbs from 61.9% to 69.0%), while providing smaller, backbone-dependent refinements for backbones pretrained on both modalities (e.g., BioMedParse GA estimation error from 6.5 to 6.0 days and SAM-Med2d plane accuracy from 83.3% to 88.1%). Code is available at https://github.com/miccunifi/WASP.

</details>


### [63] [Organize Primitives into Semantic Parts: Reinforcement Reasoning for 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04602)
*Xiaoming Gong,Ruoyu Wu,Zhenhong Sun,Chunlin Chen,Daoyi Dong,Huadong Mo,Zhi Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RePart提出一种基于强化学习的3D语义分割方法，将几何原始体（primitive）组织为语义部分，通过马尔可夫决策过程建模并优化整体分割结果，实现精确边界保留与高性能分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖几何相似性进行原始体到语义部分的组织，但无法处理单个语义部分跨越多个原始体或几何相似原始体属于不同部分的情况，缺乏对语义结构的全局理解。

Method: 将原始体组织问题建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，构建可组合原始体工作空间，采用融合-停止策略，并通过轨迹级强化学习优化下游分割性能；最后通过边界感知表面标签法将结果映射回原始网格以保持边界精度。

Result: 在PartNet上，RePart在所有四个聚合分割指标上均达到最优；在3DCoMPaT++上，无需目标数据微调即获得最高RI和SC值，验证了其在语义组织与密集分割精度上的有效性。

Conclusion: 强化推理能够有效组织几何原始体为语义部分，同时保持高精度的表面分割边界，是一种强大的结构化3D语义分割框架。

Abstract: Primitive-based 3D segmentation offers a compact and explicit alternative to dense surface prediction, naturally supporting structural abstraction and boundary localization. However, geometric decomposition alone does not determine how primitives should be organized into semantic parts: a single part may span multiple primitives, while geometrically similar or touching primitives may belong to different parts. We therefore introduce RePart (Reinforcement Part Reasoning), which formulates primitive-to-part organization as a finite-horizon Markov decision process and learns semantic organization through trajectory-level reinforcement reasoning. RePart constructs a Composable Primitive Workspace from fine-grained superquadrics and applies a merge-and-stop policy whose decisions are optimized by their downstream effects on the resulting partition rather than local primitive compatibility. The inferred part identities are then mapped back to the original mesh through Boundary-Aware Surface Labeling, preserving accurate surface boundaries beyond the primitive approximation. On PartNet, RePart achieves the strongest results across all four aggregate partition metrics; on 3DCoMPaT++, it obtains the highest RI and SC without target-dataset fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that reinforcement reasoning provides an effective mechanism for organizing geometric primitives into semantic parts while retaining dense segmentation accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/EngineeringAI-LAB/RePart.

</details>


### [64] [ConEx: Human-Interpretable Saliency Maps via Concept-Aware Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04605)
*Yehonatan Elisha,Oren Barkan,Ziv Weiss Haddad,Noam Koenigstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ConEx 是一种新型框架，通过将显著性可视化与基于概念的推理相结合，解决现有视觉解释方法在低层像素重要性与高层语义概念之间缺乏关联的问题。它自动发现类别特定的概念，并使用概念激活向量（CAVs）表示，通过特定架构的掩码机制减少分割掩码引入的噪声，提高概念纯度。ConEx 生成忠实且可解释的显著性图，揭示每个概念在图像中的位置及其对预测的贡献。提出两种互补的评估指标：向量-概念匹配（VCM）和概念-类别匹配（CCM），用于量化概念对齐并与其他方法直接比较。大量实验表明，ConEx 在忠实性、分割和概念质量基准上均达到领先性能，推动了视觉模型解释向真正可解释、基于概念的方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉解释方法多关注像素级重要性，但难以将其与语义概念关联，导致解释力和可信度受限。需要一种能够连接低层显著性与高层语义概念的新方法，以提升解释的可理解性和可靠性。

Method: 提出 Concept-based Explanations (ConEx) 框架，利用架构特定的掩码机制自动发现类别相关的概念，并通过概念激活向量（CAVs）表示。该机制降低分割掩码带来的噪声，提升概念纯度。同时生成反映概念分布和贡献的忠实显著性图。

Result: 在多个数据集和设置下，ConEx 在忠实性、分割精度和概念质量方面均优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和先进性。

Conclusion: ConEx 成功实现了从像素显著性到语义概念的桥梁，提供兼具忠实性与可解释性的视觉解释，推动了视觉模型解释向更可信赖、概念驱动的方向发展。

Abstract: Many visual explanation methods in computer vision highlight pixel importance but struggle to link these low-level cues to semantically meaningful concepts, limiting their interpretability and trustworthiness. We introduce Concept-based Explanations (ConEx), a novel framework that bridges saliency visualization with concept-based reasoning to provide both faithfulness and interpretability. ConEx automatically discovers class-specific concepts and represents them through concept activation vectors (CAVs), learned without manual supervision using an architecture-specific masking mechanism that reduces noise introduced by the segmentation masks to enhance concept purity. ConEx generates faithful saliency maps that reveal where each concept appears in the image and how it contributes to the prediction. To evaluate the reliability of these learned concepts, we propose two complementary metrics, Vector-Concept Match (VCM) and Concept-Class Match (CCM), that quantify concept alignment and enable direct comparison with existing methods. Extensive experiments across diverse settings demonstrate that ConEx achieves state-of-the-art performance on faithfulness, segmentation, and concept-quality benchmarks. Overall, ConEx advances the field toward truly interpretable and concept-grounded explanations in vision models.

</details>


### [65] [Sparse-View 4D Gaussian Splatting via Spatiotemporal Priors and Generative Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04606)
*Shengqi Wang,Zhengxian Yang,Kaiwen Tian,Yang Liu,Bowen Liu,Hua Du,Taicheng Huang,Jiamin Wu,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种4D高斯点云框架，用于稀疏视角下的动态场景重建（仅6个广基线相机），通过区域自适应空间先验、运动一致的时间先验和生成辅助机制，在验证集上将全帧PSNR从25.60 dB提升至29.75 dB，测试集上达30.04 dB（全帧）和27.88 dB（前景），排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有六个广基线相机的稀疏视角条件下，实现鲁棒的动态场景重建面临巨大挑战，传统方法难以有效处理动态前景与静态背景的分离及时间一致性问题。

Method: 引入区域自适应空间先验（基于前景掩码引导初始化与投票控制密度）、运动一致的时间先验（通过帧插值与光流约束高斯运动）、以及生成辅助（在角度空白处设置虚拟相机，使用扩散模型生成图像并作为伪监督迭代融入训练）。

Result: 在验证集上全帧PSNR提升至29.75 dB，测试集上达到30.04 dB（全帧）和27.88 dB（前景），在稀疏视角赛道中排名第一。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了稀疏视角下动态场景重建难题，通过多模块协同设计显著提升了重建质量与鲁棒性，为未来低视角动态重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework for the Sparse-View Track of the SIGGRAPH Asia 2026 Volumetric Video Challenge, which requires dynamic scene reconstruction from only six cameras with wide baselines. To achieve robust dynamic reconstruction under such sparse views, our framework integrates three components. (1) Region-adaptive spatial priors: We use foreground masks to guide Gaussian initialization and mask voting to control densification separately for the dynamic foreground and static background. Background geometry is regularized using monocular depth aligned to metric scale. (2) Motion-consistent temporal priors: We provide supervision at intermediate times through frame interpolation and constrain projected Gaussian motion with estimated optical flow. (3) Generative assistance: We place virtual cameras in the widest angular gaps and restore their rendered images using a diffusion-based model conditioned on camera poses. The restored images are iteratively incorporated into training as pseudo-supervision. On the validation set, our framework improves full-frame PSNR from 25.60 dB for the baseline to 29.75 dB. On the official test benchmark, it achieves 30.04 dB full-frame PSNR and 27.88 dB foreground PSNR, ranking first overall in the Sparse-View Track.

</details>


### [66] [Task-Sensitive Geometry of Representation Transfer for Object Detection under Image Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04627)
*Van Vung Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究在图像退化条件下，干净图像监督如何影响检测性能。通过使用配对的干净与高斯退化BDD100K图像，发现干净教师蒸馏虽提升检测准确率，但知识迁移具有对象级别的异质性。提出一种正交桥方法，将互补表示成分映射到学生空间，可控干预可恢复13.22%原本丢失的对象，显著优于随机扰动（4.30%），且对保留对象影响极小。引入任务敏感几何（基于归一化检测损失梯度的通道空间几何），发现该几何中映射补集的方向与局部干预响应强度正相关，即使控制干预大小后仍显著（部分Spearman ρ = 0.242）。该关系在独立数据和不同模型上复现，但对直接残差路径不成立，且大干预下减弱。基于此信号的路由规则与专门蒸馏目标未带来统计上可靠的增益，表明将局部结构转化为全局训练优势仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在图像退化条件下，虽然干净图像监督能提升检测性能，但其知识迁移效果并非均匀分布，存在对象级异质性。理解这种迁移机制并识别哪些表示方向真正有助于退化图像中的目标检测，是提升鲁棒检测的关键。

Method: 使用成对干净与高斯退化的BDD100K图像，采用干净教师蒸馏；提出正交桥方法以映射互补表示成分；引入任务敏感几何（基于归一化检测损失梯度构建的通道空间几何）；通过可控干预评估局部响应，并分析其与几何方向的关系。

Result: 正交桥干预可恢复13.22%原本丢失的对象，显著优于随机扰动（4.30%）；任务敏感几何中映射补集方向与局部干预响应强度呈正相关（ρ = 0.242，95% CI [0.108, 0.359]），在独立数据和不同模型上复现；但基于该几何设计的路由规则与蒸馏目标未带来统计显著提升。

Conclusion: 研究支持局部、方向家族依赖的任务敏感几何的存在，但如何将此类局部结构有效转化为全局训练优势，仍是未解决的挑战。

Abstract: Object detection under image degradation can benefit from clean-image supervision, but aggregate gains do not imply that transferred representation changes are uniformly useful. We study how clean task knowledge affects degraded-image representations and whether local responses to structured representation directions can be characterized geometrically. Using paired clean and Gaussian-degraded BDD100K images, we show that clean-teacher distillation improves observed detection accuracy while producing heterogeneous object-level transfer. We isolate a representation component complementary to direct clean-teacher alignment and map it into the distilled student space through an orthogonal bridge. Controlled interventions rescue 13.22% of objects lost under the distilled representation, versus 4.30% under norm-matched random perturbations, with very low harm on preserved objects. We introduce task-sensitive geometry, a gradient-derived channel-space geometry constructed from normalized detection-loss gradients. On a reserved cohort, mapped-complement orientation within this frozen geometry is positively associated with local intervention-response magnitude after controlling for intervention magnitude (partial Spearman $ρ$ = 0.242, 95% CI [0.108, 0.359]). The relationship eplicates on independent data ($ρ$ = 0.180) and with RT-DETR-L ($ρ$ = 0.227), but not for the direct clean-teacher residual family, and it weakens for large interventions. Routing rules and specialized distillation objectives based on these signals do not yield statistically reliable gains over CLEANKD. These results support a local, direction-family-dependent task-sensitive geometry while showing that converting such structure into improved global training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [67] [FLASHSWIN: Unlocking Large Windows and Dense Tokens in Swin Vision Transformers with Memory Efficient Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04664)
*Tushar Kataria,Gerald Sabin,Ponnuswamy Sadayappan,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLASHSWIN提出一种基于FlashAttention的新型窗口注意力机制，突破了传统Swin Transformer在窗口大小和局部标记密度之间的权衡。通过避免显式构建注意力分数矩阵，将每窗口内存从O(M⁴)降低到O(M²)，使大窗口（如32×32）和高密度标记成为可能，同时保持训练内存不变。为恢复空间位置信息，引入窗口局部可学习2D RoPE，有效保留了位置感知能力。在相同模型规模下，FLASHSWIN-T在ImageNet-1K、COCO和ADE20K上分别达到84.1%、44.1和47.28，显著优于SwinV2-T。当窗口固定为32×32时，减小块大小可带来边界质量的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Swin Transformer因标准窗口注意力需显式构建M⁴量级的注意力分数矩阵，导致内存开销随窗口增大急剧上升，限制了其使用大窗口和高密度标记的能力。此外，相对位置偏置的引入也要求完整存储分数矩阵，进一步加剧了内存压力，从而迫使模型只能采用小窗口（M=8,16）和小块大小（p=4），影响细粒度任务性能。

Method: 提出FLASHSWIN，用FlashAttention替代标准窗口注意力，实现无需显式构建分数矩阵的精确softmax注意力计算，将每窗口内存复杂度从O(M⁴)降至O(M²)。同时，为弥补移除相对位置偏置带来的空间信息损失，引入窗口局部可学习2D RoPE以恢复位置编码能力。该设计允许在不增加内存负担的前提下使用更大窗口和更密集的标记网格。

Result: 在相同模型规模下，FLASHSWIN-T在ImageNet-1K上达到84.1%准确率，比SwinV2-T提升1.3个百分点；在COCO上框检测AP达44.1，提升5.1；在ADE20K上语义分割mIoU达47.28，提升1.82。当窗口固定为32×32且块大小减半时，边界质量提升显著，远超对mIoU的增益。训练内存仅需12.4GB，与小窗口版本相当，而SwinV2/V1-T则高达70/90GB。

Conclusion: FLASHSWIN成功突破了传统Swin架构在窗口大小与标记密度之间的固有权衡，实现了高分辨率视觉建模中大感受野与高局部细节的兼顾。通过FlashAttention与可学习2D RoPE的结合，既大幅降低内存开销，又保持甚至提升了模型性能，尤其在细粒度视觉任务中优势明显，为后续高分辨率视觉模型设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-resolution vision backbones have long been forced to trade away local token density to afford larger receptive fields. Hierarchical Swin transformers impose this compromise because standard windowed attention materializes an $M^2\times M^2$ score matrix per window, incurring $O(M^4)$ memory as windows or token grids grow. Furthermore, Swin adds a learned relative-position bias elementwise to attention scores, requiring full materialization of the score matrix and its gradient. This keeps Swin and SwinV2 trapped in a small-window($M=8,16$), coarse-token regime with patch size $4\times4$ ($p=4$), limiting performance for fine-grained tasks. We introduce FLASHSWIN, which replaces standard windowed attention with a FlashAttention implementation that computes exact softmax attention without materializing the score matrix, reducing per-window memory from $O(M^4)$ to $O(M^2)$. This enables higher token density and larger receptive fields without inflating memory overhead. Training memory is flat across window sizes: at a $32\times32$ window, FLASHSWIN-T requires only $12.4$\,GB, unchanged from $8\times8$, compared to $70/90$\,GB for SwinV2/V1-T. However, applying FlashAttention directly to Swin creates a trade-off: bypassing the score matrix precludes Swin's additive relative-position bias, forfeiting spatial information in exchange for memory efficiency. FLASHSWIN restores position information as window-local learnable 2D RoPE, making large windows and dense token grids both affordable and accurate. At matched scale, FLASHSWIN-T outperforms Swin variants. With dense tokens and wide windows ($p=2,M=32$), the same Tiny model reaches $84.1\%$ ImageNet-1K, $44.1$ COCO box AP, and $47.28$ ADE20K mIoU---gains of $+1.3$, $+5.1$, and $+1.82$ over SwinV2-T at $M=16$, respectively. At fixed $M=32$, halving the patch size yields roughly $3\times$ larger gains in boundary quality than in mIoU.

</details>


### [68] [COMPASS: Comet Object Measurement Pipeline with Automated Selection and Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04680)
*Jack Roberts,Canya Lu,Alexis Michelle Lawson,Kaitlyn Holden,Gerald S. Wilkinson,Anne M. Bronikowski,Ritambhara Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COMPASS is a deep learning-based pipeline that automates comet assay image analysis, enabling robust, standardized, and reproducible DNA damage quantification with minimal manual effort.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Manual and semi-automated image analysis in the comet assay is time-consuming, inconsistent, and sensitive to image quality and comet shape variations, necessitating a more reliable and automated solution.

Method: COMPASS uses deep learning for comet segmentation, automated damage measurement, and comet selection, integrating transparent decision-making with optional manual review.

Result: The pipeline achieves accurate and consistent DNA damage measurements, reduces human labor, improves reproducibility, and performs robustly across diverse image qualities and comet morphologies.

Conclusion: COMPASS provides a powerful, accessible, and transparent tool for high-throughput, reliable comet assay analysis, significantly advancing the field of single-cell DNA damage assessment.

Abstract: Summary: The single-cell gel electrophoresis ('comet') assay is a widely used technique for quantifying DNA damage at the individual cell level. However, image analysis often relies on manual inspection or semi-automated software, which can be labor-intensive, difficult to reproduce, and sensitive to image quality and comet morphology. COMPASS automates comet assay image analysis by combining deep learning-based comet segmentation with damage measurement and automated comet selection. The pipeline produces standardized DNA damage measurements while offering robust detection, reducing manual effort and improving reproducibility through transparent selection and optional manual review.
  Availability and implementation: COMPASS is implemented in Python and is freely available at https://github.com/ rsinghlab/COMPASS. Installation instructions, pretrained weights, and example usage are provided in the repository.
  Contact: jack_roberts2@brown.edu, ritsingh@illinois.edu
  Supplementary information: Available onlime upon publication.

</details>


### [69] [Decouple, Purify and Unite: Semantic-Structural Prototype Learning for Federated Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04700)
*Xingyue Zhao,Wenke Huang,Linghao Zhuang,Yanzhou Su,Zhifeng Wang,Haoyu Zhao,Mengfan Li,Junjun He,Tao Tan,Dakai Jin,Le Lu,Mang Ye,Qiang Yang,Ming Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedBCS+提出一种联邦去耦上下文对齐与风格净化聚合方法，以解决医学图像联邦学习中的特征异质性问题。通过频域风格重校准（FSR）提取风格净化原型，利用去耦上下文原型对齐（DCPA）分离语义与结构特征并分别对齐，结合风格净化语义原型聚合（S2PA）自适应调整客户端权重，缓解共识偏差。在五个跨模态医学分割基准上表现最优，且收敛性分析揭示了对齐与聚合对优化边界的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表示的联邦学习方法在处理医疗影像中因扫描仪或协议差异导致的特征异质性时，存在上下文表征不完整和层间风格及聚合偏差的问题，限制了全局模型性能。

Method: 提出FedBCS+，包含三个核心组件：1）频域风格重校准（FSR），用于在原型构建中解耦内容与风格；2）去耦上下文原型对齐（DCPA），将多层级特征解耦为语义与结构原型，并分别进行对齐；3）风格净化语义原型聚合（S2PA），根据客户端原型与全局共识的差异动态调整聚合权重，减少偏见。

Result: 在五种异构医学分割数据集（涵盖组织病理学、MRI、超声、结肠镜）上，FedBCS+在平均Dice分数上优于所有对比方法，验证了其有效性。收敛性分析表明，合理的对齐与聚合策略能显著改善优化边界。

Conclusion: FedBCS+通过风格净化与去耦对齐机制有效缓解了联邦学习中因设备异质性带来的表征偏差，提升了跨中心医学图像分割的泛化能力，为未来医疗联邦学习提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Federated learning enables medical institutions to train a global model without sharing data, yet feature heterogeneity from diverse scanners or protocols remains challenging. Existing representation-based methods face two limitations: 1) Incomplete Contextual Representation Learning: single-layer or coupled representations overlook multi-level structural cues and entangle regional semantics with boundary details. 2) Layerwise Style and Aggregation Biases: domain-specific style discrepancies across intermediate layers degrade prototypes, while aggregation that overlooks client distribution shifts can further amplify bias. We propose FedBCS+, federated decoupled contextual alignment with style-purified aggregation. We employ Frequency-domain Style Recalibration (FSR) in prototype construction to decouple content-style representations and extract style-purified prototypes. Built upon these purified features, Decoupled Contextual Prototype Alignment (DCPA) explicitly decouples multi-level features into semantic and structural prototypes and aligns regional semantics and fine-grained anatomical structures separately. Style-purified Semantic Prototype Aggregation (S2PA) measures each client's purified prototype divergence from the global consensus and adaptively reweights aggregation toward under-represented clients to reduce consensus bias. On five heterogeneous medical segmentation benchmarks spanning histopathology, MRI, ultrasound, and colonoscopy, FedBCS+ achieves the highest mean Dice among the compared methods. A convergence analysis further characterizes how aggregation and alignment affect the optimization bound.

</details>


### [70] [Knossos and Ariadne: Benchmarking and Learning Complete Diagram Topology Extraction with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04721)
*Bangwei Guo,Xujiang Zhao,Shengyu Chen,Yanchi Liu,Wei Cheng,Xi Zhu,Guoning Zhang,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Knossos基准和Ariadne框架，用于解决结构图到图的拓扑提取问题。Knossos包含19,200个跨六个领域的图表，具备精确的符号生成与标注，支持大规模监督训练与评估。Ariadne通过节点识别与源条件边预测的分步架构，显著提升拓扑提取性能，在多个指标上优于基线方法，尤其在边缘F1值上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型虽能识别图中元素并进行推理，但对完整图拓扑的提取仍不充分，缺乏大规模、高精度的标注数据与系统性评估框架。因此亟需一个可支撑训练与评测的基准，并设计更有效的建模方法来实现完整的拓扑信息抽取。

Method: 提出Knossos基准，采用符号化生成方式构建带精确标注的图表数据集；设计Ariadne框架，将拓扑提取任务分解为节点库存提取与源条件边预测两阶段，利用结构化建模提升准确性。

Result: 在Knossos数据集上，基于Knossos训练使小型开源VLMs的拓扑提取能力显著提升；Ariadne框架在匹配监督条件下优于单阶段提取方法，且在平均边缘F1上达到最高水平；两个骨干模型在真实世界外部基准上也均优于未适配版本。

Conclusion: Knossos与Ariadne共同推动了结构图拓扑提取的研究进展，验证了结构化分解方法的有效性，为未来复杂图理解任务提供了可靠的数据与方法基础。

Abstract: Structural diagrams are widely used to represent complex systems and relational information across scientific, engineering, procedural, and spatial domains. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) have become increasingly capable of recognizing diagram elements and reasoning about their content, while complete diagram topology extraction remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study diagram-to-graph topology extraction: extracting all diagram entities and the complete relations among them. To enable large-scale supervised training and systematic evaluation of this task, we introduce Knossos, a benchmark of 19,200 diagrams across six diverse domains, with 245,179 nodes and 439,740 edges. Its symbolic generation process provides exact alignment between rendered diagrams and annotations of complete topology, relation types, and connector geometry. To address the modeling challenge of complete topology extraction, we also present Ariadne, a structured framework that decomposes the task into node inventory extraction and source-conditioned edge prediction. Extensive experiments show that training on Knossos substantially improves complete topology extraction in smaller open-source VLMs. Ariadne further improves over one-step extraction under matched supervision, demonstrating the additional benefit of structured decomposition. It achieves the highest average Edge F1 among the evaluated methods on Knossos, while both backbone variants also improve over their unadapted counterparts on the real-world external benchmark. Code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/bangwayne/knossos_Ariadne_Public.

</details>


### [71] [NAMVIS: Next-Scale Autoregressive Multi-View Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04722)
*Ramil Khafizov,Ilya Statsenko,Ruslan Rakhimov,Artem Komarichev,Peter Wonka,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NAMVIS 是一种无需扩散的多视角图像生成框架，通过几何条件下的逐尺度自回归方式，将多视角合成问题重新建模。它在少量粗粒度到细粒度的尺度步骤中并行预测离散视觉标记，避免了扩散模型反复去噪带来的高推理开销。通过引入多尺度投影姿态编码，将源视图和目标视图的相机变换注入到自注意力与跨注意力机制中，从而实现对相机几何的显式建模。同时结合全局条件与密集的几何感知交叉注意力，保持源视图外观一致性与目标视图的一致性。在 Objaverse、GSO 和 OmniObject3D 数据集上，NAMVIS 在 PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS 指标上超越扩散基线，且推理速度超过 3 倍于同类扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法在稀疏视图新视角合成中受限于迭代去噪过程，导致推理时间成本高，效率低下。因此亟需一种更高效、可并行化且能有效利用几何先验的新方法。

Method: 提出 NAMVIS 框架，采用几何条件下的多尺度自回归生成策略；通过多尺度投影姿态编码（Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding）将相机姿态信息嵌入注意力模块；结合全局条件与密集几何感知交叉注意力，实现从粗到细的并行生成。

Result: 在 Objaverse、GSO、OmniObject3D 等数据集上，NAMVIS 在 PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS 上优于扩散基线模型，且推理速度提升超过 3 倍，展现出更高的生成效率与质量。

Conclusion: 几何条件下的逐尺度自回归是一种高效且有前景的替代扩散模型的稀疏视图多视角合成方法，具有良好的性能与推理速度优势。

Abstract: Sparse-view novel view synthesis is a central problem in 3D content creation, but diffusion-based approaches remain limited by iterative denoising, making multi-view generation expensive at inference time. We introduce NAMVIS, a diffusion-free framework that reformulates multi-view image synthesis as geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression. Instead of generating target views through repeated denoising, NAMVIS predicts discrete visual tokens through a small number of coarse-to-fine scale steps, while sampling all tokens within each scale and across target views in parallel. To anchor this generation process to explicit camera geometry, we propose Multi-scale Projective Pose Encoding, which injects source and target camera transformations into both target-view self-attention and source-to-target cross-attention at every resolution. NAMVIS further combines global conditioning with dense geometry-aware cross-attention, enabling the model to preserve source-view appearance while maintaining target-view consistency. Across Objaverse, GSO, and OmniObject3D, NAMVIS outperforms diffusion-based baselines in PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS, while running over 3 times faster than the evaluated diffusion baselines under the same evaluation setting. These results suggest that geometry-conditioned next-scale autoregression is a promising and efficient alternative to diffusion for sparse-view multi-view synthesis. Additional qualitative results, videos, and resources are available at https://corl-team.github.io/namvis/

</details>


### [72] [Investigating Spatiotemporal Redundancy in Video Transformer for Collision Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04727)
*Xiaoshan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对视频变换器在工人-设备近距监测中计算开销大的问题，分析了其内部冗余并提出优化方法。通过分析VideoMAEv2-Base模型在Nexar数据集上的表现，发现运动线索在中间层最易识别，而碰撞标签判别力随深度增强；同时发现时间维度的令牌冗余远高于空间维度（时间相似度0.970，空间相似度0.297）。基于此，采用时间令牌合并策略，将计算量从356.99降至178.50 GFLOPs，延迟从12.69降至7.21毫秒，实现1.76倍加速，性能仅轻微下降（mAP从0.7478降至0.7443）。此外，通过重要性引导保留50%的MLP单元，AUC保持在0.753，显著优于随机保留（0.529），表明剪枝影响得分校准而非判别排序。研究揭示了通过针对性压缩时间令牌和神经元剪枝实现更快算法的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频变换器虽在碰撞预测中表现优异，但其高计算成本难以满足移动机器人低延迟推理与低碳计算需求，亟需在不显著牺牲性能的前提下降低计算开销。

Method: 利用线性探测分析模型各层对碰撞相关特征的表达能力；通过比较时空维度的令牌相似性识别冗余模式；采用时间令牌合并与重要性引导的神经元剪枝策略进行高效压缩。

Result: 时间令牌合并使计算量减少约50%，延迟降低43%，性能仅轻微下降（mAP下降0.0035）；重要性剪枝保留50%神经元即可维持0.753 AUC，显著优于随机剪枝；验证了目标导向的冗余消除策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过识别并去除视频变换器中的时间维度冗余和非关键神经元，可在几乎不影响预测性能的前提下实现显著加速与降耗，为构建高效、低碳的实时视觉系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In worker-equipment proximity monitoring, video transformers are widely used for collision anticipation and have demonstrated strong performance. However, their accuracy comes with substantial computational demands, creating a tension with the need for low-latency inference on mobile robots and the pursuit of lower-carbon computation in construction. To address this, this study investigates where computation within an established video transformer is redundant and whether that redundancy can be removed without materially degrading predictive performance. Using VideoMAEv2-Base on the Nexar Collision Prediction dataset, we first examine how collision-relevant information evolves across network depth and then investigate two complementary forms of redundancy: structured capacity redundancy in multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and spatiotemporal redundancy in the token stream. Linear probes show that interpretable motion cues, including flow magnitude, looming, and approach versus retreat, are most accessible at intermediate layers, whereas collision-label discrimination strengthens toward the final layer. Token redundancy is axis-specific: adjacent temporal-token similarity reaches 0.970 in later layers, while spatial similarity falls to 0.297, indicating substantially greater redundancy across time than across space. Exploiting this asymmetry, temporal token merging reduces backbone computation from 356.99 to 178.50 GFLOPs and latency from 12.69 to 7.21 ms per clip, a 1.76x speedup, while mean average precision changes only from 0.7478 to 0.7443. Importance-guided retention of 50% of MLP units preserves an AUC of 0.753, compared with 0.529 under matched random retention, and reveals that pruning alters score calibration before discriminative ranking collapses. These findings establish a new pathway for pursuing faster algorithms through targeted temporal token compression and neuron pruning.

</details>


### [73] [Probabilistic Pedestrian Forecasts from a Handheld Phone: World-Frame Heat Maps, Visual-Inertial Height Drift, and Evaluation without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04736)
*Danial Safaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个基于单目摄像头和视觉惯性里程计（VIO）的行人轨迹预测系统，可在手持设备上实时预测附近行人在未来几秒的位置。系统通过检测行人、将其投影到地面平面，并在重力对齐的度量空间中跟踪，使用小型U-Net模型结合负对数似然（NLL）损失，在鸟瞰视角（SDD）和第一人称视角（EgoTraj-Bench）数据集上进行训练。实验表明，在手持ADVIO录制视频中，尽管存在垂直VIO漂移导致的地面位置误标问题（90秒内误差达87%），但通过低通滤波器保持相机高度稳定可缓解该问题。在两个测试集上，该方法在4.8秒时相比恒定速度高斯模型分别降低了1.51和1.37 nats的NLL。由于缺乏手持视频的真实轨迹，采用追踪器自身的后续原始测量作为评估基准。在七段保留测试片段的内部预注册评估中，该模型在1.2、2.4和4.8秒时的NLL均低于基线（分别降低0.17、0.23、0.47 nats），且95%置信区间不包含零；但在保留数据上的性能提升幅度仅为开发集的一半左右。探索性分析显示，采样方式变化或重新校准后结果波动较大，部分情况下置信区间包含零，说明自一致性评分具有局限性，仅能提供有限参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人轨迹预测系统多依赖固定传感器或精确定位设备，难以在真实手持场景下应用。本文旨在构建一个仅依赖单目摄像头与视觉惯性里程计（VIO）的轻量级行人位置预测系统，实现移动设备上的实时预测，适用于城市环境中用户感知周围人群动态的需求。

Method: 系统首先利用单目摄像头检测行人，并通过射线-平面交点将其投影至地面；随后在重力对齐的度量坐标系中进行跟踪，采用视觉惯性里程计（VIO）估计运动状态；引入低通滤波器抑制相机高度的快速变化以减少垂直漂移影响；最后使用一个小型U-Net网络，基于负对数似然（NLL）损失，从鸟瞰（SDD）和第一人称（EgoTraj-Bench）轨迹数据中学习步级概率图进行未来轨迹预测。评估时采用自一致性策略，即用追踪器自身后续观测作为伪真值来衡量预测性能。

Result: 在手持ADVIO视频上，系统有效缓解了因垂直VIO漂移引起的地面位置偏差问题。在SDD和EgoTraj-Bench测试集上，该方法在4.8秒预测时间点分别比恒定速度高斯模型降低了1.51和1.37 nats的NLL。在七段保留测试片段的内部评估中，该模型在1.2、2.4和4.8秒时的预测性能均显著优于基线（差异分别为0.17、0.23、0.47 nats，95%置信区间不包含零），但性能提升幅度远小于开发集表现。探索性分析表明，评估结果受采样方式、校准策略及参考姿态来源影响较大，自一致性评分存在不确定性。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一个基于单目摄像头与视觉惯性里程计的行人轨迹预测系统，能够在手持设备上实现近似实时的未来位置预测。虽然系统在理想条件下表现出色，但其性能在真实手持场景中受到传感器漂移、姿态估计误差和评估方法局限性的显著影响。自一致性评估虽能提供一定参考，但无法完全反映真实性能，需谨慎解读。未来工作应聚焦于更鲁棒的姿态估计与更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: A pedestrian with a phone could be shown where nearby people will be in the next few seconds, if the forecast stays on the ground while the phone moves, is calibrated, and needs only a monocular camera and visual-inertial odometry (VIO). We build and evaluate such a system. People are detected, lifted onto the floor by ray-plane intersection, tracked in a gravity-aligned metric frame, and forecast as per-step probability maps by a small U-Net trained with a negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss on bird's-eye (SDD) and first-person (EgoTraj-Bench) trajectories. On handheld ADVIO recordings, vertical VIO drift and the user's own changes of level silently rescale monocular ground positions (by 87% within 90 s on one clip; on another, all tracks are lost for the last 31% of the clip); keeping the camera's height above the floor constant under a low-pass-filtered altitude avoids this, though it lags on escalators. On the SDD and EgoTraj-Bench test splits, the final forecaster lowers the NLL at 4.8 s by 1.51 and 1.37 nats relative to a fitted constant-velocity Gaussian. Lacking ground truth for people in handheld video, we score forecasts against the tracker's own later raw measurements. In an internally pre-registered evaluation on seven held-out clips, the final forecaster's NLL is lower than the benchmark-fitted baseline's at 1.2, 2.4 and 4.8 s (by 0.17, 0.23 and 0.47 nats; 95% intervals over people exclude zero), but by less than half as much as on the development clips. Exploratory analyses cut both ways: resampling clips instead of people widens the intervals to include zero at 1.2 and 2.4 s, and once both forecasters are recalibrated on the development clips the network is significantly better only at 1.2 s; but two clips run with ADVIO's reference poses favour the network much more when re-run with the phone's own poses. We discuss what such self-consistency scores can and cannot show.

</details>


### [74] [ThyCLIPNet: A BiomedCLIP-Guided Lightweight Attention-Enhanced DeepLabV3+ Framework for Robust Thyroid Nodule Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04743)
*Tasnim Jahan,Md Easin Arafat,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ThyCLIPNet 是一种轻量级的语义引导混合编码器-解码器框架，用于甲状腺超声图像分割。它结合了 BiomedCLIP 的视觉编码器提供的全局生物医学语义指导，与轻量级多尺度 CNN 结构，以提升分割精度并减少计算开销。该方法在多个数据集上表现出色，同时参数量仅 8.55M，FLOPs 为 22.99G，具备高效性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲状腺超声分割方法常受低对比度、斑点噪声和边界模糊影响，且多数依赖高资源消耗的架构或缺乏显式的多尺度特征与全局生物医学视觉引导的融合。需要一种轻量级、高效且具有强语义指导能力的分割模型。

Method: 提出 ThyCLIPNet，采用 MobileNetV2 作为主干网络并引入高效通道注意力机制；在瓶颈层使用空洞空间金字塔池化（ASPP）和自定义卷积块注意力模块增强特征表达；解码器通过层次化跳跃连接、轻量级注意力精炼及基于 BiomedCLIP 的门控融合路径，将仅来自图像的全局生物医学嵌入投影至解码器特征空间，实现语义-局部融合与空间门控集成。

Result: 在 TG3K、TN3K、DDTI 和 PKTN 四个数据集上分别达到 96.22%、87.58%、84.73% 和 80.70% 的 Dice 系数，以及 92.72%、77.91%、73.51% 和 67.64% 的 IoU，95th 百分位 Hausdorff 距离分别为 3.75、16.38、18.23 和 10.86。模型仅需 8.55M 参数和 22.99G FLOPs，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: ThyCLIPNet 是首个仅使用 BiomedCLIP 视觉编码器进行图像级全局语义引导的轻量级甲状腺超声分割框架，证明了将全局生物医学语义信息与轻量级多尺度 CNN 表示相结合，在保持高精度的同时实现高效分割的可行性，为临床应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate thyroid ultrasound segmentation is often challenged by low contrast, speckle noise, and unclear boundaries. Although recent methods have improved segmentation accuracy, many rely on resource-intensive architectures or lack explicit integration of multiscale features with global biomedical visual guidance. In this paper, we introduce ThyCLIPNet, a lightweight semantic-guided hybrid encoder-decoder framework that integrates BiomedCLIP-derived biomedical semantic guidance into a lightweight multi-scale CNN segmentation pipeline. The encoder integrates MobileNetV2 with efficient channel attention, while atrous spatial pyramid pooling and a custom convolutional block attention module enrich bottleneck features. The decoder combines hierarchical skip connections and lightweight attention refinement with a BiomedCLIP-guided gated fusion pathway that projects vision-only global biomedical embeddings into decoder feature space and selectively integrates them through semantic-local fusion and spatial gating. To the best of our knowledge, ThyCLIPNet is among the first lightweight thyroid ultrasound segmentation frameworks to use BiomedCLIP's vision encoder alone for image-only global semantic guidance without text prompting. Experiments on TG3K, TN3K, DDTI, and PKTN achieve dice similarity coefficients of 96.22%, 87.58%, 84.73%, and 80.70%; intersection over union scores of 92.72%, 77.91%, 73.51%, and 67.64%; and 95th-percentile hausdorff distances of 3.75, 16.38, 18.23, and 10.86, respectively. ThyCLIPNet uses 8.55M parameters and 22.99G FLOPs. Overall, the results support integrating global biomedical semantic guidance with lightweight multi-scale CNN representations for robust and computationally efficient thyroid ultrasound segmentation. Source code: https://github.com/Tasnim-Jahan/ThyCLIPNet. [Abstract shortened for arXiv. See PDF for full abstract.]

</details>


### [75] [ARISE: Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation for Interpretable IBD Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04777)
*Pronoma Banerjee,Anuva Shah,Jason Wu,Md. Masudur Rahman,Sanjay Mohanty,Satya Kurada,Juan P. Wachs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARISE 是一种用于炎症性肠病（IBD）影像评估的自主规划框架，通过五阶段代理工作流实现少样本医学图像理解，显著提升诊断性能并增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在医疗影像分析中受限于领域特定推理不足、幻觉问题、高质量训练数据稀缺及可解释性差，亟需更可靠的临床应用方案。

Method: ARISE 采用五阶段透明工作流：假设生成、基于图像的证据摘要、证据条件下的优化、符号验证和最终诊断，构建自适应代理推理机制。

Result: 在无线胶囊内镜（WCE）和超声图像上对克罗恩病与溃疡性结肠炎进行评估，ARISE 显著优于基线VLMs，且能揭示推理成功或失败的关键环节。

Conclusion: ARISE 提供了一种可解释、可信赖的自动化诊断框架，为炎症性肠病的临床决策支持和实际部署提供了新路径。

Abstract: Inflammatory bowel disease (IBD) requires frequent imaging-based assessment, yet interpretation of modalities such as wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) and intestinal ultrasound remains heavily dependent on specialist expertise. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate significant potential in multimodal medical image analysis, but their clinical adoption is hindered by their insufficient domain-specific reasoning, susceptibility to hallucination, scarcity of high quality training data in fine-grained diagnostics and limited interpretability. We introduce ARISE (Adaptive Agentic Reasoning with Image-grounded Self-Evaluation), an autonomous planning framework that models few-shot medical image understanding as a sequential agentic workflow. ARISE structures agent execution into a transparent 5-stage workflow: hypothesis generation, image-grounded evidence summarization, evidence-conditioned refinement, symbolic verification, and final diagnosis. We apply ARISE to IBD assessment across two independent patient cohorts: wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) images for Crohn's disease and B-mode ultrasound data for ulcerative colitis. ARISE consistently improves diagnostic performance over baseline VLMs while exposing where reasoning succeeds or fails, providing a more interpretable basis for clinical decision support and realistic deployment.

</details>


### [76] [Super-Resolution in The Right Latent Space: A Frozen Vision-Foundation Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04781)
*Wanzhou Lei,Cuifeng Sheng,Yanjin He,Maohua Li,Hua Yuan,Per-Olof Persson,Hanlin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在图像潜在空间中，高分辨率自然清晰图像的嵌入形成一个流形，退化图像的嵌入会偏离该流形。真实世界超分辨率（SR）的目标是将退化的嵌入映射回该流形上，且需保留输入的语义和像素细节。现有方法在重建导向的潜在空间或像素空间中进行恢复，但这些空间不适合作为SR的基底。低分辨率和退化图像在这些空间中远离流形，且缺乏语义信息，导致恢复困难并产生严重幻觉。作者提出使用冻结的DINOv3-L模型中23层融合后的潜在空间作为新基底，该空间使退化图像更接近流形，并包含从像素到鲁棒语义的层次信息，有助于准确恢复。基于此基底，训练了一个415M参数的解码器，在重建与对抗目标下实现单次前向传播恢复，得到RAESR模型，在RealSR、DRealSR、LSDIR和DIV2K-Val数据集上达到最优保真度与感知质量平衡，每张512×512图像仅需37毫秒，性能优于基于扩散模型和对抗模型的方法。若换用VAE潜在空间，性能全面下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
