Motivation: 现有超分辨率方法在重建导向的潜在空间或像素空间中进行恢复，这些空间因缺乏语义信息且退化图像距离流形过远，导致恢复困难、计算成本高，并在重度退化时产生严重幻觉。因此需要寻找一个更合适的潜在空间来提升超分辨率性能。

Method: 提出使用冻结的DINOv3-L模型中23层融合后的潜在空间作为新的恢复基底，该空间使退化图像嵌入更接近真实图像流形，并具备从像素记录到语义信息的层次结构。在此基础上，训练一个415M参数的解码器，通过重建与对抗损失实现单次前向传播的图像恢复。

Result: RAESR模型在RealSR、DRealSR、LSDIR和DIV2K-Val数据集上均达到当前最佳的保真度与感知质量平衡；在单个H20 GPU上处理512×512图像仅需37毫秒。若改用VAE潜在空间，性能全面下降。

Conclusion: 使用具有丰富层次语义信息且使退化嵌入靠近真实流形的潜在空间，对于实现高效、高质量的真实世界超分辨率至关重要。基于DINOv3-L融合潜空间构建的RAESR模型，显著优于现有方法，证明了合适潜在空间选择对超分辨率任务的关键作用。

Abstract: In an image latent space, the embeddings of high-resolution, natural, and sharp images form a manifold. Degradation of high-resolution images pushes their embeddings off this manifold. Real-world super-resolution (SR) then becomes the task of mapping the degraded embedding back onto this manifold --- not anywhere on the manifold, but to the point that preserves what the input still carries, both its semantics and pixel details. Every published method implements this mapping in a reconstruction-oriented latent space or pixel space. We claim these spaces are the wrong substrates for SR. Low-resolution and degraded images are embedded far from the manifold, making the mapping difficult and expensive. The lack of semantic information in these substrates also makes it difficult to navigate to the faithful point on the manifold, causing severe hallucination when degradation is heavy. Thus, restoring in a suitable latent space is crucial to the SR task. We show that the latent space of 23 fused layers of a frozen DINOv3-L is one such space that makes the SR task easier. Degraded images are embedded near the manifold. Moreover, this substrate contains a hierarchy of information, from pixel record to degradation robust semantics, guiding the model to find the faithful point on the manifold. On this substrate, a 415M decoder is trained under reconstruction and adversarial objectives to map the degraded embeddings back and decode to pixel space in one pass. The resulting model, RAESR, attains the best fidelity--perception trade-off among state-of-the-art adversarial and diffusion-based restorers on RealSR, DRealSR, LSDIR and DIV2K-Val, at 37 ms per 512 by 512 image on a single H20 GPU. Swapping the substrate for a VAE latent under an identical recipe loses on every metric.

</details>


### [77] [Lollypop: Camera-to-Motion-Capture Calibration Verification with a Reference Target](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04785)
*Tianyi Liu,Kevin Harris,Mihika Dave,Kun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lollypop 是一种用于独立校准验证的视觉-动捕参考靶标，结合 ArUco 标志和动捕标记阵列，使视觉中心与跟踪质心对应同一物理点。通过将动捕点投影到图像中并测量其与检测到的标志中心的不一致来验证校准。实验表明该方法具有亚像素级误差、对可控外参扰动敏感，并在混合操作序列中表现出误差增加趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机到动捕系统的校准在部署后可能漂移，而校准残差和视觉检查无法提供独立验证，因此需要一种能够独立验证校准准确性的工具。

Method: 设计 Lollypop 靶标，将 ArUco 标志与动捕标记阵列耦合，确保视觉中心与动捕质心为同一物理点；利用候选校准将动捕点投影至图像，计算其与实际检测到的标志中心之间的偏差作为验证指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，系统在正常情况下保持亚像素级别的误差；对人为引入的外参扰动表现出敏感性；在混合操作过程中，误差随时间逐渐增大，验证了其动态监测能力。

Conclusion: Lollypop 提供了一种有效且独立的相机-动捕校准验证方法，适用于机器人、AR/VR 等需要高精度姿态估计的场景。

Abstract: Camera-to-motion-capture (mocap) calibration is essential for using mocap as ground truth in robotics, AR/VR, and other computer vision tasks. However, the calibration can drift after deployment, while calibration residuals and visual inspection provide limited independent verification. We present Lollypop, a fiducial-mocap reference target for independent calibration verification. The target couples an ArUco fiducial with a mocap marker constellation so the visual center and tracked centroid represent the same physical point. Given a candidate calibration, verification projects the mocap point into the image and measures its disagreement with the detected fiducial center. Experiments show sub-pixel nominal error, sensitivity to controlled extrinsic perturbations, and increasing error during an illustrative mixed-handling sequence.

</details>


### [78] [Active-DiNTS: Active Differentiable Network Topology Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04787)
*Gean Trindade Pereira,Thierry Urruty,Muriel Visani,André C. P. L. F. de Carvalho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Active-DiNTS combines active learning with differentiable neural architecture search to reduce annotation costs and GPU requirements for 3D medical image segmentation. It selects the most uncertain unlabeled MRI volumes for annotation, jointly optimizing network weights and architecture in a bi-level framework. Experiments on BrainTumour task show significant Dice score improvements over baselines with fewer labels and faster search (under 0.25 GPU-days), while discovering denser, FLOP-heavy but memory-efficient architectures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing NAS methods for 3D medical segmentation are limited by high annotation costs and need for multi-GPU setups. Active-DiNTS aims to address these constraints by integrating active learning into differentiable architecture search to reduce labeling effort and computational demand.

Method: Active-DiNTS uses pool-based active learning with uncertainty measures (Entropy, Variance, Standard Deviation) to select top unlabeled MRI volumes for annotation. A bi-level optimization framework updates network weights (outer loop) and U-Net-style topology (inner loop). Three regimes—weights-only, topology-only, joint—are evaluated to analyze speed-accuracy trade-offs.

Result: Active-DiNTS achieves up to 10% higher Dice scores on Edema and 5% on Non-Enhancing core compared to DiNTS, C2FNAS, and nnU-Net, using only a fraction of labeled data and a single GPU. The fastest variant completes in under 0.25 GPU-days—over 27x faster than multi-GPU DiNTS. Discovered architectures are denser and more FLOP-heavy but remain efficient in parameters and memory.

Conclusion: Integrating differentiable NAS with active data acquisition is an effective and practical approach for accurate 3D medical image segmentation under real-world resource constraints.

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has proved to be a strong alternative to manual network design, but applying it to 3D medical image segmentation is limited by two well-known costs, large annotation budgets and multi-GPU clusters. Thus, this paper introduces Active-DiNTS (Active Differentiable Network Topology Search), an approach that embeds pool-based Active Learning (AL) into a bi-level differentiable topology search to perform architecture discovery and label curation jointly. At each query round, unlabeled MRI volumes are ranked by one of three uncertainty signals (Entropy, Variance, or Standard Deviation), and only the top-ranked volumes are sent to an oracle for annotation. The new labels feed two interlocked stages. Network weights are updated in an outer loop, while the macro/micro topology of a U-Net-style backbone is refined in an inner loop. Three AL regimes (weights-only, topology-only, joint) expose the speed-accuracy trade-off. Evaluations on the Medical Segmentation Decathlon (MSD) Task01 BrainTumour benchmark showed that Active-DiNTS surpasses DiNTS, C2FNAS, and nnU-Net in Dice, with gains of about 10 percentage points on Edema and 5 points on Non-Enhancing core, on a single GPU and using a fraction of the labeled volumes. The discovered architectures are denser and more FLOP-heavy than prior baselines, but remain competitive in trainable parameters and peak memory; the fastest search variant finishes in under 0.25 GPU-days, over 27x faster than the eight-GPU DiNTS search. Together, these results indicate that pairing differentiable NAS with active data acquisition is a practical recipe for accurate 3D segmentation under realistic constraints.

</details>


### [79] [MOXIE: Discovering Alternative Explanations for Biomedical Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04814)
*Abiha Tahsin Chowdhury,Rahul Dubey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOXIE 是一种基于进化框架的多目标解释方法，旨在寻找既能保持分类器置信度又能最小化图像使用量的片段子集。与 LIME 只提供单一解释不同，MOXIE 生成一组帕累托最优的替代解释，涵盖从紧凑到高保真度的不同权衡。在 BloodMNIST 和 HAM10000 数据集上的实验表明，MOXIE 在每个图像上均优于 LIME 的超体积表现，揭示了模型决策所依赖的上下文区域及其所需信息量。同时，研究发现分割方法对解释结果影响显著，尤其当片段大小不均时，仅以数量衡量紧凑性会产生误导。结果强调了多解释比单解释更能全面理解模型决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于片段的解释方法（如 LIME）仅提供单一解释，且依赖固定图像分割，忽略了预测可能由多种片段组合支持的事实，也未考虑分割本身对解释结果的影响。这导致解释可能看似合理但缺乏鲁棒性，无法反映模型决策的真实依赖关系。

Method: 提出 MOXIE（Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine），采用 NSGA-II 进化算法，在多个分割方法（SLIC、Felzenszwalb、Watershed、Voronoi）下搜索满足两个目标的片段子集：尽可能减少图像使用量，同时保持分类器的原始置信度。通过构建帕累托前沿，获得多样化的解释方案。

Result: MOXIE 在 BloodMNIST 与 HAM10000 上均实现了比 LIME 更高的超体积，表明其能发现更优或更多样化的解释；LIME 的解释虽表面可信，但在仅保留突出片段时置信度急剧下降；而 MOXIE 的解释前沿揭示了维持置信度所需的信息量及关键上下文区域；此外，分割方法的选择显著影响解释的紧凑性评估，尤其是当片段大小不均时，单纯计数会误导判断。

Conclusion: 多解释方法能提供比单一解释更完整、更可靠的模型决策洞察。解释的可靠性不仅取决于解释内容，还受分割策略和评估方式的影响。未来应重视解释的多样性与稳健性分析。

Abstract: Segment-based explanation methods such as LIME return a single explanation for each prediction, computed from one fixed image segmentation. This hides two important facts: a prediction can be supported by many different sets of image segments, and the segmentation itself shapes which explanations can be found. We introduce MOXIE (Multi-Objective eXplanation Imaging Engine), an evolutionary framework that searches for segment subsets that preserve the classifier's confidence while keeping as little of the image as possible. Instead of one explanation, MOXIE returns a Pareto front of alternative explanations that range from compact to highly faithful. We evaluate MOXIE with NSGA-II and four segmentation methods (SLIC, Felzenszwalb, Watershed and Voronoi) on BloodMNIST and HAM10000 datasets, using the same evaluation budget as LIME. Results show that MOXIE achieves a higher hypervolume than LIME on every image. LIME's explanations often appear convincing, yet the classifier's confidence collapses when only the highlighted segments are shown. MOXIE's fronts reveal how much of the image is needed to preserve the model's confidence and which contextual regions influence it. We also find that segmentation strongly affects evaluation: methods with unequal segment sizes appear most compact when segments are counted. These results show that alternative explanations provide a more complete view of a model's decision than a single explanation.

</details>


### [80] [DriftSR: One-Step Real-World Image Super-Resolution via Distribution Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04819)
*Wei Zhu,Kai Zhang,Yu Zheng,Zhaopeng Yang,Lei Luo,Yong Guo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftSR 是一种无需额外训练组件的一步真实世界图像超分辨率框架，通过在预训练扩散模型的冻结中间表示空间中进行分布漂移，利用空间特征漂移实现更密集的监督，并引入结构调制引导以保持局部结构一致性，仅优化单步生成器即可实现高效高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步真实世界图像超分辨率方法依赖分数蒸馏或对抗学习，导致额外可训练组件和复杂优化过程，本文旨在简化流程并提升重建质量。

Method: 提出在预训练扩散模型的冻结中间表示空间中进行分布漂移，引入空间特征漂移以增强监督密度，并设计结构调制引导以适应局部结构一致性，从而仅优化一个一步生成器。

Result: 在三个真实世界基准上的实验表明，DriftSR 实现了高质量的超分辨率重建，且推理效率高，无需辅助蒸馏分支或对抗判别器。

Conclusion: DriftSR 通过利用预训练扩散先验和创新的漂移机制，在不增加复杂性的情况下实现了高效的一步真实世界图像超分辨率，为实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: One-step real-world image super-resolution (Real-ISR) offers efficient inference, but recovering realistic and perceptually rich details often relies on score distillation or adversarial learning, introducing additional trainable components and making optimization more cumbersome. To this end, we propose DriftSR, a one-step Real-ISR framework that leverages pretrained diffusion priors through distribution drifting. Specifically, we perform drifting in the frozen intermediate representation space of a pretrained diffusion model, without introducing an additional task-specific feature encoder. Building on this space, we introduce Spatial Feature Drifting, which treats spatial features rather than entire images as distributional samples, enabling denser supervision for distribution alignment. To mitigate structural deviations, we further introduce Structure-Modulated Guidance, which adaptively refines drifting guidance according to local structural consistency with the LQ input. Consequently, DriftSR optimizes only the one-step generator, without auxiliary distillation branches or adversarial discriminators. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DriftSR delivers high-quality super-resolution reconstruction with efficient one-step inference.

</details>


### [81] [RSure-Agent: Reliable Use of Tool Observations for Remote Sensing Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04836)
*Fuyuan Liu,Nayu Liu,Wenhao Yu,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Fanglong Yao,Liang Wan,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对遥感代理在执行任务时因工具观测存在不确定性而导致错误传播的问题，提出RSure-Agent框架。通过引入可验证的观测协议和基于离线反馈构建的任务-工具可靠性先验，使代理能够验证、拒绝或请求补充证据，从而减少错误传播。在多个基准测试中，该方法显著降低错误传播率并提升准确率，同时减少工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感代理依赖工具进行感知与分析，但其观测结果常含不确定性和错误，即使工具成功执行，也可能导致后续推理错误并引发任务失败。现有方法缺乏对工具观测可靠性的验证机制，错误易传播。因此需要一种能验证工具观测并限制错误传播的框架。

Method: 提出RSure-Agent框架，包含：1）可验证观测协议，要求工具返回过程证据供代理验证；2）任务-工具可靠性先验，基于历史任务反馈构建，提供各工具配置在不同任务类型中的表现参考；3）基于证据和先验的决策机制，决定是否接受、请求补充或拒绝观测。

Result: 在EarthBench、ThinkGeo、TerraLogic和CHOICE-420等基准上，RSure-Agent将错误传播率降低21.3至25.9个百分点；在CHOICE-420上，平均比直接回答提升5.71个百分点准确率；在EarthBench上，工具调用次数减少25.9%。

Conclusion: RSure-Agent通过引入可验证观测与可靠性先验，有效识别并抑制了错误观测的传播，显著提升了遥感代理的任务准确性和效率，为高可靠性地球观测智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Remote sensing agents rely on perception, measurement, and raster analysis tools to solve Earth observation tasks. We refer to their judgments and quantitative results about ground objects as tool observations. However, these observations are subject to substantial uncertainty and may be incorrect even when the tools execute successfully. When agents accept incorrect observations, the errors can propagate through subsequent reasoning and cause task failure. We analyze 1,229 execution trajectories across three remote sensing agent benchmarks. On each benchmark, at least 88.1% of tasks depend on tool observations. Among these tasks, at least 22.7% contain incorrect observations despite successful tool execution. These errors propagate to the final answer in at least 82.0% of affected tasks on each benchmark. To address this problem, we propose RSure-Agent, a framework for verifying tool observations and limiting error propagation. We introduce a verifiable observation protocol that requires tools to return process evidence for the agent to verify their observations. We also construct a task-tool reliability prior from offline task feedback. The prior summarizes each tool configuration's past performance across task types and provides a task-specific reference for verification. Using process evidence and this prior, RSure-Agent decides whether to accept an observation, request additional evidence, or reject it. We evaluate RSure-Agent on EarthBench, ThinkGeo, TerraLogic, and CHOICE-420. Across the three agent benchmarks, RSure-Agent reduces the error propagation rate by 21.3 to 25.9 percentage points relative to the base configuration with verification and the prior disabled. On CHOICE-420, it improves overall accuracy over direct answering by 5.71 percentage points on average across 11 backbone models. On EarthBench, it reduces the tool-call ratio by 25.9% relative to Earth-Agent.

</details>


### [82] [One Tile, Multiple Instances: Rethinking MIL for Sparse Diagnostic Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04853)
*Runsheng Liu,Cheng Jin,Hao Jiang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DI-MIL 是一种无需训练的后编码模块，通过将每个图像块分解为多个独立加权的实例嵌入，提升弱监督全切片图像分类中细粒度证据的利用。在细胞病理学数据集上，其在72次评估中改进了67次，最大平均提升达3.64分，并在对比七种主流MIL基线时表现最优。相比小块分割带来的高计算开销和非单调性能，DI-MIL无额外提取开销，是稀疏诊断信号下的高效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在弱监督全切片图像分类中将每张图像块压缩为单一全局嵌入，导致滑动窗口聚合器只能在粗粒度尺度上操作，无法捕捉细粒度子块证据，尤其在诊断信号稀疏的场景下表现受限。

Method: DI-MIL 通过聚类冻结基础模型生成的密集空间标记，将单个图像块转换为多个独立加权的实例嵌入，实现编码上下文与实例粒度的解耦；作为训练无关的后编码模块，可无缝集成至现有流程，无需重新编码或修改下游结构。

Result: 在四个数据集、三种冻结基础模型和两种注意力聚合器上，DI-MIL 在72项指标中有67项表现更优，最大平均提升达3.64分；在与七种主流MIL基线的比较中，在36组组合中取得33组最高均值性能；消融实验表明，直接减小图像块尺寸会带来高达43.3倍的图像提取量增长且性能非单调，而DI-MIL无额外开销却获得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 实例构建是MIL中的一个独立设计维度，DI-MIL 作为一种低成本、高效率的解决方案，特别适用于诊断信号稀疏的场景，显著提升了细粒度信息利用能力。

Abstract: In weakly supervised Whole Slide Image (WSI) classification, feature extractors typically compress each image tile into a single global embedding. Consequently, slide-level aggregators are restricted to this coarse tile scale, concealing fine-grained sub-tile evidence from the attention mechanism. We introduce DI-MIL, a framework that decouples encoding context from instance granularity through decomposed instances. By clustering dense spatial tokens from a frozen foundation model within each tile, DI-MIL converts a single tile into multiple independently weighted instance embeddings. As a training-free post-encoding module, DI-MIL integrates seamlessly into existing pipelines without requiring re-encoding or downstream architectural modifications. We evaluate DI-MIL on cytopathology, a challenging testbed where sparse diagnostic signals are easily diluted within standard tiles. Across four datasets, three frozen foundation models, and two attention-based aggregators, DI-MIL demonstrates consistent efficacy, improving 67 of 72 metric-level comparisons, with the largest mean gains reaching 3.64 points under cytopathology-specific backbones. In a broader comparison against seven representative MIL baselines, DI-MIL paired with ACMIL achieves highest mean performance in 33 of 36 backbone-dataset-metric comparisons. Ablations show that direct smaller tiling inflates the extracted tile count by up to 43.3$\times$ with non-monotonic performance, whereas DI-MIL incurs zero additional image-extraction overhead while achieving the strongest overall results. These results establish instance construction as an orthogonal design dimension in MIL, supporting DI-MIL as a cost-efficient solution under sparse diagnostic evidence.

</details>


### [83] [Enhancing Long-Video VLM Embeddings with Query-Aware Streaming Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04864)
*Haozhe Chi,Song Jin,Yang Jin,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QASLR框架，通过查询感知的流式潜在推理，在有限视觉标记预算下有效整合长视频中的稀疏相关证据。该方法选择有限帧、恢复时序顺序，逐片段处理，并利用持久性思考令牌与嵌入令牌协同生成固定维度的视频表示，支持多步对比学习和自蒸馏训练。实验表明，该方法在长视频检索、时刻检索和视频问答任务上均显著提升性能，尤其在2B和8B Qwen3-VL-Embedding模型上，Hit@1分别从54.2/57.4提升至70.7/72.8，验证了流式潜在聚合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中关键事件可能短暂且分散，传统均匀采样易遗漏，而增加帧数会带来内存与计算开销。如何在固定嵌入维度下高效聚合跨片段的稀疏证据，是当前长视频理解的核心挑战。

Method: QASLR采用查询感知的流式处理机制：从视频中选择有限帧并恢复时序；使用视觉语言主干逐片编码；引入持久性思考令牌跨注意力融合各片段特征，嵌入令牌读出归一化表示；通过最终对比学习、步骤级监督及最终嵌入自蒸馏进行联合训练，实现多步证据积累与稳定表示学习。

Result: 在HourVideo数据集上，使用2B和8B Qwen3-VL-Embedding模型时，检索任务的Hit@1分别从54.2/57.4提升至70.7/72.8；在时刻检索和视频问答任务中也取得显著增益；该架构可迁移至其他同系列模型，具备良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: QASLR通过流式潜在聚合机制，成功在固定维度下整合长视频中的多片段证据，无需共享单个上下文即可实现高效、高精度的视频理解，为长视频嵌入提供了可扩展、高性能的新范式。

Abstract: Long-video embedding requires capturing sparse query-relevant evidence under a limited visual-token budget. Uniform sampling can miss brief events in videos spanning minutes or hours, whereas encoding more frames in a single context increases memory and computation. We introduce \textbf{Query-Aware Streaming Latent Reasoning} (QASLR), a post-training framework that accumulates evidence across clips while keeping the embedding size fixed. QASLR selects a bounded set of frames, restores their temporal order, and processes them clip by clip with a vision-language backbone. A compact set of persistent think tokens cross-attends to each clip's features, while an embed token reads out a normalized representation after every update. This design integrates evidence across multiple backbone calls without requiring all selected frames to share a single context. Training combines final contrastive learning, step-wise contrastive supervision, and final-embedding self-distillation. Intermediate supervision trains partial-video readouts for retrieval, while self-distillation regularizes them toward the final representation. Query-aware selection produces query-conditioned representations for candidate-set scoring and reranking, whereas query-independent selection enables reusable corpus indexing. Under the full training recipe, HourVideo retrieval Hit@1 increases from 54.2 to 70.7 and from 57.4 to 72.8 for 2B and 8B Qwen3-VL-Embedding backbones, respectively. Gains extend to the evaluated moment-retrieval and video-QA tasks, and the streaming head transfers to a second Qwen-family embedding backbone. These results support streaming latent aggregation as an effective approach to integrating long-video evidence into fixed-dimensional representations.

</details>


### [84] [Reflection-Robust 6DoF Object Tracking with Light Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04883)
*Nikolai Goncharov,Donald G. Dansereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于光场的反射鲁棒6DoF跟踪方法，利用反射作为姿态估计的线索。通过恢复深度、重建点云并估计表面法线，将物体视图依赖的外观分解为漫反射材质和环境映射，构建可重光照的表面光场。在粗初始化基础上，利用恢复的环境映射进行重光照，并通过光度误差优化姿态。随着物体移动，环境映射逐渐填充，信号随时间增强。在自建光场跟踪数据集（四种可控反光率）及两个实拍光场序列上评估，该方法在非反射物体上优于最强基线，在完全反射物体上唯一保持精度，而所有基线均严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6DoF跟踪方法假设物体外观在序列中稳定，但在镜面反射表面这一假设失效，因反射导致外观动态变化。如何利用这种变化而非规避其影响，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出基于光场的反射鲁棒跟踪框架：每帧恢复深度，重建点云并估计法线；将视图依赖外观分解为漫反射分量与环境映射；构建可重光照的表面光场；从粗初始化出发，利用环境映射进行重光照，通过光度损失优化姿态。

Result: 在自建数据集和实拍序列上验证，该方法在扩散物体上性能超越最强基线，在完全反射物体上唯一保持高精度，而所有基线均显著下降。

Conclusion: 反射不仅是挑战，更是可利用的几何与光照线索。本文方法通过光场建模实现对反射的鲁棒跟踪，尤其适用于高反射场景，为机器人与自动驾驶中的动态物体跟踪提供了新范式。

Abstract: Tracking the 6DoF pose of a moving rigid object is fundamental to robotics and autonomous driving, but existing trackers assume that object appearance is stable across a sequence, an assumption that breaks down on reflective surfaces whose appearance changes as they mirror the environment. We introduce a light field based reflection-robust 6DoF tracker that turns this apparent nuisance into a pose cue. Per frame, our method recovers depth robustly against reflections, back-projects it into a point cloud, and estimates surface normals. It then decomposes the object's view-dependent appearance into a diffuse albedo and the environment map it reflects, resulting in a relightable surface light field. Starting from a coarse initialization, we relight it by the recovered environment map and optimize the pose on the photometric loss. Because a moving object mirrors new parts of the scene, the environment map fills in as the sequence proceeds, sharpening this signal over time. To evaluate this approach, we introduce a light field tracking dataset re-rendered from a robotic manipulation benchmark at four controlled reflectivity levels, each paired with simulated depth that reproduces how consumer RGB-D sensors fail on shiny surfaces. Additionally, we evaluate on two captured light field sequences. Our method trails the strongest baselines on diffuse objects and is the only one that holds its accuracy on fully reflective objects, where every baseline degrades.

</details>


### [85] [PWM: Personalized World Models with Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04920)
*Zhexin Lou,Guancheng Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Yi Zhang,Yang Zhao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Personalized World Models (PWM)，通过在线强化学习从短场景视频中定制交互式世界模型，支持用户指定特定场景。PWM利用支持轨迹和控制提供奖励反馈，采用GRPO方法更新紧凑的LoRA适配器，结合场景外观、视觉连续性和运动的一致性奖励，并通过基线策略锚定保持预训练生成先验。该方法适用于真实与渲染环境，同时引入DiffusionNFT作为替代优化方式。构建了PWM-Bench基准测试集，包含150个跨室内、室外和游戏领域的定制任务。GRPO和DiffusionNFT在71.3%和65.3%的场景中优于原生Yume模型，且在所有领域均实现正向平均提升；GRPO相比监督微调（SFT）在定制化效果和图像质量上全面更优，同时保持接近预训练模型的帧级视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户希望基于自定义视频探索特定场景，但现有预训练世界模型难以在保留动作条件生成质量的同时学习场景视觉身份，因此需要一种能高效个性化定制且保持生成质量的方法。

Method: 提出PWM框架，使用在线强化学习从短视频中学习场景视觉身份。通过支持轨迹及其控制提供奖励信号，采用GRPO（组相对优化）更新轻量级LoRA适配器，联合优化场景外观、视觉连续性与运动一致性；引入基线策略锚定以约束对预训练生成先验的修改。同时提供基于DiffusionNFT的替代优化路径。

Result: 在PWM-Bench基准上，GRPO和DiffusionNFT分别在71.3%和65.3%的场景中超越原生Yume模型，所有领域均有正向平均增益。GRPO相较SFT在定制化效果和图像质量上均更优，同时维持接近预训练模型的帧级视觉质量。

Conclusion: PWM成功实现了高效、高质量的场景个性化定制，在真实与渲染环境中均表现优异，为交互式世界模型的个性化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Pretrained world models can generate diverse environments, yet users often want to explore a particular scene specified by their own video. This requires learning the scene's visual identity while retaining the quality of action-conditioned generation. We introduce Personalized World Models (PWM), a framework for customizing interactive world models from short scene videos through online reinforcement learning. In PWM, the support trajectory and its associated controls provide reward feedback on continuations sampled from the current policy. In the GRPO instantiation, group-relative optimization updates a compact LoRA adapter using a unified reward for scene appearance, visual continuity, and motion, while base-policy anchoring regularizes changes to the pretrained generation prior of a frozen Yume-5B backbone. The same adaptation procedure is applied across real and rendered environments. We also instantiate PWM with DiffusionNFT as an alternative reward-guided optimization method for learning the scene-specific adapter. We also introduce PWM-Bench, comprising 150 customization tasks across Indoor, Outdoor, and Gaming, with paired evaluation on held-out continuations. The GRPO and DiffusionNFT instantiations of PWM improve customization over native Yume in 71.3% and 65.3% of the evaluated scenes, respectively, with positive mean gains across all three domains. For the GRPO instantiation, matched SFT comparisons further demonstrate higher mean customization gains and better mean image-quality scores in every domain, while retaining frame-level visual quality close to the pretrained model.

</details>


### [86] [TRACE: Time-Adaptive Residual Attention Control with Content-Style Decomposition for Training-Free Diffusion Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04922)
*Duc Khoan Le,Kim Ngoc Tran,Minh Nhat Le,Thanh An Tran,Viet Toan Nguyen,Khanh An Lay,Tran Thai Son,Hoang Pham Minh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE is a training-free diffusion style transfer framework that improves style fidelity and content preservation by using time-adaptive residual attention control and content-style decomposition. It separates content and style directions via offline CLIP-based analysis, removes cross-components during inference, and applies uncertainty-aware guidance for dynamic style injection.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing training-free diffusion-based style transfer methods struggle with balancing style fidelity, content preservation, and content leakage. Direct style injection risks transferring semantic content, while fixed guidance schedules fail to adapt to the dynamic nature of diffusion sampling.

Method: TRACE performs offline CLIP-based subspace analysis to decompose content and style directions. During inference, it purges content-related components from the style image and style-related components from the content image. It then injects style via residual cross-attention and uses uncertainty-aware guidance to adaptively adjust the guidance signal at each denoising step.

Result: TRACE achieves superior performance: +17.28 CSD and +34.10 SRA in style fidelity over optimal-control baselines; better content preservation with +12.80 DINO, +5.52 CLIP-I, and -8.19 LPIPS; reduces directional semantic leakage by 29.5% in DCL.

Conclusion: TRACE effectively balances style transfer quality and content integrity through adaptive, decomposition-based guidance, outperforming existing methods in both stylization and structure preservation without requiring retraining.

Abstract: Reference-guided style transfer aims to preserve the semantic structure of a content image while transferring the visual appearance of a style reference. Recent diffusion-based methods achieve impressive stylization quality by exploiting strong pretrained generative priors. However, training-free approaches still face a difficult trade-off among style fidelity, content preservation, and content leakage. Direct style injection may unintentionally transfer semantic content from the style image, while fixed guidance schedules often ignore the time- and state-dependent nature of diffusion sampling. To address these limitations, we propose TRACE, a training-free diffusion style transfer framework with Time-adaptive Residual Attention Control and Content-Style Decomposition. TRACE first performs offline CLIP-based subspace analysis to separate content and style directions from paired data. During inference, it removes content-related components from the style reference and style-related components from the content reference to reduce leakage. It then injects style information through residual cross-attention and applies uncertainty-aware guidance to adapt the guidance signal at each denoising step. Experiments show that TRACE achieves a favorable trade-off between stylization and preservation. Compared with optimal-control-based baselines, TRACE substantially improves style fidelity (+17.28 CSD and +34.10 SRA). While, compared with stylization methods, it better preserves content structure (+12.80 DINO, +5.52 CLIP-I, and -8.19 LPIPS) and reduces directional semantic leakage by 29.5% in DCL. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pixelchemy-research/TRACE.

</details>


### [87] [Robust Tensor Completion via Reflective Convolution Nuclear Norm Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04979)
*Weiguo Zhou,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianwen Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出反射卷积核范数最小化（RCNNM）以改进张量补全，通过反射取代循环移位，避免边界人工伪影，在随机采样和稀疏错误下实现高概率精确恢复，并在合成数据、彩色图像和多光谱图像上验证了其优越性，尤其在图像边界处性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卷积低秩模型使用循环延拓构造平移副本，对有限非周期数据引入人工的环绕邻域，导致边界误差；为解决此问题，提出基于反射的替代方案以更准确地建模数据边界。

Method: 采用反射卷积核范数最小化（RCNNM），用反射代替循环移位，构建具有非均匀权重乘性的提升张量，并利用加权格拉姆恒等式支持恢复分析与优化算法设计。开发两块交替方向乘子法（ADMM），实现逐元素张量更新，奇异值阈值化通过较小的右格拉姆矩阵完成。

Result: 在随机采样和稀疏扰动条件下，实现了对底层张量和稀疏误差的高概率精确恢复，并在有界密集扰动下保持稳定性；实验表明，相比传统循环方法，RCNNM在边界区域的平均PSNR提升达3.26 dB，全局重建质量也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RCNNM通过反射机制有效缓解了传统循环延拓带来的边界伪影问题，显著提升了张量补全在图像边界处的恢复质量，同时保证整体性能，是处理非周期多维数据的有效方法。

Abstract: Robust tensor completion recovers multidimensional data from partial observations corrupted by sparse gross errors. Existing convolutional low-rank models typically construct translated copies using circular continuation, which introduces artificial wrap-around neighborhoods for finite nonperiodic data. We propose reflective convolution nuclear norm minimization (RCNNM), which replaces circular shifts with endpoint-nonrepeating reflection. The resulting lifting has nonuniform entry multiplicities and satisfies a weighted Gram identity that supports both the recovery analysis and the optimization method. Under random sampling and sparse corruption, we establish high-probability exact recovery of the underlying tensor and sparse errors, together with stability under bounded dense perturbations. We further develop a two-block ADMM with a closed-form entrywise tensor update, while singular-value thresholding is implemented through the smaller right Gram matrix. Experiments on synthetic tensors, BSDS color images, and CAVE multispectral images show that RCNNM consistently improves over its circular-lifting counterpart, with the clearest gains near image boundaries. In particular, average boundary-PSNR improvements reach 3.26 dB while global reconstruction quality remains competitive.

</details>


### [88] [Geometry-Aware Preference Optimization for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04980)
*Lei Wang,Zhen Wang,Yuexiang Xie,Yaliang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Anisotropic Geometry-Aware Preference Optimization (APO)，通过引入基于参考模型的几何感知各向异性度量，改进扩散模型中偏好对齐的优化过程。与传统方法在全空间均匀优化不同，APO根据局部流形几何结构自适应调整正则化强度，在敏感方向加强约束，安全方向放宽限制，从而更好保持图像质量与多样性。实验表明，APO在多个基准上平均胜率超60%，训练步数显著减少，且生成多样性不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法（如Diffusion-DPO）在高维空间中直接优化偏好分布，忽略了自然图像集中在低维流形上的几何特性，导致优化动态不匹配：抑制了保持几何结构的切向更新，而对危险的法向更新约束不足，逐步损害图像质量和多样性。

Method: 提出APO方法，使用参考模型导出的几何感知各向异性度量替代传统的欧氏距离，自适应地在不同方向施加不同程度的正则化，强化对敏感方向的约束，放松对允许语义调整方向的限制，实现与局部流形结构一致的偏好优化。

Result: APO在多个基准上平均胜率超过60%，优于现有对齐方法；所需训练步骤更少，且在整个训练过程中有效保持生成多样性，同时提升图像质量。

Conclusion: 通过将偏好优化与图像流形几何结构对齐，APO显著提升了扩散模型的对齐效率与生成质量，为文本到图像生成中的偏好对齐提供了更稳健、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Preference alignment has become a standard practice for text-to-image diffusion models. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) simplifies this process by eliminating explicit reward modeling. Its diffusion variant, Diffusion-DPO, has become a widely adopted baseline. Diffusion-DPO essentially encourages the likelihood of preferred samples while suppressing dispreferred ones. In this paper, we revisit DPO-style alignment methods for diffusion models from the perspective of the manifold hypothesis. Under this view, natural images concentrate near a low-dimensional manifold embedded in the high-dimensional ambient space, whereas DPO directly optimizes preference distributions in the full space without accounting for this geometric structure. This creates a mismatch in the optimization dynamics: it suppresses geometry-preserving tangential updates, while insufficiently restricting hazardous normal-direction updates. This mismatch gradually degrades image quality and diversity. To address this issue, we propose Anisotropic Geometry-Aware Preference Optimization (APO), which replaces the uniform Euclidean treatment of prediction errors with a geometry-aware anisotropic metric derived from the reference model. Concretely, APO adaptively strengthens regularization in directions where the reference denoising function is highly sensitive, while relaxing constraints in directions that permit safe semantic adjustment. This recalibrates preference optimization according to the local manifold geometry, and maintains the original manifold structure. Experiments show that APO achieves strong performance and an average win rate exceeding 60\% against various existing alignment methods across diverse benchmarks. It requires significantly fewer training steps than prior methods, and preserves generation diversity throughout training.

</details>


### [89] [VisualErase: Dual-Branch Visual Trajectory Redirection for Robust Concept Erasure in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05000)
*Qianlong Xiang,Miao Zhang,Kun Wang,Yupeng Hu,Junhui Hou,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisualErase新范式，通过结构保持的图像编辑构建无目标概念的图像副本，并利用双分支重定向损失将文本条件和无条件生成结果对齐到这些副本，以真正消除视觉生成轨迹中的目标概念，显著提升对抗攻击下的安全性，同时保持通用生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法仅重定向文本到图像的映射，但模型仍可能保留目标概念的视觉生成轨迹，易受对抗恢复攻击，存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 使用结构保持的图像编辑创建内容对齐且无目标概念的图像副本，基于源图与副本对生成去噪目标，采用双分支重定向损失同时约束条件与无条件生成，结合副本保留损失以减少对非目标生成的影响。

Result: 在风格、名人和裸露内容擦除任务中，最大攻击成功率分别降至0%、8%和0.1%，同时保持良好的通用生成质量。

Conclusion: 视觉生成轨迹的重定向对于实现鲁棒的概念擦除至关重要，仅依赖文本-图像映射重定向不足以彻底消除潜在风险。

Abstract: Concept erasure is essential for the safe deployment of text-to-image diffusion models, as they may reproduce harmful, copyrighted, or privacy-sensitive content learned from unconstrained large-scale data. Existing methods typically erase unwanted concepts while preserving general generation capability by redirecting target-related text-to-image mappings. However, recent studies show that erased models may still retain visual generative trajectories of target concepts, leaving them vulnerable to adversarial recovery attacks and revealing a fundamental gap between redirecting text-to-image mappings and truly removing visual knowledge. To bridge this gap, we propose VisualErase, a new paradigm that redirects concept-bearing visual generative trajectories toward explicitly defined concept-removed outcomes. To enable this redirection, we use structure-preserving image editing to construct content-aligned, concept-removed counterparts for source images, providing explicit visual endpoints that retain non-target content. We then derive a denoising target from each source-to-counterpart pair and use a dual-branch redirection loss to align both text-conditioned and unconditional predictions with this target, since conditional supervision alone does not explicitly constrain generation without textual guidance. To mitigate the adverse effects of concept erasure on non-target generation, we jointly optimize the redirection loss with a counterpart retention loss that matches denoising predictions from the frozen pretrained model. Across style, celebrity, and nudity erasure, VisualErase limits the maximum attack success rate over seven attacks to 0%, 8%, and 0.1%, respectively, while retaining general generation quality. These results highlight the importance of visual trajectory redirection for robust concept erasure beyond text-to-image mappings alone.

</details>

### [90] [Fine-Grained Emotion Classification from Mobile App Reviews: An Empirical Study with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03802)
*Quim Motger,Carlota Catot,Marc Oriol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了如何利用大语言模型实现移动应用评论中细粒度多标签情感分类，以支持需求工程中的情绪导向问题优先级排序和功能反馈分析。在严重类别不平衡的条件下，比较了编码器微调（多标签与二元集成）和解码器零样本/少样本提示（开源与专有模型）的表现，并测试了多种不平衡缓解策略（如损失重加权、重采样、生成式数据增强）。结果显示，经过优化的编码器+生成数据增强+正类加权损失可显著缩小与最优解码器方法的差距，且推理延迟低三个数量级，尤其对罕见情感提升明显。结论表明大语言模型使细粒度情感分类成为可行，但最佳方案依赖于模型架构与设计。相关代码、合成语料库及微调检查点已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度情感分类有助于需求工程中的情绪导向问题优先级排序与功能反馈分析，但现有自动情感提取研究不足，尤其是在严重类别不平衡场景下，亟需有效方法支持。

Method: 对比编码器仅微调（多标签与二元集成）与解码器零/少样本提示（开源与专有模型），结合损失重加权、重采样、生成式数据增强等不平衡缓解策略，并通过内在增强效用排名选择合成评论生成器与提示策略。

Result: 微调编码器在基线表现较差（多标签：0.387；二元集成：0.450），远落后于最佳解码器少样本提示（宏F1 0.642）；但采用生成数据增强与正类加权损失后，编码器性能提升0.204，接近解码器表现，且推理延迟降低三个数量级，对罕见情感提升最大，最高达+0.501 F1。

Conclusion: 大语言模型使应用评论的细粒度多标签情感分类在需求工程中成为可能，尽管宏观F1尚可，但最优方案取决于模型架构与任务设计。研究发布实验管道、合成语料库与微调检查点以支持复现与再利用。

Abstract: Context: Fine-grained emotion classification of mobile app reviews enables requirements engineering activities that go beyond polarity-based opinion mining, including emotionally informed issue prioritisation and feature-oriented feedback analysis. However, automatic fine-grained emotion extraction from app reviews remains understudied. Objectives: Building on a previously published annotation framework and human-labelled ground truth adapted from Plutchik's taxonomy, this paper investigates how large language models can be leveraged for automatic multi-label emotion classification under severe class imbalance. Methods: We compare encoder-only fine-tuning under multi-label and binary-ensemble formulations, decoder-only zero- and few-shot prompting across open-source and proprietary models, and a catalogue of imbalance mitigation strategies (loss reweighting, resampling, generative data augmentation), with the synthetic-review generator and prompting strategy selected via an intrinsic augmentation-utility ranking. Results: Fine-tuned encoders trail the best decoder-only few-shot prompting (macro-F1 0.642) by a wide margin at baseline (multi-label: 0.387; binary ensemble: 0.450); pairing the best multi-label encoder with generative data augmentation and positive-weighted loss closes most of this gap (+0.204) at up to three orders of magnitude lower inference latency than the decoders, with the largest gains on the rarest emotions, from undetected to gains of up to +0.501 F1. Conclusion: Large language models make fine-grained, multi-label emotion classification of app reviews feasible for requirements engineering pipelines, with modest macro-F1, and the best formulation and mitigation strategy are backbone- and formulation-dependent. We release the experimental pipeline, synthetic corpora, and fine-tuned checkpoints for replication and reuse.

</details>


### [91] [Same Output, Different Gold: Measuring How Reference Choice Moves a Multilingual Benchmark Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03825)
*Parth Kulshreshtha,Shivali Dalmia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究关注基准评分中常被忽略的参考文本部分，通过六种语言的个人身份信息标注数据集，发现参考文本的变化会导致评分显著波动（最高达7.55 F1点），并揭示了因未记录的聚合默认规则导致参考文本部分复制系统输出的问题。研究提出计算参考敏感性范围的方法，并主张将其与评分一同报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准评分方法主要关注系统输出，而忽视了参考文本对评分结果的影响。本研究旨在揭示参考文本的不稳定性如何影响评分结果，并评估其实际影响。

Method: 利用包含双标注者标签和专家复评金标准的六语言个人身份信息基准数据集，固定系统输出，替换参考文本，通过比较不同参考下的评分变化，分析参考敏感性；并通过统计检验验证结果的显著性。

Result: 参考文本变化导致评分变动最大达7.55 F1点，系统间相对表现排序可能因此改变；发现一个未记录的聚合默认规则使参考文本部分复制系统输出，造成偏差；提出可计算参考敏感性范围的方法。

Conclusion: 参考文本的敏感性应作为评估指标的一部分，建议在报告评分时同时提供参考敏感性范围，以提升评估透明度与可靠性。

Abstract: A benchmark score compares a system output against a reference, and methodological attention falls almost entirely on the first term. We measure the second. The retained annotation record of a six-language benchmark for personally identifiable information contains two independent annotator labellings, the aggregate shipped as gold, and a reviewer gold from independent expert re-annotation of a sample. Using it, we hold the scored output fixed and exchange the reference. The score moves by 4.95 F1 points for one output and 2.00 for the other (95% CIs [3.23, 6.27] and [0.26, 3.37]), and by 7.55 in the worst language. The comparison between two outputs moves as well: the paired interaction between reference and system is +2.95 points (CI [+1.97, +3.87]), survives correction for multiple testing, and changes one language's margin outright. The cause is an undocumented aggregation default that usually kept one annotator when adjudication did not fire, making the shipped reference a partial copy of an output being scored. We report the resulting reference-sensitivity band, show how to compute one from any retained annotation record, and argue that the quantity belongs beside the score.

</details>


### [92] [The Score Is Not the Structure: Brain Alignment and Cross-Lingual Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03827)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究质疑了现有方法中用于衡量模型与大脑或跨语言结构相似性的相似性分数的有效性。通过两个实验发现，当共享结构不存在或测量工具失效时，这些分数仍可能显示显著相关性，从而误导结论。第一，跨语言语法探测器在语言距离较远时性能下降，部分语言甚至达到随机水平，但若以语言为单位而非成对计算，统计显著性大幅降低；第二，语言模型模拟大脑响应的相似性得分提升，但即使目标信号被破坏，得分仍较高，表明大部分得分并非源于真实脑-模型对应关系，而是由数据基础和评分机制本身导致。研究强调，在评估任何对应关系前，必须先检验分数是否在无实际结构时依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究常依赖相似性分数来证明模型与大脑或跨语言间存在共享结构，但这些分数可能受测量工具失效、统计单位错误或基线偏移影响，导致误判。本研究旨在揭示这些分数在无真实结构时仍可能呈现高值，从而挑战现有证据的可靠性。

Method: 采用两个实验：1）分析语法探测器在不同语言间的转移性能，检验其在语言距离增加时的表现，并比较以语言对或语言为单位的统计结果；2）评估语言模型在匹配人类大脑反应时的相似性得分变化，包括在目标信号被破坏的情况下的表现，以及在无模型情况下基于目标信号排名的基准得分。

Result: 1）在17种语言中，4种语言的探测器性能降至随机水平，导致64对语言的分数不可靠；若以语言为单位分析，原显著性（p=0.0006）变为非显著（p=0.155）。2）模型在“脑-模型”匹配任务中得分从0.10升至0.34，但即使目标信号被破坏，得分仍达0.31，仅0.028–0.068的提升来自真实脑对应；无模型时，原始目标已得0.204，与目标排名有关。3）语言沿自身方向调整可提高得分，但效果不随语言距离变化，说明该效应非源于跨语言结构。

Conclusion: 相似性分数在缺乏真实共享结构时仍可能显示显著结果，因此不能单独作为模型与大脑或跨语言结构一致的证据。研究强调，必须评估分数在无结构条件下的表现，以区分真实对应与统计幻觉。

Abstract: Researchers often support the claim that a model shares structure with the brain, or across languages, by reporting a similarity score. We ask what such a score reads when the shared structure is absent, or when the tool that measures it does not work. We check two settings, and in both the score is not what it appears. First, a probe trained to tell grammatical from ungrammatical sentences in one language transfers worse to more distant languages, the usual evidence for shared structure. But the probe itself gets worse along the same axis: in four of seventeen languages it performs at chance, so 64 of 272 language pairs are scored with a tool that does not work. Dropping those languages halves the strength of the relationship, but they are also the most distant, and this design cannot separate the two effects. Counting matters too: the same data give p = 0.0006 when the 272 pairs are treated as independent and p = 0.155 when the seventeen languages are, which is the correct unit. Second, training a language model to match human brain responses raises its similarity score from 0.10 to 0.34, against a ceiling of 0.54. A model trained on a target whose correspondence to the brain was destroyed still scores 0.31, so only 0.028 to 0.068 of the rise is specific to the brain. With no model at all, a destroyed target already sits at 0.204 from the real one, a floor that tracks the target's rank divided by the number of sentences. Finally, steering a language along its own direction works (+6.2 over a random direction in sixteen of seventeen languages) yet shows no effect that varies with language distance. Before asking whether a correspondence helps, ask how much of the score would survive without it.

</details>


### [93] [OncoNoteBERT: A Foundation Representation Model for Natural Language Processing of Real-World Outpatient Oncology Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03829)
*Wuraola Oyewusi,Eliana Vasquez Osorio,Goran Nenadic,Gareth Price*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了基于BERT的肿瘤学专用编码器，使用英国29万份门诊肿瘤学笔记数据集。对比了RadBERT和PathologyBERT与两种本地策略（OncoNote-RadBERT和OncoNoteBERT），结果显示持续预训练和专用分词器在降低困惑度、提升术语分词效率方面表现更优，且本地模型对机构占位符的表示更合理。研究强调了领域特定微调与分词设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的门诊肿瘤学笔记包含大量专业术语、肿瘤分期表达、治疗名称、毒性描述及机构特有的去标识化标记，这些内容难以被通用生物医学或邻近临床语言模型有效表示，因此需要开发针对肿瘤学领域的专用语言模型以提高自然语言处理效果。

Method: 采用英国290,026份来自21,564名肺癌和头颈部癌患者的门诊肿瘤学笔记构建语料库，比较了RadBERT和PathologyBERT，并引入两种本地化策略：通过持续掩码语言模型预训练生成的OncoNote-RadBERT，以及从零开始训练并使用肿瘤学WordPiece分词器的OncoNoteBERT。评估方法包括掩码语言建模损失、验证集困惑度、分词碎片化指标、临床术语分词效果、掩码词探测任务和探索性表示分析。

Result: 外部编码器在零样本评估中表现较差（RadBERT困惑度113.04；PathologyBERT 2035.03），而持续预训练的OncoNote-RadBERT表现最佳（困惑度2.10）。OncoNoteBERT虽困惑度稍高（2.83），但分词效率最高，子词冗余低、序列长度更短，且在13个掩码词探测任务中有12个得到临床可接受预测，优于OncoNote-RadBERT的7个。这种性能差异主要源于分词碎片化而非语义学习本身。两个本地模型均将机构占位符表示为单一可学习标记。

Conclusion: 持续适应和定制化分词策略具有互补优势，且表示层设计在将邻域编码器应用于肿瘤学自然语言处理前至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world outpatient oncology notes contain specialised terminology, tumour staging expressions, treatment names, toxicity descriptions, and institution-specific de-identification markers that may not be represented efficiently by general biomedical or adjacent clinical language models. We developed and evaluated oncology-specific BERT-style encoders using a governed UK outpatient oncology corpus comprising 290,026 notes from 21,564 patients treated for lung and head-and-neck cancer. We compared RadBERT and PathologyBERT with two local strategies: OncoNote-RadBERT, produced by continued masked language model pretraining, and OncoNoteBERT, trained from scratch with an oncology WordPiece tokenizer. Models were evaluated using masked language modelling loss and perplexity on the validation set, tokenizer fragmentation metrics, clinical term tokenisation, masked-token probes, and exploratory representation analysis. Both external encoders fit the oncology corpus poorly in zero-shot evaluation (perplexity 113.04 for RadBERT; 2035.03 for PathologyBERT), while continued pretraining produced the strongest fit (2.10 for OncoNote-RadBERT). OncoNoteBERT achieved perplexity 2.83 but produced the most efficient tokenisation, with lower subword fertility and shorter normalised sequence length. It also returned a clinically acceptable prediction for 12 of 13 masked-token probes, compared with 7 of 13 for OncoNote-RadBERT. This divergence between corpus-level fit and masked-token performance was partly attributable to tokenizer fragmentation rather than learned semantics alone. Both locally developed models represented the institutional placeholder as a single learnable token. These findings show that continued adaptation and bespoke tokenisation provide complementary benefits, and that representation-layer design matters before adjacent-domain encoders are applied to oncology NLP.

</details>


### [94] [SYNLAT: Syntax-Aligned Text-Latent Compression for Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03839)
*Yifeng Zhao,Hongjun Yu,Shibo Wang,Yunjiao Zhou,Zixiao Zhu,Zhipeng Ning,Kezhi Mao,Junlang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynLat提出一种基于语法结构对齐的文本-隐变量推理框架，通过非重叠的语法对齐单元（SAUs）优化压缩边界，以在有限输出预算下保留关键答案信息。该方法利用问答条件的教师模型生成渐进式保留/隐含目标，学生模型仅根据问题和压缩级别即可生成混合推理，在多个数据集和压缩级别下均优于或持平现有基线，尤其在强压缩和长链推理任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理（CoT）轨迹带来高昂的输出令牌成本，在预算受限时需高效压缩，但传统基于词元或固定长度的边界会破坏连贯内容（如短语、公式、局部推导），而步骤级边界能更好适应不同压缩需求，因此需要一种能与语法结构对齐的智能压缩机制。

Method: 提出SynLat框架，将压缩边界与语法结构对齐，使用非重叠的语法对齐单元（SAUs）。采用问答条件的教师模型生成渐进式KEEP/LATENT目标，训练一个压缩条件的学生模型，推理时仅需输入问题和压缩级别即可生成混合推理路径。

Result: 在两个Qwen3学生规模、标准与长链推理组及三种压缩级别下，SynLat在12个任务组聚合中均达到或超过最强基线，11个场景严格领先；在中等和高强度压缩下，Qwen3-8B/14B分别获得3.6/2.6和7.0/5.5的性能提升，且在长链推理任务中优势更显著。

Conclusion: SynLat通过语法对齐的压缩边界设计，有效提升了长链推理在资源受限场景下的压缩效率与答案保真度，尤其在强压缩和复杂推理任务中表现出色，为高效推理系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces impose substantial output-token costs. Under constrained budgets, compression must preserve answer-critical information, making boundary placement central. Token-level and fixed-length boundaries can fragment coherent spans such as phrases, formulas, and local derivations, whereas step-level boundaries can bind content requiring different compression actions. We introduce SynLat, a text-latent CoT framework that aligns compression boundaries with syntactic structure through non-overlapping Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs). An answer-conditioned Teacher constructs progressive KEEP/LATENT targets for a single compression-conditioned Student, which generates mixed reasoning from only the question and requested compression level at inference. Across two Qwen3 Student scales, Standard-CoT and Long-CoT groups, and three compression levels, SynLat matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in all 12 task-group aggregates and strictly leads in 11 under the reported achieved-CR selection protocol. Overall gains reach 3.6/2.6 points at MEDIUM and 7.0/5.5 points at HIGH for Qwen3-8B/14B, with larger advantages under stronger compression, particularly on Long-CoT groups.

</details>


### [95] [When Evidence Changes: Evaluating Memory Repair and Re-reading in Language-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03902)
*Wenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了在医疗记录证据被撤销或替换时，采用记忆修复还是重新读取当前证据的效率。通过对比全量和源过滤重读与缓存、重建及图局部修复，在两个7B模型上进行实验。结果显示，在短记录情况下，局部修复比重建少用5-10倍的修订令牌，但所有记忆管道的成本至少是全量重读的两倍；当记录长度达约10,000 tokens时，记忆的平均累积成本低于全量重读，主要得益于截断提取；源过滤重读始终最经济。在替换研究中，四个主要确认性测试均未达到统计显著性。结论强调，应将记忆在证据修订后的成本与源过滤重读在整个流程中的成本进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗记录中，当支持代理推导事实的文档被撤销或替换时，如何高效更新记忆成为关键问题。传统方法如缓存或重建可能带来高成本，而局部修复或重读策略可能更优，但缺乏系统评估。因此需要明确不同策略在真实场景下的成本效益，以指导实际应用。

Method: 在公开的ICU记录中，针对药物和病历列表任务设计证据修订评估，涵盖撤销、替换和对照事件。比较全量重读、源过滤重读、缓存、重建和图局部修复五种策略，使用两个7B模型进行实验。评估指标包括摄入、修订和每次使用的成本，特别关注短记录与长记录（约10,000 tokens）下的性能差异。

Result: 在短记录下，局部修复比重建节省5-10倍修订令牌，但所有记忆策略成本至少为全量重读的两倍。当记录长度达到约10,000 tokens时，记忆的平均累积成本低于全量重读，部分因截断提取实现；源过滤重读始终是最经济的方案。在替换实验中，四种主要验证测试均未显示统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 在证据修订后，必须将记忆策略的总成本与源过滤重读在整个流程中的成本进行对比，才能做出合理选择。源过滤重读在多数情况下最具成本效益，尤其在长记录场景中表现出优势。

Abstract: When documents supporting an agent's derived facts are revoked or replaced, should it repair memory or re-read current evidence? We introduce an evidence-revision evaluation on medication- and problem-list tasks from public ICU records. Under revocation, replacement and control events, we compare full and source-filtered re-reading with caching, rebuilding and graph-local repair across two 7B models. Memory is supplied in full without retrieval, and costs include ingest, revision and every use. On short records, local repair uses 5-10$\times$ fewer revision tokens than rebuilding, yet every memory pipeline costs at least twice full re-reading in held-out conditions. In a small pre-specified development sweep, adding task-ineligible documents extended records to about 10,000 tokens; at that length, memory's mean cumulative cost fell below full re-reading's after 2-14 uses, partly through truncated extraction, while source-filtered re-reading remained cheapest. In the replacement study, none of the four primary confirmatory tests reached statistical significance. These results show why the cost of agent memory after evidence revision must be assessed against source-filtered re-reading over the full pipeline.

</details>


### [96] [General Decision Models: Benchmarking and Insights Beyond Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03935)
*Feiyu Duan,Jiayu Lin,Jia Wang,Jun Xiang,Jialiang Wu,Xinnong Zhang,Hanqi Yan,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出JEVal基准，评估25种模型在11,257个跨10个领域的实例上的表现，发现通用决策模型在可基于证据的决策中表现优异，但在需要专业知识或准确不确定性估计时表现下降；在长周期多步系统中，快速局部决策虽缩短时间但因错误累积导致整体可靠性降低；在大规模社会模拟中，决策模型推理成本远低于生成式LLM，但在用户画像和聚合估计上仍存在偏差。提出InnerJev-4B和InnerJev-27B，通过自蒸馏将LLM推理内化为单次前令牌决策，实现与Jev相当性能，且响应速度仅约0.1秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通用决策模型在不同决策场景下的可靠性边界及其在复杂系统中的行为演化，评估其在真实应用中的适用性，并探索提升其效率与准确性的方式。

Method: 构建JEVal多领域双语基准，涵盖36个数据集共11,257个实例；对比25种模型配置（包括通用决策模型与生成式LLM）的表现；设计并验证InnerJev系列模型，采用‘推理到读出自蒸馏’机制将大模型推理能力压缩至单次前令牌输出。

Result: 通用决策模型在证据充分时表现强，但对专家知识和不确定性估计能力不足，易高估概率；在长周期多步任务中，局部快速决策导致系统级错误累积，降低成功率；在社会模拟中，虽成本低但存在聚合偏差与用户画像弱项；所提InnerJev-27B在性能上媲美Jev，响应时间约0.1秒。

Conclusion: 通用决策模型在结构化、证据驱动的决策中具有优势，但在复杂、动态系统中面临可靠性挑战；通过内部化大模型推理的自蒸馏方法可显著提升其效率与表现，是高效决策系统的重要发展方向。

Abstract: General decision models, such as Jev, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to LLMs for structured judgment and selection. But what kinds of decisions can these models reliably make, and how does their behavior change when individual decisions are composed into larger systems? To study this, we introduce JEVal, a bilingual benchmark comprising 11,257 instances from 36 datasets across 10 application domains, and evaluate 25 model configurations spanning general decision models and generative LLMs. Our results show that (1) general decision models are most competitive when decisions can be resolved from available evidence, but weaken when they require specialist knowledge or faithful uncertainty estimation: they can often identify the most likely outcome while substantially overstating its probability. (2) In more dynamic and realistic systems involving long-horizon, multi-step interactions, the advantages of fast local decision making are offset by reliability failures at the system level. on $τ$-bench, faster local decisions reduce median episode time but lower task success as decision errors accumulate over long trajectories. (3) In large-scale social simulation, decision models approach strong generative LLMs on individual response prediction at substantially lower inference cost, yet remain weaker in user profiling and exhibit larger aggregate estimation errors and systematic bias. Finally, we propose InnerJev-4B and InnerJev-27B, which internalize an open-weight LLM's own reasoning into a single-pass first-token decision through Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation, with InnerJev-27B performing on par with Jev on JEVal while answering a typical query in about 0.1 s.

</details>


### [97] [A Step Towards Forgetting: Optimiser History and the Loss of Answer Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03940)
*Valeria Ruscio,Seth Nabarro,Keiran Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了优化器记忆（如Adam中的动量）如何在微调过程中导致语言模型遗忘已有知识。尽管当前梯度倾向于保留旧任务答案的概率，但历史梯度积累会推动模型向相反方向变化，导致旧答案的总概率下降。通过分解Adam更新，发现历史梯度主要引发概率泄漏，而当前梯度则起保护作用。较早的新任务梯度危害最大，而近期梯度有助于保护旧知识。干预实验表明，重置动量但保持初始更新范数可显著提升旧任务保留能力，主要通过恢复答案质量实现。此外，有限步长下的积分分析显示，大多数大损失事件可通过局部投影解释，而历史路径的曲率会放大部分事件。整体揭示了优化器记忆如何在看似保守的梯度下仍造成行为退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在微调过程中出现的遗忘现象，尤其是优化器记忆（如动量）如何在当前梯度试图保留旧知识的情况下反而促进遗忘。

Method: 通过分离旧任务损失中的混淆与概率泄漏成分，分析不同语言模型家族中答案质量与概率分布的变化；使用Adam更新分解、按历史梯度年龄分类、状态依赖干预及有限步长积分等方法，系统研究优化器记忆的影响机制。

Result: 答案总概率持续下降，而旧答案间的区分度通常提升；历史梯度贡献以概率泄漏为主，且来自较早新任务梯度的影响更严重；重置动量并匹配初始更新范数可显著改善旧任务保留，尤其通过恢复答案质量；局部投影可解释多数大损失事件，而历史曲率会放大少数关键事件。

Conclusion: 优化器的记忆效应会在微调过程中侵蚀已学行为，即使当前梯度意图保留旧知识。这种遗忘主要源于历史梯度对概率泄漏的推动，且受其相对于旧任务梯度方向的影响。通过控制历史影响，可以有效缓解遗忘，提升模型长期记忆能力。

Abstract: During fine-tuning, a language model can assign less probability to previously learned answers even when the current gradient acts to preserve that probability. With momentum, each update also carries gradients computed at earlier model states, and these stored contributions can push the model in the opposite direction. We investigate how this optimiser memory contributes to forgetting by separating old-task loss into confusion among its answers and leakage of probability outside the answer set. Across three language-model families, answer mass consistently declines while discrimination among old answers usually improves: the model becomes less likely to produce answers that it can still distinguish correctly. Decomposing Adam updates reveals opposing contributions to this loss of answer mass. Over training, accumulated history favours leakage, while the current gradient opposes it. Resolving history by age shows that the harmful contributions come mainly from older gradients of the new task, whereas recent gradients tend to protect the old answers. Changes in history's effect are dominated by its orientation relative to the old-task gradient. Interventions that reset momentum while matching the initial update norm establish that stored history affects retention, with state-dependent immediate effects and lower final old-task loss over longer Adam continuations, mainly through recovered answer mass. Finally, integration along finite updates shows that most sampled large loss increases are captured by local projections, while curvature along history amplifies some events. Together, these findings reveal how an optimiser's memory can erode learned behaviour even as its current gradient acts to preserve it.

</details>


### [98] [BAIBAICHUCHU at the NTCIR-19 FinArg-3 Task: When Is Maximum Possible Profit Predictable from Investor Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03962)
*Zong-Han Bai,Po-Yen Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BAIBAICHUCHU团队参与NTCIR-19 FinArg-3社交媒体子任务，旨在根据最大可能收益（MPP）对中文投资者帖子进行排序。采用词法特征、预微调的MacBERT排序器和大语言模型（LLM）判官的三轨集成方法在开发集上达到0.734的得分，但官方测试集表现不佳，最高仅0.517，所有12次提交结果介于0.4598至0.5402之间。后验审计发现，判官对看跌帖子应用了仅做多规则，而MPP应为立场感知，修正后28个预测改变但准确率不变，开发集准确率从0.680降至0.622，表明一种特定市场状态下的语义捷径提升了验证拟合度。研究将排名分解为方向性文本、波动率与期限，并分析成对边际效应。基于极值的模型预测出σ√T规律，该规律在另选的2026年7月502条价格对齐帖子中得到验证。发布前波动率按帖子特定观察期缩放（σ_pre,20√N_i）与后期截断期限的MPP代理指标显著相关（Spearman ρ=0.320；ticker聚类95%置信区间[0.133,0.466]），可正确排序61.4%的非等效结果对。转移文本与7月结果几乎无关（ρ=0.055），在已知波动率与立场条件下贡献甚微。随着标注的MPP差距增大，开发集可靠性从约0.60提升至0.93。早期ERAI结果0.6207排除了普遍不可预测性的简单解释。评估应综合考虑文本、市场状态、历史波动率、观察期限及成对样本构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决社交媒体中投资者帖子按最大可能收益（MPP）排序的问题，尤其关注如何有效结合文本内容、市场状态、波动率与时间跨度等多维度因素以提升排序准确性。现有方法在实际评估中表现不稳定，存在因模型偏差导致的语义捷径问题，亟需更稳健的建模框架。

Method: 采用三轨集成策略：（1）词法特征；（2）在FinArg-2数据上预微调的MacBERT排序器；（3）大语言模型（LLM）作为判官。通过后验审计识别并修正判官对看跌帖的错误处理规则。进一步将排名机制分解为方向性文本、波动率与期限、成对边际效应，并引入运行极值模型预测σ√T关系。利用2026年7月独立收集的502条价格对齐数据验证模型，使用预发布波动率与观察期的乘积（σ_pre,20√N_i）作为代理变量，评估其与后续收益的关系。同时分析文本信息的独立预测能力及其在条件下的增量价值。

Result: 三轨集成在开发集上取得0.734的高分，但官方测试集最高仅0.517，整体表现受限。修正判官的长只规则后，虽未改变准确率，但开发集性能下降，揭示了语义捷径带来的虚假拟合。σ_pre,20√N_i与截断期限的MPP代理指标呈显著正相关（ρ=0.320），且能正确排序61.4%的不等结果对。转移文本与最终结果几乎无关（ρ=0.055），在控制波动率与立场后贡献有限。随着标签中MPP差异扩大，开发集可靠性从0.60升至0.93。先前的ERAI结果0.6207表明市场并非完全不可预测。

Conclusion: 当前的排序任务不能仅依赖文本或单一信号，必须联合考虑文本内容、市场状态、历史波动率、观察周期以及成对样本结构。模型设计需警惕特定市场状态下出现的语义捷径，避免过拟合验证集。引入基于波动率与期限的量化指标可显著提升排序稳定性与可解释性，未来工作应聚焦于多源信号融合与跨周期泛化能力的增强。

Abstract: The BAIBAICHUCHU team participated in the Social Media Subtask of NTCIR-19 FinArg-3, ranking Chinese investor posts by Maximum Possible Profit (MPP). A three-track ensemble of lexical features, a FinArg-2-pre-finetuned MacBERT ranker, and an LLM judge reaches 0.734 in post-grouped development evaluation, but our best official run scores 0.517. All twelve submitted runs lie between 0.4598 and 0.5402, and our 26 unanimous three-track pairs score 0.500.
  A post-hoc audit finds that the submitted judge applied a long-only rule to bearish posts although MPP is stance-aware. Correcting it changes 28 of 87 official predictions without changing accuracy, yet lowers development accuracy from 0.680 to 0.622: a regime-specific semantic shortcut improved validation fit.
  We decompose ranking into directional text, volatility and horizon, and pairwise margin. A running-extremum model predicts $σ\sqrt{T}$ scaling, observed ex post in 502 price-aligned posts from a separate July 2026 collection. Pre-posting volatility scaled by the post-specific observation horizon, $σ_{\mathrm{pre},20}\sqrt{N_i}$, is associated with a later truncated-horizon MPP proxy (Spearman $ρ=0.320$; ticker-cluster 95% CI [0.133,0.466]) and correctly orders 61.4% of unequal-outcome pairs. Transferred text is nearly uncorrelated with the July outcome ($ρ=0.055$) and adds little conditional on this score and stance. Development reliability rises from about 0.60 to 0.93 as the labeled MPP gap widens. An earlier ERAI result of 0.6207 rules out universal unpredictability as a simple explanation. Evaluation should jointly consider text, market regime, historical volatility, horizon, and pair composition.

</details>


### [99] [Do Language Models Need a Trainable Input Embedding Table? Fixed Minimal Token Codes at 1.7B-Class Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04002)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了语言模型中是否必须使用可训练的输入嵌入表来实现强大语言建模能力，通过对比三种使用相同分词器、上下文主干和输出头架构的解码器模型，分别采用可学习嵌入表、16位固定令牌ID代码和一个在GF(2)上的固定可逆编码。结果表明，即使使用固定代码，模型仍能获得显著的语言理解能力（如HellaSwag 52.40%，PIQA 70.51%，LAMBADA 42.75%），证明独立可训练的令牌特定输入向量并非必要。尽管可学习输入表现略优，但固定接口显著减少了参数量（减少1.007亿参数，得到17.11亿参数模型），更重要的是揭示了架构必要性与经验效用的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中可训练输入嵌入表是否为实现强大语言建模能力所必需，以评估其架构必要性而非仅经验性能优势。

Method: 比较三种从零训练的解码器模型：使用可学习嵌入表、16位固定令牌ID代码、以及在GF(2)上的固定可逆编码作为输入接口，保持其他架构和训练条件一致，目标训练量为每模型1000亿预测词元。

Result: 固定代码模型在多个任务上表现出色（如HellaSwag 52.40%，PIQA 70.51%，LAMBADA 42.75%），表明无需可训练的令牌特定输入向量即可实现显著语言能力；可学习输入略有优势，但关键在于证明固定输入接口的可行性。

Conclusion: 独立可训练的令牌特定输入向量并非实现语言模型核心能力的必要条件，固定输入接口在不牺牲性能的前提下显著减少参数量，并为研究下游表示学习提供了可控环境。

Abstract: A trainable input embedding table assigns each vocabulary item an independently adjustable vector. We investigate whether this token-specific parameterization is required for substantial language-modeling capability, or whether a shared Transformer can learn from fixed token identities. We compare three decoder-only language models trained from scratch with the same tokenizer, contextual backbone, untied output-head architecture, and training recipe, with a target budget of 100 billion prediction tokens per model. Their input interfaces are a learned table, canonical 16-bit token-ID codes, and one fixed invertible recoding over GF(2). The fixed codes are repeated to model width without an additional trainable input projection. Both fixed-code models acquire substantial capabilities: canonical codes achieve 52.40\% HellaSwag normalized accuracy, 70.51\% PIQA accuracy, and 42.75\% LAMBADA accuracy. The learned-input control performs better on several evaluations, including HellaSwag and LAMBADA, so these results establish viability rather than performance parity. The fixed interfaces remove 100.7 million trainable parameters, yielding 1.711B-parameter models, but parameter reduction is not the central result. These single-run experiments distinguish architectural necessity from empirical utility: independently trainable token-specific input vectors are not required for the observed capabilities. A fixed identity interface also provides a controlled setting for studying representation learning downstream of an immutable input, without establishing where particular capabilities are localized.

</details>


### [100] [Periscope: Extending Frozen Language Models Beyond Their Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04047)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Anas Obayd,Raed Rashid,Abdulrahman Alghamdi,Abdulrahman Mousa,Abdallah Ahmed,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Periscope 是一种无需训练的推理方法，通过将文本分块并排列在 K×K 网格中，仅用 √sc 的代价完成对长文本的高效推理，显著降低内存消耗并提升长文本理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语言模型在处理长文本时受限于上下文窗口，需多次前向传播或使用大窗口，导致计算和内存开销剧增。如何在不增加成本的前提下有效利用长文本信息成为关键挑战。

Method: 将文本划分为 N 个块，构建 K×K 网格（K=⌈√N⌉），对每个局部连续块和跨块采样的间隔块分别提问，利用冻结模型获取每项答案的对数似然，并综合局部与间隔得分生成证据图，定位最相关段落。

Result: 在 LongBench v2 上，仅读取证据图排名最高的 K 个块（9k tokens）即可达到传统窗口读取 32k 到 1M tokens 的效果；在 InfiniteBench 上领先最佳窗口方法 5 分；在 BRIGHT 数据集上取得 NDCG@10 最佳表现；单次推理仅需 4.5M token 缓存，可在 80GB GPU 上运行 27B 模型，远低于传统方法所需的 296GB。

Conclusion: Periscope 实现了高效、低内存的长文本推理，使模型不再受限于显存容量，而是依赖模型本身能力，为大规模文档理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: A language model reads long text in one quadratic forward pass, stops at the context window, and loses accuracy with length before reaching it. We ask whether the read can be factorized when deciding over a finite set: which document is relevant, which option is supported, which passage is the evidence. Periscope, a training-free inference method, arranges the $N$ chunks of a text on a $K{\times}K$ grid with $K{=}\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil$ and asks a frozen model the same question about $K$ local spans of consecutive chunks and $K$ strided spans that sample the whole text, reading the log-odds of every answer at one token. Each answer takes its best local and strided score, and scoring every chunk by its two spans gives an evidence map at no further cost, whose peak is the chunk behind the answer. Every probe is about $\sqrt{sc}$ tokens for a text of $s$ tokens and chunk size $c$, so a window of $W$ tokens reaches $W^{2}/c$ tokens at $s^{1.5}$ cost. The map replaces the long read. On LongBench v2, reading only the $K$ chunks the map ranks highest, 9k tokens, matches the same model's best window read across windows from 32k to 1M tokens, and on InfiniteBench, where the median context is 150k tokens, it leads the best window read by 5 points. The same map ranks BRIGHT's long-document corpora with the best NDCG@10 of six methods. Each call caches only one probe, so a 27B model reads 4.5M-token contexts on one 80GB GPU, where a single pass would need 296GB of cache. A long read then needs a GPU that holds the model, not one that holds the text.

</details>


### [101] [IdeaScientist: Orchestrating Agents for Grounded Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04074)
*Jiarui Liu,Renjie Tao,Yiwei Liao,Chuanyang Jin,Kai Sun,Xiao Yang,Xinyuan Zhang,Xilun Chen,Zhuangqun Huang,Lechen Zhang,Yongjin Yang,Yinghui He,Weihao Xuan,Rakesh Wanga,Anuj Kumar,Mona T. Diab,Wen-tau Yih,Xin Luna Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IdeaScientist 是一种用于科学研究创意生成的系统，通过分解研究构思为发现差距、创新和报告撰写三个角色，并利用强化学习进行训练。该系统基于跨领域类比的思路，从Svalbard Idea Vault（包含277万条分解后的研究想法）中检索灵感，以生成新颖且有根据的研究方案。在限定文献时间范围的评估中，IdeaScientist 在27B规模的模型上超越了最强的开源基线14.0%，并优于Claude-4.8-Opus和GPT-5.4等闭源模型，主要优势体现在创新性方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化科学研究虽进展迅速，但生成具有前景且扎实的研究解决方案仍面临挑战。现有方法缺乏对研究创意本质的深入挖掘，尤其在跨领域灵感迁移方面表现不足。因此，亟需一种能有效激发新颖研究方向的系统化方法。

Method: 将研究创意过程分解为三个角色：差距发现、创新生成和报告撰写，分别通过强化学习进行训练；利用跨领域类比机制，从Svalbard Idea Vault中检索相关灵感；结合时间控制评估策略，验证其提出方向与后续人类研究的一致性。

Result: 在27B规模模型上，IdeaScientist 相较于最强开源基线提升14.0%，并在新颖性方面显著领先；其表现超越Claude-4.8-Opus和GPT-5.4等闭源模型，最高达5.9%的提升，证明其在生成高质量研究构想方面的有效性。

Conclusion: IdeaScientist 通过结构化建模研究创意过程并利用跨领域知识迁移，在生成高新颖性和合理性的研究提案方面展现出卓越性能，为自动化科学研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in automating scientific research, generating promising and well grounded research solutions remains a central challenge. We isolate research ideation as a standalone task and build our solution on the intuition that a challenge in one field can often be addressed by a mechanism that solved an analogous challenge in another. Accordingly, we introduce IdeaScientist, which decomposes ideation into gap finding, innovation, and report writing, and trains each role with reinforcement learning. These roles identify limitations in related work, draw solution intuitions from analogous problem settings, and develop those intuitions into complete research proposals. To facilitate discovery of insights across domains, we construct the Svalbard Idea Vault, a corpus of 2.77M decomposed research ideas for retrieval, training, and temporally controlled evaluation. Our evaluation restricts access to literature available before a cutoff date and assesses how closely proposed directions align with those later explored in 15K papers authored by human researchers. On Qwen3.6-27B, IdeaScientist outperforms the strongest open-source autoresearch baseline by 14.0%, driven mainly by gains in novelty. On this 27B open backbone, IdeaScientist even outperforms Claude Code SDK with Claude-4.8-Opus and Codex SDK with GPT-5.4, by up to 5.9%.

</details>


### [102] [Representation-Aligned Auxiliary Supervision for Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04098)
*Kyuyoung Kim,Peiyao Sheng,Ashwin Hebbar,Peiyang Xu,Yunfei Xie,Kevin Wang,Rui Xin,Chen Wei,Zhangyang Wang,Jinwoo Shin,Pramod Viswanath,Sewoong Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了语言模型在结构化领域（以国际象棋为例）适应中的表现，发现表示兼容性是关键因素。模型对语义等价的输入处理差异显著，影响学习和泛化。为此提出表示对齐的辅助监督方法，利用与环境一致的表示形式的任务提升模型适应能力。实验表明，该方法在多种模型和表示下均优于仅目标任务训练，尤其动态环境任务带来的提升最显著且稳定，甚至可媲美大幅增加目标数据的效果。此外，基于ASCII训练的模型向FEN迁移效果更好，且在生成事实性评论方面也表现出色。整体表明，使用模型兼容表示的辅助监督能有效促进结构化领域的适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型虽具备强大推理能力，但在结构化领域适应时仍面临挑战，结果不一致。现有方法缺乏对表示兼容性的考量，导致模型难以有效处理结构化任务中的不同表示形式。因此需要探索如何通过改进表示一致性来提升模型在结构化任务中的适应能力。

Method: 提出表示对齐的辅助监督方法，利用环境中衍生的任务（如动作预测、状态转移等）在与目标任务兼容的表示形式中进行训练。实验对比了符号编码（FEN）和空间格式（ASCII）两种表示下的模型性能，并评估了不同类型的辅助任务（动态环境任务、表面级任务、静态任务）的效果。同时考察了跨表示迁移的能力以及在开放性评论生成任务中的表现。

Result: 辅助监督显著提升了最优走法预测性能，尤其动态环境任务带来的增益最大且最稳定；模型从ASCII到FEN的迁移优于反向迁移，且在未见过的表示上表现超越基准；辅助监督在事实性评论生成任务中也带来显著提升；整体表明，使用兼容表示的辅助监督可有效增强模型在结构化领域的适应能力。

Conclusion: 表示兼容性是影响语言模型在结构化领域适应的关键因素。通过在模型兼容的表示中引入环境驱动的辅助任务，可以显著提升模型的学习效率、泛化能力和迁移能力，为结构化任务的高效适配提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, yet adapting them to structured domains remains challenging and can yield inconsistent outcomes. We identify representation compatibility, the extent to which a model effectively processes a representation for a structured task, as a key factor in adaptation. We study this in chess, which provides a controlled testbed with precise semantics, computable optimal actions, and multiple state representations, including a symbolic encoding (FEN) and a spatial format (ASCII). We find that models often process semantically equivalent inputs substantially differently, affecting both learning and generalization. Building on this observation, we propose representation-aligned auxiliary supervision, which uses environment-derived tasks expressed in compatible representations to improve adaptation to structured domains. Across models and representations, auxiliary supervision consistently improves optimal-move prediction relative to target-only training under identical target data. Tasks that expose environment dynamics provide larger and most consistent gains than surface-level or static supervision, while remaining competitive with substantially increasing the amount of target-task data. Moreover, ASCII-trained models transfer more effectively to FEN than FEN-trained models do to ASCII, even surpassing the FEN target-only baseline on FEN evaluation. The gains also extend beyond optimal-move prediction to open-ended, factually grounded commentary generation. Overall, our results show that auxiliary supervision in model-compatible representations can enable effective adaptation in structured domains.

</details>


### [103] [LongSocialBench: Do Long-Context LLMs Understand Online Discussion Threads?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04118)
*Xinyi Liu,Rinat Khaziev,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Tarek F. Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongSocialBench 是一个包含 1,462 道经人工验证的多选题的基准测试，基于 Hacker News、Stack Exchange 和 Reddit r/ChangeMyView 的完整讨论线程，每题需模型理解复杂的社交结构关系（如父子回复、转折点、分支对比等）。尽管长上下文大模型可处理长文本，但在社会推理任务中仍远低于人类表现。最佳模型 Claude-Opus-4.7 在优化提示下仅达 63.0%，而人类独立阅读者为 72.4%。即使提供正确证据范围，平均性能也仅提升至 55.0%，表明核心问题在于对结构化回复树的社会理解能力不足，而非上下文容量或提示技巧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文大模型虽能处理长文档，但缺乏对复杂社交对话结构（如回复关系、参与轨迹、跨分支对比）的理解能力，导致在真实社会讨论理解任务中表现不佳。需要一个高质量、结构化的评估基准来揭示模型在社会推理方面的局限性。

Method: 构建 LongSocialBench 基准，从 94 个真实在线讨论中提取完整串行化讨论与回复结构，生成 1,462 道多选题，每题配以四选项和需结构化推理才能回答的问题；所有题目经过答案可答性、选项唯一性和证据锚定性验证；在 18 个模型和 29 种设置下进行评估，包括全上下文基线、黄金证据范围引导等不同条件。

Result: 当前主流长上下文模型在 LongSocialBench 上表现普遍偏低，全上下文基线平均得分仅为 43.9%；即使提供正确的证据范围，平均得分也仅提升至 55.0%；最佳模型 Claude-Opus-4.7 在优化提示下达到 63.0%，仍显著低于人类水平（72.4%），说明模型缺失的是对结构化社交互动的理解能力。

Conclusion: 长上下文能力不足以实现真正的社会推理；当前模型在处理复杂社交对话时的核心瓶颈在于对回复树结构的理解，而非上下文长度或提示工程。未来工作需聚焦于增强模型对社交结构的建模与推理能力。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs can now ingest entire online discussion threads, but understanding their social discourse requires more than reading a long document: models must track parent-reply relations, turning points, scoped subtrees, cross-branch contrasts, and participant trajectories. To test this structure-aware social reasoning, we introduce LongSocialBench, a benchmark of 1,462 verified human-authored multiple-choice items drawn from 94 complete Hacker News, Stack Exchange, and Reddit r/ChangeMyView episodes, with a median length of approximately 73K tokens. Each item pairs a complete serialized discussion and reply structure with a four-option question, requiring models to recover structured social evidence. Released items are verified for answerability, option uniqueness, and evidence grounding. Across 18 models and 29 evaluation settings, current long-context workflows remain far below human performance. The best individual result comes from Claude-Opus-4.7, which reaches 63.0% when prompted to eliminate incorrect options before answering, compared with 72.4% for independent human readers. Averaged across all 18 models, the full-context Baseline scores 43.9%. Supplying the gold evidence scope raises this to 55.0%, showing that substantial errors remain even after the relevant thread region is identified. LongSocialBench shows that the missing capability is not context access or prompting alone, but social understanding over structured reply trees.

</details>


### [104] [Copying Before Suppression: What Drives a Below-Chance Dip During Language Model Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04119)
*Tejas Dahiya,Cole Blondin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了在模型训练早期，尽管语言模型损失持续下降，但其行为可能暂时偏离正确方向（如偏好重复出现的名称而非唯一提及的名称）。通过固定注意力头并替换其输出，发现这些头在早期对正确判断的贡献微弱，而随着训练推进逐渐发挥作用。将成熟模型中的注意力头参数移植到早期检查点可恢复35%至68%的行为改进，表明成熟模型中隐藏了早期导致错误行为的临时因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模型在训练过程中行为变化背后的机制，特别是当模型尚未达到最终性能时，其内部计算如何影响行为表现。

Method: 选择特定注意力头，在独立于因果评估的提示上分析其作用；固定头的选择，用非重复名称提示上的平均输出替换其最终输出；在多个规模的Pythia、GPT-2和OLMo模型中测试该干预的效果，并比较不同训练阶段的注意力分布与输出贡献。

Result: 替换注意力头输出显著提升了正确名称与重复名称之间的logit差值；早期模型中关键注意力头的影响力极小，但随训练增长；将成熟参数移植到早期模型可恢复大部分行为改善。

Conclusion: 成熟模型中存在一个隐藏的、临时的因果配置，它在训练初期塑造了错误行为，而在后期被更优的计算机制取代。这说明模型行为的变化不仅源于最终权重，也受训练过程中的动态演化影响。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability usually studies fully trained models, yet the computations that drive a behaviour can change while the model is still learning the task. On the Indirect Object Identification task, a model should continue with the name mentioned once rather than the name mentioned twice. Pythia models pass through an early training window in which they prefer the repeated name, so accuracy in a choice between the two names falls below one half while language-model loss on a fixed text sample keeps decreasing across the same window. The window reflects a temporary imbalance between two computations. We identify one cause of the wrong preference by selecting a set of attention heads that write the repeated name, on prompts separate from those used for causal evaluation, keeping that selection fixed, and then replacing each head's final-token output with its average output on a separate set of non-repeated-name prompts. This improves the correct-minus-repeated logit difference in a separately trained 160M model and in the official 160M, 410M, and 1B models. At 160M, the head that lowers the repeated name in the mature model shows little of its mature behaviour at this point. It directs less than one percent of its attention to the repeated mention, and its output makes almost no direct contribution to lowering that name's logit. Both properties grow over the interval in which behaviour recovers. Across the 160M, 410M, and 1B models, transplanting the corresponding head's mature parameters into the early checkpoint recovers 35 to 68 percent of the total improvement in the correct-minus-repeated logit difference seen by the end of training. Related early-to-late reversals appear at further Pythia scales, in two independently trained GPT-2 models, and in OLMo. A mature circuit can therefore conceal a transient causal configuration that shaped behaviour earlier in training.

</details>


### [105] [Representational Control over Self-Report & Behavior Coherence in LLM Risk-Taking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04125)
*Rafal Kocielnik,Peiyang Song,Pengrui Han,Myrl G. Marmarelis,Ramit Debnath,Dean Mobbs,R. Michael Alvarez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活控制技术，探究大语言模型（LLM）的自我报告与行为之间在风险承担维度上的关系。发现自我报告和行为由近正交的内部表示驱动，仅特定任务指令能同时影响两者，且其效果受表面混杂因素干扰。组合不同方向可同步调节报告与行为，甚至使其对立。研究揭示了自报-行为差异的表征本质，主张在评估中引入表征检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现大语言模型的自我报告与其行为之间仅有部分一致性，但这种差异是否源于提示方式或模型内部表征的本质仍不清楚。本文旨在通过激活控制技术，深入分析自我报告与行为在内部表示层面的关系，以揭示其根本机制。

Method: 采用激活转向（activation steering）技术，在同一内部干预下测量九种不同方法提取的转向向量（包括任务特定指令、模型自身任务表现、两种粒度的特质描述），在四个开源大模型上评估其对自我报告和行为的影响，并使用两个行为任务和两个心理测量工具进行验证。

Result: 1. 共享内部干预并不保证一致响应：基于特质描述的向量影响自我报告但不影响行为；基于模型自身任务选择的向量影响行为但不影响报告；只有任务特定指令能弱效地同时影响两者。2. 诊断分析显示，去除表面混杂因素后，任务指令的行为效应下降至32%。两通道由近乎正交的方向驱动，每条通道可通过多种独立构造实现。3. 组合一个行为移动方向与一个自报移动方向可同时影响两者；反转其中一个方向可使两者对立，导致报告与实际风险行为相反的情况在所有模型中占69%-89%。

Conclusion: 自我报告与行为之间的差异并非黑箱现象，而是由内部表征决定的。研究结果支持将表征检查纳入模型评估流程，为理解与控制大模型的内在决策机制提供了新路径。

Abstract: Self-report is an appealing low-cost probe of an LLM's dispositions, but recent work finds only selective agreement between what models report and how they behave. Prior accounts establish these patterns by prompting black-box LLMs, leaving open whether the gap is a prompting artefact or a fact about how the underlying constructs are represented internally. We investigate risk-taking, a consequential dimension of agentic decision-making, using activation steering to measure self-report and behavior under the same internal intervention. We survey nine steering-vector extraction methods spanning task-specific directives, the model's own task behavior, and dispositional descriptions at two granularities, evaluated on two behavioral tasks and two psychometric instruments across four open-weight LLMs. We find that (1) a shared internal intervention does not ensure shared responsiveness: directions built from trait descriptions move self-report but leave behavior at chance, directions built from the model's own task choices do the reverse, and only task-specific directives reach both, weakly. (2) Diagnosis dissolves that exception: removing surface confounders leaves the directives only 32% of their behavioral effect. The two channels are otherwise steered by near-orthogonal directions, each channel reachable by several independent constructions. (3) An intervention composing one behavior-moving and one self-report-moving direction moves both together; flipping one sign sets them in opposition, with reported and enacted risk pointing in opposite directions on 69-89% of flipped compositions in all models. These findings move the self-report-behavior relationship from a black-box observation to a representational one that can be inspected and controlled, motivating representational checks alongside behavioral evaluation.

</details>


### [106] [PB-GRPO: Learning Socially Adaptive LLM Agents from Persona-Driven Simulation with Preference-Batched GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04132)
*Jingquan Wang,Jun Yin,Xu Han,Yongsheng Mei,Jie Hao,Bin Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PB-GRPO算法，通过基于人物角色的社会模拟环境，利用对话级反馈学习社会适应性策略，相比传统GRPO在多样性社交群体中更稳定，提升模型社会行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽能生成局部有帮助的回复，但缺乏对用户未明言目标的理解、偏好尊重及动态适应能力，而这些社会性行为依赖多轮互动，难以优化，因真实交互数据稀缺且用户偏好隐含不可直接观测。

Method: 构建基于人物角色的社会模拟环境（含人物库、基于LLM的用户模拟器、用户满意度评分系统），提出偏好分批GRPO（PB-GRPO）算法，利用相似偏好用户组的归一化优势估计，实现从对话级反馈中学习社会适应性策略。

Result: 在模拟环境中，PB-GRPO相较于强基线强化学习方法，在提升模型社会行为方面表现更优，训练过程在多样化社交群体中更稳定。

Conclusion: PB-GRPO通过引入偏好分批机制和对话级反馈，有效提升了LLM在复杂社会交互中的适应性和行为质量，为构建真正社会智能的代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Building LLMs that behave well socially, not merely correctly, requires Building LLMs that behave well socially, not merely correctly, requires more than producing locally helpful responses.
