Recent work automates this process with LLM-based systems that generate and iteratively improve reward code using policy feedback. However, these methods are often hard to compare because they differ in implementation details, feedback assumptions, and evaluation environments. To address this, we introduce a Benchmark for Iterative Reward Design (BIRD) that expresses existing methods in a unified configuration and evaluation space. This lets us compare algorithms directly, ablate individual design choices, and prototype new components under matched feedback conditions and policy-training budgets. Across MuJoCo, Meta-World, Assistax, and HumanoidBench, we identify a small set of simple design choices that consistently improve performance. Combining these choices yields significantly better performance than the evaluated methods from prior work. Our results highlight the strength of simple baselines and motivate further study of when additional algorithmic complexity improves iterative reward design. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/bird.

</details>


### [238] [A multi-stage probabilistic framework to estimate gas-fired generator performance during extreme winter weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04368)
*Sajjad Uddin Mahmud,Anamika Dubey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种三阶段贝叶斯概率框架，利用公开的气象和电力需求数据以及匿名化的发电机故障记录，评估冬季极端天气对燃气发电机组的影响。研究以纽约州（2013–2022年）为例，量化了低温、高用电需求下发电机组故障概率上升、可用容量下降及事件持续时间延长的趋势。在最严重条件下，故障发生概率可达24%，平均净可用容量降至额定容量的13%；完全停机事件中位持续时间为12.7小时，部分降载事件持续时间随容量损失加重从2.4小时增至7.1小时。该框架可为有电厂级数据的电力公司提供可迁移的基准，支持运营规划与资源充足性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电厂级数据难以获取，无法系统评估极端冬季天气对燃气发电机组的停电风险，亟需一种基于公开数据的可靠分析方法来量化风险并支持电力系统可靠性管理。

Method: 采用三阶段贝叶斯概率建模框架：第一阶段估计每小时发电机组故障事件的发生概率；第二阶段估计事件发生时的预期净可用容量；第三阶段估计事件持续时间。结合公开气象数据、电力需求数据与NERC提供的匿名化发电机故障记录进行建模。

Result: 在极端寒冷和高需求条件下，发电机故障概率最高达24%，净可用容量降至额定容量的13%；完全停机事件中位持续12.7小时，部分降载事件持续时间随容量损失加剧从2.4小时增至7.1小时。模型结果表明气候压力显著影响发电可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯框架可有效利用公开数据估算冬季极端天气下的发电机组性能退化，为电力公司提供可扩展的可靠性评估工具，有助于改进运营规划和资源充足性分析。

Abstract: Extreme winter weather has repeatedly disrupted gas-fired power generation in the United States, yet the plant-level data needed to systematically quantify outage risk remain proprietary. Using publicly available weather and electricity demand data together with anonymized generator contingency records from the North American Electric Reliability Corporation (NERC), we develop a three-stage Bayesian probabilistic framework for estimating winter-driven generator performance. Applied to New York State (2013--2022), the framework sequentially estimates: the hourly probability of a generator contingency event, the expected net available capacity conditioned on an event occurring, and the event duration. Colder conditions and higher electricity demand are associated with higher failure probability, lower retained capacity, and longer event duration. Under the most severe observed stress conditions, estimated mean hourly event probability reaches 24\% , while expected mean net available capacity falls to 13\% of nameplate rating. Full outage events have a median duration of 12.7 hours, while partial derating event duration increases from 2.4 to 7.1 hours with capacity loss severity. The proposed framework establishes a transferable baseline that utilities with access to plant-level records can directly extend to obtain more precise reliability estimates for operational planning and resource adequacy assessment.

</details>


### [239] [CEENs: Causality-enforced evolutional networks for solving time-dependent partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04405)
*Jeahan Jung,Heechang Kim,Hyomin Shin,Minseok Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新方法，通过在训练过程中融入时间因果性，解决物理信息神经网络（PINN）在长时间积分偏微分方程时的局限性。该方法将时间域划分为非重叠子区间，为每个子区间分配独立的神经网络，并基于积分形式构建损失函数，实现从初始时刻开始的顺序训练。相比原PINN方法，新方法在精度上显著提升，同时降低计算成本与内存消耗，并提供并行化算法以进一步加速求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原PINN方法在长期模拟中表现不佳，主要原因是未考虑时间因果性，导致模型更关注后期方程满足而忽略初始条件，从而产生错误解。

Method: 将时间域划分为非重叠子区间，为每个子区间分配一个独立神经网络，采用基于积分形式的损失函数，并按时间顺序依次训练网络，确保从初始时刻开始逐步学习。

Result: 所提方法在多个时间依赖型PDE问题上实现了比原始PINN更高的精度，且计算开销和内存占用更低；结合并行化策略后，求解速度显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入时间因果性，新方法有效克服了传统PINN在长时模拟中的缺陷，具备更高精度、更低资源消耗和更强可扩展性，适用于复杂时间依赖问题的高效求解。

Abstract: Despite the growing popularity of physics-informed neural networks (PINNs), their applicability in the long-time integration of partial differential equations (PDEs) remains constrained. We argue that this problem stems from the lack of consideration of temporal causality in the original PINN formulation, resulting in a bias towards satisfying governing equations at later times before learning the initial condition and hence leading to erroneous solutions. To this end, we propose a novel method that seamlessly integrates temporal causality into the training process. Drawing inspiration from classical numerical methods where the temporal causality is reflected, we divide the time domain into nonoverlapping subintervals, assign a unique neural network to each subinterval, and construct a loss function founded on the integral form of PDEs within these subintervals. The proposed networks undergo sequential training, beginning with the initial time step. Our method demonstrates significant improvement in accuracy for long-time simulations of various PDE problems where the original PINN method fails while it requires less computational cost and memory compared to the PINN method. A parallelization algorithm is provided to further enhance the computational efficiency, showing a significant speedup for solving time-dependent PDEs.

</details>


### [240] [Specific Algorithmic Interpretability of Neural Networks: A Case Study on Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04413)
*Yanglin Zhang,Anneke von Seeger,Gilad Lerman,Ron Levie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个原则性框架，用于构建神经网络，其参数初始化可明确对应算法逻辑。通过将数据点建模为类相关的随机过程，并假设单一样本即可估计足够区分类别的统计量，初始网络参数精确实现该统计估计算法，从而保证可解释性。随后在保持与算法初始化偏差可控的前提下进行微调，使训练后的网络仍保留原始算法解释性。基于PAC-Bayesian分析，给出统一泛化误差界，其复杂度项与微调半径相关，提供理论支持。以纹理分类为例，采用散射变换估计判别性统计量，验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受算法启发的神经网络架构虽能解释网络计算结构，但标准训练后参数往往失去与原始算法的清晰关联，导致可解释性丧失。因此需要一种方法，在训练过程中保持网络参数与初始算法之间的可解释关系。

Method: 将每个数据点视为类依赖的随机过程样本，假设仅需单一样本即可估计足以区分类别的统计量；构造初始参数恰好实现该统计估计算法的神经网络，使其完全可解释；通过控制微调过程中的参数偏离程度，确保训练后网络仍大致保持算法解释性；使用PAC-Bayesian分析建立泛化误差上界，其复杂度随微调半径增长，为方法提供统计合理性。

Result: 所提框架在纹理分类任务中成功应用，结合散射变换估计判别性统计量，实现了既具备良好性能又保持算法可解释性的神经网络。实验表明，微调后网络仍能维持初始算法的语义解释，且泛化性能得到理论保障。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架有效弥合了算法可解释性与训练后模型性能之间的鸿沟，使得神经网络在保持高性能的同时，其参数变化仍具有明确的算法意义，为可解释深度学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We develop a principled framework for constructing neural networks whose specific parameter realizations admit an explicit algorithmic interpretation. Existing algorithm-inspired architectures can explain the computational structure of a network, yet after standard training the learned parameters need not retain a clear relation to the motivating algorithm. We address this gap as follows. First, we model each data point as a sample of a class-dependent stochastic process and assume that statistics of this process can be estimated from a single sample and these statistics are sufficient to distinguish the classes. We then construct a neural network whose initial parameters exactly implement an algorithm for estimating these statistics, making the network fully interpretable. To account for mismatch between the idealized model and real data, we fine-tune this network while controlling its deviation from the algorithmic initialization. The trained network hence roughly retains the interpretation of the initial network. A PAC-Bayesian analysis yields a uniform generalization bound whose complexity term scales with the fine-tuning radius, providing a statistical motivation for our approach. We instantiate the framework for texture classification using the scattering transform to estimate the discriminative statistics.

</details>


### [241] [MaDeL: Manifold-Decomposed Feature Losses for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04419)
*Beomsu Kim,Jong Chul Ye,Kwanyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需显式估计流形即可区分流形内变化与流形外偏差的生成目标——流形分解特征损失（MaDeL）。通过在特征瓶颈下训练两个互补表示：一个恢复干净样本，另一个恢复噪声，其雅可比矩阵分别对齐流形切空间和法空间，从而构建各向异性损失，有效提升在合成数据、地球与气候科学及蛋白质骨架等任务中的样本质量与支持恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成模型使用各向同性损失（如均方误差），在数据集中在低维流形时会混淆沿流形的变化与偏离流形的无效变化，尤其在稀疏、高约束领域中导致非流形插值问题。需要一种能直接从数据中区分这两种变化的生成目标，而无需显式估计流形结构。

Method: 提出曼德尔特征损失（MaDeL），利用受污染观测学习两个互补表示：一个用于重建原始样本，另一个用于重建扰动；在特征瓶颈条件下，这两个表示的雅可比矩阵分别对齐流形的切空间和法空间，从而实现对内在变化与离流形偏移的分离度量。

Result: 在合成数据、地球与气候科学、扭转角基准测试中，MaDeL显著提升了单步采样下的支持恢复能力和平均角度W1距离；在蛋白质骨架任务中，无论是一步还是少步采样，均有效减少了立体冲突。

Conclusion: MaDeL通过隐式学习流形结构，实现了对流形内与流形外变化的分离建模，是一种有效的各向异性生成目标，在复杂约束数据生成任务中表现优越。

Abstract: Generative models are often trained with isotropic objectives such as mean-squared error. For data concentrated near a low-dimensional manifold, however, such losses conflate displacement along the manifold, which may represent valid variation, with displacement away from it, which produces invalid samples. This mismatch is especially problematic in sparse, highly constrained domains, where ambient-space regression can encourage off-manifold interpolation. We ask whether a generative objective can distinguish manifold-parallel variation from manifold-orthogonal deviation directly from data, without explicitly estimating the manifold. We introduce a manifold-decomposed feature loss (MaDeL) that learns complementary representations from corrupted observations: one is trained to recover the clean sample, while the other is trained to recover the corruption. We show that, under a feature bottleneck, their Jacobians align with the tangent and normal spaces, exactly for linear manifolds and locally for smooth manifolds. Together, these representations define an anisotropic objective that separately measures intrinsic variation and off-manifold deviation. Across synthetic, Earth and climate science, and torsion-angle benchmarks, MaDeL improves support recovery and average angular $W_1$ under single-step sampling; on protein backbones, it reduces steric clashes across one- and few-step sampling budgets.

</details>


### [242] [Measuring Effective Data Resolution in Guided Diffusion Posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04422)
*Ridham Patel,Defu Cao,Jiacheng Pang,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效数据分辨率（effective data resolution）来评估黑箱生成后验分布中数据对重建结果的实际影响，通过扰动估计器测量采样器实现的分辨率（dof_samp），并与反问题应有的分辨率（dof_ref）对比。该方法仅需从采样器查询中即可获得空间映射R(x,x)和dof_samp，验证表明其能揭示引导权重对信息传递的显著影响，并指出均值、方差与分辨率并非由单一权重统一修正，且分辨率保真度并不随引导规则的合理性而可靠提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准诊断无法衡量物理场重建中数据实际贡献的程度，尤其在使用引导扩散采样器时，需要一种可量化数据信息利用程度的新指标。

Method: 提出有效数据分辨率概念，采用扰动估计器从采样器查询中估计dof_samp，并结合参考分辨率dof_ref进行比较，同时通过R(x,x)空间映射分析采样行为。

Result: 实验验证了估计器的有效性；发现引导权重显著改变信息传递表现；均值、方差与分辨率不能被单一权重联合校正；分辨率保真度与引导规则的理论合理性无直接关联。

Conclusion: 现有引导策略在重建中对分辨率的影响复杂且非直观，必须引入有效数据分辨率来准确评估数据驱动能力，避免对引导机制的误判。

Abstract: Guided diffusion samplers are increasingly used to reconstruct physical fields from sparse observations, but standard diagnostics do not say how much of the reconstruction was actually determined by the data. We introduce effective data resolution for black-box generative posteriors: a comparison between the resolution warranted by the inverse problem, $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{ref}}$, and the resolution realised by the sampler, $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{samp}}$. A perturbation estimator measures $\mathrm{dof}_{\mathrm{samp}}$ and the spatial map $R(x,x)$ from sampler queries alone. We validate the estimator against exact references and use it to study guided diffusion. The resulting measurements show that guidance weight can strongly alter apparent information transfer, that mean, spread and resolution are not jointly corrected by one weight even with an exact prior and score, and that resolution fidelity does not follow reliably from the apparent principledness of a guidance rule.

</details>


### [243] [On the Trade-off Between Information Loss and Generalization in Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04424)
*Zhongqi Fan,Zheng Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了稀疏注意力机制的詹森-香农（JS）散度和泛化差距，揭示了稀疏性在信息保真度与模型泛化能力之间的权衡。通过假设注意力分数为独立同分布的亚高斯随机变量，推导出全注意力与稀疏注意力分布间JS散度的闭式表达式，并基于Rademacher复杂度和互信息理论分别给出泛化界。结果表明，稀疏性虽降低假设类复杂度，但引入可量化的近似误差，为稀疏Transformer提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏Transformer虽在实践中表现良好，但其理论理解仍不清晰，尤其缺乏对稀疏化如何影响注意力的信息保真度以及该信息损失如何影响模型泛化行为的系统分析。

Method: 采用基于顺序统计的集中分析方法，研究注意力分数为独立同分布亚高斯随机变量时的截断质量α；通过詹森-香农散度量化全注意力与稀疏注意力之间的近似误差；利用Rademacher复杂度及互信息与熵、覆盖数相结合的方法，推导泛化边界。

Result: JS散度具有闭式解：log 2 + ((1 - alpha)/2) log(1 - alpha) - ((2 - alpha)/2) log(2 - alpha)；泛化界分别为O(gamma * sqrt(M/n) * (sqrt(log(3eL/M)) + sqrt(pi)/2)) 和 O(sqrt((M/(2n)) * (log(eL/M) + log(1 + 2/epsilon))))。稀疏性降低模型复杂度，但引入可量化的近似误差。

Conclusion: 本研究从理论上阐明了稀疏注意力中信息保真度与泛化性能之间的权衡关系，为稀疏Transformer的设计与优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: To mitigate the quadratic complexity bottleneck of the Transformer, sparse attention has emerged as a pivotal technology. Despite the extensive empirical success of sparse Transformers, the theoretical understanding of sparse attention remains fragmented. In particular, two fundamental questions remain unclear: (1) How does sparsification affect the information fidelity of attention mechanisms? (2) How does this information loss interact with the generalization behavior of the model? To bridge this gap, this paper proposes a systematic analysis of the Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence and of the generalization gap of sparse attention mechanisms. Specifically, we first characterize the approximation error via the JS divergence. Through an order-statistics-based concentration analysis of the truncation mass alpha --- where the attention scores are assumed to be independent and identically distributed sub-Gaussian random variables with parameter sigma --- the JS divergence between the full attention distribution and the sparse attention distribution is shown to admit the closed form log 2 + ((1 - alpha)/2) log(1 - alpha) - ((2 - alpha)/2) log(2 - alpha). Subsequently, we derive a generalization bound through Rademacher complexity, quantified by O(gamma * sqrt(M/n) * (sqrt(log(3eL/M)) + sqrt(pi)/2)). Furthermore, building on a mutual-information-based generalization bound together with an entropy and covering-number analysis of the sparse hypothesis class, we obtain the sparsity-dependent generalization bound O(sqrt((M/(2n)) * (log(eL/M) + log(1 + 2/epsilon)))). Our analysis shows that sparsity reduces the complexity of the considered hypothesis class while introducing approximation error that can be quantified by the JS divergence. These findings provide a theoretical characterization of the trade-off between information fidelity and generalization in sparse Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [244] [UnAct: Gradient-Free Unlearning via Targeted Activation Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04426)
*Saeed Abdul Muizz,Aayat Rafiq,Iqra Altaf Gillani,Janibul Bashir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UnAct is a gradient-free, retrain-free method for class unlearning that requires only forward passes over forget images. It scores and attenuates responsive connections in late layers without gradients, labels, or retained data, outperforming SSD and LFSSD—especially with scarce forget data—while maintaining high retain accuracy and reducing computational cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing retrain-free unlearning methods like SSD and LFSSD require backpropagation and full-dataset importance computation, which are inefficient and unstable when forgetting data is limited. This work addresses the challenge of efficient and stable unlearning with minimal forget data.

Method: UnAct performs forward passes on forget images, scores late-layer units by their activation responses, attenuates the most responsive connections, and iteratively repeats this process up to 20 rounds without using gradients, labels, or stored training data.

Result: On ResNet-18 with CIFAR datasets, UnAct matches or exceeds SSD and LFSSD performance when forgetting entire classes and remains stable even with very few forget images (e.g., 5). It achieves retain accuracy within 2.5 points of full retraining, while SSD and LFSSD drop up to 86 points. With 5 forget images on CIFAR-10, UnAct's error is 0.21 vs. 67 (LFSSD) and 90 (SSD). In ViT-B/16 transfer, UnAct is 19x faster than SSD and has lower error (11.5 vs. 33.7).

Conclusion: UnAct enables efficient, stable, and scalable class unlearning with minimal data and no gradients, offering significant advantages over existing methods—particularly in low-data scenarios—and is suitable for real-world deployment where fast, reliable deletion is needed.

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data from a trained model without retraining from scratch. Retrain-free methods such as Selective Synaptic Dampening (SSD) and its label-free variant LFSSD avoid full retraining but still require backpropagation and parameter importance computed over the entire dataset. We ask: what happens when a deletion request arrives with only a few images of the class to be forgotten? To answer this question, we introduce UnAct, a gradient-free class-unlearning method that needs only forward passes over the forget images. UnAct scores late-layer units by their responses, attenuates the most responsive connections, and repeats this for up to 20 rounds using no gradients, no labels, and no retained data. On ResNet-18 trained with CIFAR-10, CIFAR-20, and CIFAR-100, UnAct is competitive with SSD and LFSSD when forgetting entire classes and, unlike them, never collapses the network when forget data is scarce. On ResNet-18, across all tested sizes, UnAct's retain accuracy stays within 2.5 points of retraining, while SSD and LFSSD, at their full-class operating points, lose up to 86 points on some classes. With five forget images on CIFAR-10, UnAct's distance to retraining is 0.21 points, against 67 for LFSSD and 90 for SSD, and re-selecting SSD's threshold at each size with an oracle does not close the gap. In preliminary transfer to ViT-B/16, UnAct's distance to retraining is 11.5 against 33.7 for SSD, and a request is 19x faster than SSD when SSD computes its importance at request time. The code is available at https://github.com/abdulmuizz0903/UnAct

</details>


### [245] [COSMOS: Soft Mechanism Mixtures with Verifiable Routing for Long-Horizon PDE Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04427)
*Anupam Rawat,Manikandan Padmanaban,Jagabondhu Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COSMOS提出了一种基于机制级软路由的协作专家神经算子，通过四个持续活跃的专用模块和可学习门控实现物理过程的连续混合，支持运输、扩散、波动和反应等复合过程的联合建模。引入了一个具有已知混合权重的算子分解基准测试，验证了软路由相比硬路由的优越性。尽管在部分任务上表现优于传统FNO，但性能稳定性受种子影响，且学习到的路由与物理机制不完全对应，表明其优势在于组合精度而非机制识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实系统中的物理过程（如运输、扩散、波传播和反应）通常是同时发生的复合过程，而现有神经算子多采用单映射或稀疏的top-K路由，难以捕捉连续混合的特性，因此需要一种能够自然表达多过程协同作用的新型神经算子架构。

Method: 提出COSMOS框架，包含四个过程偏置的专用模块，通过可学习的软路由门进行连续混合，并使用小网络融合特征；共享粗粒度潜在网格，通过零初始化的全分辨率残差恢复细节信息；引入一个带有已知混合权重的真实轨迹基准以评估组合建模能力。

Result: 在20步滚动预测中，初始3种子实验显示对扩散-反应系统的性能提升，对纳维-斯托克斯方程保持相当，浅水方程表现较弱；11种子审计表明扩散-反应的增益不稳定，无法确立可靠优势。消融实验表明均匀路由或移除混合机制会显著降低稳定阶段的性能。在标注基准上，密集软路由误差比硬路由低2.2倍，使用真实生成权重可将误差降至0.034，揭示了学习门控的局限性。

Conclusion: COSMOS成功实现了对复合物理过程的连续混合建模，在组合精度上优于传统方法，但其学习到的路由机制与实际物理机制不一致，表明其核心优势在于准确模拟复合动态，而非识别具体机制类型。

Abstract: Neural operators offer an efficient alternative to classical PDE solvers, but most learn a monolithic map per equation family and discretization. Real systems are compositional: transport, diffusion, wave, and reaction processes can act simultaneously. Existing mixture-of-experts operators typically use sparse top-$K$ routing, although concurrent physics is naturally a blend rather than a discrete choice.
  We propose COSMOS (Cooperative Operator Specialists with Mechanism-level Operator Soft-routing), a soft mechanism-mixture neural operator. Four process-biased specialists remain active at every step and are continuously mixed by a learned gate, with their features fused by a small network. Specialists share a coarse latent grid, while a zero-initialized full-resolution residual restores detail lost through the bottleneck. We also introduce an operator-splitting compositional benchmark with known mixture weights $w^\star$ per trajectory.
  Against family-tuned FNO under 20-step rollouts, an initial 3-seed evaluation suggested gains on diffusion--reaction, parity on Navier--Stokes, and weaker shallow-water performance. An 11-seed audit showed that the diffusion--reaction gain was unstable, motivating caution in small-seed rollout comparisons and precluding a reliable accuracy-win claim on these families. Ablations show that uniform routing or removing the specialist mixture substantially degrades stable-regime rollouts. On the labeled benchmark, dense soft routing yields $2.2\times$ lower error than hard top-1 routing at identical fusion; using generator weights $w^\star$ at inference further lowers rollout error to $0.034$, diagnosing limitations of the learned gate. However, routing labels do not align with the specialists' intended mechanisms: COSMOS supports compositional accuracy, not mechanism identity.

</details>


### [246] [LocusRL: Diagnosing LLM Reward and Policy Interventions in Competitive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04441)
*Chengyu Luan,Bo Xin,Songyan Guo,Yuxiang Zuo,Ahmed Yazdan,Jiahang Li,Yicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LocusRL 是一个诊断框架，用于分析大语言模型在强化学习中的干预机制，通过对比奖励与策略、审计奖励判断、信号传递、优化目标和执行动作，揭示性能差异的可测试解释，并通过可执行规则和反事实重播验证修正方案。研究发现，相同表现可能源于不同学习机制，且奖励虽能影响学习但未必提升性能。在多种游戏环境中的实验表明，需追踪语言模型输出如何转化为学习信号和行动，才能准确评估其干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法仅依赖聚合性能，无法揭示语言模型干预背后的机制差异，可能导致对干预效果的误判。需要更深入的诊断工具来理解干预如何影响学习过程。

Method: 提出 LocusRL 框架，结合受控奖励-策略比较与多维度审计（奖励判断、信号传递、优化目标、执行动作），并使用可执行规则和反事实重播验证修正方案。

Result: 在 Connect Four 等任务中发现种子依赖的干预效果反转；证明奖励可影响学习但不提升性能；反事实重播验证了诊断出的动作错误修正；在不完美信息游戏中扩展验证有效性。

Conclusion: 评估大语言模型在强化学习中的干预必须追踪其输出如何转化为学习信号和动作，LocusRL 能将聚合结果转化为可行动诊断与可验证修正，提升干预评估的透明性与准确性。

Abstract: Large language models can intervene in reinforcement learning through both reward design and action selection, yet aggregate performance offers an incomplete account of what these interventions actually do. Similar returns can conceal different learning mechanisms, while plausible rewards can induce undesirable behavior. We introduce LocusRL, a diagnostic framework that connects controlled reward-policy comparisons with audits of reward judgments, signal delivery, optimization objectives, and executed actions. The framework traces performance differences to testable explanations and checks targeted corrections through executable rules and counterfactual replay.
  Across two evaluation batches covering ten Connect Four training seeds, we uncover seed-dependent reversals in intervention effects and show how tracing actual updates changes their interpretation: historical Qwen training operates through reward-weighted teacher-action likelihood. A separate matched three-seed reward-direction experiment distinguishes sensitivity to a learning signal from its usefulness. With terminal rewards held fixed, a sign-reversed dense oracle yields a 2.8% aggregate win rate, compared with 57.2% for terminal-only training and 46.7% for the positive dense oracle. Thus, a reward can strongly influence learning without improving performance. At the decision level, counterfactual replay verifies a winning correction to a diagnosed action error. Complementary experiments in Leduc and reward-validation studies in Goofspiel extend the analysis to imperfect-information settings, revealing how reference-label definitions and validation-data exposure affect intervention assessment.
  Together, these findings show why evaluating LLM interventions requires tracing how their outputs become learning signals and actions. LocusRL turns aggregate outcomes into actionable diagnoses and verifiable corrections.

</details>


### [247] [One-Step Generation via Riemannian Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04454)
*David Li,Chanhyuk Lee,Jaehoon Yoo,Nikita Gushchin,Floor Eijkelboom,Eric Moulines,Maxim Panov,Alexander Korotin,Seunghoon Hong,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在约束空间中使用几何感知的场来改进漂移模型和Wasserstein梯度流，以实现快速推理。通过在一个统一的训练框架下比较多种几何感知场，实验证明了不同任务下最优场的选择不同，强调了目标选择的实际重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对连续欧几里得空间（如图像空间），但在约束空间中，传统的加性更新可能导致粒子离开有效域或忽略其几何结构，因此需要开发更合适的更新机制。

Method: 提出并比较了几种在统一训练框架下的几何感知场，用于单步生成器，确保更新符合约束空间的几何特性。

Result: 在具有不同结构的数据上测试，获得了竞争性的单步生成结果；最佳性能的场因任务而异，说明目标选择对实际应用至关重要。

Conclusion: 几何感知场的选择对约束空间中的生成效果有显著影响，合理的目标设计对于提升生成质量至关重要。

Abstract: Recently, Drifting Models and Wasserstein Gradient Flows have attracted substantial attention because they move iterative distributional refinement to training and amortize it into a generator, enabling fast inference. However, existing formulations have been developed largely for continuous Euclidean domains, such as image spaces, where particles admit unconstrained additive updates. On constrained spaces, these updates can leave the valid domain or ignore its geometry, making them unsuitable targets for training. Recent work has adapted updates to these spaces, but has focused on particular fields or offered limited empirical comparison. We derive and compare several geometry-aware fields within a common training framework for one-step generators. We test the method on data with different structures and obtain competitive one-step results in each setting. The best-performing field varies by task, showing why the choice of objective matters in practice.

</details>


### [248] [Target-free Latent Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04467)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Yuxian Huang,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无目标的对抗训练框架，通过无监督方式生成语义多样化的对抗样本，以扩大有害行为的覆盖范围，从而提升大语言模型对越狱攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练方法生成的对抗样本通常导致同质化的有害行为，无法充分反映真实越狱攻击中多样的行为模式，限制了模型的安全性与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种无目标的对抗训练框架，通过放大和多样化模型隐空间中的行为级变化，无监督地生成语义丰富的对抗样本，以激发多样化的有害行为。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能有效诱发多样化的有害行为，显著缓解行为狭窄问题，提升模型对越狱攻击的防御能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入语义熵作为对抗行为多样性的度量，所提方法显著增强了模型在面对复杂越狱攻击时的安全对齐能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that induce harmful behaviors and circumvent safety alignment. To defend against such attacks, adversarial training paradigms have been proposed to first simulate failure modes and then train the model to correct them, yielding promising improvements in safety alignment. However, these methods typically construct adversarial samples either by encouraging fixed harmful target completions or by performing targeted activation ablation derived from fixed benign--harmful data pairs. As a result, the generated adversarial samples tend to induce homogeneous harmful behaviors that poorly reflect the diversity of behaviors elicited by real-world jailbreak attacks. This behavior-level narrowness fundamentally limits their robustness. To address this issue, we propose a target-free adversarial training framework that generates adversarial samples in an unsupervised manner. By amplifying and diversifying behavior-level shifts in the model's latent space, our approach produces semantically diverse adversarial samples that induce a wide range of harmful behaviors. This expanded behavioral coverage exposes more diverse failure modes and thereby improves safety alignment. To quantify this effect, we use semantic entropy as an output-level measure of adversarial behavioral diversity. Empirically, our method elicits diverse harmful behaviors in the target model, substantially mitigating behavioral narrowness and improving robustness to jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [249] [BARQ: Balanced Codebook Refinement for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04490)
*Chenhang Cui,Xu Xie,Linrui Xu,Xiaohao Liu,Xingyu Zhu,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BARQ提出一种基于平衡分配精炼的量化方法，通过熵正则化最优传输和加权重心更新，实现代码本中每个码字的均衡拟合，提升量化质量，在多个大语言模型上实现了更低的困惑度和更高的零样本准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码本的权重量化方法在拟合过程中存在码字分配不均的问题，导致部分码字更新不足，限制了代码本的有效利用，因此需要一种能均衡分配并优化代码本的方法以提高量化性能。

Method: 采用熵正则化最优传输计算权重块与码字之间的联合软分配，确保每个码字在解中具有相等的正拟合质量；通过赋权重心更新方式精炼码字，并证明该更新在固定分配下最小化拟合目标；最后丢弃软分配，使用优化后的代码本进行标准硬最近码字编码。

Result: 在多个大语言模型上，BARQ在相同比特预算下相比基线方法实现了更低的困惑度和更高的平均零样本准确率，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: BARQ通过平衡分配和代码本精炼有效提升了量化质量，为大语言模型的高效部署提供了更优的量化方案。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow in parameter count, model storage and parameter memory traffic have become major bottlenecks to efficient deployment. Codebook-based weight quantization reduces these costs, but imbalanced nearest-codeword assignments during fitting can leave some codewords insufficiently updated, limiting effective codebook utilization. To address this limitation, we propose Balanced Assignment Refinement for Quantization (BARQ), which improves quantization quality through balanced fitting of existing codebooks. Specifically, we first compute joint soft assignments between weight blocks and codewords through entropically regularized optimal transport with uniform marginals and curvature-weighted reconstruction costs, ensuring equal positive fitting mass for every codeword in the exact solution. We then refine the codewords through an assignment-weighted barycentric update, which we prove minimizes the fitting objective for fixed assignments. For finite Sinkhorn iterations, the implemented update retains this optimality provided all codeword masses exceed the denominator floor. Finally, we discard the soft assignments and use the refined codebook for standard hard nearest-codeword encoding, with our analysis establishing sufficient conditions for reducing hard-quantization distortion and evaluation loss. Across multiple LLMs, BARQ achieves lower perplexity and higher mean zero-shot accuracy than the evaluated baselines at comparable bit budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/chenhangcuisg-code/BARQ.

</details>


### [250] [Understanding Clustering in Slot Attention via Particle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04493)
*Vasudev Joy,Rajat Rasal,Avinash Kori,Anthea Monod,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过粒子动力学视角研究注意力机制，提出简化版槽注意力（SSA），一种无参数的变体，其动态与软k均值聚类相关，可解释对象中心表示的形成。在Pascal VOC数据集上，SSA性能接近原始槽注意力，表明无需学习神经网络组件即可实现高效对象中心分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有槽注意力虽能生成对象中心表示，但其内部机制因学习组件而模糊；需更清晰理解注意力动态如何促成聚类行为。

Method: 提出简化槽注意力（SSA），移除所有可学习参数，使其动力学与软k均值聚类直接关联，从而提供可解释的机制分析。

Result: SSA在Pascal VOC上表现与原始槽注意力相当，证明无需复杂神经网络组件亦可实现高质量对象中心分割。

Conclusion: 对象中心表示的形成可由基础注意力动力学解释，且不依赖深度学习组件，为理解注意力机制提供了更简洁、可解释的框架。

Abstract: Studying attention through the lens of interacting particle dynamics has shown how token clustering can emerge from the underlying dynamics. We extend this perspective to slot attention, a method for object-centric image segmentation and representation learning in which learned components obscure how much of the clustering behaviour is intrinsic to the attention dynamics. We therefore introduce simplified slot attention (SSA), a parameter-free variant whose dynamics are connected to soft $k$-means clustering and which provides a straightforward mechanistic explanation for the emergence of object-centric representations. On the Pascal VOC dataset, SSA achieves performance comparable to that of slot attention, demonstrating that competitive object-centric segmentation can be achieved without learned neural-network components.

</details>


### [251] [LoRA's Second Descent Extends Beyond Parameter Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04507)
*Yueran Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了低秩适应（LoRA）中适配器秩与双下降现象之间的关系，通过模块匹配的秩扫描（MMRS）在四个视觉主干网络和一个7B语言模型上进行实验。结果显示，在存在标签噪声的情况下，秩为1时风险最低，随着秩增加导致过拟合噪声标签而出现风险陡增的“插值悬崖”，随后风险再次下降，但所有仍节省参数的后峰值秩均高于密集微调的风险。秩为1的LoRA在三个视觉主干网络上优于密集微调，表明小秩具有强正则化作用。尽管LoRA表现出第二次下降，但只有当失去参数优势后（如DeiT-Tiny在4倍于密集投影权重时）才能达到密集微调的风险水平。代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低秩适应（LoRA）中适配器秩与双下降现象的关系，理解其在不同模型和噪声条件下的表现，特别是参数效率与泛化性能之间的权衡。

Method: 采用模块匹配的秩扫描（MMRS），系统性地测试不同秩的LoRA，并与相同模块的密集微调进行对比，所有实验均按种子配对以保证可比性。实验覆盖四个视觉主干网络和一个7B语言模型，引入标签噪声以观察双下降行为。

Result: 在存在标签噪声的情况下，秩为1的LoRA风险最低，随后因过拟合噪声标签而出现风险陡升；之后风险下降，但所有仍节省参数的后峰值秩均高于密集微调风险。秩为1的LoRA在三个视觉主干网络上优于密集微调，表明小秩具有强正则化效果。仅当损失参数优势后（如DeiT-Tiny在4倍密集权重时），LoRA风险才降至密集微调水平。

Conclusion: LoRA表现出双下降现象，但其第二次下降仅在放弃参数效率后实现，说明小秩带来的正则化优势是关键，而参数节省并非总是带来更好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Double descent has sparked considerable interest, with recent work relating it to the data, the model and the learning configuration. Practical fine-tuning commonly involves training a small adapter on top of frozen pretrained weights, as in low-rank adaptation (LoRA). The adapter's rank is the hyperparameter that sets its capacity, yet how this rank relates to double descent has not been well explored. We quantify this relation under label noise on four vision backbones and a 7B language model with a module-matched rank sweep (MMRS), which extends past full rank and compares every rank with dense fine-tuning of the same modules, paired by seed. On DeiT-Tiny, risk is lowest at rank one and rises sharply as the adapter becomes able to fit the noisy labels, forming an interpolation cliff. Past the peak, risk falls again, but every tested post-peak rank that still saves parameters remains above dense risk. Rank-one LoRA outperforms dense fine-tuning on three of the four vision backbones, consistent with strong regularization at small rank. LoRA thus exhibits a second descent, but matches dense risk only after losing its parameter advantage, first on DeiT-Tiny at four times dense's projection weights. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/lora-second-descent-C7B5.

</details>


### [252] [Length Generalization Needs Proper Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04518)
*Pavlo Vasylenko,Matthias Lindemann,André F. T. Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序列模型在未见上下文长度上的泛化能力，发现长度泛化不仅受架构设计（如位置编码、注意力机制）影响，还与训练过程密切相关。研究揭示权重衰减会阻碍外推性能，而重新设计的丢弃（dropout）策略则能显著提升外推能力。特别是将丢弃置于线性投影之前而非层归一化前，可解决分布不匹配问题。通过改进的SmolLM3和Mamba2模型验证了该方法的有效性，且提出的新方法VPAD进一步减少了预激活方差失配，显著提升了变换器模型的长度外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何提升序列模型在超出训练上下文长度范围时的表现，即长度泛化能力，尤其关注训练过程中的正则化技术对这一能力的影响。

Method: 系统分析权重衰减与丢弃在不同位置对长度泛化的影响；提出新的丢弃放置策略及变体——方差保持仿射丢弃（VPAD），以缓解预激活阶段的分布失配问题。

Result: 改进后的模型在多个基准测试中表现出色，包括在Needle-in-a-Haystack任务上实现64倍外推完美表现，在RULER和HELMET任务上也远超预训练上下文长度；表明该方法具有架构无关性，适用于Transformer和Mamba等模型。

Conclusion: 训练过程中的正则化策略，尤其是丢弃的位置和形式，对长度泛化有决定性影响。优化丢弃策略并引入方差保持机制，是实现高效长度外推的关键。

Abstract: Length generalization is the ability of sequential models to perform well on context lengths unseen during training. In this work, we show that the challenge of achieving length generalization is related not only to architectural choices such as positional encoding and the attention mechanism but also to the training procedure itself. We study how regularization affects length generalization and find that weight decay can hinder extrapolation. In contrast, dropout improves extrapolation when its placement within the architecture is reconsidered. In that regard, we show that the standard placement of dropout before layer normalization introduces a systematic distributional mismatch, and that applying dropout just before the linear projection resolves this issue. For example, a modified SmolLM3 with sliding window attention, continually pre-trained with dropout, can extrapolate perfectly to 64$\times$ on Needle-in-a-Haystack and far beyond the pre-training context size on RULER and HELMET. Mamba2 also benefits from dropout, suggesting an architecture-agnostic nature of the problem. We further propose Variance-Preserving Affine Dropout (VPAD), a new dropout strategy that substantially reduces the resulting pre-activation variance mismatch, leading to further extrapolation improvements in transformers.

</details>


### [253] [Proximal Causal Learning under Unmeasured Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04519)
*Ying Tang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PCL-U 是一种新框架，可从观测协变量中直接学习代理变量，以解决未测量混杂问题。它利用神经编码器分解协变量为治疗诱导、结果诱导和共享代理变量，并通过极小极大互信息目标进行指导，使用基于矩的实用风险函数获得因果估计。在基准测试和合成数据集上，PCL-U 的表现与现有方法相当或更优，且在不同维度和混杂强度下保持稳定的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖于预定义的代理变量，但在实践中难以获取；因此需要一种能够自动学习代理变量的方法来应对未测量混杂问题。

Method: PCL-U 使用神经编码器将协变量分解为三类：治疗诱导、结果诱导和共享代理变量，通过极小极大互信息目标进行优化，并采用基于矩的风险函数进行因果估计。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和合成数据集上，PCL-U 的因果估计表现优于或等同于现有方法，且在不同维度和混杂强度下具有稳定的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PCL-U 有效解决了未测量混杂问题，无需预先指定代理变量，具备良好的泛化能力与稳定性，是处理观测数据中因果推断的有效工具。

Abstract: Estimating treatment effects from observational data typically relies on the No Unmeasured Confounding Assumption (NUCA), which rarely holds in practice. Proximal causal learning (PCL) addresses unmeasured confounding via proxy variables, yet existing methods require the proxy variables to be pre-specified. Thus, we propose PCL-U, a framework that learns proxy variables directly from observed covariates. PCL-U uses neural encoders to decompose covariates into treatment-inducing, outcome-inducing, and shared proxies, guided by minimax mutual information objectives, and obtains causal estimates through a practical moment-based risk function. Experiments on benchmarks show that PCL-U matches or outperforms existing baselines. Besides, there are two types of synthetic datasets with varying dimensions and confounding strengths that illustrate that our method maintains stable estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [254] [Weight Decay and Neuron Condensation: A Three-Stage Analysis of Two-Layer ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04533)
*Cheng Xu,Pengxiao Lin,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了权重衰减在神经网络训练中对神经元压缩（参数方向对齐）的作用。通过分析两层ReLU网络的训练动态，揭示了三个阶段：快速拟合、幅度压缩和神经元压缩。权重衰减在快速拟合阶段维持残差的准静态平衡，而在幅度压缩早期，核衰减放大残差相关场，其孤立吸引极值点成为压缩方向的候选。当神经元幅度稳定后，这些极值点的漂移被约束，神经元方向逐渐收缩至其附近，最终实现神经元压缩。该研究从动力学角度阐明了权重衰减如何促进紧凑表示，而不仅仅是减小参数范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解权重衰减在神经元压缩中的作用机制，现有研究未能明确其在参数方向对齐方面的动态影响。

Method: 基于两层ReLU网络，通过分析残差相关场，划分训练过程为三个阶段，并利用数学工具分析各阶段的动力学行为。

Result: 揭示了权重衰减通过放大残差相关场并引导神经元方向向吸引极值点收缩，从而促进神经元压缩；该过程超越了单纯降低参数范数的效果。

Conclusion: 权重衰减不仅控制参数规模，还通过调控残差相关场的演化，驱动神经元方向收敛，实现紧凑表示，为理解深度学习中的隐式正则化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularization technique in neural network training, yet its role in neuron condensation (parameter direction alignment) remains unclear. Starting from a parameter initialization in the neural tangent kernel regime, we characterize training dynamics under weight decay through three stages: rapid fitting, amplitude compression, and neuron condensation. Using a two-layer ReLU network, we analyze a residual correlation field that governs both neuron amplitudes and directions. During rapid fitting, the residual approaches a quasi-static equilibrium maintained by weight decay while the tangent kernel remains nearly unchanged. In the early stage of amplitude compression, kernel decay amplifies the residual correlation field, whose isolated attracting extrema provide candidates for condensation directions. As neuron amplitudes stabilize, we bound the drift of attracting extrema and demonstrate contraction of neuron directions around them, leading to neuron condensation. This staged analysis provides a dynamical understanding of how weight decay promotes a condensed representation, beyond reducing parameter norms.

</details>


### [255] [PhaseGate: Phase-Aware CPU Retrieval Scheduling for On-Device LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04537)
*Seoyoon Yum,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PHASEGATE通过检测LLM推理阶段来动态调节CPU检索的并发度，显著提升检索吞吐量并控制延迟。在多任务负载下，相比固定策略，其在保持低延迟的同时将检索吞吐量提高2倍；而忽略阶段信息的TimeGate虽有相似吞吐量，但频繁违反延迟约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一内存系统中，GPU-based LLM推理与CPU检索共存时，高并发检索会显著增加解码延迟，尤其在本地检索饱和场景下。现有方法缺乏对LLM推理阶段的感知，导致资源分配不合理，影响整体性能和响应延迟。因此需要一种能根据推理阶段动态调整检索并发度的机制。

Method: 提出PHASEGATE，利用LLM推理阶段（prefill或decode）作为准入信号，为预填充和解码阶段分别设置独立的并发上限。通过在受控负载下校准得到最优并发限制（如base-M4配置下为4个prefill和1个decode），实现高效资源调度。同时设计了相位盲区对照方案TimeGate以验证阶段感知的重要性。

Result: 在七次独立测试中，PHASEGATE使聚合检索吞吐量达到最佳固定策略的2.0倍，且95%分位解码延迟和预填充延迟均不超过无检索基线的1.25倍。相比之下，TimeGate虽有相近吞吐量，但在所有运行中均超出输出令牌延迟上限。M2/M2 Pro设备复现了相同趋势，且随着解码占比上升，优势逐渐缩小。

Conclusion: LLM推理阶段是有效的资源调度信号。基于阶段感知的动态并发控制（如PHASEGATE）可显著提升系统吞吐量，同时严格保障延迟指标，优于传统固定策略和相位盲区方法。

Abstract: On-device assistants run GPU-based LLM inference alongside CPU retrieval on unified-memory systems. Under a saturated local-retrieval workload, four concurrent retrieval workers raise 95th-percentile (p95) decode latency by 60-61% on two M4 systems, whereas prefill latency rises by only 5.7-6.9%. We study LLM phase as an admission signal for independent CPU retrieval under controlled LLM workloads. PHASEGATE calibrates separate concurrency limits for prefill and decode, selecting four and one on our base-M4 configuration. Under a backlogged queue, it achieves 2.0 times the aggregate retrieval throughput of the best tested feasible fixed policy, with both p95 LLM latency metrics within 1.25 times their no-retrieval baselines in all seven held-out runs. A phase-blind control, TimeGate, uses the same two limits on a calibration-derived schedule without observing LLM phase. It achieves similar retrieval throughput but violates the output-token latency limit in every run. M2 and M2 Pro Mac minis reproduce the policy ordering, while output-length sweeps show that the advantage narrows as decode occupies more of each request.

</details>


### [256] [TMAML: Temporal Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning for Cold-Start Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04547)
*Wannes Janssens,Matthias Bogaert,Dirk Van den Poel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TMAML，一种针对冷启动时间序列预测的元学习方法，通过构建时序一致的支持-查询窗口，在少量历史数据下实现快速适应和更好的泛化能力。在TFT模型上验证，表现优于或相当标准ERM训练的TFT，且在两个场景中校准性更优，但概率预测准确性未持续领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动预测面临历史数据极少的问题，现有全局模型虽能利用多序列共享信息，但未专门优化于未见序列的泛化与短历史适应。需要一种能在少量数据下快速学习并借助相关序列信息的方法。

Method: 将冷启动预测建模为小窗口学习问题，引入时间模型无关元学习（TMAML），构造时序一致的支持-查询窗口，并设计时间元训练与元测试流程，使模型专为冷启动预测优化。

Result: 在三个冷启动场景中，TMAML始终优于或等同于标准ERM训练的TFT；在两个场景中提供更优的校准预测；但在概率预测准确性上未持续超越基准方法。

Conclusion: TMAML有效提升了冷启动时间序列预测的性能与校准性，尤其在数据稀缺条件下具备优势，是应对冷启动挑战的有效方法。

Abstract: Cold-start forecasting, the task of forecasting a time series with little to no historical data, is a common challenge. Addressing it requires approaches that learn quickly from few datapoints and leverage information from related series, typically through static covariates, to generalize well to unseen series. While some global forecasting models can generate cold-start predictions by leveraging information shared across multiple series, they are not optimized for out-of-train-set generalization or adaptation from short histories. In this work, we formulate cold-start forecasting as a few-window learning problem and introduce Temporal Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (TMAML), which tailors the model-agnostic meta-learning algorithm, originally developed for few-shot adaptation of neural networks, to deep time series forecasting. TMAML constructs meta-tasks as temporally consistent support-query windows and pairs them with a temporal meta-training and meta-testing procedure, yielding forecasting models that are explicitly optimized for cold-start forecasting. We instantiate TMAML on the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) and present an initial empirical analysis of forecast accuracy and calibration across three cold-start scenarios: TMAML consistently outperforms or matches a standard ERM-trained TFT, yields better-calibrated forecasts than naive on two of the three scenarios, but does not consistently outperform naive on probabilistic forecast accuracy.

</details>


### [257] [Coupling Noisy Pairwise Knowledge to the DAG Posterior for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04559)
*Guoliang Xu,James E Corter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-NoisyKG 是一种结合观测数据与来自大语言模型等来源的重复因果报告的贝叶斯框架，通过特征条件化的 Beta 先验和共享错误矩阵建模报告可靠性与系统性错误。交替推断利用图后验优化可靠性估计，进而影响图结构更新。该方法在离散和连续数据上均显著提升结构学习性能，降低 SHD 和报告对数损失，优于基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部因果报告的可靠性不一致，且在有限观测下难以有效利用，需一种能融合多源报告并自适应评估其可信度的结构学习方法。

Method: 提出 HB-NoisyKG 框架，采用特征条件化 Beta 先验聚合报告可靠性，引入共享错误矩阵捕捉系统性偏差，通过交替推断迭代优化图结构与报告可靠性估计，并支持离散与连续数据的统一似然计算。

Result: 在五个离散基准上，平均 SHD 从 22.39 降至 16.06；在物理光隧道数据上，SHD 从 39.00 降至 27.30；在连续 Sachs 数据上，AUROC 提升 0.121；合成实验显示持续更新可降低可靠性估计误差与报告对数损失。

Conclusion: HB-NoisyKG 能有效整合不可靠但重复的外部因果报告，在有限观测下显著提升结构学习精度，且具备良好的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: External causal reports can improve structure learning from limited observations, but their reliability varies across sources and variable pairs. We introduce HB-NoisyKG, a Bayesian framework that combines observational data with repeated causal reports from sources such as large language models. Each report is a noisy observation of a direct pair state implied by one DAG. A feature-conditioned Beta prior pools information about pair reliability, and a shared error matrix captures systematic mistakes. Alternating inference uses the graph posterior to refine reliability estimates, which determine how reports influence subsequent graph updates. The report likelihood uses only graph pair-state marginals, so the same observation layer supports discrete and continuous likelihoods in graph-only and joint inference. Against an 80-restart no-KG baseline, HB uses at most 80 total restarts and lowers mean SHD from 22.39 to 16.06 on five discrete benchmarks. On a physical light tunnel with random variable IDs and retained descriptions, HB lowers SHD from 39.00 for no-KG to 27.30. On continuous Sachs, graph-only BGe raises AUROC by 0.121 over no-KG Top-K. In a controlled synthetic study, continued updating also reduces mean reliability estimation error and held-out report log loss compared with one-time estimation.

</details>


### [258] [Only Project Once: Projection-Adaptive Loss for Exact Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04572)
*Tim Aebersold,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为投影自适应损失（PAL）的新方法，通过单次独立的投影步骤实现精确约束满足，显著提升训练效率并保持对复杂非线性约束的高可行性。相比传统多步修复方法，PAL在计算和内存上更高效，且在约束难以评估时仍可适用，实验表明其在多个基准测试中表现优异，训练速度比主流方法快2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖多步修复过程进行约束满足，导致计算和内存开销大，且在修复不收敛时鲁棒性差，同时将大部分约束满足任务交由修复模块完成。因此需要一种更高效、更稳健的替代方案。

Method: 提出投影自适应损失（PAL），在训练中仅使用一次脱离主网络的投影步骤，并利用该步骤后的约束残差自适应调整原始预测的约束惩罚权重。

Result: PAL在极端非线性约束下保持近乎完美的可行性，在合成与工程基准测试中表现优于或匹配现有方法；在ACOPF基准上训练速度比DC3快2.5倍；可在约束评估成本高的场景（如使用神经代理）中训练，而传统方法因内存限制无法应用。

Conclusion: 单次分离投影结合自适应损失足以实现高效且可靠的约束满足，PAL是一种高效、鲁棒且适用于复杂场景的新型约束修复方法。

Abstract: Precise constraint satisfaction is a prerequisite to deploying learned models in many areas, motivating methods that repair raw neural predictions with a repair procedure. Current methods unroll multiple repair steps in training and softly penalize constraint violations that remain after the unroll. This is compute- and memory-intensive, lacks robustness when the repair fails to converge, and surrenders most of the constraint satisfaction work to the repair. Our central finding is that, contrary to common practice, a single detached projection step suffices in training. We accomplish this with a Projection-Adaptive Loss (PAL), which uses the constraint residual after this single step to adaptively weigh constraint penalties on the raw prediction. In experiments, PAL is the only method that retains virtually perfect feasibility on extremely nonlinear constraints, and matches or outperforms current methods on synthetic and engineering benchmarks. Because it only requires a single detached projection step, PAL trains 2.5x faster than the canonical repair-based method (DC3) on its own ACOPF benchmark. PAL can also be trained when constraints are expensive to evaluate (e.g., via neural surrogates), a setting where current unrolled methods are memory-intractable.

</details>


### [259] [DiffGate: Difficulty-Gated Teacher Guidance for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04596)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffGate提出一种结合GRPO与选择性、有限教师指导的成果门控目标，通过验证器决定哪些轨迹接受教师指导，并在这些轨迹内提供密集的词元级更新方向，有效弥补了OPD和RLVR各自的盲点。在代码和数学任务上，DiffGate显著提升pass@8指标，同时保持avg@8稳定，整体表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-policy distillation（OPD）方法为局部词元级监督，缺乏对轨迹层面结果的考量；而强化学习带可验证奖励（RLVR）如GRPO虽能提供结果级监督，但面临奖励稀疏和信用分配粗糙的问题。两者存在互补性盲区，亟需融合以实现更优的模型训练效果。

Method: 提出DiffGate，利用验证器判断哪些轨迹失败，仅对失败轨迹施加教师指导，且指导强度按组难度调整并平滑限制，避免极端差异主导优化过程，从而实现教师指导与结果反馈的有效协同。

Result: 在Qwen3-0.6B和Qwen3-1.7B模型上，DiffGate相比匹配的GRPO，在代码任务中分别提升avg@8 1.7和1.8点，pass@8提升1.6和5.7点；在数学任务中avg@8与GRPO相差不超过0.5点，pass@8提升1.1和3.9点。所有四组设置下pass@8均有所改善，表明解决方案覆盖率更高。

Conclusion: DiffGate通过动态整合教师引导与结果验证信号，实现了对失败轨迹的精准干预与高效优化，在多个任务和模型规模上均展现出优越性能，是提升大语言模型推理能力的有效方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely used paradigm for post-training large language models, reducing the train--test mismatch of conventional distillation by supervising the student on its own generated trajectories. However, existing OPD objectives remain largely token-local and outcome-agnostic, optimizing teacher--student agreement at each prefix despite reasoning quality being determined at the trajectory level. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), provides complementary outcome-level supervision but suffers from sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment. We show that OPD and RLVR exhibit complementary blind spots: teacher signals provide dense local guidance but are weakly aligned with rollout correctness, whereas group-relative rewards capture task success but provide coarse token-level credit and vanish on all-failure groups. We introduce DiffGate, an outcome-gated objective that combines GRPO with selective, bounded teacher guidance. Teacher supervision is applied only to failed trajectories, scaled by group difficulty, and smoothly bounded to prevent extreme teacher--student discrepancies from dominating optimization. The verifier therefore determines \emph{which trajectories} receive teacher guidance, while the teacher provides dense token-level update directions within those trajectories. Across Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B students, DiffGate improves code avg@8 over matched GRPO by $+1.7$ and $+1.8$ points and pass@8 by $+1.6$ and $+5.7$ points, respectively. On mathematics, avg@8 remains within $0.5$ points of GRPO while pass@8 improves by $+1.1$ and $+3.9$ points. Overall, DiffGate improves pass@8 across all four model--domain settings, demonstrating improved solution coverage under our evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [260] [Asymptotically Optimal Best Arm Identification with Fixed-Budget under Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04600)
*Keqin Chen,Jie Bian,Yulian Wu,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在纯ε-差分隐私约束下，固定预算的最优臂识别问题，提出一种名为AO-Pri-BAI的自适应算法，通过拉普拉斯树机制保持私有运行估计，并通过极小极大交互学习采样设计。该算法满足纯ε-差分隐私，且其失败概率的指数率与隐私感知基准一致，在非渐近设置下也优于基准算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保证统计效率的同时实现差分隐私保护，解决固定预算下最优臂识别中的隐私-效率权衡问题。

Method: 提出AO-Pri-BAI算法，结合拉普拉斯树机制进行私有估计维护，并通过极小极大优化确定采样策略，以应对隐私敏感的运输指数挑战。

Result: AO-Pri-BAI算法满足纯ε-差分隐私，其失败概率的指数衰减率达到隐私感知理论上限，在多种实例上表现出色，包括非渐近场景。

Conclusion: AO-Pri-BAI在理论上实现了最优的隐私-效率平衡，实验验证了其在实际应用中的优越性能，为差分隐私下的最优臂识别提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Best arm identification under differential privacy is a pure-exploration problem in which both statistical efficiency and privacy protection must be achieved simultaneously. We study fixed-budget best arm identification for bandits under pure $ε$-differential privacy, where the learner must recommend an arm after a prescribed sampling budget while protecting the full transcript. We prove that the optimal exponential decay rate of the error probability is upper bounded by an instance-dependent privacy-aware transportation exponent that differs from the analogous quantity used to characterize the stopping time in fixed-confidence analysis by Jourdan and Azize [2025]. Guided by this exponent, we propose AO-Pri-BAI, an adaptive algorithm that maintains private running estimates through Laplace-tree mechanisms and learns a sampling design through a min--max interaction between hard alternatives and arm allocations. We prove that AO-Pri-BAI satisfies pure $ε$-differential privacy. We also establish that the exponent of the failure probability of AO-Pri-BAI matches the privacy-aware benchmark. Numerical studies show that even in the non-asymptotic setting, AO-Pri-BAI outperforms benchmark algorithms on various instances, complementing the theoretical analyses.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Training Numerical Intelligence via Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03872)
*Peter Chen,Wotao Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADSD 是一个将数值诊断与可重用的求解器自我改进相结合的框架，通过诊断问题根源并指导发现合适的数值方法，实现求解器的结构化改进。在四个挑战性领域中显著提升了求解器的精度、鲁棒性和效率，例如在 GOC-500 功率流问题上将平均误差降低近 71 倍，并具有跨拓扑和运行条件的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成科学代码的能力虽强，但难以提升底层算法性能；执行反馈虽能暴露性能问题，却无法揭示根本原因及改进方向。因此需要一种系统性方法来实现求解器的自诊断与自优化。

Method: 提出 ADSD 框架，采用‘诊断优先’范式：先分析求解器性能差的原因，再基于诊断结果指导数值方法的发现，最终将知识封装为可复用的求解器技能，实现从试错编辑到结构化改进的转变。

Result: 在功率流方程、交流最优功率流控制、刚性常微分方程和异质扩散偏微分方程等四个复杂领域中，ADSD 持续提升了求解器的准确性、鲁棒性和效率；在 GOC-500 上，平均误差降低近 71 倍，且改进效果可迁移至未见过的电网拓扑和运行状态。

Conclusion: ADSD 成功将求解器改进从经验试错转变为基于诊断的系统性过程，实现了性能的显著提升与跨场景泛化，为智能科学计算提供了新范式。

Abstract: AI agents are becoming increasingly capable of generating scientific code, but generating code is not the same as improving the algorithms behind it. For numerical solvers, execution feedback can expose poor performance, but rarely reveals its underlying cause and how to address it. We introduce Auto-Diagnosis and Skill Discovery (ADSD), a framework that links numerical diagnosis to reusable solver self-improvement. ADSD follows a diagnosis-first paradigm that first explains why a solver performs poorly, then uses this diagnosis to guide the discovery of appropriate numerical methods. The resulting knowledge is packaged into reusable solver skills, turning solver improvement from trial-and-error editing into a structured process of diagnosis, discovery, and implementation. Across four challenging numerical domains--power flow equation, AC optimal power flow control, stiff ordinary differential equations, and heterogeneous diffusion PDEs--ADSD consistently improves solver accuracy, robustness, and efficiency. On GOC-500 power flow, for example, ADSD reduces mean solver error by nearly $71\times$, with improvements further transferring to unseen grid topologies and operating regimes.

</details>


### [262] [REACT: Physically and Chemically Consistent Reconstruction of Marine Active Tracers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03888)
*Wenbin Dai,Hao Zheng,Shiyu Liang,Chaofan Sun,Xueying Zhang,Hanbo Huang,Xuan Gong,Yiran Zhang,Enhui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REACT是一种基于碳先验的海表pH重建框架，通过解耦传输、主动修正和化学解码过程，有效解决传统方法在处理活性碳酸盐示踪物时的不一致问题。它在模拟数据上显著降低pH的归一化均方根误差（NRMSE）和化学一致性误差，且在跨时间尺度评估中表现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统海洋酸化监测与碳循环研究需要从稀疏观测中重建全球海表pH，但现有物理模型计算成本高，而黑箱或物理引导型AI模型多适用于被动示踪物，难以处理pH作为活性示踪物、DIC为守恒量的复杂情况，易导致化学不一致。

Method: REACT采用碳先验设计：利用保守平流-扩散求解器传输潜在碳酸盐状态，通过源模块捕捉非守恒碳循环变化，再将修正后的状态解码为pH，并通过碳酸盐平衡约束输出，确保底层碳状态的一致性。

Result: 在模拟数据上，REACT相比最佳基线将pH NRMSE降低14.7%，化学一致性误差降低24.0%；跨时间尺度评估显示其对误差累积具有鲁棒性，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: REACT通过碳先验框架实现了高精度、化学一致的海表pH重建，为海洋酸化监测与碳循环研究提供了高效且可靠的工具。

Abstract: Reconstructing global sea surface pH from sparse observations is critical for monitoring ocean acidification and understanding marine carbon cycling. Traditional assimilation and inverse models are physically grounded but costly for large-scale reconstruction. Recent black-box and physics-guided AI models improve efficiency, but are mainly designed for passive tracers, where the reconstructed variable is also the transported inventory. In contrast, pH is an active carbonate tracer: it is the prediction target, while dissolved inorganic carbon (DIC) is the conserved carbon inventory. This mismatch can produce low pH error while violating carbonate closure and source-free carbon conservation. To address this, we introduce \textbf{REACT}, a carbon-first reconstruction framework that decouples transport, active correction, and chemical decoding. REACT transports a latent carbonate state with a conservative advection--diffusion solver, captures non-conservative carbon-cycle variations with a source module, decodes the corrected state into pH, and constrains the output through carbonate equilibrium. This design keeps pH as the target while enforcing consistency on the underlying carbon state. On simulation data, REACT reduces pH NRMSE by (14.7%) and chemical consistency error by (24.0%) over the best baseline. Cross-temporal-scale evaluations show robustness against error accumulation from coarse to fine temporal scales, and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component.

</details>


### [263] [Proxy Confidence: Auditing Black-Box LLM Agents with a Surrogate's Log-Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03894)
*Yikai Zhao,Saurabh Pandey,Pradeep Kumar Misra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种低成本的开源替代模型并行运行方案，用于检测部署中的大语言模型（LLM）代理在工具调用、查询和代码生成中的潜在错误。由于前沿聊天API隐藏了模型的概率信息，且代理的置信度在关键错误上仅略高于随机水平，传统重采样无效（因模型高度重复）。本文通过一个与主代理并行运行的开源小模型，基于相同的上下文、模式和提议动作，利用多种互补读出方式（如教师强制、请求PMI、判别性判断、工具选择竞争）对调用进行评分。该方法无需训练、不依赖代理内部结构，仅需一次预填充计算开销。在复杂编码任务中，其性能达到AUROC 0.825，显著优于代理自身置信度（0.598），且在多个代理上表现提升0.07至0.28。相比自一致性方法，在近确定性代理上提升0.14至0.19，成本仅为1/K。该信号支持两种部署模式：实时门控机制可提升最少可信调用的审查率（准确率提升0.05至0.30，覆盖50%），以及置信度反馈机制使代理能根据得分调整下一步行动，显著提升实际执行任务的成功率（+0.119和+0.137，p≤1e-4），并优于随机值控制组（+0.078，p=0.003）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型代理在部署时会产生无声错误，而前沿聊天接口隐藏了模型概率信息，导致代理的置信度在关键错误上几乎无区分能力；同时，模型高度重复，重采样无法有效纠错。因此亟需一种低成本、无需访问代理内部、不依赖训练的方法来恢复缺失的置信信号，以及时发现并纠正错误。

Method: 引入一个低代价的开源权重替代模型，与主代理并行运行。该模型接收相同上下文、模式和提议动作，通过四种互补读出方式评估工具调用：教师强制与请求PMI用于评估参数值的合理性，判别性判断评估调用整体正确性，工具选择竞争对比函数与其兄弟函数的表现。采用‘生成似然局部定位错误参数值，判别判断捕捉整体错误’的原则进行决策。当错误类型未知时，使用集成策略作为低风险默认方案。整个过程无需训练，不接触代理内部，仅需一次预填充计算。

Result: 在困难编码任务中，该方法的AUROC达0.825，远超代理自身置信度的0.598；在三个其他代理上，生成式读出结果提升0.07至0.28。相较于自一致性方法，在近确定性代理上提升0.14至0.19，但成本仅为1/K。在部署层面，实时门控机制使接受动作准确率提升0.05至0.30（50%覆盖率），置信度反馈机制显著提升任务成功率（+0.119和+0.137，p≤1e-4），优于随机值控制组（+0.078，p=0.003）。

Conclusion: 本方法通过并行运行开源替代模型，有效恢复了前沿大模型代理中缺失的置信信号，具备高性价比、无需训练、无需访问内部结构等优势，能够显著提升工具调用的可靠性与任务成功率，适用于实时纠错与自主适应的部署场景。

Abstract: A deployed LLM agent emits tool calls, queries, and code that can be silently wrong -- by the time the error surfaces, the action has run. Frontier chat APIs hide the model's token probabilities; the agent's stated confidence barely beats chance on the mistakes that matter; and resampling does not help, since frontier models are highly repetitive, reproducing the same call across samples.
  We recover the missing signal from a low-cost open-weight surrogate run in parallel. It reads the same context, schema, and proposed action as the agent, then scores the call from its own log-probabilities through a family of complementary readouts: teacher forcing and request-PMI weigh the likelihood of each argument value, a discriminative verdict judges the call as a whole, and tool-choice competition tests the function against its siblings. One principle says which to trust: a generative likelihood localizes wrong argument values, while the verdict catches holistically wrong calls. When the error type is unknown, an ensemble is the low-regret default. The readout is training-free, needs no access to the agent's internals, and costs one prefill pass alongside the tool call.
  On difficult coding tasks it reaches AUROC 0.825 where the actor's stated confidence is near chance (0.598), and the generative readouts beat it by +0.07 to +0.28 across three further actors. Against self-consistency it gains +0.14 to +0.19 on near-deterministic actors, at 1/K the cost. The signal drives two deployment modes: a real-time gate escalating the least-trustworthy calls for review (+0.05 to +0.30 accepted-action accuracy at 50% coverage), and confidence feedback, returning the tool result with the score so the agent adapts its next step -- lifting task success on live-execution benchmarks (+0.119 and +0.137, p <= 1e-4) and beating a random-value control where step errors are silent (+0.078, p = 0.003).

</details>


### [264] [SGAnalog: An End-to-End Circuit Benchmark from Open-Source Silicon Tapeouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03934)
*Yueting Li,Weihang Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于人类设计的开源电路的新基准，用于评估模拟集成电路设计模型的可迁移技能和在特定工艺与测试条件下的有效性。该基准包含273个拓扑各异的顶层设计，所有源文件均在固定容器环境中处理，确保一致性。通过提取原理图图像和SPICE网表，提供精确的结构参考，并利用提交时间进行训练截止分析，作者测试平台则提供仿真上下文。实验涵盖66个转录任务和17个器件尺寸分配任务。最强模型在转录任务中达到56.1%的精确图同构率，多数模型在中等规模任务上性能显著下降；移除人工标签虽降低精确匹配率但保持整体结构F1，表明标签有助于连接性追踪。在尺寸任务中，领先模型成功完成全部17项提议，得分91.2/100，而两个最新Claude模型拒绝4和11个本可转录的任务，未指定尺寸的提案得分为零。两类任务导致不同模型排名，揭示出视觉理解与设计能力的差异，以及某些模型存在策略而非能力限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟集成电路设计基准难以判断模型是否真正学习了可迁移的电路设计技能，而非仅记忆已知案例；同时无法验证其输出在特定制造和测试条件下的有效性。因此需要一个更可靠、结构清晰且可复现的基准来评估模型的真实设计能力。

Method: 构建一个基于Tiny Tapeout制造批次的开源电路集合，共273个拓扑不同的顶层设计。所有设计源文件按提交版本获取，并在统一容器环境中处理，以生成标准化的原理图图像和SPICE网表。使用提交日期进行模型训练截止分析，利用作者提供的测试平台进行仿真验证。评估任务分为两部分：（1）从原理图到网表的转录（66个任务），衡量图同构精度；（2）器件尺寸分配（17个任务），评估尺寸建议与人类参考的匹配度。

Result: 在66个转录任务中，最强模型达到56.1%的精确图同构率，六种模型在中等规模任务上性能明显下降。移除人工标签后，精确匹配率下降但结构F1保持稳定，说明标签对连接性推断有帮助。在17个尺寸任务中，领先模型成功完成所有任务，平均得分91.2/100；而两个新Claude模型分别拒绝4和11个任务，即使它们能正确转录。未指定尺寸的提案得分为零，凸显尺寸生成的重要性。两类任务产生不同模型排名，表明模型在视觉识别与设计推理方面的能力差异，部分模型表现受限于策略选择而非能力本身。

Conclusion: 该基准有效区分了模型的可迁移设计能力与记忆行为，揭示了当前模型在图结构理解、连接性追踪及尺寸生成方面的优势与局限。转录与尺寸任务的不同表现说明模型具备多维度设计能力，但其性能受标签依赖、策略选择等因素影响，未来需发展更鲁棒的评估机制以推动真实设计能力提升。

Abstract: Existing analog integrated circuit design benchmarks make two questions hard to answer: whether a model has learned transferable circuit skills rather than recalled familiar examples, and whether its output works under defined process and test conditions. We introduce a benchmark built from human-designed, open-source circuits associated with Tiny Tapeout manufacturing shuttles. The collection contains 273 topologically distinct top-level designs. Every source is retrieved at the revision recorded for its shuttle submission and processed in a fixed containerized environment. The pipeline exports each eligible schematic image and its SPICE netlist from the same source file, giving transcription an exact structural reference. Commit dates support model-specific training-cutoff analysis, while author testbenches provide the simulation context for sizing. The benchmark evaluates schematic-to-netlist transcription and device sizing. Across seven models on a fixed set of 66 transcription tasks, the strongest model reaches 56.1% exact graph isomorphism, and six of seven models drop sharply from the small to the medium tier. For one frontier model, removing author-chosen labels reduces exact matches while preserving aggregate structural F1, suggesting that labels can aid connectivity tracing. On the 17 sizing tasks, the leading model converges on all 17 proposals and reaches 91.2 out of 100 against the human reference, while the two newest Claude models refuse 4 and 11 of the same prompts they transcribe without objection; a proposal without sizes scores zero. The two tasks produce different model rankings, exposing distinct visual and design capabilities and, in one family, a policy rather than capability limit.

</details>


### [265] [MLLMs Fail to Refuse when Using Tools Agentically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03938)
*Rikiya Takehi,Ryo Hachiuma,Shaona Ghosh,Dan Zhao,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Yusuke Hirota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了代理型多模态大模型（MLLMs）在使用工具时存在严重的安全缺陷，即对有害请求的拒绝能力显著下降。实验表明，在三种主流安全基准测试中，所有测试的顶尖开源与闭源MLLMs在工具使用模式下的安全性均明显低于非工具模式，拒绝失败率最高上升68.7%。通过对超过10万条响应的分析，研究提出了两个可能导致安全性能下降的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究代理型多模态大模型在调用工具时的安全性问题，发现其在处理有害请求时的拒绝能力显著减弱，揭示当前工具使用范式中的潜在风险。

Method: 通过在三个主流安全基准上对比模型在工具使用与非工具使用场景下的表现，收集并分析超过10万条响应数据，识别安全退化现象，并深入探讨其可能原因。

Result: 所有测试的顶级开放与闭源多模态大模型在工具使用模式下表现出显著更低的安全性，拒绝有害请求的能力下降，最高拒绝失败率增加达68.7%。

Conclusion: 当前代理型多模态大模型的工具使用机制存在严重安全隐患，亟需重新审视和改进其安全设计，以防止在实际应用中引发风险。

Abstract: Agentic multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently pushed the frontier of visual reasoning by calling tools such as zooming and tagging. Despite the recent strong success of agentic MLLMs, this work uncovers a critical safety failure in the tool-use paradigm: agentic tool-using MLLMs become less capable of refusing harmful requests. Our experiments confirm that, across three popular safety benchmarks, all the top open- and closed-weight MLLMs we test exhibit significantly lower safety in tool-using settings than in non-tool settings, with a relative refusal failure rate increase of up to 68.7%. Based on analysis of 100,000+ responses, including extended experiments, we also propose two possible reasons for this safety degradation.

</details>


### [266] [Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Model Decision-Making for Autonomous Driving Guided by Chinese Philosophical Wisdom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03948)
*Xiaojun Bi,Xiaoyuan Ma,Yiwen Sun,Tianren Huang,Chaoran Liu,Bokai Huang,Hao Yang,Baichuan Mo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CPW-Drive 是一种基于中国哲学智慧的闭环检索增强生成框架，通过融合儒家思想中的价值原则，提升自动驾驶决策系统的安全性与社会规范契合度。该方法利用LLM提取经典文本关键词，经人工筛选形成可检索的价值指导，并结合物理感知的空间相似性检索（PSSR）实现情境化案例匹配。在多车道高速公路场景中，其成功率分别达到93.0%、86.0%和72.0%，显著优于最强基线模型，同时具备更高的无碰撞步数和更低的变道频率，表明结构化价值引导有助于提升系统安全性和稳定性，但存在效率与延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶决策系统多依赖数值优化、序列预测或大语言模型，缺乏对哲学与伦理维度的深入考量，尤其在复杂交通交互中难以兼顾安全、效率与社会规范。因此亟需引入具有文化深度的价值引导机制以增强决策的合理性与可接受性。

Method: 提出CPW-Drive框架，包含两大部分：一是从中国儒家经典中提取并人工验证驱动相关价值原则，构建可检索的价值知识库；二是设计物理感知空间相似性检索（PSSR）模块，根据车辆布局与速度外推状态匹配历史案例，实现情境化价值引导的闭环生成。

Result: 在Highway-env多车道驾驶任务中，CPW-Drive在三种交通配置下的成功率为93.0%、86.0%、72.0%，分别超越最强基线8.0、22.5、25.0个百分点；同时保持最高无碰撞步数与最低变道频率，证明其在安全性和稳定性上的优势，但也存在一定效率与延迟代价。

Conclusion: 结构化的价值引导机制能够有效提升自动驾驶系统在复杂交互中的安全性与稳定性，将中国哲学智慧融入决策过程具有现实可行性与应用潜力，未来可在更广泛交通场景中拓展其适应性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving decision systems must balance safety, efficiency, and social norms in complex traffic interactions. Philosophical and ethical considerations have received limited attention in existing autonomous driving decision-making approaches based on numerical optimization, sequence prediction, and large language models (LLMs). We propose Chinese Philosophical Wisdom-Guided Driving (CPW-Drive), a closed-loop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that incorporates value guidance derived from Chinese philosophy into autonomous driving decision-making. Using Chinese Confucian thought as its knowledge source, CPW-Drive consolidates LLM-extracted keywords from relevant classical texts into driving-relevant value principles through manual screening and validation. It then contextualizes these principles through scenario-specific cases to form retrievable and reusable value guidance. We further propose Physics-aware Spatial Similarity Retrieval (PSSR), which compares vehicle layouts and velocity-extrapolated states to retrieve physically relevant historical cases. On Highway-env's multilane highway-driving task, CPW-Drive achieves success rates of 93.0%, 86.0%, and 72.0% across three traffic configurations. These results outperform the strongest baseline by 8.0, 22.5, and 25.0 percentage points, respectively. Across all configurations, CPW-Drive achieves the highest collision-free step count and maintains a low lane-change frequency. The results suggest that structured value guidance can improve simulated closed-loop safety and stability while introducing efficiency and latency trade-offs.

</details>


### [267] [LatentQuant: Preserving the Policy-Facing Latent Contract under NVFP4 VAE Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03959)
*Ziye Deng,Lufang Chen,Shuyu Feng,Zhenwei Duan,Zicong Ye,Yu Sun,Xiaofan Li,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LatentQuant框架，通过两阶段NVFP4量化感知训练，在保持高重建质量的同时解决因量化导致的策略性能下降问题，显著提升机器人任务成功率并实现1.17x-1.26x的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在使用低精度量化时，无法有效保留政策面对的潜在表示一致性，导致下游策略性能严重下降，尤其在复杂任务中表现不佳。

Method: 设计了一种两阶段的NVFP4 QAT框架：第一阶段对量化后的编码器进行对齐以恢复高精度特征，第二阶段冻结编码器并仅优化解码器，从而缓解梯度漂移和重建信号失真问题。

Result: 在Wan2.1和Wan2.2数据集上，该方法在保持接近基线控制性能的同时实现了95.75%的LIBERO成功率和68.8%的RoboTwin成功率；在NVIDIA B300 GPU上，相比BF16 cuDNN实现1.17x-1.26x的端到端VAE加速。

Conclusion: LatentQuant有效解决了低精度量化下潜空间表示与策略间不一致的问题，兼顾了模型压缩效率与任务性能，为高效视觉动作模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent world action models (WAMs) reuse pretrained video VAEs whose encoder latents directly condition downstream action policies. Quantization must therefore preserve not only reconstruction fidelity but also the policy-facing latent contract expected by the frozen policy. Direct NVFP4 leaves W4A4 quantization error uncompensated, whereas joint quantization-aware training (QAT) can recover reconstruction by moving this representation. On Wan2.1, joint QAT nearly matches FP32 VBench-7 (0.7403 versus 0.7409), yet LIBERO success collapses from 95.5% to 10.5%. Controlled decoder-only experiments show that activation quantize-dequantize operations alter the reconstruction signal and decoder Jacobian, redirecting the gradient returned to the encoder and inducing persistent latent drift. Based on this mechanism, we introduce LatentQuant, a two-stage NVFP4 QAT framework that first aligns the quantized encoder with its high-precision counterpart, then freezes it while adapting the decoder. Across Wan2.1 and Wan2.2, LatentQuant preserves near-baseline control and high reconstruction quality, achieving 95.75% success on LIBERO and 68.8% on RoboTwin. On NVIDIA B300 GPUs, NVFP4 execution achieves 1.17x-1.26x end-to-end VAE speedups over BF16 cuDNN.

</details>


### [268] [ROAR: Unifying Runs across Heterogeneous AI-Driven Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03966)
*Leo Y. Lin,Vishakha Ramani,Z. Berkay Celik,Paul Castro,Marquita Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ROAR 是一个用于统一和分析异构 AI 驱动研究系统（ADRS）输出的框架，解决数据碎片化问题。通过关系型模式和解析层，它能标准化不同 ADRS 的输出，保留数据血缘与时间结构，并支持新系统的无缝集成。基于超过 900 次运行的聚合数据，研究揭示了搜索过程中的共性规律：多数收益集中在早期，不同策略在不同问题上的有效性差异显著。此外，该数据集不仅可用于分析，还能指导 ADRS 配置优化，凸显跨系统洞察的价值。ROAR 是首个专门为此目的设计的基础设施，推动了大规模、可比较的 ADRS 研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 驱动研究系统（ADRS）的运行成本高、数据分散，各团队独立运作，输出格式不一，缺乏共享基础设施，导致难以进行跨系统、跨问题的大规模分析。这种数据孤岛限制了对搜索行为本质的理解。因此需要一种统一机制来整合异构数据，实现高效分析与再利用。

Method: 提出 ROAR 框架，采用关系型数据模型与可扩展的解析层，将来自不同 ADRS 的输出标准化，同时保持数据的来源追踪与时间序列结构。该设计无需修改模式即可支持新系统接入。基于此构建了一个包含 900 多次运行的大型数据集，用于后续分析与配置优化。

Result: 通过聚合数据发现：相同配置的运行可能收敛到不同结果；多数性能提升发生在早期阶段；引入先验解的策略效果因问题而异。更重要的是，该数据集可直接用于优化 ADRS 配置，证明其不仅是分析工具，也是实用决策支持系统。

Conclusion: ROAR 实现了异构 ADRS 数据的系统性整合，使跨系统、跨问题的深度分析成为可能。它揭示了隐藏在单个系统或基准测试中无法察觉的问题结构特征，为未来智能搜索系统的改进提供了重要依据。这是首个专为统一 ADRS 运行数据而设计的基础设施，具有显著的科学与工程价值。

Abstract: Each run of an AI-driven research system (ADRS) is an expensive search over a vast solution space, and dependable evaluation requires many runs, making run data both costly to produce and valuable to retain for large-scale analysis. Yet this data remains fragmented: teams operate in isolation, ADRS frameworks emit results in different formats, and no shared infrastructure exists to aggregate or compare runs across problems and systems. We present ROAR, a solution for systematically unifying and analyzing heterogeneous ADRS outputs. ROAR addresses two challenges: reconciling heterogeneous ADRS outputs and enabling analytics across runs with different objectives and scoring functions. We achieve this through a relational schema and parsing layer that normalize heterogeneous ADRS outputs while preserving data lineage and temporal structure, and accommodating new systems without requiring schema modifications. From building a corpus of more than 900 runs from multiple ADRS, we show how pooled data can reveal properties of problem landscapes that are difficult to observe. Consistent with prior work, runs with identical configurations may converge to different scores. We find that many runs realize most gains early, and that the effectiveness of different strategies for incorporating prior solutions into the search process varies across problems. We further show that the pooled corpus is actionable and not merely analytical by using ROAR to configure ADRS runs. Together, these results illustrate how pooled ADRS data can expose problem-dependent structure in search behavior that is difficult to detect from any single system, team, or benchmark. Such cross-cutting insights are difficult to obtain while runs remain siloed; ROAR is the first infrastructure designed to unify them.

</details>


### [269] [Teaching Agents to Code Reliably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03984)
*Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Myeongsoo Kim,Kangrui Ruan,Shweta Garg,Varun Kumar,Murali Krishna Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种可训练的自主编码代理，通过执行反馈指导搜索并基于回滚树评估补丁，显著提升代码修复效率。通过加权监督微调和强化学习优化策略与验证器，使在多个基准上修复率和验证精度大幅提升，且在不同规模模型上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主编码代理在推理时增加计算量仅在产生有效修复并提供可靠选择证据时才有效；但现有方法受限于位置多样性窄、编辑多样性未被利用、验证误导等问题，导致修复效率低下。因此需要设计可学习的行为模式来提升修复能力。

Method: 采用执行反馈指导搜索，以自身回滚树为基准评分每个补丁；通过加权监督微调将关键行为融入策略；使用强化学习训练验证器识别正确修复与错误变体，并提高其判别能力。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上解决52.8%的问题，仅用48.1%的代理步数；加权微调后pass@1提升至35.2%，pass@8至51.1%；强化学习进一步提升pass@1至43.0%，pass@8至60.7%，验证器精确度从26.8%升至41.7%，误接受率减半；在多个分布外测试集上表现更优，且在7B、14B、30B模型上均优于已有基线。

Conclusion: 关键修复行为（如位置多样性、编辑多样性、可靠验证）可通过策略学习实现，而非依赖大规模模型的副产品。通过合理训练策略与验证机制，可显著提升自主编码代理的修复性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents solve repository issues by reading code, running commands, editing files, and submitting patches. Extra inference-time compute yields gains only when it produces a useful repair and supplies reliable evidence for choosing one. Three behaviors decide both, and we argue they are teachable rather than byproducts of scale, so a policy can carry them instead of a scaffold. Location diversity remains narrow, since attempts return to the same site and extra samples add no coverage. Edit diversity is left unexploited, since methodologies that differ resolve complementary issues no single run reaches. Verification misleads, since a test the agent writes for its own patch accepts many incorrect ones. Directing search by execution feedback and scoring each patch against its own reverted tree resolves 52.8% of SWE-bench Verified using 48.1% of the agent-steps an eight-sample baseline spends. Training moves these behaviors into the policy. On the 270 issues held out from SFT and RL training, weighted supervised fine-tuning raises pass@1 from 31.9% to 35.2% and pass@8 from 46.7% to 51.1%. A reinforcement objective then trains the verifier against gold-labeled repairs and incorrect variants, crediting the assertions that detect them. It raises pass@1 to 43.0% and pass@8 to 60.7%, lifts verifier precision from 26.8% to 41.7%, and more than halves false acceptance. Resolution improves on two of three out-of-distribution suites and verifier precision on all three, and the gains hold at 7B, 14B, and 30B against published coder baselines.

</details>


### [270] [Behavioral History Outperforms Descriptions of the Person for LLM Synthetic Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03998)
*Khashayar Pourtaheri,Ahmad Zareei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）作为调查受访者合成人格的效度，重点分析不同信息条件（无个人信息、人口统计、性格特质、认知分数、行为历史）对预测个体后续选择的影响。结果表明，仅靠描述性信息的合成人格在个体层面预测能力有限（仅7-12%的人类再测信度），而加入行为历史后预测能力显著提升至28%，并更准确反映个体间差异。总体上，行为历史是提高合成响应有效性的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型生成的合成受访者是否能真实代表个体决策行为，特别是在缺乏真实人类数据时，需明确哪些信息有助于提升合成人格的预测准确性。

Method: 基于包含845名美国成年人的两波面板数据，测量14种行为偏差（如风险偏好、时间偏好、过度自信等）。通过五种逐步增加信息量的条件进行实验：无个人信息、人口统计、性格特质、认知分数、以及受访者的早期调查答案作为行为历史。在行为历史条件下，目标偏差相关题目被隐藏以测试其预测能力。使用人类再测一致性作为基准进行比较。

Result: 在群体层面，各条件下的平均偏差数量接近人类水平（7.1–8.1，人类为7.1），但方差恢复不一；仅描述性信息恢复53–67%的人类个体间变异，而加入行为历史后接近人类水平。在个体层面，描述性人格仅实现7–12%的人类再测知情度，加入行为历史后提升至28%。在所有17个人口统计组中，含行为历史的条件表现最优，而描述性条件对某些群体几乎无效。合成响应还表现出更强的教育与收入差异，高于真实人类反应。

Conclusion: 对于大型语言模型生成的合成人格而言，受访者的过往回答比对其个人特征的描述更能有效预测其个体决策。行为历史是提升合成响应个体预测准确性的核心要素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as synthetic personas representing survey respondents. Their validity as substitutes for particular respondents depends on whether they reproduce individuals' decisions. We examine what information helps synthetic respondents predict each individual's later choices, using five conditions that add progressively richer information: no personal information, demographics, personality traits, cognitive scores, and finally the respondent's earlier survey choices as behavioral history. We use a two-wave panel of 845 US adults who completed measures of 14 behavioral biases (spanning risk, time preferences, overconfidence, and reasoning), so each respondent's earlier answers provide a human test-retest benchmark; in the behavioral-history condition, all items that score the target bias are withheld. At the population level, the average number of biases per respondent in every condition is close to the human average (7.1-8.1 biases, against 7.1 for humans). This aggregate similarity masks differences in variance: persona descriptions recover only 53-67% of human between-person variation, whereas adding behavioral history restores it to approximately the human level. At the individual level, description-based personas achieve only 7-12% of the informedness observed in human test-retest responses, while adding behavioral history raises this to 28%. The condition including behavioral history has the highest estimated informedness in all 17 demographic groups, whereas description-based conditions provide little or no information for some groups. Synthetic responses also exhibit stronger education- and income-related differences than human responses. For LLM synthetic personas, a respondent's past answers add more to individual-level prediction than a description of who they are.

</details>


### [271] [SkillScriptBench: Benchmarking Self-Evolution of Executable Agent Skill Packages Beyond Markdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04008)
*Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Hongyu Luo,Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Kawai Chung,Junle Chen,Changxuan Fan,Qing Zong,Lingyun Xie,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillScriptBench基准，用于系统评估技能包中文档修复、脚本修复和行为保持能力；引入AST-Guided Skill Revision方法，利用抽象语法树和调用关系精准定位修改位置，提升修复成功率与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法有效区分文档修复、脚本修复和行为保持，导致对技能自演化能力的评估不全面；需构建更精细的评估体系并改进修复方法。

Method: 构建350任务的SkillScriptBench基准，包含150个故障注入任务和200个受控实验任务；提出AST-Guided Skill Revision方法，通过抽象语法树和调用关系指导代码修改与文档同步。

Result: 在四个LLM上，新方法在故障包上的修复成功率分别提升21.9%（Raw Package）和27.7%（CoEvoSkills）；三轮重复测试中任务解决率提升20.8%和31.5%，表明修复效果更稳定可靠。

Conclusion: AST-Guided Skill Revision能有效提升技能包的自修复能力，尤其在复杂修复任务中表现出更强的准确性和一致性，为大模型代理技能演化提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Executable Agent Skills combine natural-language instructions and scripts into reusable packages for LLM agents, and revising them requires fixing errors without breaking correct behavior. Existing benchmarks do not systematically distinguish documentation repair, script repair, and preservation when evaluating skill self-evolution. We introduce SkillScriptBench, a 350-task benchmark designed to evaluate these capabilities separately. From a survey of over 35,000 GitHub-hosted Skill roots, we select 100 packages and construct 150 repair tasks. Each task pairs a package containing injected script faults with a maintenance request and executable checks of the required behavior. A complementary controlled track contains 200 tasks from 50 packages, each evaluated under the same maintenance request in four states: clean, documentation faults, script faults, and faults in both. Across four LLMs, methods that edit both documentation and scripts can repair script faults but do not consistently outperform Markdown-only revision on documentation repair or preservation.
