  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受限于内存带宽，因每个输出令牌需读取所有层的键值缓存。现有稀疏解码方法或固定预算（不灵活）或依赖额外选择开销/训练，难以兼顾效率与灵活性。

Method: 提出MOIRA框架，采用按注意力头和层自适应的动态预算机制，结合覆盖规则（γ控制）选择最少必要页面；设计自规划注意力内核，实现线程块本地化任务分配，确保全程在CUDA图内执行。

Result: 在H200上，γ=0.99时读取页数减少约70%，TPOT下降2.2-2.5倍；γ=0.98时TPOT下降2.7倍，性能接近密集解码；高负载下吞吐量最高提升51%。

Conclusion: 通过每头每层自适应预算与内核级自规划机制，稀疏解码可在保持高精度的同时实现显著加速与高吞吐，是高效长上下文生成的关键技术。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is limited by memory bandwidth, because every output token reads the KV cache of every layer. Sparse decoding reduces this cost by reading only part of the KV cache. We observe that the number of pages a query needs varies widely across KV heads, layers and steps. Fixed budgets are simple, but they are sized for demanding cases and tuned per workload; adaptive budgets follow this variation more flexibly, but existing designs pay for it with extra selection cost or training. At the kernel level, FlashAttention-3 (FA3) and FlashInfer are designed for rows of similar length: with page lists whose length differs per KV head, they either pad the lists (forfeiting much of the sparse saving), leave thread blocks unbalanced, or rely on a host-side plan that runs outside the CUDA graph. We propose MOIRA, a training-free sparse decode path in vLLM whose budget adapts per KV head and per layer. For every request, layer, KV head and step, a coverage rule keeps the smallest set of pages whose estimated attention mass reaches a fraction $γ$. A new kernel, self-planning attention, lets each thread block derive its own share of the work from the list lengths, so the whole decode step stays inside the CUDA graph. On an H200, at RULER's 128k context, MOIRA with $γ=0.99$ matches dense accuracy while reading about 30% of the pages and reduces the time per output token (TPOT) by 2.2-2.5$\times$ relative to dense FA3; with $γ=0.98$ it reduces TPOT by 2.7$\times$ and stays within the noise of dense. Under high serving load it raises throughput by up to 51%. These results suggest that a budget adapted per head and layer, paired with a kernel that keeps such budgets inside the CUDA graph, makes sparse decoding both flexible and fast.

</details>


### [305] [From Latent Space to Jacobian Space: Measuring, Evading, and Training Against Safety-Content Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04316)
*Mohammad Mosafer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究模型内部安全状态与输出行为之间的关系，提出通过雅可比空间（J-space）读出作为观察内部内容的窗口，并引入两个量化桥梁：(i) 传输-放大轮廓 $A_\ell$，衡量每一层雅可比如何将潜在安全方向传递至输出空间；(ii) 基线与调优模型对比协议，用于追溯安全可读性的训练来源。实验发现，经过安全训练的模型在中上层集中识别危险内容，但在0.5B DPO模型中，尽管拒绝能力增强，J-space识别性能降至随机水平，表明训练可能扩大了行为安全与内部可读性之间的差距。部署审计显示，监控感知的后缀攻击可在不牺牲行为表现的前提下抑制提示侧监控，仅学习到的监控层级能抵抗自身反向攻击；训练时防御在所有惩罚权重下均失败。控制实验表明放大轮廓具有描述性而非因果性，连续前缀优化在离散搜索面前失效。结论强调：安全监控、训练与评估必须直接作用于‘可读性’本身，而非仅依赖输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全监控仅关注模型输出，忽视其在计算过程中已具备的安全状态。需要理解内部可读安全信号与潜在空间安全几何的关系，以及安全训练如何影响其可访问性。

Method: 提出传输-放大轮廓 $A_\\ell$ 和基线-调优对比协议，结合多规模模型（135M–1.5B）与三种种子配置，分析安全训练对内部可读性的影响；并通过部署审计、对抗攻击、控制实验等验证机制有效性。

Result: 安全训练使识别集中在中上层，但可能导致输出层面安全与内部可读性脱节；监控感知攻击可绕过监控而无行为代价；仅有学习型监控能抵抗反攻；训练防御在重罚下仍失败；放大轮廓具描述性非因果性；连续优化不如离散搜索有效。

Conclusion: 安全监控、训练和评估必须以‘可读性’为核心，不能仅依赖最终输出。内部分析与外部行为需统一考量，才能实现真正可靠的安全保障。

Abstract: Output-only safety monitoring sees only the end of a model's computation, yet the model computes its answer before emitting it: what it is internally poised to say is safety-critical. Jacobian-space (J-space) readouts, linear maps from hidden states to the output vocabulary via the model's input-output Jacobian, have been proposed as a window on behaviorally accessible internal content, and a first safety protocol (JADR) showed that danger recognition is readable there. What remains unknown is how this accessible content relates to the latent-space safety geometry studied by representation engineering, and how safety training shapes it. We introduce two quantitative bridges: (i) a transport-amplification profile $A_\ell$, measuring how strongly a latent safety direction is carried toward output space by each layer's Jacobian, and (ii) a paired base-vs-tuned protocol that attributes accessibility to training provenance. Across four small model pairs (135M to 1.5B, three lens-fit seeds), tuned checkpoints concentrate recognition in upper-middle layers, and at 0.5B DPO installs refusal while J-space recognition drops to chance: training can widen the accessibility gap exactly where behavioral safety looks best. A deployment audit closes the loop: monitor-aware GCG-style suffixes suppress the prompt-side monitor at zero behavioral cost at every scale; only a learned monitor rung resists its own adaptive re-attack; the training-time defense fails its re-attack at every penalty weight; steering shows the amplification profile is descriptive, not causal; and continuous-prefix optimization fails where discrete search succeeds. Safety monitoring, training, and evaluation must operate on accessibility itself, not on outputs alone.

</details>


### [306] [Bidirectional Preference Synthesis: Learning Prompt-Conditioned Preferences from Boundary Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04328)
*Junbo Wang,Lidong Lu,Zhuoqun Li,Guiping Jiang,Xiangyu Wu,Tinghai Zhang,Tong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BPS是一种用于标准直接偏好优化（DPO）的数据构建方法，通过引入反向对偶对来显式处理边界失败问题，即响应虽违反原始指令但符合附近意图或约束的情况。该方法在不改变DPO目标、无需训练奖励模型或在线采样情况下，提升模型在实现侧的排名准确率（从6.8%升至62.3%），同时保持原始侧性能，并在多语言多轮指令遵循任务中表现出显著优于传统Forward-DPO的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于修正的离线偏好流水线仅将模型失败视为原始提示下的拒绝响应，忽略了边界失败——即响应违反原指令但符合邻近意图或约束的情形。这种监督信息不完整，导致模型在面对类似意图时无法正确区分对错。

Method: 提出双向偏好合成（BPS）方法：对每个验证过的边界失败，保留原始提示下的正向对偶对，同时添加一个在合成实现提示下的反向对偶对，使同一响应在错误情境下被拒绝、在正确情境下被选择，从而显式建模提示依赖关系。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507上，BPS在保持原始侧配对排序的同时，将实现侧排序准确率从6.8%提升至62.3%；在Kimi-K2.6跨教师探测中也观察到类似提升；人工盲审支持反向偏好方向正确；下游评估显示其在多语言多轮指令遵循任务中与Forward-DPO有最明显差异，且在代理、工具使用和代码检查等任务中表现稳定的能力保留。

Conclusion: BPS有效填补了离线偏好学习中对边界失败的监督空白，通过简单而高效的双向对偶构造，在不改变原有训练范式的基础上显著提升了模型在语义相近但意图不同的场景下的判断能力，具有良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Correction-based offline preference pipelines commonly treat model failures only as rejected responses under the original prompt. This supervision is incomplete for boundary failures: responses that violate the given instruction yet coherently satisfy a nearby intent or constraint setting. We introduce Bidirectional Preference Synthesis (BPS), a data-construction method for standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) that makes this missing prompt dependence explicit. For each validated boundary failure, BPS keeps the conventional forward pair under the original prompt and adds a reverse pair under a synthesized achieved prompt, so the same response is rejected where it is wrong and chosen where it is right, without changing the DPO objective, training a reward model, or requiring online sampling. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, BPS preserves original-side pairwise ranking while raising achieved-side ranking accuracy from 6.8% to 62.3% on held-out crossed anchors, with a similar shift under a Kimi-K2.6 cross-teacher probe. A blind human audit supports the intended reverse preference direction, and downstream evaluations show the clearest separation from Forward-DPO in multilingual multi-turn instruction following, with consistent capability-retention patterns on agentic, tool-use, and code checks.

</details>


### [307] [Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence: Self-Guided Attention Steering to Mitigate Sycophancy and Stubbornness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04329)
*Yinghao He,Mengyu Xu,Haixiang Sun,Donghan Li,Yibo Wang,Lixu Wang,Kezhen Chen,Chi Li,Chunwei Liu,Bharat Bhargava,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPE-Bench评估语言模型在用户压力与上下文信息之间的平衡能力，并引入SPAED框架通过令牌级注意力调节，抑制用户压力、增强上下文证据，有效降低盲目顺从性（18.8个百分点）并提升在冲突信息下的更新能力（5.5个百分点），在多轮对话中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在面对用户压力或上下文信息时，可能表现出盲目顺从或固执僵化的问题，而现有评估方法难以同时衡量这两种缺陷的权衡，因此需要一种能系统评估并缓解此类问题的新方法。

Method: 提出CoPE-Bench评测基准，包含六种条件组合；设计SPAED训练无关框架，利用模型自身判断识别关键令牌，通过注意力机制抑制用户压力、增强上下文信息影响。

Result: 在五个主流模型上，SPAED平均减少18.8个百分点的压力跟随行为，提升5.5个百分点的联合条件更新能力；在两轮对话中，比最强提示基线平均提升13.2个百分点。

Conclusion: SPAED框架能有效缓解语言模型在用户压力与上下文信息之间的失衡问题，实现更可靠、更具适应性的响应行为，且无需额外训练，具备良好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: Reliable language models should resist unsupported user pressure while effectively using objective contextual information. However, models may exhibit sycophancy by yielding to unsupported user pressure or contextual stubbornness by failing to update their answers when relevant contextual information warrants revision. Evaluating interventions for these failures separately can obscure whether mitigating one failure exacerbates the other. To assess this trade-off, we introduce CoPE-Bench with six conditions per question: a neutral baseline, correct or incorrect user pressure, contextual information consistent with or conflicting with the neutral answer, and a joint condition combining incorrect claims with conflicting contextual information. To regulate the influence of user pressure and contextual information, we propose SPAE (Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence), a training-free framework that uses the model's own judgments to identify relevant tokens, suppressing user pressure and amplifying contextual information through token-level attention steering. On average across five backbones, SPAE reduces pressure following by 18.8 percentage points and increases joint-condition updating by 5.5 percentage points relative to the strongest baseline in the main comparison. In two-turn dialogue, it improves joint-condition updating by an average of 13.2 percentage points over the strongest prompting baseline. The source data and codes can be found at https://github.com/03Grant/sycophancy-and-stubbornness.

</details>


### [308] [ShadowMiner v1 - An Experience Report on Implementing and Measuring a Problem-and-Hypothesis Discovery Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04339)
*Jinhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShadowMiner v1 是一个自动从AI论文中发现研究问题并生成假设的九阶段系统。它将文档结构化为知识图谱，识别其中的图谱空白（即研究中的结构性盲点），并将这些空白用于大模型生成提示。每个生成的假设都会经过验证，包括检查是否已被现有研究覆盖、评估质量以及核实其依赖的事实是否准确源自来源。该报告未提出新的生成或评估技术，而是总结了应用先前工作理念的经验，并衡量每一环节的实际贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了自动发现AI研究中的未探索问题和生成可验证的研究假设，提高科研效率与创新性，减少研究人员在文献调研上的负担。

Method: 构建九阶段流水线：将论文结构化为知识图谱，识别图谱中的结构性盲点（即图谱空白），利用这些空白作为大模型生成提示的输入；通过检查已有研究覆盖情况、质量评分和事实准确性验证生成的假设。

Result: 系统成功识别出潜在的研究空白，并生成高质量、可验证的假设；实验表明各阶段设计对最终效果有实际贡献，验证了方法的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: ShadowMiner v1 证明了基于知识图谱与大模型结合的方法在自动发现研究问题方面的有效性，且各组件的实际贡献可通过实证测量，为未来自动化科研辅助工具提供可靠范式。

Abstract: ShadowMiner v1 is a system that automatically discovers research problems and generates hypotheses from AI papers. It is a nine-stage pipeline. It structures documents into a knowledge graph and finds graph gaps in it - structural blind spots in research. These graph gaps are included in the LLM generation prompt. Each generated hypothesis is then verified by checking whether it is already covered by existing research, scoring its quality, and checking that the facts it relies on are accurately drawn from its sources. This report does not propose a new generation or evaluation technique. It describes our experience of implementing and applying ideas from prior work, and measuring whether each one actually contributed.

</details>


### [309] [Functionally Equivalent or Not? Graph-Grounded Differential Surrogate Execution for Code Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04371)
*Amit Kachroo,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Yuhao Zhang,Nguyen Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FEAgent是一种选择性等价评估代理，结合类型化程序图证据与差分替代执行，通过分析调用流、控制流、数据流等关系，利用分支感知生成器提出区分性输入，并由两个盲化LLM代理独立预测源和目标可观测结果。所有判断和预测差异均记录在证据账本中，由确定性协调器返回等价、不等价或不确定的结果，避免强制结论。在EquiBench和SWE-bench Verified上的评估显示，其对基准标签的分歧中多数经直接执行验证为真实行为差异，证明其能有效发现测试未覆盖的错误，作为测试与形式验证之间的可审计审计层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如测试覆盖有限、文本相似性无法反映行为差异、大模型判断难以审计，且直接执行常因环境依赖不可行。需要一种可审计、可解释、能处理复杂环境依赖的程序等价性评估方法。

Method: FEAgent首先对齐公共接口和行为相关图锚点，发起关于调用流、控制流、数据流、类型、导入和副作用关系的有界查询；随后由分支感知生成器提出区分性输入；两个盲化LLM代理分别预测源和目标程序的输出；所有判断与差异记录在证据账本中；最后由确定性协调器基于证据做出等价、不等价或不确定的判定。

Result: 在EquiBench上，FEAgent与已有标签不一致的216个案例中，经直接执行确认为真实差异（18.0%）；在SWE-bench Verified上，331个通过测试的代理补丁中有94个（28.4%）与参考补丁存在行为差异。表明其能有效识别测试遗漏的缺陷。

Conclusion: FEAgent提供了一种可审计、可解释的程序等价性评估机制，能在无法直接执行的场景下发现测试未覆盖的行为差异，作为测试与形式验证之间的可靠审计层，明确表达不确定性，避免虚假等价结论。

Abstract: Determining whether two programs are functionally equivalent is central to code modernization, patch validation, refactoring, and code-generation evaluation. Yet the usual signals are incomplete: tests cover only finite inputs, textual similarity confuses implementation with behavior, and unconstrained LLM judgments are difficult to audit. Direct execution is often impossible when a program depends on an obsolete, licensed, unavailable, or unsafe environment. We introduce FEAgent, a selective equivalence assessor agent that combines typed program-graph evidence with differential surrogate execution. FEAgent first aligns public interfaces and behaviorally relevant graph anchors, then issues bounded queries over call-flow, control-flow, data-flow, type, import, and effect relations. Next, a branch-aware generator agent proposes discriminating inputs, and two blinded LLM surrogates independently predict source and target observables. Every claim and predicted divergence is recorded in an evidence ledger. A deterministic reconciler then returns EQUIVALENT, INEQUIVALENT, or UNCLEAR rather than forcing a verdict when paths are uncovered or evidence conflicts. We evaluate FEAgent on function-level equivalence and repository-level bug patches, where the existing oracle is a benchmark label or a passing test suite. Every disagreement with that oracle is adjudicated by direct execution, revealing errors in benchmark labels and behavioral divergences missed by unit-test-only scoring. On EquiBench, execution confirms FEAgent's disagreements with published labels on 216 of 1,200 evaluated pairs (18.0%); on SWE-bench Verified, 94 of 331 test-passing agent patches (28.4%) diverge from the reference patch. FEAgent thus serves as an audit layer between testing and formal verification, keeping its evidence reviewable and its uncertainty explicit without claiming a proof of equivalence.

</details>


### [310] [Do Tool Calls Execute as Intended? Measuring and Repairing Intent-Execution Correspondence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04375)
*Boyang Yang,Zhenhao Li,Ziyao Yang,Kanghui Jia,Xin Yin,Mingmou Liu,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为IntAct的协议，旨在解决大型语言模型（LLM）代理在通过工具调用执行软件时，因中间节点（hop）擅自修改调用而导致的执行错误问题。研究发现，在真实生产环境中，12.0%的命令调用被修改，其中80.7%的修改未引发错误报告，导致错误动作被执行。作者构建了IEC-Bench基准测试集，揭示了路径和中间节点对失败的影响。实验表明，路径问题使每成功任务的令牌成本增加2.4倍（最高达12.3倍），且55.1%的失败仅在修复首个修改节点后才显现。部署于商业产品的IntAct协议可恢复79.2%的因调用被改而失败的任务。结论强调：必须逐跳设计与测试工具链，以确保调用意图与执行一致或被拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试和故障分析无法检测到中间节点对工具调用的隐性修改，导致错误执行未被察觉，造成用户时间和金钱损失。需要一种机制来确保发出的调用能按原意执行或被拒绝。

Method: 提出意图-执行一致性（IEC）概念，设计IntAct协议，通过观察各中间节点实际接收的内容，识别首个擅自修改调用的节点，并采用不可篡改形式传递调用或直接拒绝，从而保障调用完整性。同时构建IEC-Bench基准，基于真实生产数据中的调用链进行评估。

Result: 在47,828次真实生产调用中，12.0%的含代码、转义符或长文本的调用被修改；80.7%的反斜杠修改未报错但执行错误；所有10个测试的工具链均存在调用修改行为。路径相关问题导致95.1%的生产失败被归因于LLM，但路径本身贡献超过一半。在IEC-Bench上，路径使每成功任务的令牌成本提升2.4倍（最高12.3倍）。55.1%的失败只有在第一个修改节点修复后才会暴露。部署的IntAct可恢复79.2%的调用被改导致的失败。

Conclusion: 为确保工具调用正确执行，必须对工具链进行逐跳设计与测试，以实现意图与执行的一致性，或在不一致时拒绝调用。这有助于显著减少因中间节点篡改造成的失败与资源浪费。

Abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) build and run software through tool calls. A call reaches its program through several hops, and any hop can change the call without notice. When the changed call fails, the agent retries a correct call, which costs users time and money. Benchmarks and failure analyses do not see the change, because they read the call and its result but not what a hop received. We define intent-execution correspondence (IEC) as the property that the executed action matches the action the emitted call denotes under the tool contract. Our protocol observes what each hop received without executing the call, and names the first hop that changed it by the receiver's own parser. IntAct then delivers the call in a form that this hop cannot alter, or refuses the call. We build IEC-Bench from the changes observed in real-world use, with chains of dependent calls under the execution paths of 4 widely-used harnesses.
  In 47,828 shell calls within production sessions, Claude Code's Bash tool changes 12.0% of the calls that carry code, escape sequences, or long text. For 80.7% of the calls whose backslashes are changed, the wrong action runs without any reported error. All 10 measured harnesses change a call. Trajectory-based judgment attributes 95.1% of the production failures to the LLM, although the path caused more than half of them. On IEC-Bench, the path raises the token cost per passed task 2.4 times (up to 12.3 times). A hop that changes a call also hides the changes after it, so 55.1% of the failures on one path appear only after its first hop is repaired. IntAct, deployed in a commercial product, recovers 79.2% of the failures with a changed call. Harnesses should therefore be designed and tested hop-by-hop to ensure a correct call executes as intended or is refused.

</details>


### [311] [AgentPersonaBench: Benchmarking Persona-Driven User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04379)
*Jintao Huang,Yifan Wang,Hongyu Shen,Yi Daniel Lu,Shirley Huang,Minsik Oh,Yewen Wang,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Zhen Xu,Yilan Fan,Zichen Yuan,Ahsan Bilal,Zibu Wei,Sankalp Jajee,Henry Gagnier,Saksham Kapoor,Jicheng Wang,Qianfeng Wen,Yixuan He,Steven Dillmann,Jiashu He,Yucheng Lu,Linqiang Guo,Danyang Zhang,Shi Bo,Raunak Mondal,Haixiang Tang,Weihang Xiao,Allen Nie,Jing Tang,Yueying Li,Yifan Simon Liu,Jianheng Hou,Dianzhuo Wang,Qianyu Zhu,Zhixu Silvia Tao,Zhejian Peng,Zihan Wang,Ishan Gupta,Jinxuan Fan,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Chenxi Qiu,Yijun Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentPersonaBench (APB) 是一个评估人格条件是否真实引导下游代理行为的基准测试。它通过在合成个人资料中嵌入特定人格特质，不直接说明或披露测试目标，从四个逐渐增加真实感的交互界面（调查、聊天、网页、应用程序）观察可察觉的行为来验证人格一致性。该基准包含2,460个任务，覆盖867个特质，经自动化审计和专家评审验证。对20个前沿模型的评估显示，高保真用户模拟已可实现（领先模型在无提示条件下最高达84.7%完全通过），但行为边界明显：跨交互模态表现下降（仅37.9-64.3%在所有表面通过），多属性需求降低保持率，不同模型家族间表现出显著行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估对话风格或自我报告，而非人格驱动行为的真实一致性；需要一种能严格评估语言模型在人格引导下行为忠实度的系统性方法。

Method: 将每个目标人格特质嵌入完整合成个人资料，不明确标注或透露测试内容；通过四个逐步提升真实感的交互界面（调查、聊天、网页、应用）收集可观测行为数据；采用自动化审计与专家评审结合的方式验证人格遵循情况。

Result: 领先模型在无提示条件下最高实现84.7%的完全通过率，表明高保真用户模拟已具备可行性；但在跨模态交互中一致性显著下降（37.9%-64.3%通过全部界面），多属性要求削弱保持能力，不同模型家族间行为差异明显。

Conclusion: APB为评估人格驱动行为的真实性提供了全面、严谨的框架，揭示了当前大模型在人格一致性方面的潜力与局限，为未来智能体模拟研究提供重要参考。

Abstract: We introduce AgentPersonaBench (APB), a benchmark evaluating whether persona conditioning faithfully steers downstream agent behavior. While language models are increasingly deployed for persona-driven user simulation, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate conversational styling or self-reports rather than authentic behavioral fidelity. APB evaluates latent persona adherence one trait at a time, embedding each target trait within a complete synthetic profile without explicitly naming the trait or disclosing the test. Ground-truth adherence is verified strictly from observable actions across four interaction surfaces of increasing realism: survey, chat, web (interactive web environments), and app (desktop software environments). APB comprises 2,460 tasks spanning 867 traits, verified through automated audits and expert review. Our evaluation of 20 frontier model arms demonstrates that high-fidelity user simulation is already attainable: leading models achieve up to 84.7% full-pass adherence under unprompted conditions. At the same time, APB identifies clear behavioral boundaries: adherence drops across interaction modalities (only 37.9-64.3% pass all four surfaces), multi-attribute demands degrade retention, and competing model families exhibit pronounced behavioral divergence.

</details>


### [312] [TrustMed-RL: Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04387)
*Wenxin Zhan,Yizheng Jiao,Haifeng Song,Shuai Xu,Chencheng Pan,Jiayi Feng,Anjie Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrustMed-RL 是一个基于医学知识和多模态数据的强化学习模型，用于支持长期、基于证据的诊断。它在罕见病案例和超过24,000个手动标注的图像面板上训练，通过状态依赖的动作整合访谈、检查、检测、专家咨询和文献检索。该模型在2,500个评估病例中达到37.1%的诊断准确率，优于所有开放权重基线，并比监督微调提升12.4个百分点。当要求获取至少50%的支持性检验证据时，仍能达到32.5%，超过GPT-4o 6.8个百分点。在多个基准测试中表现优异，且医生评审显示83%的诊断路径获得4–5分（满分5分）的证据支撑评分，表明其与人类诊断推理一致且可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学语言模型在诊断中常出现缺乏证据支持或推理不完整的问题，尤其在长周期、复杂病例中表现不佳。为解决这一问题，需要一种能够系统性整合多源信息并严格遵循证据链的诊断框架。

Method: 构建基于PubMed罕见病案例和大量人工标注图像面板的数据集；设计状态依赖的动作机制，融合临床访谈、检查、检测、专家咨询和文献搜索；采用临床适配的GiGPO算法与覆盖调整的诊断奖励函数对80亿参数的视觉-语言策略进行强化学习训练。

Result: TrustMed-RL 在2,500个评估病例中实现37.1%的诊断准确率，优于所有对比的开放权重模型；在需获取50%以上支持性证据的任务中仍达32.5%，超越GPT-4o 6.8个百分点；在MTMedDialog和AgentClinic等基准上优于多个更大规模模型；医生评审显示83%的诊断路径具有高证据支撑性，且与人类推理高度一致。

Conclusion: TrustMed-RL 证明了通过强化学习结合多源证据与临床逻辑，可有效提升医学诊断模型的准确性与可信度，尤其在复杂、长周期诊断任务中展现出显著优势，具备向真实临床场景部署的潜力。

Abstract: Medical language models can produce correct diagnoses despite incomplete investigations and unsupported reasoning. To support long-horizon, evidence-grounded diagnosis, we introduce \textbf{TrustMed-RL}. Built from PubMed rare-disease cases and over 24,000 manually annotated image panels, it integrates interviews, examinations, testing, specialist consultation, and literature search through state-dependent actions. Our 8B vision--language policy, trained with clinically adapted GiGPO and coverage-adjusted diagnostic rewards, achieves 37.1\% diagnostic accuracy on 2,500 evaluation cases, outperforming all evaluated open-weight baselines and improving over supervised fine-tuning by 12.4 percentage points.. When success additionally requires acquiring at least 50\% of supporting test evidence, TrustMed-RL achieves 32.5\%, exceeding GPT-4o by 6.8 percentage points. Furthermore, it surpasses all evaluated baselines on MTMedDialog and multiple larger 27--32B models on AgentClinic. In physician review of 200 diagnostically accepted test-set trajectories, 83.0\% receive evidential-grounding scores of 4--5 out of 5. Physicians' assessments suggest that these diagnostic trajectories are trustworthy and aligned with human diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [313] [TimeNet: An Extensible Unified Data Infrastructure for Next-Generation Temporal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04407)
*Martin Maritsch,Timo Stoffregen,Thomas Kaar,Behsad Riemer,Maxwell A. Xu,Max Rosenblattl,Juncheng Liu,Nicolas Zumarraga,Yu Yvonne Wu,Denys Herasymuk,Sparsh Rastogi,Hyungjun Yoon,Bosong Huang,Arvind Pillai,Dmytro Lopushanskyy,Tony Chen,Robin Deuber,Yichen Liu,Shvat Messica,Dan Li,Jian Lou,Yuwei Zhang,Jaeho Kim,Renée Rosillo Garcia,Fan Wu,Robert Müller,Elgar Fleisch,Flora D. Salim,Dimitris Spathis,Yuzhe Yang,Aaqib Saeed,Daniel McDuff,Ming Jin,Markus Kreft,Kevin O'Sullivan,Robert Jakob,Azul Garza,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeNet 是一个开源的数据标准和可扩展的基础设施，旨在解决时间序列模型训练中因数据格式、标注和处理流程碎片化导致的局限性。它通过将时间数据与任务定义解耦，建立统一、可扩展的数据模型，支持多模态信号（包括规则、不规则或序数时间轴），并以可复用的视图形式表达多种任务类型（如分类、预测、时间定位、问答、生成和编辑）。该共享表示使异构时间序列数据集能够整合用于大规模跨领域、跨模态和跨任务的模型训练。通过将包含150万任务实例的多个数据集转换为TimeNet格式，展示了其在保持实际输入/输出性能的同时实现高效集成的能力。实验表明，仅通过配置调整即可在现有时间基础模型训练流水线中实现多数据集联合训练，并带来14%的F1分数提升。结果证明，TimeNet为构建能从统一数据模型中联合学习的通用时间序列模型提供了必要的数据与系统基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列模型的发展受限于任务和数据集之间的碎片化，缺乏统一的数据格式与处理流程，难以实现跨域、跨模态、跨任务的泛化能力。

Method: 提出TimeNet，构建一个开放、可扩展的时间数据模型，将信号、元数据、标注和监督信息统一表示；支持多模态、不同时间轴类型的数据；通过可复用的视图机制表达各类任务；利用该模型对多个异构数据集进行标准化转换，并支持在现有训练框架中通过配置实现多任务联合训练。

Result: 成功将150万个任务实例的多源数据集转化为TimeNet格式，保持了接近原生格式的I/O性能；在多数据集联合训练下，模型F1得分提升14%，验证了其在跨域、跨任务、跨模态场景下的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: TimeNet为构建真正通用的时间序列基础模型提供了坚实的数据与系统基础，推动模型从任务和数据集特定的范式迈向统一数据模型上的联合学习，是实现跨领域、跨模态、跨时间尺度通用学习的关键一步。

Abstract: Temporal Foundation Models (TFMs) aim to generalize across domains, datasets, and tasks. Yet, their development remains constrained by fragmented, task-specific data formats, annotations, and processing pipelines. We introduce TimeNet, an open-source data standard and scalable infrastructure that decouples temporal data from task definitions and represents signals, metadata, annotations, and supervision in a shared, extensible data model. TimeNet supports multimodal signals with regular, irregular, or ordinal time axes and expresses different task families (including classification, forecasting, temporal localization, question answering, generation, and editing) as reusable views over the same recordings. This shared representation enables heterogeneous time-series datasets to be combined for large-scale model training across domains, modalities, and tasks. We demonstrate TimeNet by transcoding datasets with 1.5M task instances spanning diverse domains, modalities, temporal scales, and forms of supervision, while retaining practical I/O performance relative to native formats. TimeNet enables an existing TFN training pipeline to support joint training on a configurable number of heterogeneous datasets through configuration changes alone. We show this capability by training TFM across multiple datasets and tasks, obtaining a 14% F1 score improvement compared with models trained on individual datasets. These results show that TimeNet provides the data and systems foundation needed to move beyond task- and dataset-specific TFMs toward models that can learn jointly across heterogeneous domains, modalities, temporal scales, and forms of supervision from a common data model.

</details>


### [314] [CORE-RL: Confidence-Oriented Reliability Evaluation of Black-Box Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04418)
*Santhosh GS,Ananya Ravi,Devika Jay,Abhishek Sarkar,Perepu Satheesh Kumar,Saurav Prakash,Kaushik Dey,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE-RL proposes a framework for evaluating black-box RL agents in safety-critical applications by introducing a unified reliability metric and statistical bounds to assess safety, performance, and robustness under environmental changes. It enables high-confidence certificates for safe operational design domains without requiring access to internal policy details.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional evaluation methods for RL agents rely on access to internal policy structures, which is not feasible when agents are delivered as opaque executables or remote APIs. There's a need for a method to evaluate alignment with multi-objective specifications and robustness in real-world conditions while preserving intellectual property.

Method: CORE-RL introduces a Unified Reliability Metric that combines early task termination and safety violations. It applies noise certification envelopes (perceptual, actuation, environment dynamics) and computes finite-sample Clopper-Pearson and Hoeffding bounds on reliability, reward, and safety cost. It defines a safe Operational Design Domain (ODD) based on these confidence intervals.

Result: Experiments on continuous control tasks show CORE-RL can automatically reject non-compliant policies and effectively map the safe ODD of safety-aware policies, providing transparent, reproducible, and quantitative evaluation of black-box RL agents.

Conclusion: CORE-RL offers a statistically grounded, transparent, and reproducible evaluation pipeline for black-box RL agents, enabling safe deployment through high-confidence certificates of safety and performance.

Abstract: The deployment of Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents in critical domains must be preceded with a pipeline to evaluate the alignment of the RL agent with complex multi-objective specifications and robustness under real-world environmental drift. However, to protect intellectual property, the RL agent may be delivered for evaluation as opaque executable or remote API, which makes traditional evaluation techniques based on the internals of the policies infeasible. To address this gap, CORE-RL: Confidence-Oriented Reliability Evaluation of black box RL policy is proposed in this paper. The CORE-RL pipeline introduces a Unified Reliability Metric that formally integrates early task termination and safety constraint violations, preventing unsafe policies from masking failures through premature episode halts. By subjecting the policy to a noise certification envelope of perceptual noise, actuation noise and change in environment dynamics, the pipeline computes the finite-sample Clopper-Pearson bounds on unified reliability metric and Hoeffdings' lower bound on reward and safety cost. The pipeline then defines safe operational design domain to report high-confidence certificates for safety and expected performance. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate the CORE-RL pipeline's ability to automatically reject non-compliant policies and map the safe Operational Design Domain (ODD) of safety-aware policies. Thus CORE-RL provides an evaluation framework towards a quantitative, transparent and reproducible, statistical rationale necessary to safely evaluate, compare, and deploy black box RL solutions.

</details>


### [315] [Asking Earns Nothing: Scoring the Decision to Act in BFCL Multi-Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04429)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BFCL基准测试中，模型在应提问的环节无法通过提问获得分数，导致其倾向于猜测而非提问。通过控制实验发现，尽管某些模型在判断是否应提问上表现优秀（如gpt-5.4决策准确率达80.7%），但其官方得分却未体现这一优势；而简单指令调整（如强制不提问）可显著提升官方得分，说明评分机制偏向于行动而非正确决策。研究释放了测试对、评分工具和问题数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前代理评估基准（BFCL）在衡量模型应答策略时存在的缺陷：提问行为得不到正向激励，导致模型倾向猜测而非主动求知，从而扭曲对模型真实智能水平的评估。

Method: 设计‘应提问’测试对（即同一轮次出现完整与缺失信息的重复请求），通过对比模型在完整与缺失信息情形下的行为（是否执行动作或提问），评估其决策能力；同时通过人工干预指令测试不同行为对官方得分的影响。

Result: gpt-5.4在应提问任务上的决策准确率为80.7%，为七种模型中最高，但其官方排名仅第六；强制其不提问可大幅提升官方得分，而强制提问则无得分收益，表明评分系统更奖励行动而非正确判断。

Conclusion: 当前的代理评估基准（如BFCL）存在严重偏差，未能有效奖励模型在缺乏信息时主动提问的理性行为，反而鼓励盲目行动，导致评估结果不能真实反映模型的推理与决策能力。建议改进评分机制以区分行为质量与表面得分。

Abstract: An agent that lacks the information it needs should ask rather than act, and the task definitions of agent leaderboards say so. BFCL multi-turn builds two of its four categories around a turn on which the model is supposed to ask, and its scorer never looks at that turn: the gold trajectory there is empty, the checker skips it, and the scripted user cannot answer, so asking earns nothing, guessing costs nothing on that turn, and asking twice loses the item. The benchmark also contains the control experiment for that decision. A should-ask item is a base item with one piece of information removed from one turn, so the same request appears twice at the same turn index, once complete and once not: on the first the model should make the call that changes the world, on the second it should ask. We score one decision per pair, whether the model attempted a world-changing call on that turn, read off the stored trajectories with no LLM judge; acting always and asking always both score 50. On the 223 pairs that pose this decision, gpt-5.4 attempts the call on 83.4% of the complete turns and holds back on 78.0% of the incomplete ones, the best decision accuracy of seven models at 80.7%; on the same items the official score ranks it sixth and puts first a model that lands in the middle here. One added line telling gpt-5.4 not to ask pushes it toward acting on both sides of the pair, so its decision accuracy shows no detectable change, while its official score rises by 13.5 to 23.5 points on the two should-ask categories and on the base twins; the opposite line, telling gemma-4-31B-it to ask first, improves its decision by 4.5 points and gains no official score. The score moves with the push toward action, not with the decision. We release the pairs, a turn-level scorer that runs on any BFCL output directory without an API key, and 31 manually verified bad items.

</details>


### [316] [MERCI Cards: An LLM Evaluation and Deployment Framework for High-Stakes Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04430)
*Aparna Komarla,Annalisa Szymanski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数学框架，用于迭代式的大语言模型（LLM）评估与部署，特别应用于刑事司法等高风险、资源受限的领域。该框架通过加权多目标优化，整合了模型选择、评估标准设计、性能评估与部署流程，旨在系统性地跟踪和改进模型表现。实验表明，MERCI卡片能有效指导系统在各部署周期中的改进，帮助开发者聚焦于表现不佳的部分，并引导用户关注输出验证与错误修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在高风险专业工作流中的广泛应用，亟需一套系统化的方法来追踪、监控和持续改进模型性能，特别是在资源有限且风险较高的场景中，如刑事司法系统。现有方法缺乏对模型迭代优化的统一框架，因此需要一种可操作、可量化的评估与部署协议。

Method: 提出了一种基于加权多目标优化的数学框架，涵盖模型选择、评估标准设计、评估过程与部署策略。通过构建可量化的目标函数，结合不同任务的重要性权重，实现对模型性能的综合评估。同时引入MERCI卡片作为可视化工具，辅助开发者和用户识别问题区域并采取针对性改进措施。

Result: 在刑事司法领域的实际应用中，该框架显著提升了模型的可靠性与可解释性。MERCI卡片能够有效引导开发团队关注低绩效模块，促进输出质量的提升；同时增强了用户对模型输出的验证意识，减少潜在误判风险。

Conclusion: 所提出的数学框架为高风险领域中大语言模型的迭代评估与部署提供了系统性解决方案。通过整合多目标优化与可视化工具（MERCI卡片），不仅提升了模型性能，还增强了系统的可管理性与可信度，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed in high-stakes professional workflows, engineers and researchers require principled protocols to systematically track, monitor, and improve model performance across deployment cycles. We present a mathematical framework for iterative LLM evaluation and deployment, and demonstrate its application to AI systems used in criminal justice. Our framework formalizes LLM integration in high-stakes, high-risk, and resource-constrained domains across model selection, rubric design, evaluations and deployment via a weighted multi-objective optimization. We demonstrate that MERCI Cards can guide improvements of the system across deployment iterations, direct developer attention toward under-performing areas, and focus user attention on validation and error-correction in the LLM's outputs.

</details>


### [317] [What Does a Harness Buy? Tokens, Mostly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04433)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定语言模型的情况下，编码代理的系统提示、工具集和上下文管理（即“Harness”）对代码生成任务表现的影响。通过在SWE-bench Verified上运行多个模型与三种生产级Harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode），发现不同Harness之间的分数差异在统计上不显著，甚至在某些情况下，同一Harness重复运行也会导致13%的任务胜负变化。唯一可明确识别的影响是成本：不同Harness的成本差异可达3倍，主要由初始系统提示和工具模式决定，并随调用次数线性放大。此外，实验表明，现有评估方法对微小改进的检测能力有限，难以可靠区分真实性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码代理的性能评估常混杂系统设计（Harness）与模型本身的影响，但缺乏对Harness独立贡献的量化分析。尤其在实际部署中，不同Harness的成本差异巨大，而其对性能的实际影响却未被充分理解，亟需澄清其真实作用。

Method: 在SWE-bench Verified数据集上，使用五种语言模型，在三个生产级Harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode）下进行多轮测试。分别在447个常规任务和45个高难度子集上重复运行相同配置，以测量仅因重新运行带来的分数波动；同时对比不同Harness间的性能差异和成本开销。

Result: 在447个任务上，最重与最轻的Harness之间性能差距在5分以内，无显著差异；在45个高难度任务上，更换Harness与重复运行相同Harness导致任务胜负变化的概率均为13%，且胜出任务不具一致性。唯一稳定差异为成本：不同Harness间成本最高相差3倍，主要源于初始系统提示与工具定义，且随调用步数累积。缓存输入价格也影响总成本但不改变排序。

Conclusion: Harness对性能的影响远小于表面所见，其核心作用并非提升准确率，而是决定成本。现有评估体系无法可靠区分微小性能提升，建议未来应更关注成本-性能比，并采用更稳健的基准测试设计。

Abstract: A coding agent is a language model wrapped in a harness: the system prompt, the tool set, and the context management that turn a chat model into something that can work inside a repository. Production harnesses ship releases daily, vendors advertise pass-rate gains from harness changes, and leaderboards mix harnesses freely. What is rarely measured is how much the harness itself moves the score when the model is held fixed. We run five models through three production harnesses, Claude Code, mini-SWE-agent, and OpenCode, on SWE-bench Verified, and rerun the same configurations to calibrate how much a score moves when nothing changes but the run. On 447 tasks and the two models we ran there, Claude Code and mini-SWE-agent, the heaviest and the lightest harness, are equivalent within five points. On a 45-task hard subset and five models, swapping the harness flips as many tasks as rerunning the same harness, 13% in both cases, and the tasks a harness wins in one run are not the tasks it wins in the next. The one harness effect that clears the noise is a loss, not a gain: OpenCode trails by up to 9 points on the large pool, and on one model half of that gap sits in runs its output cap cut short. What the harness does decide is the bill. With the same model, the same tasks, and one price list, cost per task differs by up to 3x across harnesses. The gap is set at the first call, by the preamble of system prompt and tool schemas each harness sends with every step, and scaled by the number of steps; per-step growth and per-call tool output differ far less. The provider's price for cached input scales the bill and does not reorder it. The rerun data also give the resolution a harness comparison needs: at the discordance we observe, 45 tasks catch a 13-point gap only half the time and no gap with 80% power, and 447 tasks resolve 5 points, still coarser than the gains many harness changes claim.

</details>


### [318] [CRAFT: An Agentic Spreadsheet Form Filling System with Template Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04437)
*Leyao Gu,Yingjie Xiong,Zirui Tang,Jiangtao Zhou,Yeye He,Chunwei Liu,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种模板感知的智能体框架，通过将反思验证与受限局部修复相结合，解决电子表格填写中的错误传播问题。它通过矩形感知的槽位定位器（RASG）识别可编辑单元格，并利用标签-槽提示和受保护区域约束后续编辑，从而在保持模板结构的同时修正错误。实验表明，CRAFT在FormFillBench基准上显著优于现有方法，分别提升8.51和23.38个百分点的配对准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格填写过程中，早期编辑错误可能导致标签覆盖或字段错位，影响后续决策。传统方法将反思视为自由重生成，缺乏对错误位置的精准定位和模板状态的恢复能力，因此需要一种能够精确识别错误区域、恢复模板结构并重新定位可编辑槽位的机制。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，结合反射验证与受限局部修复；采用矩形感知的槽位定位器（RASG）识别可写单元格；利用标签-槽提示和受保护区域限制编辑范围；通过结构化反馈实现错误定位与模板状态恢复。

Result: 在FormFillBench基准上，相较于最强基线，CRAFT在Instruction-Only和Multi-File两个赛道上分别提升8.51和23.38个百分点的配对准确率；组件移除实验验证了结构判断与槽位再定位的有效性；在更换主干模型后仍保持相对优势。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过模板感知的反思与局部修复机制，有效应对电子表格填写中的错误传播问题，提升了准确性与鲁棒性，具备良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Spreadsheet form filling requires agents to consolidate external evidence, ground values to precise cells, and preserve irregular template structure. Errors in early edits can overwrite labels or misalign fields, undermining later decisions. We propose CRAFT, a template-aware agent framework that connects reflective validation to constrained local repair. Instead of treating reflection as a free-form request to regenerate the workbook, CRAFT grounds detected errors to spreadsheet regions, restores corrupted template state when necessary, and re-grounds plausible writable slots before subsequent edits. A Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder (RASG) proposes writable cells, while label-slot hints and protected regions constrain subsequent edits. We introduce FormFillBench, with 327 forms across Instruction-Only and Multi-File tracks. Compared with the strongest baselines, CRAFT improves pair accuracy by 8.51 and 23.38 percentage points on these tracks, respectively. Component-removal experiments support structural adjudication and slot re-grounding within the pipeline, and the framework retains its relative advantage among the methods evaluated with a second backbone. The code and benchmark FormFillBench are available at https://github.com/Glllllly/CRAFT.

</details>


### [319] [RAGrasp: Geometry-Semantic Template Retrieval and Grasp Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04438)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Chengxiao He,Jan Harder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGrasp 是一种基于检索增强的平面平行夹爪抓取方法，利用少量本地采集的带抓取标注的RGB-D模板，在无需重新训练的情况下实现新场景部署。系统通过自监督DINOv2特征与SAM2模型分割目标，结合几何-语义两级检索和置信度门控选择最优抓取策略，并通过掩码支持和轮廓接触约束优化抓取姿态。在真实实验中，对已见物体成功率达100%，对未见物体达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取方法依赖大规模公开或合成数据集训练，难以适应特定部署环境；而实际应用中获取大量标注数据成本高且不现实。因此需要一种能基于少量本地数据、无需重训练即可适应新环境的抓取系统。

Method: RAGrasp采用局部采集的RGB-D模板构建模板记忆库，使用DINOv2提取视觉特征并结合深度信息调用SAM2进行对象分割；通过两阶段几何-语义检索筛选最相似模板，利用置信度门控选择合适的抓取转移估计器；最后通过掩码支持与轮廓接触约束优化抓取姿态，并完成2D到3D的校准转换。

Result: 在真实世界测试中，对已见物体实现20/20的成功率，对未见物体实现19/20的成功率；系统展现出对视角和光照变化的良好鲁棒性，以及在有限本地标注下的高效部署适应能力。

Conclusion: RAGrasp实现了无需重新训练的部署自适应抓取，仅需少量本地采集的带标注模板即可在复杂环境中稳定工作，具备良好的泛化能力和实用性，为实际机器人抓取任务提供了一种高效、轻量级的解决方案。

Abstract: We present RAGrasp, a retrieval-augmented pipeline for planar parallel-jaw grasping from a compact set of locally collected, grasp-annotated RGB-D (color and depth) templates. Unlike task-specific predictors trained primarily on large public or synthetic grasp datasets, RAGrasp requires no end-to-end retraining for a new deployment.Its template memory is constructed from observations collected with the deployment camera, robot, and gripper in the target workspace, thereby aligning stored examples with the local sensing and embodiment conditions. The system uses self-supervised DINOv2 visual fea- tures together with appearance and depth cues to prompt the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2), which isolates the query object. A two-stage geometry-semantic retrieval cascade then selects a template, and a confidence gate chooses one of two grasp- transfer estimators. The transferred grasp is refined using mask- support and silhouette-contact constraints before calibrated 2D- to-3D conversion. In real-world trials, RAGrasp achieves 20/20 successful grasps on seen objects and 19/20 on unseen objects. Within the evaluated setting, the results demonstrate deployment- specific grasp adaptation from limited local annotation and tolerance to the tested viewpoint and illumination changes.

</details>


### [320] [Causally Fair Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04444)
*Patrik Okanovic,Torsten Hoefler,Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为因果公平生成（CFG）的新方法，用于在大语言模型（LLM）生成过程中识别并消除歧视性因果效应。该方法通过提取相关概念、构建参考人群和因果图，实现对特定因果路径的可选择性干预，同时保留任务所需的合理路径（如法律中的‘业务必要性’），并在适当假设下提供形式化保障。实验在三个真实世界场景和一个具有已知因果真值的合成数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成信息时可能加剧或产生基于人口特征的不公平现象，而传统统计方法无法识别这些差异的根本原因。因此需要一种能够基于因果推理来评估和缓解公平性问题的方法，尤其是在生成过程涉及多个相互关联变量且提示顺序灵活的情况下。

Method: CFG 方法首先提取与任务相关的概念，将生成过程锚定于参考人群和因果图模型；然后通过干预因果路径，有选择地移除用户指定的歧视性因果效应，同时允许被认为对任务有用且合理的路径保留，从而实现更精细的公平性控制。

Result: 在四个不同大语言模型上的实验表明，CFG 能有效减少不公平性，特别是在具有已知因果结构的合成数据集上表现良好，并在真实世界数据中显示出对人口差异的有效缓解能力。此外，该方法在理想条件下提供了形式化的公平性保障。

Conclusion: CFG 为大语言模型中的公平生成提供了一个基于因果推理的系统框架，不仅能够识别并消除不公正的因果影响，还能保留任务必需的合理性路径，是迈向负责任AI生成的重要一步。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate, complete, and transform information in settings where their outputs can shape consequential decisions, raising concerns about their impact on demographic disparities. In this context, causal inference provides a principled basis for assessing fairness, because it attributes observed disparities to the mechanisms that generated them, which a purely statistical approach cannot do even with infinite data. In LLM generation, a query may request several causally related variables, each of which is both an outcome of interest and a possible cause of other outputs, and the information supplied in the prompt need not follow a topological or a temporal order. This calls for methods that can analyze and selectively remove disparities from such a flexible generation process. In this paper we introduce Causally Fair Generation with LLMs (CFG, for short). CFG extracts relevant concepts, grounds generation in a reference population and causal diagram, and removes user-selected causal effects. CFG also allows pathways deemed justifiable for the task's utility to be retained, which is known in legal literature as business necessity. Further, we provide formal guarantees for our method when eliminating all discriminatory causal effects in the adapted population model, under appropriate causal assumptions. We evaluate CFG with four LLMs in three real-world settings based on population data and on a synthetic dataset with a known causal ground truth.

</details>


### [321] [Understanding Generative AI Use in Programming MOOCs: The Role of Course Context and Learner Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04447)
*Marina Lepp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在两门不同长度和工作量的编程MOOC中，学习者使用生成式人工智能（GenAI）工具的情况。结果显示，GenAI在两门课程中广泛采用，且使用频率和目的受课程时长与复杂度影响，而非学习者个人特征。较长课程的学习者更频繁使用GenAI进行调试和构思，但其使用与学习成果关联有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式人工智能工具如ChatGPT的普及，了解学习者如何在MOOC中整合这些工具成为重要课题，尤其在编程教育领域。本研究旨在揭示课程设计因素对GenAI使用行为的影响。

Method: 通过非参数统计方法分析两门编程MOOC的课后问卷数据，比较不同课程及学习者背景下的GenAI采纳率、使用频率和用途。

Result: GenAI使用在两门课程中均普遍，性别、年龄、教育程度和编程经验无显著差异；但长期课程参与者使用频率更高，主要用于调试和创意生成；使用频率与学习结果关联较弱。

Conclusion: 课程上下文（如时长、工作量）比学习者个体特征更能影响GenAI的使用方式。研究结果对编程教育的教学设计与使用指导具有启示意义。

Abstract: The increasing availability of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools, such as ChatGPT and code-completion assistants, raises questions about how learners integrate these tools into learning activities, particularly in MOOCs that attract diverse participant populations. This study examines the use of GenAI in two programming MOOCs taught in Estonian that differ in duration, workload, topic complexity, and assignment volume: About Programming (4 weeks, 26 expected hours, n = 187) and Introduction to Programming (8 weeks, 78 expected hours, n = 182). Post-course questionnaire data were analyzed using non-parametric statistical methods to examine self-reported adoption, usage frequency, and purposes of GenAI use across courses and learner backgrounds. The results show that GenAI adoption was widespread in both MOOCs, with no statistically significant differences by gender, age, education level, or prior programming experience. However, participants in the longer, more extensive MOOC reported significantly higher usage frequency and were more likely to use GenAI for debugging and idea generation. Reported usage frequency for code explanation and debugging was also higher in the longer course. Exploratory analyses found limited relationships between GenAI use and learning-related outcomes. The findings suggest that course context may play a greater role than learner characteristics in shaping how GenAI tools are used. These results provide implications for instructional design and guidance in programming education.

</details>


### [322] [Trinity: Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models with a Self-Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04469)
*Youngwan Lee,Yong-Ju Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trinity 是一种自演化视觉语言模型，通过单一模型扮演提问者、求解者和验证者三重角色，利用自身作为验证器（基于指数移动平均的自我验证机制），无需外部标签或裁判。它能筛选生成问题的图像相关性与答案正确性，评估求解推理过程，并在参考答案与强求解共识冲突时修正答案并惩罚提问者。该方法在无标注科学图像上训练，显著提升了 Qwen3-VL-8B 在数学视觉推理和生物科学任务上的表现，如在 SciVQR 生物子集上提升 8.6 分，MathVerse 上提升 12.8 分，且奖励机制呈现健康自洽的自强化学习曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化视觉语言模型依赖问答对之间的一致性作为奖励信号，但一致性无法准确反映问题是否基于图像、答案是否正确，也无法区分错误但一致的多数意见。因此需要更可靠的自我验证机制来提升自演化过程的质量。

Method: 提出 Trinity 框架，让单个 VLM 同时承担提问者、求解者和验证者角色；验证者为模型自身的指数移动平均（EMA）策略，实现无监督自我验证；通过验证者判断问题的图像根基性、答案正确性、推理合理性，并在参考答案与求解共识不一致时修正参考答案并惩罚提问者。

Result: Trinity 在仅使用未标注图像的情况下，使 Qwen3-VL-8B 在数学视觉推理（MathVerse）和含生物学内容的科学基准（SciVQR）上分别取得 +12.8 和 +8.6 的性能提升；其奖励机制表现出健康的自洽演化轨迹，证明模型可从无标注科学图像中自主提升科学推理能力。

Conclusion: Trinity 验证了单一模型通过自我验证机制实现自演化是可行的，能够有效提升多模态模型在科学推理任务中的能力，且无需依赖外部标注或人工评判，为构建真正自主进化的智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving vision-language models (VLMs), a form of self-improvement in which a model generates its own training data from unlabeled images, are a promising route toward agents that expand their reasoning capability in an unsupervised manner, without relying on ever-larger annotation budgets. Existing methods pair a Questioner that proposes problems with a Solver that answers them, but reward both roles mainly by agreement among sampled answers. Agreement is a weak proxy for truth: it cannot tell whether a question is grounded in the image, whether the proposed reference answer is right, or whether a confident majority is wrong in the same way. We present Trinity, in which one VLM plays three roles, Questioner, Solver, and Verifier, and the Verifier is a self-verifier: an exponential moving average (EMA) of the policy itself, requiring neither labels nor an external judge. The Verifier screens every generated question for image grounding and answer correctness before it becomes supervision, scores Solver reasoning against the image, and adjudicates disputes between the reference answer and a strong Solver consensus, correcting the reference and penalizing the Questioner when the consensus is right. Trained on images alone, Trinity improves Qwen3-VL-8B on mathematical visual reasoning and on science benchmarks with biology content, for example, +8.6 points on the biology split of SciVQR and +12.8 on MathVerse, and its reward dynamics behave as a healthy self-play curriculum should. These results suggest that a self-evolving multimodal agent can strengthen its scientific reasoning from unlabeled scientific images alone, with the model itself serving as the verifier.

</details>


### [323] [Reactivating Alignment: Defending LLMs from Jailbreaks via Intention-Aware Input-Output Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04470)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTINEL是一种即插即用的生成阶段对抗越狱攻击防御方法，将防御问题重构为意图提取任务。其核心思想是利用指令微调大模型在输入-输出间强语义一致性特性，通过匹配语义对齐的输入-输出区域，提取揭示恶意意图的子序列，并基于拒绝方向投影评分有害性，在必要时中断生成。实验表明，SENTINEL在多个大模型上将越狱成功率降至接近5%，同时保持低过拒率，且对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法多依赖输入扰动或有害输出抑制，但未能建模恶意意图的实际位置，导致防护脆弱且易产生过度拒绝。

Method: SENTINEL通过识别输入与输出中语义对齐的区域，提取可能揭示恶意意图的子序列；利用拒绝方向投影对这些子序列进行有害性评分，并在风险过高时提前终止生成过程。

Result: 在HarmBench基准测试中，SENTINEL将多种大模型的越狱成功率降至约5%，同时显著降低过拒率；对自适应攻击也表现出良好鲁棒性；机制分析显示其将越狱特征从对齐盲区重新分布至对齐区域。

Conclusion: SENTINEL通过语义一致性建模实现更精准、鲁棒的越狱防御，有效缓解了传统方法的过拒与脆弱问题，为大模型安全提供新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that conceal harmful intent within complex adversarial prompts. Existing defenses primarily rely on input perturbation or harmful-output suppression, but they rarely model where malicious intent resides, resulting in brittle protection and excessive over-refusal.
  We propose SENTINEL, a plug-and-play, generation-time jailbreak defense that reframes mitigation as an intent extraction problem. Our key insight is that instruction-tuned LLMs exhibit strong input--output semantic consistency: regardless of jailbreak complexity, generated outputs tend to align with the attacker's true intent. SENTINEL exploits this property by matching semantically aligned input--output regions to extract intention-revealing subsequences, scores these subsequences using refusal-direction projections to estimate harmfulness, and halts generation when necessary. Experiments on HarmBench across multiple LLMs show that SENTINEL reduces jailbreak success rates to close to 5\% while maintaining low over-refusal. We further demonstrate robustness to adaptive attacks and provide a mechanistic interpretation: SENTINEL re-distributes jailbreak features from alignment blind spots to aligned regions.

</details>


### [324] [Semantic Causal-Factor Inference from Aviation Incident Narratives Using A Variational Autoencoder with Cosine-Similarity-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04472)
*AZIIDA NANYONGA1,HASSAN WASSWA,UGUR TURHAN,KEITH FRANCIS JOINER,GRAHAM WILD*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合自然语言处理与变分自编码器（VAE）的语义因果因素推断框架，用于从航空事故调查报告中自动提取因果信息。通过将调查文本和专家判定的可能原因转化为数值表示，并利用编码器映射到概率潜在空间，解码器则基于Kullback-Leibler散度与余弦相似性重建目标因果表示，实现了对因果关系的有效学习。在20,919份美国国家运输安全委员会（NTSB）报告上测试，预测结果与专家标注的因果表示平均余弦相似度达0.786（标准差0.120），且生成的可解释关键词能有效反映报告中的因果信息。该方法展示了概率潜在表示学习在辅助提取航空安全叙事中因果信息方面的潜力，同时保持专家判断作为正式因果认定的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故调查报告包含大量非结构化文本，其中蕴含着事故原因和影响因素的关键证据，但这些信息常分散于冗长复杂的叙述中，手动提取效率低且易遗漏。因此需要一种自动化方法来识别和提取因果信息，以支持更高效的安全分析与预防。

Method: 提出基于变分自编码器（VAE）的语义因果因素推断框架。将调查报告与对应可能原因转换为数值表示；编码器将文本表示映射至概率潜在空间；解码器根据潜在表示重建可能原因表示，训练目标结合了Kullback-Leibler散度与余弦相似性，以增强语义一致性。

Result: 在20,919份美国NTSB调查报告上的测试表明，预测的因果表示与专家标注的表示之间平均余弦相似度为0.786（标准差0.120），模型能够生成具有可解释性的关键词，准确反映报告中的因果信息。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了概率潜在表示学习在自动提取航空安全叙事中因果信息方面的有效性，能够在不取代专家判断的前提下，实现对复杂文本中因果关系的高效、可解释的建模与推断。

Abstract: Aviation accident and incident investigations generate extensive unstructured textual information containing evidence relevant to the causes and contributing factors of safety occurrences. Automatically extracting such information is challenging because causal evidence may be distributed across long and complex investigation narratives. This study proposes a semantic causal-factor inference framework combining natural language processing with a variational autoencoder (VAE) to learn the relationship between aviation investigation narratives and expert-reported probable causes. Investigation narratives and their corresponding probable causes are transformed into numerical representations, after which the encoder maps narrative representations to a probabilistic latent space. The decoder estimates representations of the corresponding probable causes and is trained using an objective that combines Kullback-Leibler divergence with cosine-similarity-based semantic reconstruction. The framework was evaluated using 20,919 finalized U.S. National Transportation Safety Board investigation reports from 2005 to 2020. On the held-out test set, the predicted and expert-reported probable-cause representations achieved a mean cosine similarity of 0.786 (SD = 0.120). The predicted representations also yielded interpretable terms associated with causal information in the reports. The results demonstrate the potential of probabilistic latent representation learning for AI-assisted extraction of causal information from aviation safety narratives while retaining expert investigation as the basis for formal causal determination.

</details>


### [325] [InferOpt: Constrained Multi-Objective Search for LLM Inference Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04473)
*Qi Chen,Yingying Cheng,Zhaoyi Sun,Li Zhou,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InferOpt 是一个可复用的推理配置搜索框架，将 LLM 推理参数调优建模为受约束的多目标黑箱优化问题。它通过冻结采样的代理集进行搜索，提前剔除超预算候选方案，自适应收紧预算，并在全规模数据集上重新验证帕累托最优解。该框架成功应用于 28 维连续 KV 空间（Qwen2.5-7B）和 26 维离散 MoE 空间（DeepSeek-V2-Lite），在减少缓存与专家数量的同时保持性能接近原始模型。相比随机搜索、NSGA-II 和 MOTPE，InferOpt 在代理集上的超体积表现最佳，且在 KV 空间中降低缓存保留率，在 MoE 空间中更接近无压缩参考点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理配置涉及大量超参数（如每层 KV 保留、专家数量等），现有方法依赖特定机制的启发式规则，仅能返回单一工作点，难以扩展到分层搜索空间。需要一种高效、可扩展的自动化调优框架以平衡资源消耗与性能表现。

Method: 将推理配置视为受约束的多目标黑箱优化问题；构建 InferOpt 框架，仅需变量边界、确定性资源成本和评估接口；在冻结的采样代理集上搜索，预先过滤超预算候选，自适应调整预算，并在真实数据集上重验证帕累托解。

Result: 在 KV 空间中，后预填充剪枝使 16K 缓存减少 64.4%，TPOT 下降 22.9%-48.5%；分层预算优化比均匀分配提升 7.3% 和 14.0% 性能。在 MoE 空间中，所搜顶 k 调度移除 43.0% 的路由对，性能损失仅 0.59 分，优于匹配预算基线。在所有对比算法中，InferOpt 在代理集超体积、缓存保留率和专家数量方面均表现最优。

Conclusion: InferOpt 提供了一种高效、可扩展的多目标推理配置优化框架，能够自动搜索复杂分层空间，显著降低资源开销并逼近最优性能，适用于大规模语言模型的部署优化。

Abstract: Serving an LLM means setting dozens of inference-time knobs, from per-layer KV retention to per-layer expert counts. Practice sets them with mechanism-specific heuristics that return a single operating point and do not scale to layer-wise search spaces. We recast inference configuration as constrained multi-objective black-box optimization and build InferOpt, a reusable search framework that requires only variable bounds, a deterministic resource cost, and an evaluation hook. InferOpt searches on a frozen sampled proxy set, rejects over-budget candidates before any model call, tightens the budget adaptively, and re-validates Pareto representatives on full-scale dataset. One pipeline covers a 28-dimensional continuous KV space (Qwen2.5-7B) and a 26-dimensional discrete MoE space (DeepSeek-V2-Lite). On KV, post-prefill pruning cuts the 16K cache by 64.4% and TPOT by 22.9--48.5%, and the searched layer-wise budget by InferOpt beats a matched uniform budget by 7.3% and 14.0% of the Full KV reference points. On MoE, a searched top-k schedule by InferOpt removes 43.0% of routed token--expert pairs while staying within 0.59 points of the default, closer than the matched-budget baselines. Against Random Search, NSGA-II, and MOTPE, InferOpt leads on both spaces, taking the best proxy hypervolume and the lowest retention on KV and staying closest to the uncompressed reference at the lowest experts on MoE.

</details>


### [326] [VCLMU: Mechanism-Centric Virtual Cell World Modeling for Perturbation Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04475)
*Yuwei Miao,Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Scott Oloff,Junzhou Huang,Tianyu Cui,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机制中心的虚拟细胞世界模型VCLMU，通过将细胞状态表示为一组潜在机制单元（LMUs），将基因扰动视为对这些潜在状态的操作。每个LMU结合了基于多模态生物证据的可重用身份和观察特定的状态，使扰动能够诱导机制特异性的随机状态转移，从而解码转录响应。模型通过两阶段预训练，在约20万伪批量扰动数据和基因对齐的单细胞扰动数据上进行训练。在六个不包含扰动的基准测试中，VCLMU在扰动特异性响应恢复方面持续优于强基线，同时保持了良好的全局响应准确性。通过分析学习到的LMUs与扰动响应之间的富集关系，表明它们捕捉到了结构化的生物应答程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常直接从未扰动的分子谱和扰动映射到结果，但未显式表示干预引起的潜在细胞状态转变。本文旨在通过引入机制中心的潜在状态表示，更准确地建模和预测细胞对遗传扰动的响应，提升可解释性与预测性能。

Method: 提出并训练了机制中心的虚拟细胞世界模型VCLMU，使用潜变量机制单元（LMUs）表示细胞状态，每个LMU结合可重用的身份和观察特定状态；通过两阶段预训练策略，先在伪批量扰动数据上训练，再在单细胞扰动数据上微调；采用机制特异性随机转移机制来模拟扰动对细胞状态的影响，并解码最终转录响应。

Result: 在六个独立于扰动的基准测试中，VCLMU在扰动特异性响应恢复方面显著优于多个强基线模型，同时保持了较高的全局响应预测准确性。对学习到的LMUs进行功能富集分析显示其与生物应答程序高度相关，证明其能有效捕捉结构性的生物学响应机制。

Conclusion: 机制级潜在状态转移是构建虚拟细胞模型以预测和解释细胞对生物干预响应的一种有效范式，支持了基于机制的建模在系统生物学中的重要价值。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations is a central capability for virtual cells and a key step toward computational modeling of biological interventions. Most existing models directly map an unperturbed molecular profile and perturba- tion to the resulting observation without explicitly representing the latent cellular transition induced by the intervention. We introduce a mechanism-centric virtual cell world model that represents cellular state as a set of Latent Mechanism Units (LMUs) and treats genetic perturbations as actions on these latent states. Each LMU combines a reusable identity grounded in multimodal biological evidence with an observation-specific state, allowing a perturbation to induce mechanism- specific stochastic transitions before decoding the resulting transcriptional response. We train VCLMU through two-stage pretraining, first on around 200K pseudo-bulk perturbation profiles and then on gene-aligned single-cell perturbation data. Across six perturbation-disjoint benchmarks, VCLMU consistently improves perturbation- specific response recovery over strong baselines while maintaining competitive global response accuracy. We further analyze learned LMUs through enrichment between perturbation responses and LMU gene sets and show that they capture structured biological response programs. These results support mechanism-level latent state transition as a useful formulation for virtual cell models that aim to predict and interpret cellular responses to biological interventions.

</details>


### [327] [ManifoldCache: Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration via Constraint Manifold Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04510)
*Prashant Pandey,Devineni Sri Venkatraya Chowdary,Brejesh Lall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ManifoldCache的新方法，用于加速约束流形扩散模型（CMDMs）的推理。该方法基于条件得分在正交方向上的分解：法向分量确保满足约束，切向分量在流形内导航。证明了噪声调度中点是安全缓存边界的严格界限——在此前缓存可保证有界误差，而在此后缓存则必然导致模式混淆。深层网络块能提供更大的认证缓存步长，因得分分解通过块雅可比矩阵传播。整个方案无需训练、无需数据校准，具有理论保障的加速性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速方法在处理具有硬几何约束的科学生成任务时均失效：量化占用过多内存，剪枝破坏约束保真度，快速常微分方程求解器使轨迹偏离约束流形，特征缓存启发式方法忽略约束几何，导致高噪声状态下模式混淆。

Method: 提出约束流形扩散模型（CMDMs）统一框架，并设计ManifoldCache加速器。其核心是利用条件得分的正交分解结构，识别出噪声调度中的安全缓存边界。通过理论分析证明：在中点之前缓存可控制误差，之后则必然引入模式混淆。同时证明更深网络允许更长的认证缓存步长。

Result: ManifoldCache实现了无需训练、无需校准数据的加速，且具有严格的理论保证。在多种科学生成任务（如分子构象、蛋白质骨架设计、晶体结构预测等）上显著提升采样效率，同时保持约束满足性与分布保真度。

Conclusion: ManifoldCache是首个从第一原理出发为约束流形扩散模型设计的训练自由、数据自由加速器，通过揭示得分结构的正交分解特性，建立了安全缓存的理论边界，为科学生成中的高效推理提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models for structured scientific generation must produce samples satisfying hard geometric constraints imposed by physics, chemistry, or biology, yet inference in these settings is prohibitively slow, demanding hundreds to thousands of neural-function evaluations per sample. We unify eight state-of-the-art models spanning medical volumetrics, molecular conformations, protein backbone design, crystal structure prediction, and multi-view 3D scenes under a single abstraction, Constraint-Manifold Diffusion Models (CMDMs), in which the target distribution is supported on a manifold defined by an externally specified constraint map. All existing acceleration families fail on this class: quantization exhausts memory on high-dimensional volumetric operators; pruning breaks constraint fidelity; fast ODE solvers allow trajectories to drift off the constraint manifold; and feature-caching heuristics are blind to constraint geometry, inducing mode confusion in the high-noise regime. We introduce ManifoldCache, the first training-free, data-free accelerator designed from first principles for CMDMs. The key insight is that the conditional score decomposes orthogonally into a normal component, which enforces constraint satisfaction, and a tangential component, which navigates within the manifold. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the noise-schedule midpoint is a sharp safe-caching boundary: caching before it incurs provably bounded error, while caching after it guarantees a strictly positive fraction of trajectories suffer mode confusion, a gap that persists up to the boundary. We further prove that deeper network blocks admit provably larger certified cache strides within the safe phase, as a consequence of the score decomposition propagating through block Jacobians. The resulting schedule requires no calibration data, along with zero training overhead.

</details>


### [328] [Towards Credible Agent-Based Policy Simulations: Disentangling Opportunities and Preferences in a Financial Inclusion Case Study of Egypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04515)
*Alba Aguilera,Georgina Curto,Nardine Osman,Ahmed Al-Awah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于能力方法的通用建模框架，用于构建可信的政策模拟，通过数据和领域专家知识实现。以埃及金融包容性问题为例，利用基于代理的模型，结合真实数据进行初始化与校准，区分制度与社会障碍的作用，并分析不同群体的动机与优先级，为政策制定提供透明且分组特定的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升基于代理的政策模拟在支持决策时的可信度，确保其假设、参数和输出具有实证基础并足够准确。

Method: 采用基于能力方法的通用建模框架，结合真实数据对模型进行两阶段处理：初始化（构建代表性合成人口）和校准（估计行为参数），通过固定可行性参数和校准偏好参数来分析制度与社会障碍的影响。

Result: 成功区分了制度与社会障碍的作用，揭示了不同群体在机会与实际结果之间的差距，提供了可解释的、分组特定的政策驱动因素分析，增强了模型与现实系统及利益相关者之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该研究推进了政策模拟的可信度与实用性，加强了模型与现实世界及使用者之间的关联，为未来政策仿真提供了可复现的方法与代码支持。

Abstract: Credibility is a central topic for agent-based models intended to support policy-making. Simulations must not only represent the target scenarios and their core dynamics but also demonstrate that their assumptions, parameters, and outputs are empirically grounded and sufficiently accurate for their intended use. This paper addresses this challenge by presenting a general modelling framework, aligned with the Capability Approach, for building credible policy simulations that rely on data and domain-expert knowledge. It then demonstrates how it can be contextualised and implemented to study the social challenge of financial inclusion in Egypt, building on an agent-based model that represents heterogeneous individuals and firms behaving according to their financial states, barriers, opportunities, and preferences. The model is fitted to real-world data in two stages, initialisation and calibration, which respectively build representative synthetic populations and estimate behavioural parameters. By fixing the feasibility parameters, which determine agents' opportunities, and calibrating preference parameters across different population groups, we are able to distinguish and analyse the role of institutional and social barriers in the system, as well as the role of agents' motivations and priorities. This calibration stage provides transparent and group-specific hypotheses about the drivers of observed financial-inclusion gaps, which can further be analysed as gaps between agents' opportunities and realised outcomes, a very relevant insight for policy-making. This paper is thus a step towards improving the credibility and usefulness of policy simulations, strengthening the relationship between the model, the real target system, and the stakeholders who will use it. The code is available at: \url{https://www.comses.net/codebase-release/df8383cb-f49b-4f09-8cce-73603b59adcc/}.

</details>


### [329] [EvoCast: Reliable Autonomous Research Agents for Iterative Forecasting Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04517)
*Kaipeng Xu,Xianli Yan,Yan Wang,Xiang Liu,Shan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoCast是一个全自主的研究代理系统，用于迭代式时间序列预测架构演化。它通过执行机制消融建立并诊断特定任务的基线，然后基于数据特征、诊断结果、前期回合和失败记录生成有证据支持的研究方向。其核心设计为认知与权限分离：大语言模型（LLM）代理负责开放式假设生成和代码实现，而确定性程序权威则控制源码编辑边界、标准评估和模型晋升决策。实验结果表明，EvoCast在复杂架构修改中实现了更高的实施成功率和更低的代理端令牌/时间成本，并在三个真实世界预测案例中开发出优于选定基线、强大预测模型和代理基线的任务特定架构。代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AutoML方法受限于预定义搜索空间，而通用大语言模型研究代理缺乏对实验协议和模型晋升的可靠控制。因此需要一种能够自主、高效且可信赖地完成时间序列预测模型架构演化的系统。

Method: EvoCast采用认知-权威分离的设计，将开放式假设生成和代码实现交由大语言模型代理执行，同时由确定性程序权威控制源码编辑范围、标准化评估流程及模型晋升决策。通过多轮迭代积累实验结果作为证据，指导后续研究方向。

Result: EvoCast在三个真实世界预测案例中成功完成了复杂的架构修改，表现出更高的实现成功率，更低的代理端令牌消耗与时间成本，并构建出超越基线模型、强预测模型以及同类代理基线的任务专用架构。

Conclusion: EvoCast展示了在无需人工干预的情况下，自动完成时间序列预测模型架构演化的能力，具备高效率、高可靠性与强适应性，为自动化机器学习研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep time-series forecasting models have rapidly diversified, yet adapting them to a specific task still requires extensive expert effort in model selection, mechanism diagnosis, architecture design, implementation, and evaluation. Existing AutoML methods are constrained by predefined search spaces, while general-purpose LLM research agents lack reliable control over experimental protocols and model promotion. We introduce EvoCast, a fully autonomous research-agent system for iterative forecasting architecture evolution. EvoCast first establishes and diagnoses a task-specific baseline through executed mechanism ablations, then generates evidence-grounded research directions from dataset characteristics, diagnostic results, prior rounds, and failure records. Its central design, cognition-authority separation, assigns open-ended hypothesis generation and code implementation to LLM agents, while deterministic program authorities control source-edit boundaries, canonical evaluation, and promotion decisions. Experimental outcomes are accumulated as evidence to guide subsequent rounds. Results show that EvoCast completes complex architecture modifications with higher implementation success and lower agent-side token/time cost, and develops task-specific architectures that outperform selected baselines, strong forecasting models, and agent baselines in three real-world forecasting cases. The code is available at https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast.

</details>


### [330] [Action-Consequence Alignment for Reliable Planning and Self-Improving in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04539)
*Jinping Wang1,Zhiqiang Gao,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了世界模型在预测动作后果时的可靠性问题，发现即使预测误差低，模型也可能因动作与感知结果不对应而导致规划失误。为此提出动作后果对齐（ACA）机制，通过惩罚局部搜索动作相对于真实动作的预测误差优势，提升规划准确性。实验表明，ACA能有效改善多种环境下的规划性能并减少目标误差，且引导数据收集优于随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能实现低预测误差，但缺乏对动作与后果间对应关系的保证，导致规划不可靠；受神经科学中自搔痒实验启发，研究动作与感知结果之间的对应性对规划的影响。

Method: 提出动作后果对齐（ACA）训练目标，通过比较真实动作与附近替代动作的预测误差，惩罚后者的优势，无需额外模型组件或环境交互即可增强模型对真实动作后果的敏感性。

Result: ACA显著提升了不同环境中的规划表现，降低了实际目标误差；基于ACA的数据收集策略优于随机局部采样，证明其在自我改进中的有效性。

Conclusion: 动作后果对齐是连接预测学习与可靠规划的重要实践原则，有助于构建更稳健的世界模型。

Abstract: Latent world models learn to predict observed transitions, yet low prediction error alone does not guarantee reliable planning. Inspired by self tickling experiments in neuroscience showing that disrupting motor sensory correspondence increases prediction mismatch, we examine whether learned world models preserve an analogous action consequence correspondence.The results show nearby alternatives can receive lower prediction errors despite producing physical outcomes farther from the recorded target. With that future treated as a goal, this reveals a concrete prediction planning mismatch: the model assigns a lower cost to an action that achieves the target less accurately. To mitigate this gap, we introduce Action Consequence Alignment (ACA), a training objective that complements forward prediction by penalizing the prediction error advantage of locally searched alternatives over factual actions without additional model components or environment interactions during training. The same principle can also guide additional data collection for self improvement. We demonstrate that across diverse environments and evaluation settings, ACA improves planning performance and reduces real goal error, while ACA guided data collection outperforms random local sampling. These results support action consequence alignment as a practical principle for bridging predictive learning and reliable planning.

</details>


### [331] [Autonomous Structuring of Radiology Reports Across Modalities at Archive Scale Using an Open-Weight Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04541)
*Friedrich Puttkammer,Fabian Drexel,Marlene Fritzsche,Era Stambollxhiu,Miriam Kumpf,Lena Schmitzer,Lea Schumann,Lina Xu,Johannes Moll,Jannik Lübberstedt,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Anirudh Narayanan,Hartmut Häntze,Renato Cuocolo,Antonios Billis,Alexander Löser,Jawed Nawabi,Marcus R. Makowski,Cosmin I. Bercea,Shahrooz Faghihroohi,Lisa C. Adams,Keno K. Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一种开源大语言模型（LLM）流水线，可将整个自由文本放射科报告档案无监督地转换为结构化报告。该流水线在150个分层组织的模板基础上运行，使用gpt-oss-120B模型在单个GPU上完成模板选择与填充。在914份随机抽样的报告中，最优模板集选择率达74.4%（95% CI: 71.5%, 77.1%），适当模板集达82.3%；放射科和CT报告的语义相似度分别为0.95和0.97，居民修正时仅修改了88.7%的字段，1.0%至1.5%的报告存在未支持内容。最终，218万份档案报告中有96.5%被成功结构化，处理速度达每小时1,258份。结论：该流水线实现了多模态报告档案的全自动高保真结构化，但多区域报告仍是主要错误来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射科报告多为自由文本，难以高效检索与分析。为实现大规模报告结构化以提升临床与科研效率，亟需一种无需人工干预的自动化方法。开源大语言模型提供了潜力，但其在真实世界医学数据上的应用仍需验证。因此，本研究旨在构建一个完全自动化的开放权重模型流水线，用于将全量历史放射科报告转化为结构化格式。

Method: 采用150个分层模板设计结构化框架，基于gpt-oss-120B模型通过三步受限解码机制选择最适模板，并在本地GPU上完成填充。模板选择性能由专家标注评估，结构化质量由五名住院医师逐字段校对。流水线在第二中心的完整报告档案（2010–2025年）上进行端到端测试，评估指标包括模板匹配率、语义相似度、字段变更比例及未支持内容比例。

Result: 最优模板选择率为74.4%（95% CI: 71.5%, 77.1%），适当模板选择率为82.3%（95% CI: 79.7%, 84.6%）。单区域报告准确率较高（87.7%），多区域报告较低（54.1%）。放射科与CT报告的宏观语义相似度分别为0.95和0.97。5名居民仅修改了88.7%的字段，表明输出质量高。未支持内容占比低（1.0%–1.5%）。总2,186,982份报告中，96.5%完成结构化，生成2,401,544份结构化报告，处理速率达每小时1,258份。

Conclusion: 该开源大语言模型流水线可在无须人工干预的情况下，高效、高保真地完成多模态放射科报告档案的结构化。尽管多区域报告仍是主要误差来源，整体系统表现优异，具备大规模部署潜力。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate an open-weight large language model (LLM) pipeline that converts an entire archive of free-text radiology reports into structured reports without human oversight. Materials and Methods: In this retrospective study, a pipeline with 150 hierarchically organized templates was developed at one center and tested at a second center on reports from 2010 to 2025. The open-weight model gpt-oss-120B selects the template in three constrained-decoding steps and fills it on one local graphics processing unit. Template selection was scored against expert labels on 914 randomly sampled reports of five modalities, structuring quality on 920 radiography and CT reports corrected field by field by five residents. The pipeline then processed the complete archive of the second center. Proportions are reported with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Results: An optimal template set was selected for 74.4% of reports (680 of 914; 95% CI: 71.5%, 77.1%) and an appropriate set for 82.3% (752 of 914; 95% CI: 79.7%, 84.6%), 87.7% for single-region and 54.1% for multi-region reports. Macro semantic textual similarity between output and corrected reference was 0.95 for radiography and 0.97 for CT, residents left 88.7% of 24,638 fields unchanged, and unsupported content was flagged in 1.0% and 1.5% of reports. Of 2,186,982 archive reports, 96.5% received structured output, 2,401,544 structured reports, at 1,258 reports per hour on one graphics processing unit. Conclusion: An open-weight LLM pipeline structured a complete multimodality report archive without human oversight with high content fidelity. Multi-region reports remained the main source of template errors.

</details>


### [332] [Decide, Ask, or Defer: Clinical LLMs under Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04542)
*Mingzhan Yang,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出DECIDE/ASK/DEFER框架，以更精细地评估临床大模型在诊断决策中的行为，相较于传统的二元决策（DECIDE/ABSTAIN），新框架能揭示模型在信息获取与决策间的选择差异。在200个匹配的临床证据场景下，Qwen、Gemini和GPT表现出显著的行为差异，137个场景中存在分歧。结果显示，信息选择比诊断准确性更能影响自主决策概率；同时，三类动作策略虽可减少错误决策，但也导致部分正确决策被放弃，表明安全与自主性之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统二元决策框架（DECIDE/ABSTAIN）将多种非决策状态合并，未明确评估信息获取过程，可能导致对模型决策能力的误判。为更真实反映临床决策中的信息需求与判断逻辑，需引入更细致的决策分类机制。

Method: 构建基于DDXPlus的200个匹配临床证据状态，采用盲法协议防止模型利用证据完整性元数据，评估Qwen、Gemini和GPT在DECIDE/ASK/DEFER与基准的DECIDE/ABSTAIN框架下的行为表现，并通过配对分析比较信息选择与诊断正确性的相对影响。

Result: 模型在相同证据下表现出显著行为差异，在137/200案例中不一致；信息选择比诊断正确性更显著影响自主决策倾向；三类动作策略虽提升安全性，但牺牲了部分正确决策，揭示安全与自主性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 分离信息获取与临床医生延迟决策的行为分析，能够暴露二元弃权框架所隐藏的模型决策特征，但并未带来统一的性能提升，表明临床大模型的决策策略需在安全性与自主性之间进行精细化权衡。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs must decide not only what diagnosis to produce, but also whether the available evidence is sufficient for autonomous decision making. Binary DECIDE/ABSTAIN formulations merge distinct non decision states and do not explicitly evaluate information acquisition. We introduce a DECIDE/ASK/DEFER formulation together with a blinded protocol that prevents models from using evidence completeness metadata. We evaluate Qwen, Gemini, and GPT on 200 matched clinical evidence states constructed from DDXPlus. The models show substantial differences in action selection under identical evidence, with disagreement in 137 of 200 states. For Qwen, a matched targeted versus random analysis shows that selected information changes the likelihood of a subsequent autonomous decision more clearly than diagnostic correctness. Its matched DECIDE/ABSTAIN baseline further reveals a safety autonomy tradeoff: the three action policy rescues some erroneous autonomous decisions but also removes some correct autonomous deci sions. These results show that separating information acquisition from clinician deferral exposes behavior that binary abstention hides, without yielding a uniformly improved decision policy.

</details>


### [333] [Label Agreement Does Not Measure Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04544)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Bradley Thomas Baker,Theodore J. LaGrow,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在神经影像学数据标注中，基于代理的大型语言模型（LLM）管道在标签一致性度量上的局限性。尽管整体得分看似健康，但实际存在多种未被传统指标捕捉的失败模式。研究通过在COBRE和FBIRN两个精神分裂症与对照组数据集上进行独立评估，发现标签一致性、行动选择一致性和证据敏感性等维度可分离，且各自表现差异显著。例如，引入上游建议仅轻微提升标签一致性（0.857→0.870），但对行动选择的一致性提升巨大（0.409→0.830）；部分模型仍会遗漏关键字段（10%~33%）；而当证据改变时，重复先前答案的代理得分从完美降至零。此外，下游的数据行顺序错误严重削弱诊断信号，但现有指标无法反映此问题。为此，作者提出将各属性独立测量，并设置控制条件以触发人工介入，从而暴露并路由故障。然而，由于参考标签基于规则而非真实正确性，一致性不等于正确性。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理式LLM标注系统依赖标签一致性作为主要评估指标，但该指标无法揭示代理是否具备提出标签的正当性、输出是否完整、或结果是否随证据变化而合理调整。这些隐性失败在聚合分数下被掩盖，导致系统不可靠。因此需要更全面的评估框架来识别并暴露这些隐藏缺陷。

Method: 在COBRE和FBIRN两个独立神经影像数据集上，分别测量标签一致性、行动选择一致性、输出完整性及证据敏感性四个维度。通过对比代理在不同输入条件下的行为变化（如引入上游建议、改变证据内容、调整数据顺序），分析各维度的表现及其相互独立性。同时设计控制机制，将每项评估结果作为决策门控，一旦不达标即触发人工干预。

Result: 标签一致性与行动选择一致性、输出完整性、证据敏感性之间无强相关性，各维度表现独立。引入上游建议对标签一致性影响微小，但大幅提高行动选择一致性；部分模型仍会丢失关键字段；代理在证据变动后无法自适应，得分骤降；下游行顺序错误严重破坏诊断性能，但未被任何现有指标检测到。通过独立测量并设置控制门控，可有效暴露失败，实现故障可见与可路由。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不能代表代理的授权能力或输出质量。必须将多个评估维度拆分测量，结合控制机制，才能真正发现并管理代理系统的潜在失效。即使如此，因参考标签为规则生成，其一致性仍不代表正确性。未来需建立更接近真实性的评估基准。

Abstract: Many groups now delegate label ontology and metadata harmonization to agentic LLM pipelines. We built one and audited it. Our aggregate scores looked healthy, but the pipeline kept failing in ways they did not show, so we set out to find what they hid. Label agreement asks whether a proposed label matches a reference. It does not ask whether the agent was entitled to propose it, whether the output was complete enough to act on, or whether the label moved when the evidence moved. We measured those three separately on COBRE and FBIRN, two schizophrenia and control neuroimaging cohorts from different consortia, and they come apart, from label agreement and from each other. Showing the agent an upstream proposal barely moves label agreement, 0.857 to 0.870, while agreement on the chosen action doubles, 0.409 to 0.830. Output that parses as JSON still drops a required field on 10% of one model's cases and 33% of the other's. And an agent that replays its first answer scores perfectly on original cases and zero once we change the evidence that decides them. Downstream, a row-order error that none of these metrics reports erases most of the diagnostic signal. So we measure these properties apart, pair each with a control, and gate commitment on the result, which makes failures visible and easy to route to a person. None of this prevents failure. Our reference labels are rule-derived, so agreement with them means consistency, not correctness. Code is available at https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization.

</details>


### [334] [$\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$: Guiding Agent Evolution from Pattern Recognition to Solution Invention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04555)
*Wenyin Liu,Yiheng Huang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TRIZ^a，一种结合TRIZ创新理论与大模型驱动的智能体演化搜索的通用研发自动化范式。通过TRIZ的40条发明原则和矛盾矩阵，实现有理论指导的创意生成，替代随机或无约束的变异。引入功能信息（FI）与TRIZ理想度，在统一的信息尺度上衡量有用与有害功能，并通过硬门控机制区分推广与性能优化。在网络安全领域验证，涵盖PowerDuck GOOSE、CICIoT2023和CIC-DDoS2019数据集，采用配对重跑协议与指纹识别、硬门控验证，分别获得+2.88、+4.23、+0.15的绝对F1提升；45活动的完整CICIoT2023实验进一步将宏观F1从0.8325提升至0.8483，但未通过冻结推广门控。所有结果均可追溯至矛盾识别、TRIZ原则选择、代码变换、评估指标及推广决策全过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R&D自动化依赖随机或无结构的变异，缺乏可解释性与方向性。在快速演化的网络安全等复杂领域，亟需一种兼具理论引导、可解释性和可追踪性的自动化创新方法。

Method: 融合TRIZ的40条发明原则与矛盾矩阵，构建理论指导的智能体演化搜索框架；引入功能信息（FI）与理想度概念，在统一信息尺度上量化功能价值；通过硬门控机制确保推广行为与性能优化分离；全程可追溯设计，实现从矛盾识别到代码生成与决策的闭环流程。

Result: 在三个网络安全数据集上实现显著的F1提升：PowerDuck GOOSE (+2.88pp)，CICIoT2023 (+4.23pp)，CIC-DDoS2019 (+0.15pp)；45活动的完整实验使宏观F1提升至0.8483，虽未通过推广门控，但验证了系统持续优化能力；所有结果具备完全可追溯性。

Conclusion: TRIZ^a成功实现了理论驱动、可解释、可追踪的研发自动化，尤其适用于对抗性强、演化迅速的复杂系统优化任务，为智能研发提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ (TRIZ exponentiated by an agent), a general R\&D automation paradigm that combines TRIZ inventive theory with LLM-driven agent evolutionary search. TRIZ's 40 inventive principles and contradiction matrix provide structured, explainable directions for solution generation, replacing random or untyped mutation with theory-guided ideation. Functional information (FI), operationalized under a frozen reference contract, is combined with TRIZ Ideality to measure useful and harmful function on a commensurable information scale, while hard gates keep promotion distinct from metric improvement. We validate $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ in cybersecurity--an adversarial and rapidly evolving domain--on PowerDuck GOOSE, CICIoT2023, and CIC-DDoS2019. Under paired-rerun protocols with protocol fingerprinting and hard-gate validation, the legacy experiments yield absolute F1 improvements of $+2.88$, $+4.23$, and $+0.15$ percentage points, respectively. A completed 45-activity CICIoT2023 campaign further increases macro-F1 from $0.8325$ to $0.8483$, but does not pass its frozen promotion gate. Every result remains traceable from contradiction identification and TRIZ principle selection to code transformation, evaluation metrics, and promotion decision.

</details>


### [335] [Recursive Improvement of a Differentiable Scientific Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04561)
*Pengcheng Hou,Xiaojun Tan,Sihan Hu,Ruisi Wang,Shuo Chen,Lei Wang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由AI代理驱动的可微分科学软件演化环境，通过统一的微分接口实现跨20个物理、化学和生物建模包的自动微分扩展。研究需求引导开发，结合独立导数检验、工作流测试与性能评估验证软件质量，推动可复用资源积累。实证表明该方法显著提升梯度计算效率，支持复杂参数估计，并通过物理反馈优化量子控制与热设计任务，实现衍生计算的准确性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可微分编程在异构科学软件生态中推广受限于手动实现导数、接口集成与质量评估的高成本。传统方法难以满足跨领域科研对自动化、可复用导数能力的需求，亟需一种系统化机制将AI编码代理的能力转化为实际可用的科学计算工具。

Method: 构建一个集成需求识别、开发与质量评估的闭环环境；采用统一差异化接口暴露可重用导数规则；基于研究任务需求驱动开发流程；通过独立导数校验、工作流测试和性能基准评估实现代码质量保障；支持迭代优化与资源共享。

Result: 成功在20个科学计算包中实现自动微分扩展；多个研究工作流展示跨任务复用能力；相比有限差分法显著降低梯度计算开销；完成完整参数估计；修复不可微工作流、修正科学可观测量导数、消除冗余计算；量子控制与热设计案例通过物理反馈优化目标函数，提升设计质量并复用已有导数。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一种实用的可微分编程扩展框架，能够有效推动现有科学软件向可微分方向演进，并以递归改进方式组织AI代理，构建共享的、可持续进化的计算生态系统。

Abstract: Differentiable programming connects scientific computation with gradient-based inference, learning and design.
